peṇkaḷ puraṭci
muṉṉuri
peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷukku aṭimikaḷ; avarkaḷ āṭavarukku ellāvakiyilum kaṭṭuppaṭṭu oḻukum kaṭappāṭuṭiyar; peṇ piṟavi pāvamikutiyiṉāl uṇṭāvatu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa pollāta koḷkikaḷ iṉṟiya makkaḷiṭiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. uṇmiyil peṇkaḷē āṭavarilum uyarntavarkaḷākavum, avarkaḷic cīrtiruttiyavarkaḷākavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. iṉṟu peṇkaḷ āṭavarukku aṭimippaṭṭu iruppatu poruḷātāra nili oṉṟiṉālēyē yākum. peṇkaḷ vāḻkkic celavukku āṭavariṉ kiyi etirpārātu tāmē poruḷ īṭṭum niliyil iruppārkaḷēyāyiṉ avarkaḷ āṭavarukkuk kaṭṭup paṭṭu naṭakkum kaṭṭāyam uṇṭākātu. toṭakkattil peṇkaḷē payirt toḻil kittoḻilkaḷ purintu āṭavarukkum uṇavu aḷittu vantārkaḷ. ataṉāl āṭavar, makaḷirukkup paṇintu naṭantārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ āṭciyē ātiyil naṭipeṟṟatu. piṟkālattil āṭavar cuyanalaṅ karuti avarkaḷiṉ poruḷātāra niliyip parittu avarkaḷit tamakku aṭaṅki naṭakkumpaṭi ceytaṉar. peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷukku aṭaṅki naṭattal iyaṟki vitiyaṉṟu; āṇkaḷ peṇkaḷukku aṭaṅki naṭattalē iyaṟki vitiyākum. ivipōṉṟa peṇkaḷiṉ piṟamāṇpukaḷi varalāṟṟu muṟiyil ārāyntu kūṟukiṉṟatu innūl.
ceṉṉi
10.05.46 na.ci. kantiyā


peṇkaḷ puraṭci
tōṟṟuvāy
ātiyil peṇkaḷ āṭavariviṭa uyar niliyil iruntārkaḷ. avvaralāṟu iṉṟu maṟakkappaṭṭatu. cila ceyaṟkik kāraṇaṅkaḷāl iṉṟu avarkaḷ tamatu uyar niliyiṉiṉṟum vīḻcciyaṭintaṉar. āṭavarukkup paṇintu naṭakkumpaṭi peṇkaḷ kaṭavuḷāṟ paṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟum avarkaḷ āṇkaḷukkup paṇintu naṭattalē iyaṟki vitiyeṉ ṟum nampappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. peṇkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟi ārāyumiṭattu uṇmi itaṟku nērmāṟākavuḷḷatu. āṭciyum cotturimiyum nīṇṭa kālam peṇkaḷiṭamē iruntu vantaṉa. peṇkaḷiṉ atikāram ciṟitu ciṟitāka āṭavariṉ kikku māṟiyapōtu peṇkaḷik kaṭṭuppaṭuttu vaṉavum, kaṭṭi āḷvaṉamākiya camūkak kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷi āṭavar uṇṭu paṇṇiṉar. itaṉāl āṭavar āḷavum peṇkaḷ āḷappaṭavum piṟan tārkaḷ eṉṉum tavaṟāṉa karuttu makkaṭ camūkaṅkaḷiṭiyē tōṉṟi valuvaṭintuḷḷatu. varalāṟṟu muṟiyil peṇkaḷiṉ perumiyi uḷḷavāṟu eṭuttu viḷakkuvatē innūliṉ ilakkākum.
uyirp paṭippukkaḷum avikaḷiṉ toḻilkaḷum
ivvulakiṉkaṇ kāṇappaṭum acivuḷḷa, acivaṟṟa ellā uyirp poruḷkaḷiyum nōkkuvōmāyiṉ avi, uṇavu tēṭutal cantatiyip perukkutal eṉṉum iru toḻilkaḷilēyē oyātu uḻaṉṟu varukiṉṟaṉa eṉattōṉṟum, cantatiyip perukkum vaṉmi iḻanta uyirkaḷ virivil maṭintu pōkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl cantatiyip perukkuvatu uyirkaḷiṉ mukkiya nōkkam eṉat terikiṉṟatu.
āṇ peṇ vilaṅkukaḷ cōṭikaḷāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉavā?
paṟavikaḷuḷ muṭṭiyiṭṭu nīṇṭakālam aṭikāttuk kuñcu porippavikaḷ cōṭikaḷāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. vilaṅkukaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyi naṉku payiṉṟavarkaḷ avi cōṭikaḷāka vāḻātu taṉittaṉit kūṭṭaṅ kaḷāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. kāṭṭil vāḻum vilaṅkukaḷ paṟavikaḷukkuk karukkoḷḷum uṇarcci (Seasonal rut) āṇṭiṉ ovvōr kālaṅkaḷilēyē uṇṭākiṉṟatu. *peṇ vilaṅkukaḷum āṇ vilaṅkukaḷum taṉittaṉik kūṭṭaṅkaḷākap pirintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. peṇ ṇiṉaṅkaḷ karuttarikkum kālattil māttiram āṇ vilaṅkukaḷ peṇ vilaṅkukaḷiṭiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṇ vilaṅkukaḷiṭiyē peṇ vilaṅkukaḷ kāṇappaṭuvatilli. karuttarikkum poruṭṭu oru peṇ oru āṇit terintu eṭuppatu iyaṟkit terivu (Natural Selection) eṉappaṭum. eppoḻutum peṇ vilaṅku vīramuṭiya oru vilaṅkiyē terintu koḷḷukiṉṟatu. oru peṇ māṉiṉ poruṭṭup pala āṇ māṉkaḷ pala nāḷāka oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu muṭṭip pōrāṭiyatiyum, ap pōril paṅku paṟṟātu taṉiyē appōrip pārttu niṉṟa peṇmāṉ iṟutiyil veṟṟiyaṭinta aṇmāṉiyē viruppattuṭaṉ terintu koṇṭatiyum paṟṟi ṭārviṉ eṉṉum iyaṟki nūṟ pulavar eṭuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.1 āṇ peṇ cērkkikkup piṉṉāl āṇ vilaṅkukaḷ tam kūṭṭatti aṭintu vāḻum. iyaṟkit terivu āṇkaḷālaṉṟu peṇkaḷālēyē teriyappaṭukiṉṟatu.2
kūṭṭattukkuppiṉ peṇ vilaṅkukaḷ āṇ vilaṅkukaḷi uṭaṉ vāḻa aṉumatippatilli
“āṇ peṇ kūṭṭattukkuppiṉ iru iṉattukkum oruvaki veṟuppu uṇṭākiṉṟatu.3 kuṭṭikkup pāl koṭuttu vaḷarkkum vilaṅkukaḷiṭiyē ivveṟuppu mikak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. amerikka turuva māṉkaḷ (Rein deer) oriṭattiṉiṉṟum iṉṉōriṭattil taṅki vāḻum poruṭṭuc cellumpōtu āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum taṉittaṉik kūṭṭaṅkaḷākac cellum. ellā iṉamāṉkaḷiṭiyum ivviyalpik kāṇalām. puraṭṭāci mātattukkuppiṉ erumikaḷuḷ āṇ peṇ iṉaṅkaḷ taṉittaṉi pirintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. āṇ peṇ cērkkikkuppiṉ peṇ veḷavāl āṇ veḷavālik kaṭittut turattiviṭukiṉṟatu. peṇ veḷavālkaḷiṉ mattiyil āṇ veḷavālkaḷ kāṇappaṭuvatilli. karukkoṇṭapiṉ peṇ yāṉikaḷ āṇ yāṉikaḷit turatti viṭu kiṉṟaṉa. peṇ yāṉik kūṭṭattuḷ āṇ yāṉiyik kāṇṭal aritu. paruvam aṭiyum variyum māttiram āṇ yāṉik kaṉṟukaḷtāy yāṉikaḷuṭaṉ taṅkum; peṇ nīrnāykaḷ karukkoḷḷum kālattukkup piṉ āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum pirintu taṉittaṉik kūṭṭaṅkaḷākavāḻum. aṇilkaḷum ivvāṟē pirintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. kuraṅkukaḷip paṟṟiyum ivvāṟu collap paṭukiṉṟatu. ‘orāṉ ūttāṉ’ eṉṉum kuraṅkukaḷ āṇum peṇṇum kalanta kūṭṭamāka vāḻvatilli. āṇkaḷ oru taṉik kūṭṭamākavum, peṇkaḷum kuḻantikaḷum maṟṟoru kūṭṭamāka vum, korilāk kuraṅkukaḷ vāḻum.” - tāymār. (The mothers).
āṭavarum makaḷirum taṉittaṉik kūṭṭaṅkaḷāka vāḻntārkaḷ
vilaṅku nūl muṟiyil maṉitaṉ vilaṅkiṉattic cērntavaṉ. ākavē avaṉum oru kālattil vilaṅkukaḷi oppavē vāḻntāṉ eṉṟu kūṟutal piḻiyākātu. āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum taṉittaṉik kūṭṭaṅkaḷāka vāḻntārkaḷ eṉpataṟkuriya cila vaḻakkaṅkaḷ iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷiṭiyē iyaṟkit terivu peṇkaḷālēyē ceyyappaṭṭatu.4 makaḷirum āṭavarum kalantu vāḻātu taṉittaṉik kūṭṭattiṉarkaḷākavē nīṇṭakālam vāḻntu vantārkaḷ eṉpataṟkuriya ātāraṅkaḷ mēṟkūṟappaṭum poruḷil āṅkāṅku varukiṉṟaṉa. ākavē avikaḷi ivviṭattil virittu eḻutiṟṟilēm.
“amēcaṉ” eṉṉum peṇ pōr vīriyar 
muṟkālattil irāṇiyāl āḷappaṭṭa peṇ irācciyam oṉṟu ulakiṉ cila pakutikaḷil iruntateṉṉum mēlnāṭṭup paḻaṅkati oṉṟu uḷḷatu. peṇkaḷ āṭavariṉ kalappiṉṟit taṉittu vāḻntārkaḷ; avarkaḷ āṭavarkaḷippōlap pōr ceyyavum vēṭṭiyāṭavum paḻakiyiruntārkaḷ. āṇṭiṉ ōru muṟiyō iru muṟiyō āṭavar peṇkaḷic cantikkumpaṭi aṉumatikkappaṭṭārkaḷ. cila nāṭkaḷiṉ piṉ avarkaḷ pirintu tamatu kūṭṭattic cērntuviṭutal kaṭṭāyamāka viruntatu amēcaṉ eṉappaṭṭa peṇkaḷ avarkaḷāl uṇṭākkappaṭṭa caṭṭattiṭṭaṅkaḷikkoṇṭu araciyarāl āḷappaṭṭārkaḷ. tayatōras (Diodorus) srāpō (Strabo) mutaliya varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ amēcaṉ eṉṉum peṇ irācciyam kākkēciya malikaḷukkum yukciṉ (Euxine) karikaḷukkum iṭiyil iruntateṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. avarkaḷ villi naṉṟāka iḻuttu vaḷikkum vāyppup peṟuvataṟkāka valatu taṉatti veṭṭi eṭuttiruntārkaḷ.
pōrp piriyamuḷḷa ik kūṭṭattiṉar pirkiya (Phrygia) eṉṉum nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭi eṭuttārkaḷ. urōcarukku (Trojans) utaviyākap pōr ceyyum poruṭṭup paṭikkut talimi tāṅkic ceṉṟa amēcaṉ irāṇi accillīs eṉpavaṉāṟ kollappaṭṭāḷ.
kākkēcas maliyiṭattilē kaṇavaric cantippatāl piṟakkum piḷḷikaḷ peṇkaḷāyiṉ avarkaḷ avikaḷit tāmē vaḷarttārkaḷ, āṇkaḷ āyiṉ koṉṟārkaḷ; allatu āṭavariṭam aṉuppi vit tārkaḷ.5
tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum āravalli cūravalli kati, alli aracāṇi kati, pavaḷakkoṭi katikaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭu amēcaṉ katikaḷē yākum.
kāmam eṉpatu yātu?
ivvulakap paṭippukkaḷ ellām āṇ peṇ eṉṉum iru vakikaḷāka amintuḷḷaṉa. ivviru iṉaṅkaḷiṉ āṇ peṇ cērkkiyāl ulakil avikaḷi otta cantatikaḷ tōṉṟip peruku kiṉṟaṉa. cantati perukuvataṟku āṇ peṇ cērkki avaciyam vēṇ ṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. iyaṟkiyākavē ellā uyirkaḷiṭattum tammip pōṉṟa uyirkaḷit tōṟṟuvikkum iyaṟki uṇarcci patintu uḷḷatu. tammippōṉṟa uyirkaḷit tōṟṟuvikkum paruvam eytiya āṇ iṉaṅkaḷum peṇṇiṉaṅkaḷum etirppaṭumpōtu uḷḷē maṟintirunta uṇarcci kiḷarntu eḻuntu veṟipōl veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu kiḷarnteḻum veṟiyē kāmam eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu.
āṇ peṇ cērkkik kālaṅkaḷil ūṉ uṇavu koḷḷum vilaṅkukaḷ oṉṟi oṉṟu paṟkaḷāṟ kaṭittum nakaṅkaḷāṟ kiḻittum uṭam pil kāyaṅkaḷi uṇṭu paṇṇukiṉṟaṉa. vīṭukaḷilē pūṉikaḷ ivvāṟu ceyvati nām pārkkiṉṟōm. ciṅkaṅkaḷum pulikaḷum āṇ peṇ cērkkik kālaṅkaḷil kāṭṭil vāḻum uyirkaḷ ellām naṭuṅkumpaṭi muḻakkañ ceykiṉṟaṉa. vilaṅkukaḷiṭattiṟ kāṇappaṭum. iv vaki iyalpukaḷ makkaḷiṭiyum iruntu ciṟitu ciṟitākap paṇpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉpa nūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum paṟkuṟi nakakkuṟi eṉpaṉa āṇ peṇ cērkkik kālattut tōṉṟum veṟiyiṉāṟ kaṭikkum paṟṟaḻumpukaḷum, nakaṅkaḷ kīṟum nakattaḻumpukaḷumēyākum. muttam eṉpatum kāma uṇarcciyāṟ kaṭikkum kaṭiyiṉ paṇpāṭēyākum.6 āṇ peṇ cērkkikkuppiṉ oruvaril oruvar paṟṟu iṉṟip pōkum kātal veṟiyik kāmam eṉak kūṟutal amiyum.6
kātal
kāmattiṉ paṇpāṭē kātal eṉappaṭum. kātal uṇarcci āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupālāriyum avarkaḷiṉ iḷami kaḻinta kālattum iṇittum vippatākiya uyarnta paṇpu.
tāy, piḷḷikaḷip peṟukiṉṟaṉar; peṟṟa piḷḷikaḷ mītu aṉpu koḷkiṉṟāḷ. ivvāṟu tōṉṟum aṉpu talimuṟi talimuṟiyāka vaḷarcciyaṭikiṉṟatu. ivvaki iḷakiya uḷḷamuḷḷa peṇ iyaṟkit terivu ceyyumpōtu tāṉ terinteṭutta āṭavaṉmītu kacinta uḷḷamum aṉpukalanta uṇarcciyumuṭiyavaḷākiṉṟāḷ. āṇ peṇ cērkkik kup piṉpum avvuṇarcci avaḷi viṭṭuppiriyāmal irukkiṉṟatu. ip paṇpaṭṭa uḷḷappiṇippē kātal eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. kātal uṇarcci peṇkaḷiṭattilēyē mutaliṟṟōṉṟi vaḷarcciyaṭivatāyiṟṟu.*
tāy āṭci
tāy vaṭṭaṅkaḷ
nām ellōrum tēṉ kūṭṭil īkkaḷ eppaṭi vāḻkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu aṟivōm. tēṉ kūṭṭiṉ mattiyil irāṇi, allatu tāy ī iruk kiṉṟatu. ataṉic cūḻnta ataṉ piḷḷikaḷākiya maṟṟiya īkkaḷ iruk kiṉṟaṉa. itaṉi oru tāy vaṭṭam eṉak kūṟalām. itaṉi oppavē makkaḷum tāy vaṭṭaṅkaḷākap perukukiṟārkaḷ. piḷḷikaḷuḷ āṇkaḷ vēṭṭiyāṭu vōrāka alintu tirintaṉar. peṇkaḷ oriṭattil taṅki vāḻntaṉar. vayatiṉ mūtta tāyē kuṭumpattiṉ taliviyāka viruntāḷ. avaḷ collukku ellārum aṭaṅki naṭantārkaḷ. tāykku aṭuttapaṭi yil avaḷiṉ mūtta kumāri atikāramuṭiyavaḷāṉāḷ.
peṇkaḷ oriṭattiṟ kūṭṭamāka vāḻntamiyiṉ avarkaḷ vāḻum iṭaṅkaḷilēyē uṇavippeṟa vēṇṭiyiruntatu. ākavē avarkaḷ tām vāḻum iṭattilum ataṉ aṇmiyilum paḻa maraṅkaḷiyum tāṉi yaṅkaḷiyum payiriṭṭut tamakku vēṇṭiya uṇavup poruḷkaḷip peṟṟaṉar.7
uṇaviṉ poruṭṭu ōyviṉṟi alintu tiriyum niliyil makkaḷiṉ nākarikam utayamākavilli. uṇavu eḷitiṟ kiṭittapōtu avarkaḷ āṟutal aṭintu amitiyāka vāḻat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ; vāḻkkikkut tēvippaṭum caṭṭi, pāṉi, peṭṭi, kaṭakam, payirt toḻilukku vēṇṭum āyutaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟic ceytārkaḷ. ataṉāl ciṟitu ciṟitākak kittoḻil vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. alintu tiri yātu oriṭattil taṅki vāḻntavarkaḷākiya peṇkaḷē kit toḻiliyum payirttoḻiliyum vaḷarttavarkaḷāvar. ēr pūṭṭi nilatti uḻum kālam variyil āṭavar peritum vēṭṭiyāṭum niliyilēyē iruntārkaḷ. āṭavar cila camayaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷiyē uṇaviṉ poruṭṭu etir pārkka vēṇṭiyiruntatu. peṇkaḷ āṭavarilum kaṇṇiya muṭiyavar kaḷākak karutappaṭṭārkaḷ. āṭavar peṇkaḷukkup paṇintu oḻukiṉar. uṇavup poruḷē makkaḷiṉ celvamākak karutappaṭṭatu. peṇkaḷiṭatti lēyē atika uṇavu iruntatu.
orē tāy vaṭṭattuḷ tirumaṇak kalappu illi
nuoṭipayaala
oru tāy vaḻiyākap perukiyavarkaḷ ellōrum oruvarukku oruvar uṭaṉpiṟappu muṟiyiṉarākak karutappaṭṭārkaḷ. ākavē oru tāy vaṭṭattiluḷḷa āṭavarum makaḷirum tirumaṇak kalappuṭiyavarāyp piḷḷikaḷip peṟutal kaṭiyappaṭṭatu. oru tāy vaṭṭattēyuḷḷa peṇ paruvamaṭintapōtu iṉṉoru tāy vaṭṭattiluḷḷa āṭavaṉic cērntu piḷḷikaḷi īṉṟāḷ. peṇṇiṉ kaṇavaṉ taṉatu tāy vaṭṭattil ceṉṟu vāḻntāṉ. peṇṇiṉ tāy vaṭṭattil avaṉ taṅkavilli. tāy peṟum kuḻantikaḷ kuṭumpattukkuc contamāka viruntaṉa. piḷḷikaḷ tantiyari aṟiyār. ākavē piḷḷikaḷ tāy vaḻiyil aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar. tantikkum piḷḷikkum evvakit toṭarpum uṇṭākavilli. peṇṇukkum āṭavaṉukkum kaṇavaṉ maṉivi eṉṉum kaṭamikaḷum tōṉṟavilli.8
peṇkaḷ pala tāra maṇam
iyaṟkit terivu āṇkaḷukkaṉṟu peṇkaḷukkē uriyatu eṉa muṉṉōriṭattiṟ paṭittōm. paruvamaṭinta peṇkaḷ cantati virutti yiṉ poruṭṭut takutiyuḷḷa āṭavarit terinteṭuttaṉar. iv vaḻakki liruntu peṇkaḷ tām virumpiya palarik kaṇavarākat terinteṭukkum vaḻakku vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. avarkaḷ peṇṇiṉāl variyaṟukkappaṭṭa muṟippaṭi avaḷi aṭintārkaḷ. ivvulakam muḻumiyilum iv vaki maṇaṅkaḷē naṭipeṟṟaṉa;9 iṉṟum ciṟcila nāṭukaḷil kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. ivvaki maṇaṅkaḷ naṭipeṟṟa kālattil viyapicārameṉpatu poruḷaṟṟatāyiruntatu. tanti āṭcik kālattilēyē viyapicāram eṉṉum kuṟṟaccāṭṭi uṇarttum col uṇṭāyiṟṟu.10
nāyar cātiyiṉarum avariṉ tirumaṇaṅkaḷum
maliyāḷattil nāyar eṉṉum oru vakuppiṉar kāṇappaṭukiṉ ṟaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ maṇamuṟi paḻiya tāy vaṭṭa maṇamuṟiyi ottatu. nāyark kuṭumpaṅkaḷ tāy vaṭṭaṅkaḷākavuḷḷaṉa. ovvoru kuṭumpat tukkum tāyē taliviyāvaḷ. avarkaḷukkuḷ niliyāṉa tirumaṇaṅkaḷ illi, nāyarkaḷum nampūtirikaḷum peṇṇiṉ cakōtaraṉatu virup pattip peṟṟup peṇṇic campantam vittuk koḷvārkaḷ. cammanta meṉpatu cilakālattukku māttiram irupālārum kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyarāka irukkum uṭaṉpaṭikki. eppoḻutu iruvaril evarukkum ikkūṭṭuṟavu piṭikkavilliyō appoḻutē avarkaḷ ataṉi veṭṭiviṭuvar. piṉpu avarkaḷ tamakku viruppampōl vēṟu vēṟu campantaṅkaḷi vittukkoḷḷalām. tantiyiṉ cottil, piḷḷikaḷukku yātum urimi illi. piḷḷikaḷ tāyvaṭṭattic cērntaṉavākum. tāy vaṭṭattukkuriya cottilēyē avikaḷiṉ vāḻkkic celavu naṭi peṟukiṉṟatu. tāyiṉ uṭaṉ piṟantāṉukkē piḷḷikaḷip pātukākkum urimi uṇṭu. avaṉuṭiya cottu avaṉuṭiya piḷḷikaḷic cērātu marumakkaḷiyē cērkiṉṟatu.
pala āṭavari orē kālattil kaṇavarākak koṇṭu oḻukum oḻukkam nāyarp peṇkaḷiṭattil uṇṭu. itaṉi avarkaḷ camūkam tavaṟāṉateṉṟu uṇarvatilli. tantiyiṉ cottu piḷḷikaḷi aṭiyāmal marumakkaḷi aṭiyum vaḻakku marumakkaḷ tāyam eṉap paṭum. tiruvitāṅkūr aracurimiyum ivvakiyiṉatē. irāṇi eṉappaṭupa var aracaṉiṉ maṉiviyallaḷ, avaṉatu uṭaṉ piṟantāḷē, aracurimi avaḷuṭiyatē araciyiṉ mūtta peṇṇukkē aracurimi celkiṉṟatu.
nāyarp peṇkaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭu kaṇavarvariyil uṭiyavarkaḷā yiruntārkaḷ eṉpataṟku eḻuttu vakiyāṉa atāraṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyāl vakukkappaṭṭa muṟippaṭi ovvōr nāḷil avaḷi aṭivāṉ. perumpālum akkāla elli oru nāḷ mattiyāṉam mutal aṭuttanāḷ mattiyāṉam variyumāka viruntatu. iv vaki vaḻakku intiyāvil māttiramaṉṟu ulakam muḻumiyilum iruntu vantatu.11
tāyk kaṭavuḷ
tāyē ellā atikāramumuṭiyavaḷāyiruntāḷ. ak kālattē makkaḷ ellā atikāramumuṭiya tāyik kaṭavuḷāka vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. ulakil mutaṉmutal tāy vaḻipāṭē uṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉa makkaḷ nūlār ārāyntu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ellā atikāramumuṭiya tāykku vilaṅkukaḷukkuḷ vīra muṭiya ciṅkam vākaṉamākkappaṭṭatu. avaḷē vīratteyvam (koṟṟavi) ākavum viḷaṅkiṉāḷ. ulakil ellāp pākaṅkaḷilum tāyk kaṭavuḷ kaṉṉit teyvamākavē koṇṭu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭāḷ. muṟkālat tāy piḷḷikaḷip peṟṟapōtum kaliyāṇamākātavaḷ eṉṟē koḷḷap paṭṭāḷ. kaliyāṇam eṉpatu kaṇavaṉ maṉivi eṉṉum muṟiyil orumittu vāḻutal. tāyk kaṭavuḷ kaṉṉi eṉappaṭutal ātikālat tirumaṇa muṟiyi oruvāṟu viḷakkuvatākum.12
vilaṅkuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷit talimi tāṅkik kāppatu peṇṇē
kāṭukaḷilē vilaṅkukaḷ kūṭṭaṅkaḷāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. āpattu nērumpōtu muṉṉēvantu avikaḷik kāppatu mūtta peṇ vilaṅkē yākum.13 ituvē iyaṟki vitipōlum. makkaḷuḷḷum mūtta peṇṇē taliviyāyiruntāḷ eṉṟu muṉ paṭittōm. peṇkaḷ āṭavarilum pārkkac ciṟanta pōr vīriyarāyiruntārkaḷeṉṟu nām varalāṟukaḷiṟ paṭikkiṉṟōm.
pōrvīrap peṇkaḷ
“kaṉeriyiluḷḷa kuvāñces (Guanches) peṇkaḷ aṇikaḷil niṉṟu pōr ceytaṉar. morakkōviluḷḷa perpar (Berber) peṇkaḷ kutirikaḷmītu ēṟic cavāriceytu pōr purivar. arāpiyariṉ varalāṟṟil peṇ pōr vīrarkaḷippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷ peritum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. peṇ pōrvīrarkaḷ palataṭavikaḷil pōr muṉiyiṟṟōṉṟi pōrp pōkki māṟṟit tamatu uṭaṉ piṟantār, kaṇavar, piratāṉikaḷiṉ uyir kaḷik kāttirukkiṉṟaṉar. vakāpiyiṉ (Wahabi) maṉiviyākiya kāliyā eṉpavaḷ makamatali eṉpavaṉip pōr muṉiyil palataṭavi etirttāḷ. vāṉri (Fanti) makkaḷuḷ peṇkaḷum āṭavaruṭaṉ pakkattē niṉṟu pōr purintaṉar. cāṉcipār cultāṉiṭattil 1000 peṇ pōr vīrar niliyāka iruntaṉar. mattiya āciya, tāttāriyap peṇkaḷ mikavum vīramuṭiyavarkaḷāka viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ āṭavarilum pārkka vēkamākak kutirikaḷic celuttip pōr ceytārkaḷ. cīyam (Siam) aracaṉ peṇ kāvalāḷari amarttiyiruttal vaḻakku. kaṇṭi aracaṉum ivvāṟu viṟpiṭitta peṇ kāvalari vittiruntāṉ. pāracīka aracaṉ ‘amēcaṉ’ meykkāppāḷari amarttiyiruntāṉ. cīttiya (Seythian) peṇkaḷ āṭavari oppap pōr purikiṉṟārkaḷ eṉak kotōtacu kūṟiyuḷḷār. keltiya (Celt) peṇkaḷ āṭavariṉ iṭi iṭiyē niṉṟu pōr purintaṉar. ayarlāntiṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ peṇvīrar palarippaṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.”14
peṇkaḷ āṭavari oppa allatu avarkaḷilum tiṟamiyākap pōrceyyum tiṟaṉ aṭintiruntārkaḷ. tanti āṭcikkālattil avarkaḷ oṭukkappaṭṭu vīṭṭil aṭikkappaṭṭārkaḷ. pala talimuṟi kaḷāka avarkaḷiṉ vīram payaṉpaṭuttappaṭāmal iruntamiyiṉāl vīram kuṉṟi maṟintuviṭṭatu. iṉṟu payam allatu kōḻittaṉattukkup peṇkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
paḻiya ekiptu mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷiṟ cotturimi
paḻiya15 ekiptil āvaṇaṅkaḷil (Documents) kaṇavaṉiṉ peyar kāṇappaṭavilli. pillōmīṟṟar (Philometer) eṉṉum aracaṉ kaṇavaṉiṉ urimiyiyum pukuttutaṟku maṉivi eḻutum āvaṇaṅ kaḷukkuk kaṇavaṉum kiyeḻuttiṭal vēṇṭum eṉṉum caṭṭatti uṇṭupaṇṇiṉāṉ.
“cakārā vaṉāntaraṅkaḷiluḷḷa ṭurek (Touareg) peṇkaḷ tāy vaḻi urimiyiyē peṟṟiruntaṉar. pāpilōṉiya makaḷirum ekiptiya peṇkaḷippōlavē tāyāṭci uṭiyavarkaḷāyiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ tam uṭaṉpiṟanta āṭavarip pōlavum kaṇavarippōlavum ellāvaṟ ṟilum cama urimi peṟṟiruntaṉar.” kirēttā (Crete) makkaḷ kirēttāvi tāynāṭeṉṟē kūṟiṉar; tanti nāṭeṉṟum kūṟavilli eṉap puḷuṟṟāk (Plutarch) eṉṉum varalāṟṟāciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. paḻiya atēṉcil piḷḷikaḷ tāyvaḻiyāl aṟiyappaṭṭārkaḷ. kikurops (Cecrops) aracaṉ kālattil ivvaki vaḻakku iruntatu. iliciyāvil tāyāṭciyē irunta teṉa heratētacu kūṟiyuḷḷār. kirēttiya (Crete) peṇkaḷukku ekiptiya peṇkaḷiviṭa atika atikāram iruntatu. kācis (Khasis) eṉṉum iṭattil āṇ kaṭavuḷariṉ muṉṉar peṇ kaṭavuḷar vikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. kirēkkar vaḻipaṭṭa eṭṭu mukkiya kaṭavuḷaril aivar peṇpāliṉar. speyiṉil tāy tanti eṉṉum iruvariṉ peyar kaḷiyum iṇittup piḷḷikaḷukkup peyar iṭappaṭṭatu.16
paḻiya mecapettēmiyāvil peṇkaḷ āṭavari otta urimi peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉar. maṇamāṉa peṇkaḷukkum maṇamākāta peṇkaḷuk kum cotturimi iruntatu peṇkaḷ tām virumpiya vaṇṇam (kaṇava ṉiṉ uṭaṉpāṭiṉṟi) paṇam koṭuttal vāṅkal campantamāṉa karumaṅ kaḷic ceytārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ vāṇikamum purintaṉar; tēcātipatikaḷ, eḻuttāḷarkaḷākak kaṭami āṟṟiṉar; ālayaṅkaḷil pūcārikaḷāka (Priestess) viruntaṉar. avarkaḷukkup peruñcelvākkum poruḷum irun taṉa. ekiptiya peṇkaḷ tāy tantiyarip parāmarikkak kaṭamippaṭṭirun taṉar. avarkaḷ āṭavari oppa ellāt toḻilkaḷum purintaṉar; payiriṭun toḻiliṟ palar īṭupaṭṭiruntārkaḷ. nāṉkāvatu allatu aintāvatu ekiptiya paramparikkālattil eḻutappaṭṭa ōviyam oṉṟil peṇ orutti kappal oṭṭukiṉṟāḷ.17 makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki ārampattil toṭaṅkiya tāyāṭci, kālattil ciṟitu ciṟitāka māṟutalaṭintu iṟutiyil tanti āṭciyuṭ pukuntu maṟintuviṭṭateṉa varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ kūṟuvar.18
āṇkaḷic cīrtiruttiṉavarkaḷ peṇkaḷē
ivvulakil vāḻum ellā āṇ peṇ uyirkaḷuḷ āṇkaḷ valiya taṉmiyuṭiyaṉavākavum, peṇkaḷ meṉmit taṉmiyuṭiyaṉa vākavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṇkaḷ mūrkka kuṇamum, muraṭṭuttaṉmi yum pōr viruppam uṭiyar. āṇkaḷiṭattil ākkum kuṇaṅkaḷiṉum pārkka aḻikkum kuṇaṅkaḷē peritum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. peṇ kaḷiṭattu iyalpāka meṉmik kuṇaṅkaḷuṇṭu. avi piḷḷikaḷip peṟṟu avikaḷik karuttuṭaṉ pātukākkum iyalpiṉāl aṉpu tōṉṟi meṉmiyak kuṇaṅkaḷōṭu vaḷarcciyaṭikiṉṟatu av vaṉpuk kuṇaṅkaḷ, av vuyirkaḷ peṟum kuḻantikaḷiṭattum talimuṟi tali muṟiyākac cuvaṟi vaḷarcciyaṭikiṉṟaṉa. im muṟiyiṉālēyē pōrk kuṇamum muraṭṭuc cupāvamumuṭiyavarkaḷāyirunta āṭavar nīṇṭa kālat til ciṟitu ciṟitāka cāntakuṇamuṭiyavarkaḷāka māṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉa ārāycci vallār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.19
peṇkaḷ potuvil iḷakiya maṉamum irakkamum, āṭavariṭattil urukkamuṭiyavarkaḷākavum irukkiṉṟaṉar avarkaḷ paṟavikaḷ vilaṅkukaḷ mutaliya ellāc ciṟṟuyirkaḷiṭattilum ivviyalpiṉar; tāvaraṅkaḷ pūkkaḷ, piṟa aḵṟiṇip poruḷkaḷ, tām vittup payaṉpaṭuttum poruḷ mutaliyavikaḷi meṉmiyākavē eṭuttuk kiyāḷuvar. āṭavarō avikaḷi muraṭṭuttaṉamākap payaṉpaṭutti oṭittu viṭuvar - tāymār.
muṟpaṭṭa camūkattil tāyē mutaṉmi iṭattip peṟṟiruntāḷ. tāyk kaṭavuḷippaṟṟi varum paḻaṅkatikaḷālum, payirttoḻili virutticeytu vīṭṭu vāḻkkiyiyum nākarikattiyum vaḷarttami yālum, piḷḷikaḷukkuk kalvi kaṟpittuc camūkattukkup puttimati kūṟum taṉmiyiṉālum tāymika mukkiyam vāyntavaḷ. amerikka civappinti yarkaḷukkut tāyē ālōcaṉi kūṟuvāḷ. intiyāviṉ teṉmēṟkuk kariyil vāḻum nāyar cātiyiṉaruḷ tāyē mutaṉmi iṭatti vakik kiṉṟāḷ. ulakiṉ paḻiya nākarika varalāṟukaḷiyum camaya varalāṟukaḷi yum payilumiṭattut tāyē mukkiya muṭiyavāḷāka viruntāḷeṉṉum uṇmi naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟatu - uyir varalāṟṟiṉ kāṭci.
tāyāṭciyum cotturimiyum
tāy āṭci kuṭumpa āṭciyākavē iruntuvantatu. taṉippaṭṭavar kaḷukkuc cottu irukkavilli. kuṭumpac cottu ellōrukkum potuvil iruntatu. kuṭumpattic cērntavarkaḷ putitākat tēṭum cottum kuṭumpattic cērntatākavē koḷḷappaṭṭatu. perumpālum cottukkaḷ nilampōṉṟa acivaṟṟa poruḷkaḷākaviruntaṉa avi peṇkaḷukkē uriyaṉa. vil, ampu pōṉṟa cila acivuṭip poruḷkaḷē āṭavarukkuriyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. avarkaḷ iṟantapōtu avi avarkaḷukku aṭutta ulakil payaṉpaṭum poruṭṭuc camātikaḷuḷ vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
tāyāṭciyiṉ vīḻcciyum tanti āṭciyiṉ toṭakkamum
toṭakkattil peṇkaḷē cottukkuriyavarkaḷā yiruntārkaḷ. peṇkaḷi maṇantukoḷvatāl māttiram āṭavar cottip peṟakkūṭiyavarkaḷāṉārkaḷ. payiriṭutal kittoḻilpōṉṟavi, nīṇṭakālam peṇkaḷ kiyilēyē iruntaṉa. āṭavar it toḻilkaḷiṟ paṅku paṟṟātu alintu tirivōrākavum vēṭṭiyāṭuvōrākavum iruntaṉar. peṇkaḷiṉ payirttoḻilukkuriya karuvikaḷ maṇveṭṭi pāri pōṉṟavi. avarkaḷ erutukaḷik kalappiyiṟ pūṭṭi uḻa aṟin tirukkavilli, piṉ kalappiyil erutukaḷip pūṭṭi nilatti uḻum muṟi kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl atika nilatti uḻutu payiriṭṭu atika tāṉiyaṅkaḷi viḷivikkak kūṭiyatākaviruntatu. āṭavar ērip piṭittu uḻat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. ataṉāl atika uṇavu kiṭittatu. ākavē avarkaḷ alintu tiriyum vāḻkkiyi viṭṭu ōriṭattil taṅkuvārāyiṉar. ituvariyil peṇkaḷ ceytuvanta payirttoḻil kit toḻil eṉpaṉa ciṟitu ciṟitāka āṭavarkikku māṟiṉa. peṇkaḷ vēli yaṟṟavarkaḷāka vīṭṭiṟṟaṅkiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ poruḷ varuvāy kuṉṟiṟṟu. avarkaḷ tamatu vāḻkkikku āṭavar kiyi etirpārkkum nilimi uṇṭāyiṟṟu. veḷiyiṟ ceṉṟu veyyiliṟ kāyntu vēli ceyyāmiyāl avarkaḷiṉ mēṉi ciṟitu ciṟitāka aḻakupeṟṟatu. mēṉi miṉukkup poruḷkaḷālum, aḻakiya āṭikaḷālum avarkaḷ tammi alaṅkarittaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ aḻaku āṭavariṉ uḷḷattik koḷḷi koṇṭatu. avarkaḷ aḻakiṉ mikka peṇkaḷit tamakkē urimi yākkik koṇṭu vāḻa virumpiṉārkaḷ. oruvaṉ, tāṉ contamākkiya peṇṇōṭu iṉṉoruvaṉ, naṭpuk koṇṭiruppati virumpavilli. ākavē avaḷ maṟṟavarkaḷuṭaṉ aḷavaḷāvi naṭpukkoḷḷātapaṭi avaḷi vīṭṭil irukkumpaṭi ceytāṉ. itu variyil veḷiyiṟ ceṉṟu peṇkaḷ cēkarittuvanta vāḻkkikku vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷi ivaṉē cēkarittu varuvāṉāyiṉāṉ. ivvaki vāḻkki nāḷaṭivil uṟutip paṭṭatu. appōtu āṇukkum peṇṇukkum taṉit toṭarpu uṇṭā yiṟṟu. piḷḷikaḷ piṟantaṉa. appoḻutu āṭavaṉ taṉatu cottup piḷḷikaḷic cēra vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpiṉāṉ. ippoḻutu tāy tanti eṉṉum iruvariṉ cottum piḷḷikaḷic cērvatākiya makkaḷ tāyam uṇṭāyiṟṟu, marumakkaṭṭāyamum tāyāṭciyum paṭippaṭiyē maṟintaṉa.20
tanti āṭciyum iyaṟkit terivum
iyaṟkit terivu peṇkaḷukkē uriyatu eṉṟu muṉ viḷakkap paṭṭatu. ivviyaṟki viti ippoḻutu māṟat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. ippoḻutu āṭavaṉē tāṉ virumpiya peṇṇit terinteṭukka ārampittāṉ. ivvāṟu āṭavaṉ peṇṇit terinteṭukkum muṟikaḷ palavāṟu vaḷarcciyaṭintaṉa. cila makkaṭ kūṭṭaṅkaḷil oruvaṉ oru peṇṇi maṇakka virumpiṉāl avaṉ avaḷ tanti vīṭṭil oru āṇṭu vari yil taṅki avaṉ ceytuvarum toḻilkaḷukku utaviyākaviruntāṉ. peṇṇiṉ tanti avaṉatu toḻil ceyyum tiṟamiyil tiruptiyaṭin tāl avaṉ taṉ putalviyi avaṉukku maṉiviyākak koṭuttāṉ. cila kūṭṭattiṉar peṇṇiṉ tantikkup paṇattik koṭuttup peṇṇip peṟṟaṉar. iv vaḻakkiṉ tēyvē iṉṟu tamiḻ makkaḷiṭiyē pariyam eṉa vaḻaṅkuvatu. itu peṇṇiṉ tantikkuttāṉ peṇṇik koḷḷat takutiyuṭiyavaṉ eṉpatik kāṇpippataṟku maṇamakaṉ koṭukkum poruḷākum. ivvaḻakkamē peṇkaḷi maṉiviyarāka vilikoṭuttu vāṅkum vaḻakkākavum māṟiyuḷḷatu.21
ekiptil tāy cottum tanti cottum 
piḷḷikaḷic cērumpaṭi caṭṭañ ceyyappaṭṭa vaki
peṇkaḷip pōlavē āṭavarum cottu uṭiyavarkaḷāyirun tārkaḷ. ak kālattum caṭṭappaṭi tāyiṉ cottē piḷḷikaḷic cērva tāyiruntatu.22 oru peṇṇi maṇakka virumpiya āṭavaṉ taṉ cottu muḻuvatiyum allatu ataṉ oru pakutiyi peṇṇukku eḻutivit tāṉ. tāy tanti eṉṉum iruvariṉ cottum piḷḷikaḷic cērum poruṭṭuc ceyyappaṭṭa muṟi ituvākum. ivvāṟu tāyiṉ urimi yōṭu tantiyiṉ urimiyiyum piḷḷikaḷ peṟalāṉārkaḷ.
āṭavaṉ peṇṇiṉ tantiyiṉ kīḻiruntu paṇiceytum, paṇikkup patil poruḷ koṭuttum peṇṇi maṉiviyākap peṟṟa kālattil perumpālum peṇ, cottuṭiyavaḷāy irukkavilli. peṇ, cottillātirunta kālattilēyē āṭavar peṇkaḷi aṭimip paṭuttiṉārkaḷ.
ilaṅkiyiṉ vaṭa pākattil tamiḻ makkaḷ vāḻntu varukiṉṟaṉar. aṅku āṇ peṇ eṉṉum vēṟupāṭu iṉṟit tāy tantiyiṉ cottup piḷḷikaḷic cērukiṉṟatu. oru āṭavaṉum oru peṇṇum kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyāka vāḻkki naṭattiya kālattil tēṭiya cottiṉ nērpāti maṉiviyic cērvatākum. ivi tāy urimi tanti urimikaḷip piḷḷikaḷ aṭiyum muṟikaḷiṟ cila pakutikaḷākum.
oruvaṉ pala peṇkaḷi maṇattal
āṭavaṉ cottuṭiyavaṉāy irunta kālattil tanti āṭci uṇṭāyiṟṟu. tanti tāy eṉṉum iruvarum cottuṭiyarāyirunta kālattil iruvarum camaperumiyuṭiyavarkaḷākak karutappaṭṭārkaḷ. tanti āṭci tāyāṭciyic ciṟitu ciṟitāka viḻuṅki valimiyaṭintatu. aññāṉṟu peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷukkuppaṇintu naṭakkum paṭiyāṉa caṭṭaṅkaḷ āṭavarāl vakukkappaṭṭaṉa. tanti nilatti uḻutu payiriṭu vatāl celvatti īṭṭiṉāṉ. kuṭumpattil ettaṉipēr atikamāka iruntārkaḷō avvaḷavukku atika celvatti īṭṭakkūṭiyatāka virun tatu. pala piḷḷikaḷiyuṭiyavaṉ avarkaḷ mūlam atika celvatti īṭṭiṉāṉ. poruḷ īṭṭum viruppuḷḷa āṭavar peṇkaḷ palari maṇantu pala piḷḷikaḷip peṟṟārkaḷ.
itaṟku muṟpaṭṭa oru kālattil peṇ oruttiyiṉ kaṇavariṉ eṇkaḷik koṇṭu avaḷiṉ celvattiyum atikārattiyum aḷakkattakkatāyiruntatu. ippoḻutu oruttaṉiṉ maṉivikaḷiṉ eṇkaḷik koṇṭu avaṉ celvattiyum atikārattiyum aṟiyattakkatāyiruntatu.
āṭavar makaḷir eṉṉum iru pālārilum uyarntavar yār?
āṇ peṇ eṉṉum iru pālārilum uyarvākak koḷḷat takuntavar yār eṉṉum kēḷvi kēṭkappaṭukiṉṟatu. aṟivāṟ ciṟanta oḷaviyārē “aṉṉiyum pitāvum” eṉa aṉṉiyi muṉṉāl vittuk kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka. peṇṇiṉ karuppi, vayalpōṉṟatu. āṭavaṉ atilē vitti vittupavaṉ pōlvaṉākiṉṟāṉ. nilamiṉṟi vittu muḷittu ōṅkumāṟilli. vittiṉṟi vayalil muḷi tōṟṟumā ṟilli. im muṟiyik koṇṭu āṇ peṇkaḷiṉ uyarvu tāḻvukaḷi muṭivu ceyya muṭiyātu. irupālārum tattamakku iyaṟkiyāl aḷikkap paṭṭa kaṭamikaḷi niṟivēṟṟuvatil taṉittaṉi perumiyuṭiyavar kaḷāka virukkiṉṟaṉar.
iv vulakattilē ātiyil catti (tāyk kaṭavuḷ) vaṇakkam, tāy uyirkaḷit tōṟṟuvikkiṉṟāḷ eṉṉum perumippaṟṟi eḻuntu naṭipeṟuvatāyiṟṟu. itaṉāl paṭippu muṟiyil āṇkaḷilum pārkkap peṇkaḷē ciṟantavarkaḷ eṉa ātimakkaḷ karutiṉārkaḷ eṉat terikiṉ ṟatu. paṇṭi nāḷil tamiḻar tāy, tanti eṉṉum iruvarum camurimi yiṉar eṉṟē karuti vantārkaḷ. ivvuṇmiyiṉi avarkaḷiṉ camaya nūlākiya ākamaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇalākum.*
manti mēykkum makkaḷuḷ peṇkaḷiṉ nilimi
manti mēykkum makkaḷuḷ āṭavarē cottuk kuṭiyavarkaḷā yiruntārkaḷ; ākavē avarkaḷiṭiyē tanti āṭci vaḷarcci yaṭintatu. ivarkaḷ veḷiyiṟ ceṉṟu manti mēyppavarkaḷātaliṉ tam maṉiviyar piṟaruṭaṉ naṭpuk koḷvatiyō, allatu naṭpukkoṇṭu tammi viṭṭup pirintu viṭuvatiyō niṉintu añciṉārkaḷ. ākavē avarkaḷ peṇkaḷ piṟa āṭavari nōkkātirukkumpaṭi avarkaḷi vīṭu allatu kūṭāraṅkaḷiṉ upuṟaṅkaḷil vittiruttal, avarkaḷit tamakkuk kīḻppaṭutti vittiruttal pōṉṟa muṟikaḷik kiyāḷuvārā yiṉar. toṭakkattil manti mēykkum niliyil iruntavarkaḷākiya āriyar intiya nāṭṭil vantu kuṭiyēṟiyapōtu avarkaḷiṉ peṇkaḷ ivvāṟu oṭukkappaṭṭiruntāhkkaḷ eṉpatu apastampam pōtāyaṉam mutaliya avarkaḷiṉ paḻiya nīti nūlkaḷāl naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. avarkaḷukkuk kalvi maṟukkappaṭṭiruntatu. peṇkaḷukku vētam maṟikkap paṭṭiruppatum itaṟkuc ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭākum.23
tanti āṭcikkālattila peṇkaḷ āṭumāṭukaḷ pōṉṟa āṭavaṉiṉ uṭimikaḷāṉārkaḷ
tanti āṭcikkālattil peṇkaḷ cottillātavarkaḷāyi ṉārkaḷ . āṭavaṉ taṉatu maṉivi makkaḷi āṭu māṭukaḷ pōṉṟu viṟka vum vāṅkavum kūṭiya acivuṭip poruḷkaḷākap peṟṟiruntāṉ.24 avvāṟu viṟkavō vāṅkavō iraval koṭukkavō avaṉukku atikāram iruntatu. mēṟku āciyāvilum kirēkka nāṭṭilum oru vakic caṭṭam vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. oruvaṉ iṉṉoruvaṉiṉ maṉiviyōṭu tiruṭṭut taṉamāka uṟavu vittiruntāl avaṉmītu viyapicārak kuṟṟam cāṭṭappaṭa villi; tiruṭṭuk kuṟṟamcāṭṭappaṭṭatu. ākavē avaṉ tiruṭṭuk kuṟṟattukkuriya taṇṭaṉi aṭintāṉ. viyapicāram kuṟṟameṉṟu avar kaḷāṟ karutappaṭavilli.25 viyapicāram ceytavaṉukkup perumpālum peṇṇiṉ kaṇavaṉukku viruppamāṉa taṇṭaṉi vitikkappaṭṭatu.
tanti āṭci uṇṭāṉapōtum tāyāṭcikkālattirunta oḻukkaṅkaḷ arikuṟiyāka iruntuvantaṉa
tanti āṭci toṭaṅkiya pōtum paḻiya vaḻakkaṅkaḷ tiṭīreṉa maṟintuviṭavilli. pala nāṭukaḷil kaṇavaṉiṉ uṭaṉpāṭṭuṭaṉ oru peṇ piṟa āṭavarōṭu naṭpuk koṇṭiruttal kuṟṟamākak karutappaṭa villi. cila kūṭṭat tiṉariṭiyil tirumaṇattukku muṉ naṭakkum viyapicāram oḻukkakkēṭākak karutappaṭavilli. ivvāṟu oḻukkaṅkaḷ māṟupaṭṭaṉa.26
maṉiviyari iraval koṭuttal
ulakiṉ pala pākaṅkaḷukkiṭiyil maṉiviyari iraval koṭukkum vaḻakkam iruntu vantatu. itu palarukku viyappit taralām. kirēkkariṭamum urōmariṭamum iv vaḻakkam cātāraṇa vaḻakkil iruntatu. kirēkkariṉ tattuva cāttiriyākiya cokirakis eṉpavarē taṉatu maṉivi yākiya cāntappipi taṉatu mutaṉ māṇākkarākiya alcipiyātis (Alcibiades) eṉpavarukku iraval koṭuttār. ivvaki varalāṟukaḷ kirēkka urōma carittiraṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.27
“kirēkkar iṉpa nukarcci karutiyō piḷḷikaḷip peṟutal karutiyō tamatu maṉiviyari naṇparkaḷukku iraval aḷittārkaḷ. spārṭṭaṉ makkaḷukku nītinūl vakutta likurkās eṉpavar maṇamāṉa peṇkaḷiṉ tayavippeṟa muyaṉṟavarkaḷip paḻivāṅkum poruṭṭuc ceyyappaṭum pōrkaḷip paṟṟip parikacittār; vayatil mutirnta oruvaṉ iḷammaṉiviyi vittiruntāl avaṉ vīramum nērmiyumuṭiya oruvaṉi avaḷukku aṟimukappaṭutti avaṉ mūlam nalla kuḻantiyip peṟuvati aṉumatittār; nalla oḻukkam uḷḷa oruvaṉ vivākamāṉa oru peṇṇiṉ aḻakik kaṇṭu avaḷmītu ācippaṭṭāl avaṉ avaḷ kaṇavaṉiṉ viruppip peṟṟu avaḷ naṭpi aṭital tavaṟuṭiya taṉṟu eṉavum avar vitittār. piḷḷikaḷ peṟṟōrukkuriyaṉa valla aracāṅkattiṟkuriyavi eṉṉum kāraṇatti likurkās eṉpavar taṉatu caṭṭattukku ātāramākak koṇṭār. atēṉcil maṉiviyari naṇpa rukku iraval koṭuttal naṭpiṉ aṭiyāḷam eṉak karutappaṭṭatu. coki rattas eṉṉum kirēkka ñāṉiyār cāntappi eṉṉum taṉatu maṉiviyit taṉatu māṇākkarum naṇparumākiya alcipiyātis eṉpavarukku iraval aḷittār. kāṟṟō eṉpavar taṉatu taliviyākiya mēriyā eṉpavaḷi horeṉciyas eṉpavarukku iravalākak koṭuttār.”
maṉiviyari māṟṟikkoḷḷutal
vaṭa amerikkāvil vāḻum eskimōvā tamatu iruppiṭatti viṭṭu ayaliṭaṅkaḷukku vēṭṭiyāṭac ceṉṟu taṅkuvārkaḷ. avvāṟu cellumpōtu oruvaṉuṭiya maṉivikku avaṉuṭaṉ cellamuṭiyāta kāraṇam etuvum irukkumāyiṉ avaḷuṭiya kaṇavaṉ aṅku uḷḷa oruvaṉiṭam taṉatu maṉiviyik koṭuttuviṭṭu avaṉuṭiya maṉivi yit taṉṉuṭaṉ aḻittuc celvāṉ. avaṉ tirumpi vantatum maṟṟa vaṉatu maṉiviyik koṭuttuviṭṭut taṉatu maṉiviyip peṟṟuk koḷvāṉ. itu evvakiyilum oḻukkattukku māṟupaṭṭatākak karutappaṭuvatilli.
kaṟpu
peṇ, kaṇavaṉukku aṭaṅki naṭattalōṭu kaṇavaṉi oḻinta piṟa āṭavarōṭu cērkki vittiruttal kūṭāteṉṉum kaṭṭuppāṭu tanti āṭcik kālattil uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ik kaṭṭuppāṭṭik kaṭippiṭit toḻukutalē kaṟpu eṉappaṭṭatu. tirumaṇattukkup piṉpu peṇkaḷ kaṇavaṉukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭē naṭantu vantārkaḷ, āṉāl tirumaṇattukku muṉpu peṇkaḷ tamatu tayavit tām virumpiya āṭavarukku aḷittu vantārkaḷ. tirumaṇattukku muṉ peṇkaḷ āṭavar palaruṭaṉ cērkki vittukkoḷvatum, avarkaḷ mūlam piḷḷikaḷip peṟuvatum tirumaṇattukkut taṭiyākak karutappaṭavilli28. iḷami toṭṭup peṇkaḷ kaṟpāka irukkap paḻakum poruṭṭē iḷamiyil avar kaḷukkut tirumaṇa niccayam ceyyappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. iv vaḻakkiṉālēyē peṇkaḷ oru āṭavaṉi māttiram cērvatum avaṉ mūlam piḷḷi kaḷip peṟutalumākiya kaṟpu muṟiyip paḻakiṉārkaḷ. kaṟpu eṉpataṟkup pativiratam eṉpatu iṉṉoru peyar. kaṟpu allatu pativiratam uṭiyavaḷ pativirati eṉappaṭṭāḷ. pativiratam eṉpataṟku orē patiyi aṭiyum viratam allatu orē patiyiṭattil uṟutippāṭu eṉpatu poruḷ.
kuḻanti maṇam putiyatā?
intiya nāṭṭil cila makkaṭ kūṭṭaṅkaḷukkiṭiyil ciṟuvar ciṟumiyarukku maṇañceyyum vaḻakkam iruntu varukiṉṟatu. itu mikak koṭiya vaḻakkam eṉak karutappaṭṭu ataṟku etirāka cāratā caṭṭam eṉa oṉṟum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ciṉṉañ ciṟu vayatil maṇañ ceyyappaṭṭa iruvaruḷ kaṇavaṉ iḷamiyilēyē iṟantuviṭṭāl peṇ taṉatu vāḻkkikkālam muḻuvatum tirumaṇam muṭiyātu kūntal kaḷintu eḷiya vāḻkki naṭattutal vēṇṭumeṉṉum camūkak kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṉā lum, mika iḷamiyilēyē peṇkaḷ kuḻantikaḷip peṟat toṭaṅki virivil iḷittup pōvatōṭu piḷḷikaḷum palamaṟṟavarkaḷā kiṉṟārkaḷ eṉṉum karuttiṉālum cāratā caṭṭam uṇṭāvatāyiṟṟu. peṇkaḷ kaṟpāka irukkap paḻakuvataṟkākavē ivvaḻakkam ātiyil uṇṭāyiṟṟu. peṇkaḷ kaṟpāka irukkap paḻakiya ik kālattil ciṟu piḷḷit tirumaṇaṅkaḷ vēṇṭiyatilli eṉpatu uṇmiyē29.
tirumaṇa varalāṟṟuc curukkam
makkaḷiṉ muṟparuvattil āṭavar oru taṉik kūṭṭamākavum, peṇkaḷ maṟṟoru taṉik kūṭṭamākavum vāḻntārkaḷ. appoḻutu āṭavar kūṭṭattiṉar peṇ kūṭṭattiṉariṉ kaṇavarākak karutappaṭṭaṉar. ōru kūṭṭattiluḷḷa peṇkaḷellōrukkum maṟṟak kūṭṭattiluḷḷa āṭavarellām kaṇavar eṉpatum, āṭavarkaḷ kūṭṭattiluḷḷa peṇkaḷ ellōrum maṉiviyar eṉpatum koḷkiyaḷavilō naṭimuṟi yilō iruntu vantatu. ak kālattilēyē oru kūṭṭattuḷ allatu kōttirattuḷ āṇ peṇkaḷ tirumaṇañ ceytukoḷḷālākatu eṉṉum vaḻakku uṇṭāyiṟṟu. iṉṟum teṉṉintiya makkaṭ piriviṉar palariṭiyilum, ulakiṉ pala pākaṅkaḷilum iv vaḻakku iruntu varukiṉ ṟatu. teṉṉintiya makkaḷ kōttiram eṉak koṇṭatu tāy vaṭṭattiyē yākum. kōttirameṉpatu kulakkuṟi “Totem” eṉa maṟṟiya makkaḷiṭiyē vaḻaṅkum. iṉṟu nīlakiri maliyiluḷḷa tōṭar vakuppil āṭavar cilar peṇkaḷ cilarit tamakkup potuvil maṉiviyarāka vittirukkum vaḻakku uṇṭu eṉṟu tēsraṉ eṉpār kūṟiyuḷḷār.
ivvakik kūṭṭat tirumaṇam (Group marriage) cilakālam naṭipeṟṟapiṉ peṇkaḷ tām virumpiya oru āṭavaṉiyō, pala āṭavariyō terinteṭuttārkaḷ; tām virumpātapōtu avarkaḷiṉ toṭarpi nīkkiviṭṭaṉar. cila kālattiṉpiṉ avarkaḷ pala āṭavari oriṭattil kūṭṭi avarkaḷuḷ tām virumpiya oruvaṉit terintu koṇṭaṉar. ivvaḻakkē cuyamvaram eṉappaṭṭatu.30
āṭavar kūṭṭattiluḷḷa oru āṭavaṉ peṇkaḷ kūṭṭattiṉiṉṟum oru peṇṇit taṉakku maṉiviyākkik koṇṭuvāḻa virumpiṉāṉ. appoḻutu avaṉ, peṇkaḷ kūṭṭattiṉiṉṟu peṇṇit tiruṭit taṉatu kūṭṭattukkuk koṇṭu celvāṉāyiṉaṉ. itaṉip peṇkaḷ kūṭṭattiṉar virumpamāṭṭārkaḷ. itaṉāl avarkaḷ tamatu kūṭṭatti ṉiṉṟum oruttiyi iḻantu viṭukiṉṟaṉar. piṉpu oru peṇṇip peṟa virumpiyavaṉ, akkūṭṭattukku nērum naṭṭatti niṟivu ceyyum poruṭṭu akkūṭṭattārukku āṭu māṭu mutaliyavikaḷik koṭut tāṉ. itil iruntu peṇkaḷi āṭu māṭukaḷik koṭuttup peṟum vaḻakku uṇṭāyiṟṟu. itaṉ piṉ maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷ illattiṟku ceṉṟu peṇṇiṉ cuṟṟattāriṉ icivippeṟṟu avaḷi maṇantu taṉatu iṭattukkuk koṇṭuceṉṟu vāḻtalākiya vaḻakku uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
nāṇam
nāṇam peṇkaḷukkuc ciṟanta kuṇam. atu avarkaḷukku iyalpāka uḷḷatu eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉ kāraṇatti ārāyiṉ nāṇam eṉpatu iṭikkālattiṟṟōṉṟiyatēyākum eṉpatu teḷi vākum. peṇkaḷ tām virumpiya āṭavarit terintu eṭuttuk koḷ ḷum kālattil āṇkaḷikkaṇṭu nāṇutalākiya kuṇam avarkaḷukku uṇṭāyiruntatu eṉṟu kūṟutal amiyātu.
oru peṇ pala kaṇavari maṇantirunta kālattil āṇ peṇ cērkki toṭarpāṉa poṟāmi uṇṭātalum, oru āṇ pala maṉivi yari maṇantirukkumpōtum avvakip poṟāmi tōṉṟiyirutta lum iyalpaṉṟō. ip poṟāmiyilēyē oruvaṉ oruttiyiyē maṇattalum, orutti oruvaṉiyē maṇattalumākiya maṇamuṟi uṇ ṭāyiṟṟu. ak kālattil oruvaṉukkuriya maṉiviyi piṟaṉoruvaṉ virumpik kavarāmal iruttaṟkum, oruttikkuriya kaṇavaṉip piṟaḷ orutti kavarntu koḷḷāmal irukkumpaṭiyum, avvaki viruppup piṟarukku eḻātirukkumpaṭiyum cila camūkak kaṭṭup pāṭukaḷ tōṉṟi vaḷarcciyaṭintaṉa.
oru ciṅkam allatu puli oru vilaṅkik kolkiṉṟatu. atu ataṉ iriyi maṟivāṉa ōriṭattiṟku iḻuttucceṉṟu av viṭattilēyē ataṉi uṇkiṉṟatu. atu maṟivāṉa iṭattukku ataṉi iḻuttuc celvatu māmicatti uṇavākak koḷḷum piṟa vilaṅkukaḷ avviriyik kāṇiṉ aviyum avviriyi uṇṇa virumpum; ataṉāl avi avviriyik kavarvataṟku muyaṟci ceyyum; ataṉāl ciṅkam allatu puliyiṉ amitikkut taṭiyuṇṭākum eṉṟu terikiṉṟatu. ivvakik karuttiṉik koṇṭē makkaḷum āṇ peṇ cērkkikaḷip piṟar kāṇātapaṭi maṟivil vittukkoḷvārāyiṉar. itu, kālattil nāṇattakkatākak koḷḷappaṭalāyiṟṟu. peṇkaḷ āṭavaruṭaṉ camamamākap paḻakuvatālum avarkaḷuṭaṉ naṉku aḷavaḷāvu talālum āṇ peṇ cērkki uṇarccikaḷ tōṉṟutal iyalpu. itu paṟṟiyē peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷik kāṇiṉ nāṇi otuṅki niṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṉum kaṭṭuppāṭu uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ikkaṭṭuppāṭṭiṉ vaḷarcciyi nāṇam, maṭam, accam, payirppu eṉac collappaṭum kuṇaṅkaḷākum.
uṭi nāṇattukkāka aṇiyappaṭṭatā?
uṭi nāṇattukkāka aṇiyappaṭṭatu eṉa iṉṟu nām karutu kiṉṟōm. uṭi nāṇampaṟṟit tōṉṟavilli. veppa nāṭukaḷil uṭi avaciyam tēvippaṭavilli. kuḷir nāṭukaḷil makkaḷ tōl kampaḷi mutaliyavikaḷāl tamatu uṭalip pōrttukkoṇṭārkaḷ. itiliruntu makkaḷ tamatu uṭampi alaṅkarikkum vaḻakkam uṇṭāyiṟṟu; avarkaḷ alaṅkārattiṉ poruṭṭu uṭampiṉ cila pakutikaḷ teriyumpaṭiyum cila pakuti maṟiyumpaṭiyum uṭiyaṇivarāyiṉar. itiliruntu uṭi aṇiyum vaḻakku vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. mukamūṭikaḷi aṇital uṭampi niṟamikaḷ pūci alaṅkarittal mutaliyaṉa mantira vittikkārariṉ vaḻakkaṅkaḷāka viruntaṉa. ivvaḻakkiṉiṉṟum uṭi aṇiyum paḻakkam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. paḻiya makkaḷ “kaṇvippa”tāl nōykaḷum tīmikaḷum uṇṭāvati nampiṉārkaḷ. karukkoṇṭa peṇkaḷum, piḷḷiyippeṟṟa peṇkaḷum taṅkaḷ taṉaṅkaḷip piṟar pārppiṉ kaṇṇūṟu uṇṭākum; ataṉāl pāl vaṟṟippōkum, allatu taṉaṅkaḷmītu kaṭṭi, mutaliya nōykaḷ uṇṭākum eṉa nampiṉārkaḷ. ākavē avarkaḷ tamatu mārpi maṟikkalāṉārkaḷ.
pālittīvu, maliyāḷam mutaliya nākarikamaṭinta nāṭukaḷil mārpi maṟiyātuviṭutal nāṇattakkateṉṟāvatu, nākarikak kuṟivāṉa teṉṟāvatu karutappaṭuvatilli. itaṉāl nāṇam eṉpatum oruvakip paḻakkam eṉa naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
āṭavar, tamatu āṇ uṟuppiṉip piṟar nōkkiṉ kaṇṇūṟu uṇṭāki nōyuṇṭākum, malaṭu uṇṭākum eṉak karutip piṟar kāṇā vaki ataṉi maṟippārāyiṉar. peṇkaḷum itē nōkkattuṭaṉ tamatu uṟappukkaḷi maṟikkalāyiṉar. iv vaḻakkaṅkaḷ nīṇṭakālam talimuṟi talimuṟiyāka vantaṉa. ataṉāl avvuṟuppukkaḷip piṟar kāṇutal nāṇattakkatu eṉṉum karuttu makkaḷ uḷḷattil patintu nāṇamāka māṟiṟṟu.
cīṉar tamatu peṇkaḷiṉ pātaṅkaḷic ciṟu vayatiliruntu iruppuc cappāttiṭṭu vaḷarātirukkac ceyvar. avarkaḷ tamatu pātaṅkaḷip piṟa revarum kāṇumpaṭi viṭamāṭṭārkaḷ. avikaḷip piṟar kāṇumpaṭi viṭutal āṭavarum makaḷirum tamatu irakaciya uṟuppukkaḷip piṟar kāṇum paṭi viṭutaliṉum pārkka nāṇattakkateṉa avarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar. itu nāṇam evvāṟu uṇṭākiṉṟateṉa nām naṉku aṟintu koḷvataṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭākum.31
kirēkka nāṭṭil paruvamaṭinta peṇkaḷum vāliparum nirvāṇamāka niṉṟu palar muṉṉiliyil malyuttam ceytaṉar. nirvāṇam veṭkattukkuriyatāka avarkaḷāl karutappaṭavilli. kirēkka urōmaṉ ciṟpaṅkaḷiyum teṉṉintiya ālayaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum ciṟpaṅkaḷi yum nōkkumiṭattup peṇkaḷ arikkumēl uṭiyiṉṟiyiruttalō, muḻumiyum uṭiyiṉṟiyiruttalō veṭkattukkuriyatākak karutappaṭa villi eṉṟu naṉku viḷaṅkum.
vitavikaḷi uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟṟutal
vitavikaḷi uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟṟum vaḻakku tanti āṭcik kālattilēyē uṇṭāyiṟṟu. tammuṭaṉ iṟakkum maṉivi, vēlikkārar kutiri pōṉṟavi tammuṭaṉ maṟu ulakukkuc celkiṉṟaṉa eṉa nampap paṭṭu vantatu. ākavē piratāṇi oruvaṉ iṟantapōtu avaṉ maṉiviyar vēliyāḷar mutaliyōrum uṭaṉ vittuk koḷuttappaṭṭārkaḷ allatu putikkappaṭṭārkaḷ32 maṉiviyari uṭaṉ kaṭṭi ēṟṟuvataṟkup piṟitōr kāraṇamum iruntatu. kaṇavaṉ iṟantapiṉ avaṉ maṉiviyar piṟa āṭavarik kaṇavarākak koṇṭu vāḻvār eṉṉum poṟāmi avaṉ uḷḷattil kuṭi koṇṭiruntatu. avaṉatu eṇṇatti niṟi vēṟṟum poruṭṭum peṇkaḷ uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟṟappaṭṭārkaḷ.
vitavikaḷi moṭṭi aṭikkac ceytal
maṉiviyarik kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟṟuvatākiya pollāta vaḻakkam ellā nāṭukaḷilum niṉṟupōka nam intiya nāṭṭil cila makkaḷiṭiyē māttiram camīpa kālam variyil naṭipeṟuvatāyiṟṟu. atu pirāmaṇa makkaḷiṭiyē camaya campantamum peṟṟu kiriyikaḷuṭaṉ naṭattappaṭṭu varuvatāyiṟṟu. ikkoṭiya vaḻakkam naṭavātapaṭi āṅkila āṭciyiṉar caṭṭam vakuttaṉar. avvāṟu ceytami camayattukku māṟu eṉa vitīkariṟ palar araciṉari etirttup pōrāṭiṉar. maṉac cāṭcikku etirāṉatum mirukattaṉmi uṭiyatumākiya ivvaḻakkatti araciṉar tammāl paripālikkappaṭum makkaḷiṭiyē naṭakkaviṭuvārkaḷā? araciṉari etirttu kaṇavaṉi iḻanta maṉiviyar kūntalik kaḷintu vitavi viratam kākka vēṇṭumeṉavum avarkaḷ maṟumaṇam ceyyavō allatu piṟar avarkaḷi maṇañceytu koḷḷavō kūṭāteṉavum camūkak kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷ ceyvārāyiṉar. oruvaṉuṭaṉ maṉiyiyāyirunta vaḷ iṉṉoruvaṉic cērntu cukam aṭital kūṭāteṉṉum poṟāmi uṇarcciyiṉāl tanti āṭciyāḷar uṇṭākkiya cuyanalac caṭṭaṅkaḷē ivikaḷākum. av vaḻakkattil teyvīkam etuvum irukka villi. iv vaḻakkip parikacippataṟkup pōlum arāpik katikaḷ oṉṟil oru tēcattil maṉivi iṟantāl kaṇavaṉi uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṉum caṭṭam iruntateṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. peṇkaḷi uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟṟavum, moṭṭi aṭittuk koḷḷavum camūkac caṭṭañ ceyta āṇkaḷ tam maṉiviyar iṟantāl tāmum tam maṉiviyarukku vicuvācamuṭiyavarkaḷāy iruppataṟku aṟikuṟiyākat tāmum uṭaṉ kaṭṭi ēṟa vēṇṭum, tāmum kimmi viratam kāttalvēṇṭum eṉṉum caṭṭaṅkaḷ ceyyātiruntami tāṉ vinōtam. āṭavariṉ maṉap pāṉmi eṉṉē! iraṅkattakkatu.
iyaṟki viti eṉṉa?
āṇ peṇ cērkki cantatip perukkattukkākavē uṇṭā kiṉṟatu eṉa nām nammic cūḻntuḷḷa acivuḷḷa acivillāta uyirt tōṟṟaṅkaḷi ellām nōkki naṉku aṟintukoḷḷutal kūṭum. cantatiyip perukkum poruṭṭut tām mutalil terinteṭutta āṭavaṉ mūlamē mēlum mēlum tām cantatiyip perukkutal vēṇṭum eṉap peṇkaḷ uḷḷattil utayamākum oruvaki uṇarcciyiṉālēyē orutti oruvaṉiyē kaṇavaṉākak koḷḷum uṇarcciyō, vaḻakkō, kaṟpu eṉṉum oḻukkamō uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ivvuṇarcci uṟutippaṭāta aḷavil avaḷ, tāṉ virumpiya piṟa kaṇavarit teriteṭukkum ellā urimikaḷum uṇṭu. ivvaki iyaṟki vitikaḷukku iṭiyūṟāka, muṭṭukkaṭṭiyāka irukkum cuyanalamuṭiya āṭavariṉ koṭiya uṇarcci iraṅkattakkatoṉṟē yākum.
intu “lā” vil peṇkaḷukku urimikaḷ
maṟukkappaṭṭatiṉ kāraṇam
intu “lā” eṉṉum caṭṭattiṉāl intiya makkaḷ āḷappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. itu intiya makkaḷiṉ oru cārārukkuriya caṭṭa tiṭṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭatēyākum. uṇmiyil atu intiya makkaḷ ellōrukkum uriya caṭṭaṅkaḷ ākamāṭṭā. kiḻakku intiyak kampaṉi yār intiyāvil āṭci toṭaṅkiyapōtu intiya makkaḷiṭam vaḻaṅkum caṭṭa nūlkaḷik koṇṭuvarumpaṭi makkaḷukku aṟivittaṉar. avarkaḷiṟ cilar ak kālattil āriyap pirāmaṇarkaḷāl tamatu ākkaṅ karuti eḻutivitta pōtāyaṉam apastampam mutaliya nīti nūlkaḷiyum piṟa smiruti nūlkaḷiyum koṭuttārkaḷ. makkaḷiṉ āṭcikkuriya caṭṭaṅkaḷ ak kālattil eḻuti vikkappaṭavilli eṉṟum, avikaḷi niyāyat tār (Judges) naṉku aṟintiruntārkaḷ eṉṟum mekastīṉas eṉpavarē kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. ākavē intiya makkaḷiṉ uṇmiyāṉa caṭṭaṅkaḷ kiḻakkintiyak kampaṉiyariṉ kikku eṭṭavilli. ākavē avarkaḷ kiṭittavikaḷik koṇṭu intu lā eṉṉum caṭṭaṅkaḷic cey tārkaḷ. āriyar tanti āṭciyiṉar. avarkaḷ peṇkaḷi kīḻppaṭutti yiruntaṉar, ātaliṉālēyē ‘intu lā’vil peṇkaḷiṉ urimikaḷ maṟukkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. teṉṉintiya cātikkuḻukkaḷ (Castes and Tribes of Southern India) eṉṉum nūlil tēsraṉ eḻutiyuḷḷavikaḷi nōkku vōmāyiṉ intiyarkaḷiṉ caṭṭaṅkaḷ vēṟu “intu lā” vēṟu eṉṟu naṉku viḷaṅkum. intu lā intiyar “lā”vāka illāmiyiṉālēyē atu tiruttappaṭal vēṇṭum eṉṉum kūccalkaḷ kiḷampukiṉṟaṉa.
tāli tarittal
tirumaṇak kālattil kaṇavaṉ, maṉivikkut tāli kaṭṭum vaḻakkam teṉṉintiya nāṭṭil māttiram kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tāli kaṭṭutal eṉpatu oru māṭṭukkuk kayiṟu kaṭṭutalpōl tāliyik kaṭṭip peṇṇi āṭavaṉ aṭimi koḷḷutal eṉṉum ōr karuttum ulavukiṟatu. teṉṉintiya makkaḷ iṉṟu pōlavē paḻaṅkālattilum pūkkaḷi māli yākavum viṭu pūvākavum aṇiyum viruppuṭiyar. peṇ, or āṭavaṉik kaṇavaṉākat terinteṭukkum kālaṅkaḷil avaḷ avaṉukkup pū māliyic cūṭṭiṉāḷ. āṭavaṉum, peṇṇum cama urimiyuṭiya varkaḷ eṉṉum nilimiyil tirumaṇam nikaḻumpōtu āṭavaṉ peṇṇukku māli iṭṭāṉ; peṇ āṭavaṉukku māli yiṭṭāḷ. muṟkālap peṇkaḷum ciṟuvarum pulip paṟkōtta mālikaḷi aṇivatu vaḻakkam. āṭavaṉ tirumaṇak kālattil peṇṇukku avvaki māli oṉṟik koṭuttāṉ. iṉṟum teṉṉintiya makkaṭ kūṭṭattiṉar palaruk kiṭiyil tirumaṇak kālattil peṇṇukkut tāli kaṭṭuvōr purōkitar, kaṇavaṉiṉ uṭaṉ piṟantāḷ mutaliyōrāvar. cila kūṭṭattiṉariṭiyē tirumaṇam niccayamāṉavuṭaṉ kaṇavaṉiṉ uṭaṉ piṟantāḷ peṇ vīṭṭukkuc ceṉṟu avaḷ kaḻuttil tāliyik kaṭṭiviṭukiṟāḷ. iṟṟikku iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉmuṉ tirumaṇak kālattil tāli tirikkum vaḻakku iruntatākat teriyavilli. kimmi aṭinta peṇ taṉatu ki vaḷikaḷi uṭitteṟintuviṭuvāḷ. iṉṟu tālitarit tiruttal oru peṇṇiṉ kaṇavaṉ uyirōṭirukkiṟāṉ eṉpati uṇarttukiṉṟatu. iṉṟu kayiṟṟil allatu poṉ nāṇiṟ kōkkappaṭṭirukkum tāli pulip palliṉ vaṭivuṭiyatā yirukkiṉṟatu. cila kūṭṭattiṉar poṭṭut tāliyi aṇivar. mēlnāṭṭavarkaḷ tālikkup patil mōtiram koṭuppar.
uyirkaḷil āṇiṉaṅkaḷ peṇṇiṉaṅkaḷi viṭa aḻakuṭiyaṉa. ēṉ?
paṟavikaḷil peṇ paṟavikaḷi viṭa āṇ paṟavikaḷē aḻakuṭiyaṉa. kōḻi, mayil mutaliya paṟavi iṉaṅkaḷip pārttu iv vuṇmiyi nām naṉku aṟintu koḷḷalākum. perumpālum paṟavikaḷ āṇṭiṉ ciṟ cila kālaṅkaḷilēyē muṭṭiyiṭṭuk kuñcu porik kiṉṟaṉa. peṇ paṟavikaḷ āṇ paṟavikaḷuṭaṉ cērntu karukkoḷḷum kālattil āṇ paṟavikaḷ peṇ paṟavikaḷik kavarakkūṭiyavāṟu niṟattāl pirakācamaṭikiṉṟaṉa. vilaṅkukaḷilum peṇṇiṉaṅkaḷi viṭa āṇ vilaṅkukaḷē aḻakuṭiyaṉa. āṇ ciṅkattukkuc caṭiyiruttal, cila peṇ vilaṅkukaḷukkuk kompillā tiruttal āṇ vilaṅku kaḷukku aḻakiya kompiruttal pōṉṟavikaḷālum piṟavaṟṟālum itaṉi nām naṉku aṟintu koḷḷalām. puṉuku, cavvātup pūṉikaḷiṉ āṇkaḷ āṇ peṇ cērkkikkuriya paruva kālaṅkaḷil naṟumaṇatti veḷippaṭuttip peṇ iṉaṅkaḷukkuk kavarcci uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉa.33 makkaḷuḷḷum āṭavarē aḻakiṟ ciṟantavarāvar. peṇkaḷ veḷiyiṟ ceṉṟu vēli ceyyātiruppatiṉāl avarkaḷ ciṟitu miṉu miṉuppu aṭikiṉṟaṉar. iṉṉum mēṉi miṉukkup poruḷkaḷ, aṇikalaṉkaḷ aḻakiya āṭikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttik koḷvatāl avarkaḷ āṭavari lum pārkka aḻakāṟ ciṟantavar pōlak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. iru pāliṉa riyum āṭiyaṇi vakikaḷiṉṟi niṟutti nōkkiṉ iyaṟkiyil āṇkaḷē aḻakuṭiyar eṉat tōṉṟum. iyaṟki, āṇiṉaṅkaḷip peṇṇiṉaṅkaḷilum aḻakākap paṭittiruppatu peṇṇiṉaṅkaḷ āṇkaḷiṉ aḻakāṟ kavarappaṭṭu avikaḷi iyaṟkit terivu ceytu koḷvataṟkē yākumeṉka. peṇkaḷiṉ poruṭṭu oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu pōriṭuvaṉa āṇkaḷē; peṇkaḷ orupōtum āṇkaḷiṉ poruṭṭup pōriṭuvatilli.
potu makaḷir
oru kālattil tirumaṇamākāta peṇkaḷ tamatu tayavit tām virumpiya āṭavarkaḷukku aḷippavarkaḷāyiruntaṉar. oruvaṉ oruttiyi maṇañ ceytukoḷvatāl avaḷ, kuṭumpattiṉ potu uṭimi eṉpatiṉiṉṟum nīṅki oruvaṉiṉ taṉi uṭimiyāṉāḷ. ivvāṟu maṇamāki oruvaṉukku urimiyākāta peṇkaḷ potu makaḷi rākavē iruntuvantaṉar. iṉṟum cila camūkaṅkaḷukkuḷ tirumaṇamāṉa peṇkaḷ tamatu tayavit tam kaṇavariṉ uṟaviṉarākiṟa āṭavarukku aḷikkak kaṭamippaṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. piṟpōkkuṭiya teṉṉintiya makkaḷ cilariṭiyē iṉṟum ivvaḻakkattik kāṇalākum.34
mēṟkut tēcaṅkaḷil potu makaḷirāka vāḻkki naṭattum peṇkaḷiṉ toki mikap palavākum. nītipati liṉci eṉpār kūṟiyirup patu varumāṟu: ‘niyūyōrk nakaril taṅkaḷ kaṇavarallāta āṭavaruṭaṉ vāḻum peṇkaḷ aimpattiṉāyirattukku mēṟpaṭṭavarāvar. ilaṇṭaṉ paṭṭiṉattippaṟṟi aṟiyumiṭattu aṅku immuṟiyil vāḻum peṇkaḷiṉ tokiyum ivveṇṇukku nērākakkūṭum eṉat teri kiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu vāḻum peṇkaḷ perumpālum 18 vayatukku iṭip paṭṭavarkaḷāvar.’35 potu makaḷir kālattil vilimātar ākamāṟiṉar. 1926ām āṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭa oru nūlil pāris nakaril māttiram kāṇappaṭum pativu ceyta vilimātariṉ eṇ eḻupatiṉāyiram eṉṟum, pativu ceyyātavarkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki ittokiyilum paṉmaṭaṅku ākum eṉavum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.36
tēvaṭiyāḷ vaḻakkam
muṟkālattil camayattiṉ peyarāl tīya oḻukkaṅkaḷ pala nilaviṉa. avikaḷuḷ tēvaṭiyāḷ vaḻakkamum oṉṟākum. itu akkāla makkaḷāl kuṟṟamākak karutappaṭavilli. oru kālattil peṇkaḷi uyiruṭaṉ iṟanta kaṇavarōṭu vittuk koḷuttutali makkaḷ cilar uyarnta aṟamākak karutiṉārkaḷallavā? āpirikkāviṉ cila pākaṅkaḷil vayatu mutirnta peṟṟōrip piḷḷikaḷ koṉṟu avarkaḷiṉ iṟicciyik kaṭikaḷil viṟṟārkaḷ. itu avarkaḷ camūkattil kuṟṟamākak karutappaṭa villi. airōppiya nāṭukaḷil nōyiṉāṟ piḻikkamāṭṭātavarkaḷi avar cuṟṟattiṉar ālayaṅkaḷukkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu taṇṭāyutattiṉāl aṭittuk koṉṟārkaḷ. iv vakit taṇṭāyutaṅkaḷ kuṭumpat taṇṭā yutaṅkaḷ eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ iṉṟum cuviṭaṉ tēcattiluḷḷa kiṟittuva ālayaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa. tēvaṭiyāḷ vaḻakkam ulakam muḻumiyiṉum orukāl kāṇappaṭṭatāyiṉum atu iṉṟum intiya nāṭṭil kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu. avvaḻakkam nāḷ vītam maṟintu varukiṉṟatu.
tēvaṭiyāḷ vaḻakkam uṇṭāṉa varalāṟu īṇṭu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. muṟkālattil āṭavarum makaḷirum taṉit taṉik kūṭṭattiṉarāka vāḻntu vantārkaḷ. āṇṭil oru muṟiyō iru muṟiyō iru pālarum potu iṭaṅkaḷiṟ cantittukkoḷvatākiya vaḻakkam iruntu vantatu. piṉpu potu iṭamākiya ālayattil nikaḻum āṇ peṇ cērkki paricuttamuṭiya tākak karutappaṭṭatu. tanti āṭci uṇṭāki viyapicāram kuṟṟamākak koḷḷappaṭṭa kālattilum ālayaṅkaḷiṟ ceyyappaṭum viyapicāram kuṟṟamākak karutappaṭavilli. itu papilōṉiya, acīriya paḻañ caṭṭaṅ kaḷik koṇṭu naṉku aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. ak kālattilē mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷil peṇkaḷ ālayaṅkaḷiṟ ceṉṟu āṭavaric cērta lākiya nērttik kaṭaṉkaḷ ceytal carva cātāraṇamāka iruntu vantatu.37 mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷil ivvaḻakkam evvāṟu mika malintu kāṇap paṭṭaṉa eṉpataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭu kīḻē kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
muṟkālattil tāykkaṭavuḷariṉ kōyilkaḷil peṇkaḷ pūcāri kaḷākaviruntaṉar. kaṉṉippeṇkaḷ palar tēvaṭiyāṭkaḷāka vantaṭin taṉar. ālayaṅkaḷil nikaḻum āṇ peṇ cērkki puṉitamuṭiyateṉṟu karutappaṭṭatātaliṉ appeṇkaḷ ālayaṅkaḷukku varum āṇ tēva aṭiyāric cērvārāyiṉar. tēva aṭiyār ākiya peṇkaḷ āṇ kaṭavu ḷariṉ ālayaṅkaḷukku nērntu viṭappaṭṭa pōtu avarkaḷ kaṭavuḷiṉ maṉiviyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kaṭavuḷiṉ kaṭamiyik kurumār niṟivēṟṟi vantaṉar. ivipōṉṟa paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyē tēvatāci vaḻakkamāka nilavukiṉṟatu.
kaṉṉi aḻittalil accam
muṟkāla āṭavar peṇkaḷiṉ kaṉṉimiyi aḻippatil peritum accaṅkoṇṭiruntārkaḷ. ākavē kaṉṉip peṇkaḷ ālayaṅ kaḷukkuc ceṉṟu tām virumpiya āṭavarik kūṭit tamatu kaṉṉimiyi aḻittuk koṇṭārkaḷ. cila nāṭukaḷil aracarum kurumārum ik kaṭamiyi niṟivēṟṟi vantārkaḷ.38 maliyāḷattil ivvaḻakkam camīpakālam variyil nikaḻvatāyiṟṟu.39 viyapicāraṅkaḷukkuc camayam ātaravaḷittapaṭiyālē ālayaṅkaḷil viyapicārampuriyum tēvaṭiyāṭkaḷ niṟintiruntaṉar. tañcāvūr periya ālayattil 400 tēvaṭiyāṭkaḷ iruntārkaḷ eṉpataṟkuk kalveṭṭu toṭarpāṉa cāṉṟu uṇṭu. makamat kacaṉi cōmanāta ālayattit takarttapōtu aṅku munnūṟu tēvaṭi yāṭkaḷ iruntaṉar.
tāymār kuḻantikaḷukkup pālūṭṭi vaḷarkkuṅ kālam
karukkoḷḷuṅkālattil māttiram peṇ vilaṅkukaḷum āṇ vilaṅkukaḷum cērkiṉṟaṉa. karukkoṇṭa piṉ peṇ vilaṅkukaḷ āṇ vilaṅkukaḷit turatti viṭukiṉṟaṉa. kuṭṭi yīṉṟu avikaḷ peritāṉapiṉ maṟupaṭiyum karukkoḷḷum kālattilēyē avi maṟupaṭiyum āṇ vilaṅkukaḷic cērukiṉṟaṉa. karuttarittapiṉ avi āṇ vilaṅku kaḷic cērvatilli. itē viti makkaḷiṭiyum iruntatu. iṉṟum piṟpōkkuṭiya makkaḷiṭiyē ivvoḻukku iruntu varukiṉṟatu. cila kūṭṭattiṉariṭiyē peṇkaḷ āṟu ēḻu āṇṭukaḷ variyil piḷḷi kaḷukkup pālkoṭuttu vaḷarppārkaḷ. avvaḷavu kālamum avarkaḷ āṇ cērkkiyiṉṟiyiruppar. nākarikamē ippapoḻutu ivviyaṟki vitiyi māṟṟiviṭṭatupōlum.40
ik kālattu maruttuva aṟiñarum aṟivāḷikaḷum tāḻnta makkaḷiṭiyē kāṇappaṭum āṇ peṇ cērkki vitikaḷē mika ēṟpuṭiyaṉa eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.41
āṇ peṇ cērkkiyiṉ nōkkam
āṇ, peṇ cērkki cantatikaḷip perukkum nōkkam oṉṟaṟ kākavē uḷḷatu. ivviyaṟki vitiyi mīṟi naṭappavarkaḷ uṟutiyākat taṇṭaṉikkut tappamāṭṭārkaḷ. āṇ peṇ cērkkiyiṉāl āṇ kaḷē peritum tam valivi iḻantuviṭukiṉṟaṉar. maṉitaṉ tāṉ payaṉ paṭuttum māṭu kutirikaḷiṉ āṇiṉaṅkaḷip peṇ vilaṅkukaḷōṭu cērāmal irukkumpaṭi taṉiyē vittu vaḷarkkiṉṟāṉ; iṉṟēl avi kaḷiṉ vitiyi aṭittu viṭukiṟāṉ. āṇ peṇ cērkkiyiṉāl āṇkaḷ tamatu valivi iḻantu viṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉṉum karuttup paṟṟiyē avaṉ avvāṟu ceytu varukiṟāṉ.
muṉivar oruvar nīṇṭa kālam tavam ceytu koṇṭiruntār. oru nāḷ avar peṇ oruttiyikkaṇṭu avaḷmītu ācikoṇṭu avaḷic cērntār. ataṉāl avar taṉatu tava valivi iḻantuviṭṭār eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa paḻaṅkatikaḷ purāṇaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ikkati kaḷē peṇkaḷiṉ cērkkiyāl āṭavar tamatu uṭal vali maṉa valikaḷi iḻantuviṭukiṟārkaḷ eṉpati uṇarttuvataṟku eḻuntavikaḷēyākum. peṇkaḷiṉ cērkkiyil atikam aḻuntum āṭavaṉ aimpatu āṇṭu kaḷuḷḷēyē iḷittu ōyntuviṭukiṉṟāṉ. amerikkāvil maṉiviyi iḻantavarkaḷilum pārkkak kaṇavaṉi iḻanta peṇkaḷiṉ eṇ 2,00,000 atikam eṉṟu kaṇakku iṭappaṭṭatu. (1926)42 itu āṭavar aḷavukku atikam peṇpōkattil aḻuntiyataṉ payaṉ eṉap paṭukiṉṟatu.
peṇkaḷ uyarnili aṭivataṟku vaḻi
peṇkaḷ taṟpōtu tamatu vāḻakkikku āṭavariyē etirpārkka vēṇṭiya niliyil irukkiṉṟaṉar. tāmē poruḷīṭṭit tam vāḻkki yiyum kuṭumpa vāḻkkiyiyum naṭattum niliyiluḷḷa peṇkaḷ āṭavari oppavō, āṭavarilum mēlākavō atikāramuṭiyavar kaḷāka vāḻntu varutali nām kāṇkiṉṟōm. āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum vēliceytu poruḷīṭṭum vēlikkāra vakuppiṉarkaḷiṭiyē peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷ oppa atikāram uṭiyavarkaḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉar. cila camayaṅkaḷil āṭavaṉ uḻippaṟṟavaṉākiviṭa maṉiviyē āciriyi yākavō, eḻuttāḷiyākavō, vēli purintu kuṭumpattik kāppāṟṟu kiṉṟāḷ. ivvakik kuṭumpaṅkaḷil kaṇavaṉi viṭa maṉiviyē kuṭumpattil atikāram uṭiyavaḷ. ivipōṉṟa nikaḻccikaḷāl kuṭum pattil maṉivikkō kaṇavaṉukkō atikāram uṇṭāvatu poruḷā tāra niliyiṉāl eṉṟu naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. cila iṭaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷiṉ peṟṟōr peṇṇiṉ kaṇavaṉi vili koṭuttu (cītaṉam koṭuttu) vāṅkukiṉṟaṉar. peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōr evvaḷavu atikam vili koṭuk kiṟārkaḷō avvaḷavukkuk kaṇavaṉ peṇṇiṉ aṭimiyāka vāḻntu varukiṉṟāṉ. ivvaki nikaḻccikaḷip peritum ilaṅkiyiṉ vaṭapakuti yil vāḻum tamiḻk kuṭumpaṅkaḷiṭiyē nām iṉṟum kāṇalākum.
peṇkaḷ uyarnili eyta vēṇṭumāyiṉ avarkaḷ tamatu vāḻkkiyip piṟar evariṉ kiyiyum etirpārātu naṭattakkūṭiya niliyi eytal vēṇṭum. ākavē avarkaḷ tām paruvam aṭiyum kālattil tam vāḻkkic celavukku vēṇṭum poruḷ tokukkakkūṭiya kalviyi iḷavayatiliruntu payilutal vēṇṭum.
iṉṟu uṇmiyil nikaḻvatu eṉṉa?
iṉṟu āṭavaṉē peṇṇukku atikāri eṉpatu koḷki yaḷavil iruntu varukiṉṟatu. āṉāl nikaḻcci itaṟku nērmāṟākavē iruntuvarukiṉṟatu. antaraṅkattil kaṇavaṉ maṉivikku muṉ naṭunaṭuṅku kiṉṟāṉ. ivvakik kāṭcikaḷē, nāṭakaṅkaḷilum, pēcum paṭaṅkaḷilum kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tirumaṇamāṉa ovvoruvaṉukkum kaṇavaṉiviṭa maṉiviyē mēlāṉavallamiyuṭiyavaḷ eṉṟu itayattil naṉku teriyum. āyiṉum avaṉ taṉatu karutti eḷitil veḷiyiṭuvatilli.
uṇmiyāṉa aṉpu irukkumāṉāl
peṇ orutti taṉ kaṇavaṉiṭattil uṇmiyāṉa aṉpu vittiruntāl avaṉ tāṉ virumpiya iṉṉoruttiyi aṭitalik kuṟittu varuntamāṭṭāḷeṉṟum, avvāṟē kaṇavaṉum avaḷ tāṉ virumpiya iṉṉoruvaṉi aṭitalikkuṟittu kavali koḷḷa māṭṭāṉeṉṟum mēlnāṭṭaṟiñar kūṟiyuḷḷār.43
tēṉnilavu (Honey Moon)
tirumaṇattukkuppiṉ kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum ullācap payaṇañ ceytu cila nāṭkaḷ taṉittu vāḻtali haṉi mūṉ eṉṟu mēlnāṭṭa varkaḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. itu yātō ciṟanta oḻukkam eṉak karutik kīḻ nāṭṭavarkaḷiṟ palarum ataṉip piṉpaṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉar. muṟkālattil āṭavaṉ oruvaṉ oru tāyvaṭṭattiliruntu oru peṇṇit tiruṭik koṇṭu, attāyvaṭṭattiluḷḷavarkaḷiṉ ciṉam taṇiyum variyil maṟivil vāḻvatākiya ivvaḻakkamē “haṉi mūṉ” eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. nammavar mēl nāṭṭavarip pārttuk kāṟ caṭṭi, ṭi, kālar mutaliyavi kaḷi aṇivatu pōṉṟatu ivvaḻakkam. “kaṇṭa pāvaṉiyiṟ koṇṭi muṭital.”
peṇ eppoḻutum piṟa āṭavari niṉittu ācippaṭukiṟāḷā?
makkaḷukku maṇ, peṇ, poṉ eṉa mupperum ācikaḷ uṇṭu eṉat tattuva cāttirikaḷ kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. vilaṅkukaḷilum, paṟavikaḷi lum, ciṟṟuyirkaḷilum āṇkaḷē peṇṇi nāṭic celkiṉṟaṉa; makka ḷiṭiyum ivvitiyē amintaḷḷatu. iyaṟki viti ivvāṟākavum purāṇakkatikaḷilum, pāratam, irāmāyaṇaṅkaḷilum peṇkaḷ uṟutiyāṉa maṉamuṭiyavarkaḷ allar eṉṟum avarkaḷ karuttu eppoḻutum piṟa āṭavar mītu oṭikkoṇṭu irukkum eṉṟum, iyaṟkikku māṟāka eḻuti vikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṭavar peṇkaḷi aṭimippaṭuttiya kālattil tām eḻutum nūlkaḷil tām virumpiya vāṟellām peṇ kaḷippaṟṟi eḻutiviṭalāmallavā? “arukku maṅkiyar malaraṭi paravi yum” eṉa varum aruṇakiri nātar pāṭal, vilimātarāyiṉum avarkaḷ āṭavar eṇṇat tukku eḷitil iṭaṅkoṭār eṉpati viḷakkukiṉṟatu. (arukku-arukkukiṉṟa) “eṇṇaṟak kaṟṟu eḻuttaṟa vācittālum peṇputti eṉpatellām perum pētimittē” eṉpatupōṉṟa vākkiyaṅkaḷ ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu eṭukkappaṭavēṇṭum.
muttattiṉ varalāṟu
tāy kuḻantiyiṭattuḷḷa aṉpu mutal kātalvariyil palavaki uṇarccikaḷi muttam uṇarttukiṉṟatu. ākavē muttam palavakippaṭum. im muttattiṉ varalāṟu mika iṉpam aḷippatākum. ataṉi iṅku ārāyvōm.
uyarnta nākarika makkaḷ muttaṅkaḷiṉāl tamatu aṉpit teri vikkiṟārkaḷ. tāy piḷḷiyi muttamiṭukiṉṟāḷ, kātalar oruvari oruvar muttamiṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. cila nāṭukaḷil naṇparkaḷum oruvari oruvar muttamiṭṭukkoḷvar! im muttaṅkaḷil tāyiṉ muttamum kātalarkaḷiṉ muttamumē ciṟappuṭiyaṉa.
paḻiya ekiptiyar muttatti aṟiyār. paḻiya kirīcilum aciriyāvilum itu vērūṉṟiyiruntatu. intiyāvilē itu mikap paḻamiyē iruntatu. toṭutalē uṇarccikaḷuḷ mutaṉmiyuṭiyatu. muttamum itu campantamākat tōṉṟiṉa teṉak karutalām. paṟavikaḷ tamatu alakukaḷāl oṉṟi oṉṟu muttamiṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila pūccikaḷum ivvāṟu ceykiṉṟaṉa. karaṭikaḷiṭattum nāykaḷiṭattum kīḻ niliyiluḷḷa makkaḷ muttamiṭṭuk koḷvatupōṉṟa ceyalkaḷik kāṇalām. nākarika makkaḷiṉ muttattukkum, pūṉikaḷ oṉṟi oṉṟu mōppatu pōṉṟa kāṭṭumirāṇṭikaḷiṉ muttattukkum atika vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu.
muttattiṉ vakikaḷ
kīḻttaramāṉa muttaṅkaḷ mūkkōṭu mūkki uriñcutal eṉak kūṟappaṭum. mōttal pōṉṟa muttam mūkkōṭu campantappaṭṭatu. mayōriyar, cāṇṭavictiviṉar, eskimōvar, malāyariṉ perum pakutiyiṉarkaḷiṉ muttam ivvakiyiṉatē. mūkki uriñcuvatākiya muttam “malāya muttam” eṉappeyar peṟum. muttam koṭuppavaṉ muttam peṟupavaṉ mūkkil carikōṇam (Right angle) viḻumpaṭiyāka mūkki vittu aḻuttutal malāya muttam eṉappaṭum eṉṟum itu airōppiyar kikulukkuvati ottateṉṟum ṭārviṉ kūṟiyuḷḷār. teṉkaṭal tīvu makkaḷ, oruvar mūkkiṉmēl oruvar mūkki vittu aḻutti uriñci muttamiṭuvārkaḷ. āstirēliyar mukatti mukattōṭu uriñcikkoḷ var. tāḻnta makkaḷiṭiyē tāymār kuḻantikaḷi nakkuvārkaḷ. mikap paḻaṅkāla muttam mūkkik kaṉṉattil vippatākum. kayōntā (khyountha) makkaḷ vāyiyum mūkkiyum kaṉṉattil vittu uṟiñcu vārkaḷ. cīṉar, maṅkōliya cātiyiṉaruṭ palar ilāppiyar mutaliyōr mūkkik kaṉṉattukku nērāka vittu aḻutti mōntu kaṇ imikaḷit tāḻtti itaḻkaḷāl aḻuttuvar. yappāṉiya moḻiyil muttam eṉpatik kuṟikkum col illi. avarkaḷiṭiyē tāy kuḻantiyi muttamiṭuva tākiya muttam māttiram vaḻaṅkum.
airōppiyaruṭiya muttam itaḻkaḷi maṟṟavariṉ mukam tali allatu uṭalil, allatu itaḻkaḷil vippatākum. avarkaḷ mūkkip payaṉpaṭuttuvatilli. itu oru taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyākak kāṇappaṭuvatāl itu kāṭṭu mirāṇṭikaḷiṉ paḻakkattiliruntu vaḷarcciyaṭintateṉak karututal tavaṟākum.
muttam eṉpatu mōntum cuvittum pārttal eṉac cilar kūṟi yirukkiṉṟaṉar. cilar atu cuvittup pārkkum muṟiyāṉa vaṇakkam eṉpar. cilar atu kaṭittu uṟiñcip pārttal eṉpar. kāṭṭumirāṇṭit tāymār piḷḷikaḷi viḷiyāṭṭākak kaṭippārkaḷ. pala cātiyiṉa riṭiyē kātalarkaḷum ivvāṟu ceytu koḷvar. muttam eṉpatu oruvakip pēccu eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. kātalaruṭiya muttam tāyiṉ muttattiliruntu tōṉṟiyateṉac cilar kūṟuvar. tāy kuḻantikkuk koṭuppatallāta muttatti yappāṉiyar aṟiyār. āpirikka makkaḷiṭi yum aṉākarika makkaḷiṭiyum kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyarum kātalarum mutta miṭuvatilli. vaṭa amerikkap peṇ itaḻkaḷik kaṉṉattil metuvāka vittu muttamiṭukiṟāḷ. avaḷ yātum cattam ceyvatilli. airōppiya riṉ muttam uṇpatupōṉṟatu eṉac cīṉar kūṟuvar. nīkirōvaruṭiya muttam mōttal pōṉṟatu.
ilattiṉ moḻiyil mukattil allatu kaṉṉattil naṇparukku iṭum muttam acūlam (Assulum) eṉappaṭum. utaṭukaḷiyum utaṭukaḷi yum cērttu aṉpiṉāl iṭum muttam pāciyam (Basium) eṉappaṭum. kātalar itaḻōṭu itaḻ vittu muttamiṭuvatu cāviyam (Savium) eṉappaṭum. im muttaṅkaḷ pirāṉcilum maṟṟum airōppiya nāṭukaḷilum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. vāyil muttamiṭuvatu kātalarkaḷukku māttiram uriyatu. kātal kāraṇamāyallāmal vāyil muttamiṭuvatu pāvam eṉṟu pirāṉ ciyarāl karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.
kirēkka ilattiṉiya peṟṟōr piḷḷikaḷi muttamiṭṭārkaḷ; kātalarum maṇamāṉavarkaḷum oruvari oruvar muttamiṭṭārkaḷ. itu pōlavē āṇkaḷākiya naṇparkaḷum, āṇpeṇṇākiya naṇparkaḷum oruvari oruvar muttamiṭṭaṉar. palavakiyāṉa camayak kiriyikaḷil muttam payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. epirēya makkaḷiṭiyum iv vaḻakkam iruntu vantatu. āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum muttamiṭṭuk koḷvatu aṉumatikkappaṭavilli. piriyāviṭi, mariyāti, upacarittal eṉpavi kaḷukku māttiram muttam payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. cemittiya makkaḷiṭiyē muttam camaya campantamāka vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. kiṟittuvarkaḷ āṇkaḷ peṇkaḷ eṉṟa pētamiṉṟi muttamiṭuvatu paḻiya makkaḷil camūka vaḻakkattip paṟṟi yatu. kiṟittuva matattil muttam aruḷik koṭuppatākak karutappaṭṭatu. muṟkālattil āṇ peṇ eṉṟa vēṟupāṭiṉṟi ellārukkum kurumār paricuttamāṉa muttam koṭuttu vantaṉar.
ilakkiya kālattil naṭipeṟṟa muttaṅkoṭukkum vaḻakkaṅkaḷ mattiyakālam variyil iruntuvantaṉa. iṅkilīṣ peṇkaḷ āṇ naṇpari muttamiṭum urimiyic ciṟappākac peṟṟiruntārkaḷ. iṅki lāntil piṟkālattil ōru māṟṟamuṇṭāyiṟṟu. appoḻutu muttam peṟṟōrukkum, kātalarukkum uriyatāyiruntatu. piṟappiṉāl allatu tirumaṇattiṉāl uṟaviṉarallāta āṇkaḷi muttamiṭum vaḻakkam niṉṟu pōyiṟṟu. ippoḻutu kātalar, orē kuṭumpattiṉar, peṇ naṇparkaḷuk kuḷ māttiram muttamiṭum vaḻakkam iruntu varukiṉṟatu. airōppāvil āṇ naṇpar oruvari oruvar muttamiṭṭukkoḷḷum vaḻakkam iṉṉum iruntu varukiṉṟatu. itu mukkiyamākap pirāṉciltāṉ uṇṭu. itu aracarkaḷukkiṭiyil iruntu vantatu. mariyātiyiṉ aṭiyāḷamākap peṇkaḷiṉ kiyi muttamiṭum vaḻakkam mattiya kālattil ārampittatu. itu cātāraṇa vaḻakkil illi; periyavarkaḷ cilarukkiṭiyil iruntu varukiṉṟatu.
camūka camaya vaḻakkaṅkaḷ
taḻuvikkoḷḷutal, vaṇaṅkutal pōṉṟavikaḷukkup patil muttam payaṉpaṭukiṟatu. ivvaḻakkam maṟintu pōṉa nāṭukaḷil, ataṟ kākac col payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. spāṉiyar “naṇpaṉatu kiyi muttamiṭukiṉṟēṉ” eṉak kūṟuvārkaḷ. mītiyar kikaḷi māttiram muttamiṭṭārkaḷ. oṭiciyas tirumpivantapōtu avaṉatu naṇparkaḷ tali ki tōḷ eṉpavikaḷil avaṉi muttamiṭṭārkaḷ. kirīcil tāḻntavarkaḷē uyarntavarkaḷiṉ ki, mārpu allatu muḻantāḷkaḷi muttamiṭṭārkaḷ. pāracīkattil camamāṉavarkaḷ oruvari oruvar vāyil muttamiṭṭārkaḷ; ciṟitu ēṟṟat tāḻvuṭiyavarkaḷ kaṉṉattil muttamiṭ ṭārkaḷ. mikat tāḻntavaṉ muṉṉāl viḻuntu vaṇakkam celuttiṉāṉ. ēcuvā, yākkōppiṉ kaḻuttil viḻuntu muttamiṭṭāṉ. epirēyaruḷ kaṉṉam, neṟṟi, tāṭi, ki, pātam eṉpaṉa muttamiṭappaṭṭaṉa. palastīṉiya riṭiyē naṭipeṟum muttam piṉ varumāṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. muttamiṭum iruvaril ovvoruvarum taliyiyum mukattiyum kīḻē tāḻtti maṟṟavariṉ tōḷ muṭṭil vittu maṟṟavariṉ valatu kaṉṉattil muttamiṭukiṉṟaṉar; piṉpu taliyi maṟṟavariṉ valatu tōḷil vittu iṭakkaṉṉattil muttamiṭukiṉṟaṉar. mikavum mariyātiyāṉa vaṇakkam otta iruvarkaḷukkiṭiyil naṭakkumpōtu oruvar maṟṟavariṉ tāṭiyip piṭittu muttamiṭukiṟār. peṇkaḷum kaṇavar, piḷḷikaḷ, tantiyiṉari muttamiṭukiṉṟaṉar; mariyātiyuḷḷa kuṭumpattil otta nilimiyuṭiya varkaḷ valatu kiyi maṟṟavariṉ iṭatutōḷil vittu valak kaṉṉatti muttamiṭuvārkaḷ. piṉ iṭak kiyi valat tōḷil vittu iṭak kaṉṉatti muttamiṭuvārkaḷ. oruvar tamatu valak kiyi maṟṟavariṉ valak kiyip piṭittup piṉpu tamatu neṟṟiyil allatu neñcil vippar. tāḻntavarkaḷ mēlāṉavarkaḷiṉ kiyi muttamiṭṭārkaḷ. acīriya paṭṭiyaṅkaḷil kīḻppaṭivik kāṭṭuvataṟku aracaṉuṭiya pātaṅkaḷ muttamiṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉa eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. kiṟittu camayattavarkaḷ kiṟittuviṉ pātatti muttamiṭṭut tamatu vaṇakkattit terivikkum vaḻakkam iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. kiriyikaḷil pōppāṇṭavariṉ pātaṅkaḷ muttamiṭappaṭṭaṉa. ki.pi. 847il atu paḻiya vaḻakkaṅkaḷil oṉṟākac collappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu urōmar aracari vaḻipaṭum vaḻakkat tiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiyateṉak karuppaṭukiṉṟatu. viḻuntu vaṇaṅkuvatu payam, mariyāti eṉpavikaḷik kāṭṭuvatu. kirēkkar tamatu kīḻp paṭivik kāṭṭuvataṟku muḻantāḷkaḷip piṭippatu vaḻakkam. ivikaḷiṉ paṇpāṭē pātaṅkaḷi muttamiṭuvatu. kiṟittuva vētattiṉ paḻiya ēṟ pāṭṭil ‘pātatūḷiyi nakkuvatu’ eṉa varum coṟṟoṭariṉ poruḷum ituvē. paḻiya intiyāvil mariyātiyik kāṭṭum ciṉṉam ituvē. mattiya kālaṅkaḷil airōppiya aracar oruvari oruvar kaṉṉaṅkaḷil mutta miṭṭārkaḷ. kuṭikaḷ aracaṉiṉ kiyi muttamiṭṭārkaḷ. ik kālattil ācīrvātattik kuṟikkum muttam neṟṟiyil koṭukkappaṭu kiṉṟatu. turukkiyar tamatu kikaḷi muttamiṭṭup piṉ tamatu kiyi neṟṟiyil vippar. puyal kāṟṟukku mariyātiyāka arāpiyar tamatu kiyi muttamiṭuvar. ivvāṟu cantiraṉukku mariyāti ceyvatippaṟṟi kiṟittuva vētattiṉ paḻiya ēṟpāṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivvāṟē tipēttiyar cūriyaṉi vaṇaṅkiṉar.
muttam eṉpatu kikulukkuvatu pōṉṟa oru mariyātik kuṟi. paḻiya vīrarkaḷ (Knights) ik kālap pōrvīrar kikulukkuvatupōla muttamiṭṭuk koṇṭārkaḷ. camātāṉamāṉa etirikaḷ muttamiṭṭuk tamatu camātāṉatti aṟivittārkaḷ. muttam kaliyāṇattiṉ aṭiyāḷamāyu miruntatu. paḻiya pirāṉcilum mattiya kāla pirāṉcilum taṉatu kaṇavaṉal lāta piṟa āṭavaṉi muttamiṭṭavaḷ viyapicārak kuṟṟam cāṭṭappaṭṭāḷ. vēls nāṭṭil mukkiyamāṉa cila kālaṅkaḷil māttiram muttam payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. oruvaṉiṉ maṉivi piṟaṉoruvaṉi muttamiṭ ṭāl kaṇavaṉ avaḷi nīkkiviṭalām. paḻiya kiṟittuvar muttattiṉ payaṉip peritum aṭintaṉar. vēls makkaḷiṉ karuttukku māṟāka itu camaya campantamum uṭiyatāyiruntatu. paḻiya kiṟittuva kōyil kaḷil ñāṉa muḻukku (Baptize) āṭṭappaṭṭavar, muḻukkāṭṭiyavarāl muttamiṭappaṭṭār. iṉṟum putitāka picup pāṇṭavarāl kuru ākkappaṭu pavar muttattip peṟukiṉṟaṉar.
kiṟittuvaruṭiya kiriyikaḷuḷ camātāṉa muttam mikavum mukkiyamuṭiyatu. urōmarum kirēkkarum vaṇakkattil muttattip payaṉpaṭuttiṉārkaḷ. hērkulis vikkirakattiṉ taliyum itaḻum muttamiṭuvatāl avvikkirakam tēyntu pōyiruntamiyi cicirō (Cicero) eṉpavar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. urōmac cakkaravarttikaḷi mariyāti ceyvatu avar uṭiyit toṭṭuk kikaḷi itaḻkaḷil vippatu allatu uṭiyi muttamiṭuvatu. itu avar pātatti allatu muḻan tāḷi muttamiṭuvatāka māṟiyuḷḷatu. pirapu oruvaṉiṉ nilattil irukkum kuṭiyāṉavaṉ pirapuviṉ toṭiyil muttamiṭṭāṉ.
urōmarum kirēkkarum kaṭavuḷ vikkirakaṅkaḷi muttamiṭṭārkaḷ. paḻiya arāpiyarum ivvāṟē ceytaṉar, vīṭṭukku veḷiyē cellumpōtum, vīṭṭiṉuḷḷē nuḻiyumpōtum avarkaḷ il uṟi teyvaṅkaḷi muttamiṭṭārkaḷ. pīṟṟar ñāṉiyār vikkirakattiṉ peru virali urōmaṉ kattōlikkar muttamiṭṭārkaḷ.
mucilim makkaḷ mekkāviluḷḷa kāpā eṉṉum kalli mutta miṭuvārkaḷ. aṅku cuvaril oru kaṟuppukkal kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. mucilim makkaḷ atu cuvarkkattiṉiṉṟum viḻunta kal eṉa nampi varukiṟārkaḷ. atu muṉ veṇmiyāyiruntatu; āṉāl ati pāvamuḷḷavarkaḷ mutta miṭuvatāl kaṟuppākamāṟiyuḷḷatu eṉṟu avarkaḷ nampukiṉṟaṉar. ēpirēyar kaṉṟukkuṭṭikaḷi muttamiṭṭārkaḷ. pāl teyvam muttamiṭappaṭṭatu. epirēyar kōyiliṉ nilatti muttamiṭṭārkaḷ. puttakatti muttamiṭutal paḻiya kālañ cattiyañ ceyyum muṟiyākum. airōppiyar niyāyat talattil kiṟittuva vētattiṉ paḻiya ēṟpāṭṭi muttamiṭṭuc cattiyam ceytārkaḷ.


peṇkaḷum camūkamum
aṉṟum iṉṟum
muṉṉuri
peṇkaḷ āṭavari oppa ellā urimikaḷum uṭiyavarkaḷā yiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṉum kiḷarcci ellāt tēcaṅkaḷilum iṉṟu naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. “eṇṇaṟṟakkaṟṟu eḻuttaṟa vācittālum peṇ putti eṉpatellām perum pētamittē,” eṉṉum paḻiya karuttukku iṉṟu iṭamilli. peṇkaḷ viṭutali yaṭintu varukiṉṟavum, viṭutali yaṭiyat tiṇaṟik koṇṭirukkiṉṟavum ik kālattil, peṇkaḷiṉ nili camūkaṅkaḷil evvāṟu iruntu vanta teṉpatic carittira muṟiyil payiltal iṉpam aḷippatākum. ulakam muḻumiyilum peṇkaḷiṉ nili orē vakiyāka irukkavilli; paṟpalavāṟu iruntu vantatu. ākavē peṇkaḷip paṟṟiya varalāṟṟil ciṟcila māṟupāṭukaḷ kāṇap paṭuvatum iyalpēyākum. peṇkaḷ āṭavariṉ kīḻ aṭimippaṭṭu nīṇṭa kālam iruntamikkuk kāraṇam poruḷātāra nilimiyē. nām cila vilaṅkukaḷi vīṭṭil vaḷarttu,avaṟṟi uṇavukkāka nammiyē etir pārttirukkumpaṭi paḻakki yuḷḷōm. avvāṟē peṇkaḷum paḻakkappaṭṭārkaḷ. iṉṟu poruḷ varuvāyiṉāl tammittāmē pātukāttuk koḷḷak kūṭiya peṇkaḷ viṭutaliyaṭintuḷḷārkaḷ. ēṉiyar paḻiya niliyilēyē iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar. peṇkaḷ viṭutali aṭivataṟkēṟṟa vaḻi vakikaḷik kōlutaṟkup peṇ kaḷip paṟṟiya varalāṟṟi aṟintu koḷvatu iṉṟiyamiyātatu. ic ciṟiya nūl, “peṇkaḷiṉ iyaṟki campantamāṉavum, cīrtirutta vaḷarcci campantamāṉavum varalāṟu”1 eṉṉum oru jermaṉ nūliṉ orupakutiyiṉ curuṅkiya karuttākum.
ceṉṉi
15.2.1948 na.ci. kantiyā

peṇkaḷum camūkamum
aṉṟum iṉṟum
tōṟṟuvāy
varalāṟukaḷil mikamika iṉpam aḷippatu peṇṇip paṟṟiya varalāṟē. ātikālam mutal peṇkaḷiṉ nili camūkaṅkaḷil evvāṟu iruntatu? kālantōṟum atu evvaki māṟṟaṅkaḷi aṭintatu? āṇ camūkattiṉar peṇ camūkattiṉari naṭattiya muṟikaḷ ēṟṟuk koḷḷattakkaṉavā? ēṟkattakātaṉavā? peṇkaḷ evvāṟu paṭippaṭiyē tiruttamaṭintu uyarnili aṭintārkaḷ eṉpavi pōṉṟa pala ceyti kaḷi iṉṟiya āṭavarum makaḷirum aṟintu koḷḷavēṇṭiyatu avaciyamākiṉṟatu. peṇkaḷ āṭavari otta viṭutaliyuṭiyavarkaḷā yiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṉum uṇarcci ulakam muḻumiyum paraviyuḷḷatu. avvuṇarccikku etirppukkaḷ nāḷaṭivil ōyntu varukiṉṟaṉa.
mika muṟkālattil peṇkaḷiṉ nili
maṉita camūkattiṉ cīrtiruttattip paṟṟiya varalāṟu paḻiya uṟi paṉikkālattiliruntu toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. ak kālattil makkaḷ vilaṅku kaḷōṭu vilaṅkukaḷāka vāḻntārkaḷ. aṉṟu mutal peṇṇukkum āṇukkumuḷḷa camūkat toṭarpu aṟiyakkūṭiyatāka vuḷḷatu. paḻiya kaṟkāla makkaḷiṉ elumpukaḷum, ammakkaḷ elumpukaḷilum kaṟkaḷilum varinta ōviyaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷit tuṇikkoṇṭu akkālap peṇkaḷippaṟṟi muṭivāka etiyum aṟiya muṭiyāmal iruk kiṉṟatu. ituvariyum kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭa paḻiya kaṟkāla makkaḷiṉ elumpukaḷiṉ perumpālaṉa āṇkaḷuṭiyavē. cila camayaṅkaḷil māttiram peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷōṭu vittup putikkappaṭṭirukkiṉ ṟārkaḷ. itaṉāl, paḻiya uṟipaṉik kālattil āṭavar peṇkaḷi uyarntavarākak koḷḷavilli eṉṟum, mika arumiyākavē avarkaḷ peṇkaḷiṉ uṭalic ciṟappākap putittārkaḷ eṉṟum nām uyt taṟiyalām.
āṭavaṉum makaḷum oruṅkē putikkappaṭṭuk kiṭantāl, peṇ āṇukku oru vakippaliyākak koṭukkappaṭṭavaḷ, allatu avaḷ avaṉukku maṟumiyil payaṉpaṭumpaṭi maṟṟiya poruḷkaḷip pōl iṭṭup putikkappaṭṭavaḷ eṉṟum nām karutalām. uṟipaṉikkāla makkaḷ uyiriṉ aḻiviṉmiyi nampiṉārkaḷ. iṉṟiya piṟpōkkāṉa makkaḷ maraṇameṉpatu uyiriṉ mīṇṭu varāta payaṇam eṉa niṉippatu pōlavē avarkaḷum eṇṇiṇārkaḷ. iṟantavaruṭiya maṟu ulakap payaṇattukkut tuṇiyāka avar virumpip payaṉpaṭuttiya poruḷkaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa, avi aṇikalaṉkaḷ, kallāyutaṅkaḷ, aṭimikaḷ, peṇkaḷ pōlvaṉa.
iṟanta kaṇavaṉōṭu maṉiviyiyum koṉṟu putittal
camīpa kālattukku muṉ intiyāviṟpōlavē jermaṉiyilum maṉivi, iṟanta kaṇavaṉōṭu uṭaṉ vittup putikkappaṭṭāḷ. 1tirāy makkaḷ kaṇavaṉiṉ mika viruppamāṉa maṉiviyik koṉṟu avaṉōṭu putittārkaḷ. āppirikka pacuṭōs2 makkaḷ kaṇavaṉip putikku miṭattilē maṉiviyit talimītu taṭiyāl aṭittuk koṉṟārkaḷ. teṉkaṭaliluḷḷa piyūjit tīvil maṉivi tirukik kollappaṭṭuk kaṇavaṉōṭu putikkappaṭṭāḷ. in nikaḻccikaḷāl muṟkālac camātikaḷil āṇum peṇṇum putikkappaṭṭirukkak kaṇṭāl avarkaḷ nōyi ṉāl orē kālattil iṟakkavilliyeṉṟum maṉivi kollappaṭṭuk kaṇavaṉōṭu putikkappaṭṭāḷ eṉṟum nām uyttaṟiyalām.
uṟi paṉikkāla maṉitaṉ māṟṟikkoḷḷavō, koṉṟu viṭavō kūṭiya poruḷāka peṇṇik karutiṉāṉ.
peṇ āṇiṉ uṭimi
paḻiya makkaḷiṉ niliyi ārāyntu pārkkumpōtu peṇ kaḷiṉ nili mikat tāḻvāka iruntateṉap pulappaṭukiṉṟatu. putiya kaṟkāla ōviyaṅkaḷ, āṭavaṉ taṉatu iccikaḷi niṟivēṟṟuvataṟku ēṟṟa poruḷākap peṇṇik karutiṉāṉ eṉṟu terivikkiṉṟaṉa. itu muṟkāla maṉitaṉ ceyta ciṟpaṅkaḷil peṇ uṟuppukkaḷiyē ciṟappākat tōṉṟumpaṭi amintiruppatik koṇṭu aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu.
āṇ peṇ cērkkiyiṉāl karu uṇṭākiṉṟateṉa maṉitaṉ aṟintatu piṟkālattilēyēyākum. karu uṇṭāvataṟkum kuḻanti piṟappa taṟkum iṭiyēyuḷḷa kālam nīṇṭatāyiruntamiyiṉ avaṉ itip paṟṟi aṟiyavilli. kuṟukiya kālattil kuṭṭiyīṉum vilaṅkukaḷi ak kālattil vaḷarkkāmiyāl avaṉ ittoṭarpi aṟiya muṭiyāmal iruntatu. ivviyaṟki vitiyi aṟiyāta makkaḷ iṉṟum kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. āstirēliya paḻaṅkuṭikaḷ tam muṉṉōriṉ āvikaḷ vilaṅku vaṭivil peṇṇiṉ vayiṟṟil nuḻivatāl karu uṇṭākiṉṟateṉak karutiṉārkaḷ. kuḻanti, vayiṟṟuṉuḷ aciyattoṭaṅkum variyil avaḷ taṉatu niliyi aṟivatilli. ivvaki nampikki nākarika makkaḷiṭiyum, kiṟittuva matakkoḷkiyilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. tāykkum kuḻantikkumuḷḷa ivvaki uṭal campantatti ūṉṟik kavaṉippatāl karu uṇṭāvataṉ vaki aṟiyappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl tāyāṭci muṟiyāṉa nākarikam paḻiya kālattil uṇṭāvatāyiṟṟu.
peṇṇukkuk kaṭamikaḷ iruntaṉa;
urimikaḷ irukkavilli
maṉitaṉ kīḻ niliyil irunta kālattil, peṇ oruvaṉāl vili koṭuttu vāṅkappaṭṭa vīṭṭu vilaṅku allatu poruḷpōlak karutap paṭṭāḷ. avaḷ purivataṟkuk kaṭamikaḷ iruntaṉa; urimikaḷ irukka villi. āṭavaṉ talivaṉāyiruntāṉ; peṇ vēli ceypavaḷāyum piḷḷikaḷip peṟukiṉṟavaḷāyumiruntāḷ. āstirēliya paḻaṅkuṭi kaḷuḷ oruvaṉ, peṇṇit tāṉ virumpiya evaṉukkēṉum koṭuk kalām. avvāṟu koṭuttali virumpāviṭṭāl avaḷ aṭikkappaṭu vāḷ; avvāṟu aṭippatāl āpattu viḷikkum kāyaṅkaḷ uṇṭāvatu muṇṭu. iḷam vayatuṭiya peṇ taḷarnta kiḻavaṉukkuk koṭukkappaṭiṉum avaḷ mīḷutal muṭiyātu. avaḷ avaṉi viṭṭu ōṭicceṉṟāl avaḷ maṟupaṭiyum ōṭamuṭiyātapaṭi kālkaḷ mara īṭṭikaḷāl kuttappaṭṭaṉa. peṇ cērkki toṭarpāṉa karumaṅkaḷ mirukattaṉmiyāka naṭipeṟṟaṉa. camūkam allatu kūṭṭattiluḷḷa mutiyavarkaḷē atu toṭarpāṉa mukkiya pakutiyik kiyāṇṭaṉar. vayatāṉavarkaḷ tamatu mutiya maṉiviyi vāliparukkuk koṭutta pōtum avvāliparkaḷukku peṇ kiṭippatu arumiyākaviruntatu. uṟcākam kuṉṟiya peṇkaḷ mikavum tuṉpattuk kuḷḷāṉārkaḷ. iḷam peṇṇi, avaḷukku muṉṉamē maṉiviyarāyi rukkum peṇkaḷ koṭumippaṭuttiṉar. avaḷ taṭikaḷālum tōlkaḷā lum kuṭici kaṭṭavēṇṭum; irāk kālattil kuṭicikkumuṉ neruppu erittaṟku viṟaku poṟukka vēṇṭum; ciṟiya vilaṅkukaḷi vēṭṭiyāṭa vēṇṭum; kīri vakikaḷic cēkarikka vēṇṭum; kūriya pāṟikaḷāl kiḻaṅkukaḷi akaḻntu eṭukkavēṇṭum; tirikikkaṟkaḷil (entirakkal) tāṉiyaṅkaḷi arikka vēṇṭum; aritta māvik koṇṭu vantu appam cuṭa vēṇṭum.
ivaḷ ivikaḷic ceyyumpōtu kaṇavaṉ vēṭṭiyāṭu kiṉṟāṉ; tāṉ vēṭṭiyāṭip peṟṟa vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iṟicciyi maṟṟiya vēṭṭiyāṭuvārōṭu paṅku pōṭṭu uṇkiṉṟāṉ; uṇṭapiṉ vīṭṭiluḷḷa uṇaviṉ taṉatu pākattip peṟa virintu celkiṉṟāṉ. alintu tiriyum kālaṅkaḷil āṭavaṉ kiyil oru īṭṭiyi māttiram koṇṭu celkiṉṟāṉ. kuṭumpattukkuḷḷa maṟṟiya ellāp poruḷ kaḷum maṉiviyariṉ tōḷkaḷ mītu kaṭṭic cumattappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nālu allatu aintu cumikaḷi orutti koṇṭu celvatu potu nikaḻcci. kuḻanti avaḷ tōḷmītu ēṟiyiruntatu avaḷ caṭiyik keṭṭiyākap piṭittuk koḷḷum. cila camayaṅkaḷil kuḻantiyit tōl piyiliṭṭut tōḷil toṅkaviṭuvāḷ.
kuḻantikaḷik kollutal
āstirēliyariṉ alintu tiriyum vāḻkkikkuk kūṭṭamāṉa kuḻantikaḷ taṭiyāyuḷḷaṉa. ākavē kuḻantikaḷ kollappaṭṭaṉa. veḷḷiyariṉ caṭṭam nuḻiya muṭiyāta iṭaṅkaḷil iṉṟum iv vaḻakkam nilavukiṉṟatu. piṟantavuṭaṉ kuḻantiyik koṉṟuviṭṭu iraṇṭu nāykkuṭṭikaḷip pāl kuṭikkumpaṭi tāyiṉ mārpukaḷil viṭuvatu muṇṭu. itaṉāl avarkaḷukkuk kuḻantiyilum pārkka nāykaḷ evvaḷavu arumiyuṭiyaṉa eṉṟu terikiṉṟatu.
peṇ vēli puriyum vilaṅku
tiruntāta makkaḷ peṇkaḷi vilaṅkukaḷ pōl naṭattiṉārkaḷ. camōyettiya1 peṇkaḷ mikavum tāḻvāka naṭattappaṭṭārkaḷ. avar kaḷiṭiyē peṇkaḷē ellā vīṭṭu vēlikaḷiyum ceyya vēṇṭum. kuṭicikaḷiyum avarkaḷē kaṭṭa vēṇṭum; avikaḷip piṭuṅka vēṇṭiyavarkaḷum avarkaḷē. avarkaḷē caṟukki vaṇṭikaḷip pūṭṭa vum kaḻaṟṟavum vēṇṭum; avaṟṟōṭu tamatu kaṇavarukku aṭaṅki aṭimipōl vēli ceyyavum vēṇṭum. māli nērattil ullāca mākap poḻutupōkkum nērattilallāmal āṭavar peṇkaḷōṭu tayavākap pēcamāṭṭārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ acuttamāṉavarkaḷ eṉavum karutap paṭṭārkaḷ. kuṭici kaṭṭi muṭintapiṉ, ataṉik kaṭṭiya avaḷum avaḷ toṭṭa poruḷkaḷum puki kāṭṭic cuttam ceyyappaṭutal vēṇṭum. caṟukki vaṇṭiyil pūṭṭiyirukkum tuva māṉi (reindeer) aviḻkka vēṇṭumāyiṉ avaḷ vaṇṭiyiṉ kīḻē tavaḻntu ceṉṟu aviḻkka vēṇṭum. kuṭiciyiṉ oru puṟattil kampu oṉṟu naṭappaṭṭirukkum. atit tāṇṭip peṇkaḷ cellutal ākātu.
vaṭa amerikkap pulveḷikaḷil vāḻum makkaḷiṭiyē, peṇkaḷ, marantaṟippavarkaḷ taṇṇīr cumappavarkaḷ eṉṟu collappaṭuvar. am makkaḷiṭiyē vēli ceyyum peṇkaḷ aṭimikaḷ eṉavē karutap paṭuvar. avarkaḷ pattu allatu paṉṉiraṇṭu vayatu aṭintatum, avar kaḷiṉ viruppattik kēḷāmalē tanti avarkaḷi viṟṟuviṭuvāṉ. peṇkaḷ palavakiyāṉa vīṭṭuk kaṭamikaḷiyum camiyaliyum ceyya vēṇṭum. māṉ tōl erumit tōlkaḷik kūṭāraṅkaḷukkup payaṉ paṭuttuvataṟkum, veḷḷiyarukku viṟpataṟkum, uṭuppataṟkum pataṉiṭa vēṇṭum. avarkaḷē kūṭārattiyum, kutirikaḷ iḻuttuc cellum caṟukki vaṇṭiyiyum kaḻaṟṟi vikka vēṇṭum. cila camayaṅkaḷil periya tuṇic cumikaḷi mutukilum, kuḻantikaḷi mārpilum allatu tōḷilē toṅkaviṭappaṭṭa toṭṭililum iṭṭuc cumantu koṇṭu kutiri kaḷukkup piṉṉāl pala nāṭkaḷ naṭantu cella vēṇṭum. irākkālattil payaṇam niṉṟavuṭaṉ avaḷ tāṉ kaḷiyāṟuvatik kuṟittu niṉiyātu camikka vēṇṭum. peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷuṭaṉ iruntu orupōtum uṇavu uṇṇamāṭṭārkaḷ. āṭavariṉ kūttu, kaḷiyāṭṭaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷ paṅku peṟuvatilli.
āppirikka makkaḷiṭiyē peṇkaḷ ellā vakiyilum aṭimi kaḷākak karutappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. taṭṭumuṭṭu allatu cumi tūkkum vilaṅkē peṇ āvāḷ. avaḷ iḷittuviṭṭāl avaḷ iṭattukku iṉṉorutti koṇṭuvarappaṭukiṟāḷ. avaḷi vāṅkumiṭattu oru āṭṭi vili pēci vāṅkuvatu pōlavē avaḷ vāṅkappaṭukiṟāḷ. kaṇavaṉ maṉi viyit turattiviṭa virumpiṉāl, avaṉ avaḷ kiyil vikkōlik koṭukkiṟāṉ; camātāṉamākap pōkavilliyāṉāl avaḷ valukkaṭṭāya mākat turattappaṭukiṟāḷ.
kuraṅkukaḷiṭiyē āṇ peṇkaḷiṉ vāḻkki
mika muṟkālattil peṇkaḷiṉ nili mikat tāḻvākavē iruntatu. pala āyiram āṇṭukaḷāka makkaḷ vilaṅkuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷ pōlavē vāḻntārkaḷ. uṟipaṉik kālattiṉ piṉ maṉitaṉ vilaṅku niliyi liruntu mēlāṉa paṭikku uyarntāṉ. vilaṅkukaḷuḷ uṇavukkākap pōrāṭavalla uyirē nilipeṟṟatu. avvakiyiṉatē naṉṟāka uṇṭu nīṇṭakālam vāḻum. oru kūṭṭattukkut talimiyāka iruppatu vayatāṉa mūtta āṇ. in nilimiyil atikārattāl uyarnta mūtta āṇukkē ellāp peṇkaḷiṭattum urimiyuṇṭu.
vilaṅku nūlār kāṭukaḷil vāḻum korilā eṉṉum mēliṉak kuraṅkukaḷiṉ camūka vāḻkkiyip paṟṟik kavaṉittuḷḷārkaḷ. uṇavu tēṭac cellumpōtu muṉṉāl talimi vakikkum kuraṅkum, ataṉ piṉṉē iḷaṅkuraṅkukaḷum, avikaḷiṉ piṉ peṇ kuraṅkukaḷum, iṟutiyil vayatu mutirnta kuraṅkukaḷum celkiṉṟaṉa. kūṭṭattiṉ talivaṉākiya kuraṅku melleṉac celkiṉṟatu; iṭiyiṭiyē nimirntu cuṟṟup puṟaṅkaḷip pārkkiṉṟatu. āpattu oṉṟum kāṇappaṭāviṭil atu marattaṭiyil kuntiyirukkum. peṇ kuraṅkukaḷ uṇavu tēṭi vantu ataṉ pātaṅkaḷil vikkum. iṭiyiṭiyē iraṇṭu peṇ kuraṅkukaḷ vantu ataṉ pakkaṅkaḷil irukkum. appoḻutu ak kuraṅkut talivaṉ avikaḷiṉ tōḷkaḷ mītu kiyip pōṭṭu avikaḷōṭu kēḷikki ceyyum. atu ceyyum ōciyik kavaṉittup pārttāl atu cirikkiṉṟa teṉak kūṟalām. āpatti aṟintāl atu uḷḷaṅkiyi virittuk kaṉṉaṅkaḷil aṭittuk koṇṭu urakkac cattamiṭum. uṭaṉē kuraṅkuk kūṭṭam ōṭi maṟintuviṭum. talivaṉ kuraṅku etiriyik kaṇṭavuṭaṉ kiyip potti mārpil aṟintu etirimītu mūrkkamākap pāykiṉṟatu.
jermaṉ vēṭṭikkāraṉ oruvaṉ kuraṅkuk kūṭṭam oṉṟip pārttāṉ. kuṭṭikaḷum peṇ kuraṅkukaḷum āṇ kuraṅkukaḷukku uṇavu tēṭikkoṇṭiruntaṉa. avikaḷuḷ oṉṟu cuṟucuṟuppillā tiruntapōtum, atika uṇavi uṇṭapōtum āṇ ataṉ kātukaḷil aṟintu uṟumic cattamiṭṭatu.
korilāk kuraṅkukaḷ iṭamviṭṭu iṭam cellumpōtu tāṅkaḷum taṅkaḷ kuṭṭikaḷum taṅkuvataṟkup peṇ kuraṅkukaḷē marattil kūṭu kaṭṭik koḷkiṉṟaṉa. cila camayaṅkaḷil avi kūṭukaḷi nilattil allatu nēriya kāl uṭiya marattiṉ pattu allatu patiṉeṭṭaṭaṭi uyaramuḷḷa kiḷikaḷil kaṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṇ korilā taṉatu kūṭṭattit tākkavarum etiriyit tākkuvataṟku āyattamāka mara aṭiyil kiṭakkum.
mutumi ēṟēṟat talivaṉiṉ palam kuṟikiṉṟatu. iṉṉoru āṇ ataṉ iṭatti ēṟka virumpukiṉṟatu. piṉpu iru kuraṅkukaḷukku miṭiyil kaṭumpōr mūḷkiṉṟatu. veṟṟiyaṭintatē kuṭṭikaḷukkum peṇ kuraṅkaḷukkum talivaṉ ākiṉṟatu. tōlviyaṭintatu kāṭu kaḷil taṉimiyāka vāḻkiṉṟatu.
āṇ peṇkaḷiṉ kaṭamikaḷil vēṟupāṭu
korilākkaḷiṭiyē kāṇappaṭṭa ivvaki vāḻkkiyi nām iṉṟum mikamikat tāḻniliyiluḷḷa makkaḷiṭiyum kāṇalām. makkaḷiṉ uṭalamippum paṟkaḷum tāvaram, ūṉ eṉṉum iruvaki uṇavukaḷi yum koḷvataṟkēṟṟavi. ākavē mika muṟkālattil uṇavu tēṭum vēlikaḷil āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupāliṉarukkumiṭiyil pirivu uṇṭāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
āṇkaḷ vēṭṭi āṭippeṟṟa ūṉ uṇaviyum, peṇkaḷ, ciṟuvar, vayatu mutirnta vēṭṭiyāṭamāṭṭātavar eṉpōr paḻaṅkaḷ kiḻaṅkukaḷ pōṉṟavikaḷiyum tēṭiṉārkaḷ. āṭavar tām vēṭṭi yāṭiya uṇavit tāmē vēṭṭiyāṭiya iṭattil pacciyāka uṇ ṭārkaḷ; uṟiviṭattukkuc ceṉṟu peṇkaḷ cēkaritta tāvara uṇaviṉ paṅkiyum peṟṟaṉar. iṟicciyip pakkuvañ ceytu vippatu eppaṭi eṉṟu aṟiyāta paḻaṅkālattil ūṉ uṇavu atikam kiṭittamiyāl āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum uṇavup poruḷkaḷi māṟṟik koṇṭārkaḷ. itu āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupālārum oruvar mītu oruvar koṇṭa viruppiṉālum, irakkam, tarumam eṉṉum uṇarccikaḷiṉālum naṭipeṟṟatākalām.
āṭavaṉukkut tāvara uṇavu vēṇṭiyiruntatu. itaṉi avaṉ peṇṇiṭamiruntu peṟṟāṉ. itaṉāl āṭavarukkum makaḷirukkumiṭi yil oruvakit toṭarpu uṇṭāyiṟṟu. it toṭarpu peṇṇukku vāyp puṭiyatāyiruntatu. maṉitaṉ neruppi uṇṭākka aṟintu ataṉi aṇintu pōkātapaṭi kāppāṟṟa vēṇṭiya pōtu ivvāyppu mēlum palamaṭintatu.
neruppi aṟintapiṉ nērnta māṟṟam
uṟipaṉik kāla maṉitaṉ kuḷirkāyavum, ak kālattil naṭamāṭiya periya kāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷ tammi tākkuvatiṉiṉṟu piḻikkavum, iṟicciyic cuṭavum neruppip payaṉpaṭuttiṉāṉ. muṟkālattil neruppi aṇiyātapaṭi pātukāttal peṇṇiṉ kaṭamiyāka viruntatu. vēṭṭi āṭic cellum āṭavaṉ neruppiyum uṭaṉ koṇṭu cellutal āpattāyum, muṭiyātatāyum iruntatu. peṇkaḷ neruppi ōriṭattil vittukkākka vēṇṭiyiruntamiyiṉ uṟi viṭaṅkaḷi aṭikkaṭi māṟṟutal muṭiyātiruntatu. muṟkālattil āṭavaṉ peṇṇiṭattil koṇṭa kavarcciyiṉāl uṟiviṭattukkuc ceṉṟāṉ; piṉpu iṭiyiṭiyē vantu pōṉāṉ.
muṟkālattil kuṟikkappaṭṭa cila kālattilēyē āṭavaṉ peṇṇiṉ tōḻamiyuṭiyavaṉāyiruntāṉ. ippoḻutu avaṉ neruppiṉ poruṭṭu avaḷiṭam eppoḻutum ceṉṟāṉ. avaṉ neruppiṉ poruṭṭu avaḷiṭam taṅkiyiruntāṉ. avaṉ tāṉ vēṭṭiyāṭip peṟṟa ūṉ uṇavic camittu uṇṇum poruṭṭu uṟiviṭattukku vantāṉ. tāṉ ceyyum vēlikkāka uṇavil oru paṅkik kēṭkak kūṭiya uyarniliyip peṇ aṭintāḷ. itu peṇṇiṉ nili miyic ciṟitu uyarttiṟṟu. paḻiya urōmil paricutta neruppi aṇi yāmal kāppataṟkuk kaṉṉip peṇkaḷ niyamikkappaṭṭārkaḷ.
neruppi uṇṭākka aṟintapiṉ nērnta māṟṟam
maṉitaṉ vēṇṭiyapōtu neruppi uṇṭākka aṟintāṉ. ap poḻutu avaṉ maṟupaṭiyum alintu tiripavaṉ āyiṉāṉ. ippoḻutu peṇ camikkum pāṉi caṭṭikaḷic ceyya aṟintāḷ. ataṉāl uṇavu muṟiyil cila tiruttam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. peṇkaḷ ceyyak kaṇṭupiṭitta pāṉi caṭṭiyē jēms vāṭ nīrāvi iyantiram ceyvataṟkum ētuvā yiruntatu. pāṉi caṭṭi ceyya aṟintiruntamiyāl tāṉ muṟkālap peṇkaḷ āṭavarip paṇiyac ceytārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ tamatu ūkkattip pala vaḻikaḷil celuttiṉārkaḷ. peṇkaḷē mutalil vāṇikattiyum, kittoḻilkaḷiyum purintu tamatu atikārattip parappiṉārkaḷ.
peṇkaḷiṉ toḻili āṇkaḷ kippaṟṟutal
paḻaṅkaḷip poṟukkuvatum, kiḻaṅkukaḷi akaḻvatum, tāṉiya vakikaḷi oḻuṅkāka viḷivippataṟku vaḻi kāṭṭiṉa. itiliruntu oḻuṅ kāṉa tāvara uṇavu peṟuvataṟku ētuvākiya muṟkāla vēḷāṇmi toṭaṅ kiṟṟu. iṉṟum kīḻniliyiluḷḷa camūkaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷ pāṟiyiṉālum, vaḷivu taṭiyiṉālum nilattik kiḷaṟip payiṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. ātikālam mutal peṇkaḷē necavu ceytal, pāy, kūṭi muṭital mutaliya vēli kaḷiyum ceytu vantārkaḷ. kampaḷi koṭukkum vilaṅkukaḷ vaḷarkkap paṭāta kālattil tāvara vakikaḷiliruntu nār eṭuttu āṭikaḷ neyyappaṭṭaṉa. cuviṟcarlāntu vāvikaḷil uṟinta makkaḷ (ki.mu.5,000) caṇaliṉālum kampaḷiyiṉālum ceytu uṭutta tuṇikaḷiṉ tuṇṭukaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. paḻiya makkaḷuḷ āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum ōr ōr iṭaṅkaḷil āṭi neytārkaḷ eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm. paṟpala kāraṇaṅkaḷi ṉāl āṭavar peṇkaḷukkuriya vēlikaḷit tām kippaṟṟik koṇṭu, avarkaḷ mītu āṇkaḷukkuriyaṉavum, mikak kīḻmiyāṉavum uḷḷa vēlikaḷic cumattiṉārkaḷ eṉat terikiṉṟatu. jermaṉiyil, pāṉi caṭṭi vaṉivōrum āṭi neyvōrum mattiya kālam vari yil tāḻntavarkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉar. itaṟkuk kāraṇam at toḻilkaḷ peṇkaḷukku uriyaṉavāyiruntaṉavēyākum. tiyal vēli ceyvōr peṇ taṉmiyuḷḷavarkaḷākavum, parikacikkattakkavar kaḷākavum nīṇṭa kālam karutappaṭalāyiṉar.
tāy atikārattiṉ toṭakkam
muṟkāla maṉitaṉ āṇ peṇ cērkkiyiṉāl kuḻanti piṟakkiṉṟatu eṉa aṟiyat toṭaṅkiya piṉ tāyāṭci muṟiyāṉa camūka vaḷarcci uṇṭāyiṟṟu. muṟkālattil kūṭṭu maṇaṅkaḷ camūkaṅkaḷukkuḷ iruntaṉa. itaṉāl tantikkum piḷḷikkum uṟavu uṇṭākavilli. tāykkum piḷḷikaḷukkum māttiram uṇmiyāṉa iratta uṟavu uṇṭāki yiruntatu. tāy kuḻantikku nīṇṭakālam pāl ūṭṭi vaḷarttāḷ. ataṉāl avaḷukkum piḷḷikkum nīṇṭa toṭarpu uṇṭāyiruntatu. nākarika maṭinta makkaḷiṭiyē uḷḷa tāyilum pārkkap piṟpōkkāṉa makka ḷiṭiyē uḷḷa tāy, taṉ kuḻantikku nīṇṭa kālam pāl ūṭṭi vaḷark kiṟāḷ. cātāraṇa kālam iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ, āstirēliya tāy āṟu āṇṭukaḷ kuḻantikkup pālūṭṭi vaḷarkkiṟāḷ. vaṭa amerikka peṇkaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷ variyum kuḻantikkup pāl koṭukkiṟārkaḷ.
ivvāṟu tāykkum kuḻantikkum uṇṭāṉa toṭarpiṉāl tāyaṉpu atikam vaḷarntatu. ivvāṟu aminta camūkattil tanti aṉṉiya ṉākak karutappaṭṭāṉ; iratta uṟavu uṭiyavaṉākak karutappaṭavilli. peṇṇukku atikāram uṇṭāyiṟṟu. vaḷarnta putalvar kuṭumpattip pātukāppōrākaviruntaṉar; putalviyar peṇkaḷukkuriya kaṭamikaḷil utavi purivōrāyiruntaṉar.
