in nilimiyiṉāl tōṉṟiya tāyāṭciyē āstirēliyā, āppirikkā, amerikkā, āciyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṉ paḻaṅkuṭi kaḷiṭiyē nilittuḷḷaṉa. paḻiya nākarika makkaḷiṭiyē tāyāṭci muṟi iruntatu. pāpilōṉiya āvaṇaṅkaḷil “tāyum tantiyum”, “maṉiviyum kaṇavaṉum” eṉpaṉa pōlap peṇṇiṉ peyarē mutaliṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. maṉitaṉ nākarikamaṭintirunta kālattilum tāyāṭci muṟi acikka muṭiyāta paṭi valimiyaṭintiruntateṉat terikiṉṟatu. pāpilōṉiyap peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷi otta urimi peṟṟiruntārkaḷ eṉpatip paṭṭiyaṅkaḷ vaḻiyāka aṟikiṉṟōm. hamurāpi kālattil (ki.mu.2,250) peṇkaḷ kōvilkaḷil kurumārākaviruntārkaḷ. āvaṇaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷ cāṭcikaḷākavum kiyeḻuttiṭṭārkaḷ.
ekiptiya āvaṇaṅkaḷil iṉṉa tāyiṉ makaṉ eṉṟē perum pālum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. tantiyiṉ peyar arumiyāka ōrōriṭattiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. kirēkkar ekipti veṟṟi koṇṭa kālattil ekiptiyar tāy vaḻiyālēyē aṟiyappaṭṭārkaḷ. piṉpu ciṟitu ciṟiyatākat tāyiṉ peyarukkup patil tantiyiṉ peyar iṭam peṟṟatu. paḻiya ōviya eḻuttukkaḷil tāy, vīṭṭukkāri eṉak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷāḷ.” arumi yāṉa talivi” allatu “uṉatu kaṇavaṉiṉ aṉpukkuriya illāḷ” eṉṟu kaṇavaṉ avaḷi aḻittāṉ. “ekiptil peṇkaḷ kaṭitteru vukkup pōy koḷvaṉavu viṟpaṉavukaḷic ceykiṟārkaḷ. āṇkaḷ vīṭṭiliruntu tuṇi neykiṟārkaḷ. putalvarukkup peṟṟōri ātarikka vēṇṭiya kaṭami illi; putalvikkē ak kaṭami uṇṭu” eṉa eratōtacu eṉṉum paḻiya kirēkka varalāṟṟāciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. ēratōtacukku (ki.mu. 480) nāṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷ piṉ vāḻnta tayatōras ik kūṟṟiṉi valiyuṟutti, aracaṉilum pārkka aracikkuk kūṭiya atikāram uṇṭu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. tirumaṇa uṭaṉpaṭikkiyil kaṇavaṉ maṉivikkuk kīḻppaṭivatāka vākkaḷittāṉ eṉṟum avar kuṟippiṭṭār. ekiptiya peṇkaḷiṉ cottu avarkaḷ atikārattukkuṭ paṭṭu iruntatu. avarkaḷ ataṉit tamatu eṇṇappaṭi evvāṟu vēṇṭiṉum ceytu koḷḷalām.
ekiptilē aracar mutal ellā makkaḷiṭiyum cakōtaraṉ cakōtariyi maṇakkum vaḻakkam tōṉṟi vaḷarntiruntatu. iraṇṭām tālami (ki.mu.285-247) eṉṉum ekiptiya aracaṉ pilāṭalpōs eṉat taṉakku maṟu peyar vittuk koṇṭāṉ. pilāṭalpōs eṉpataṟku cakōtariyik kātalikkiṉṟavaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ. paḻiya vaḻakkattip piṉpaṟṟi avaṉ ārciṉō eṉṉum taṉatu cakōtariyi maṇantāṉ. ekiptu pala ciṟṟaracar nāṭukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntatu. ovvoru ciṟiya nāṭṭiyum ovvoru aracaṉ āṇṭāṉ. avvaracaṉukku āṭci yurimi tanti vaḻiyālaṉṟit tāy vaḻiyākavē vantatu. atu iṉṟu tiruvitāṅkūril naṭimuṟiyiluḷḷa marumakkaḷ tāyatti ottatu. etiyōppiyariṉ aracurimi aracaṉiṉ piḷḷikaḷi aṭiyāmal avaṉuṭiya cakōtariyiṉ piḷḷikaḷi aṭintatu eṉṟu nikkōlas eṉṉum paḻiya varalāṟṟāciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār.
carittirakālam variyil tāyāṭci emmaṭṭum camūkaṅkaḷil valimi peṟṟiruntateṉpati viḷakkuvataṟkut tāyāṭciyik kuṟittuk kūṟiyuḷḷōm. varavara atikārattāl vaḷarntu vanta āṭavar peṇkaḷi niyāyamāka naṭattat tavaṟiṉārkaḷ eṉak kūṟamuṭiyātu.
āṭavaṉ pala peṇkaḷi maṇattal
ekiptilē celvantaṉāyuḷḷavaṉ tāṉ virumpiya attaṉi peṇkaḷiyum vippāṭṭikaḷāka vittiruntāṉ. āṉāl avaṉuṭiya muṟiyāṉa maṉiviyē vīṭṭiṉ talivi eṉappaṭṭāḷ. araṇmaṉi kaḷil ‘peṇkaḷiṉ vīṭu’ eṉappaṭṭa taṉimiyāṉa oru iṭam iruntatu. pipilas ēṭu oṉṟil, vippāṭṭikaḷiṉ mēṟkoṇṭa kātalāl taṉ mītu paṟṟu oḻinta aracaṉip paṟṟik kūṟiya irāṇi yoruttiyiṉ vākkiyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
viyapicārak kuṟṟamum taṇṭaṉiyum
peṇṇi vilikku vāṅki allatu tiruṭic contamākkiya pōtum viyapicāram maṉitaṉukkuk kuravak kuṟivāṉatu eṉṟu koḷḷap paṭṭatu. ellā nāṭukaḷilum kīḻ niliyiluḷḷa makkaḷiṭiyē peṇkaḷ viyapicārattiṟkut taṇṭikkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ; āṇ ciṟitēṉum taṇṭaṉi aṭivatilli. peṇṇi avaḷuṭiya peṟṟōriṭam pōkki avarkaḷiṭamiruntu peṇṇukku viliyākak koṭutta poruḷip peṟṟuk koḷḷutal, peṇṇiṉ avayavaṅkaḷi veṭṭutal, allatu avaḷik koli ceytal, pōlvaṉa viyapicārak kuṟṟattukkup peṇ aṭinta taṇṭaṉikaḷākum. cila camayaṅkaḷil viyapicārak kuṟṟañ cāṭṭappaṭṭavaṉ naṭṭa īṭu koṭukka vēṇṭiyiruntatu; cila camayam kolit taṇṭaṉiyum peṟṟāṉ. ekiptil viyapicārak kuṟṟattukkuṭ paṭṭa peṇkaḷiṉ mūkku aṟukkappaṭṭatu. apāci (Apache) eṉṉum amerikkariṭiyē ivvaḻakku iṉṟum naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. mūkku aṟukkappaṭāviṭṭāl avaḷ uyirōṭu putikkappaṭṭāḷ. kuṟṟattuk kuḷḷāṉavaṉ aṭikkappaṭṭāṉ.
peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷik kavarutaṟkut tammi alaṅkarittal
ekiptiya peṇkaḷ āṭavari vacīkarappaṭuttuvataṟkut tammi evvaḷavu alaṅkarikka muṭiyumō avvaḷavukku alaṅkarittuk koṇṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ palavaki mēṉimiṉukkup poruḷkaḷ, maṇap poruḷkaḷ, cāyaṅkaḷ, uṭi alaṅkāraṅkaḷ pōṉṟavikaḷip payaṉ paṭuttiṉar. tāyāṭci valiyaṭintiruntapōtum āṭavaṉ evvāṟu camūkattil taṉṉi uyarvāka vikka muṭintatu eṉpati ivi viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa. patāhōtep (Path-hotep ki.mu.3,350) eṉṉum aracaṉ peṇkaḷi āṭavarukkup piṉvarumāṟu puttimati kūṟiyuḷḷāṉ. “nī puttimāṉāyiṉ uṉatu maṉiviyiṭam aṉpāyiru; avaḷukku nalla uṇavu koṭu; avaḷi aḻakāka uṭukkum paṭi cey; avaḷukku maṇap poruḷkaḷi vāṅkik koṭu. iṉṉum avaḷukku makiḻcci koṭukkak kūṭiyavikaḷi nī irukkumaṭṭum cey. avaḷ taṉṉiyuṭiyavaṉiṉ takutikkēṟṟavaḷāy vāḻa vēṇṭum. nī avaḷiṭam koṭumiyāka naṭavātē. muraṭṭuttaṉamāṉa atikārattilum pārkka aṉpu naṉmikaḷic ceykiṉṟatu. mukakkaṇṇāṭiyil taṉṉip pārkkumpōtu avaḷ makiḻcciyaṭivāḷ. avaḷ makiḻcciyōṭu uṉatu vīṭṭiliruntu vīṭṭu vēlikaḷi virumpic ceyvāḷ.” itaṉāl, peṇṇiṉ atikārattuk kuṭiya tāyāṭci nīṇṭa kālam naṭipeṟṟu vantapōtum atu kālattil kuṟiyat toṭaṅki viṭṭateṉat terikiṉṟatu.

yūtariṭiyē tāyāṭci muṟi
kiṟittuva vēta kālattil yūtariṭiyē tāyāṭci muṟi iruntateṉak kāṇkiṉṟōm. kiṟittuva vētattil aracarip paṟṟiya puttakattil aracaṉiṉ peyarōṭu tāyiṉ peyar iṇikkappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟatu. araca capiyil aracaṉiṉ tāykku mutaṉmi aḷikkappaṭṭatu. “ākavē oruvaṉ taṉatu tāyiyum tantiyiyum viṭṭut taṉatu maṉiviyōṭu cērntu iruvarum oruvarāvārkaḷā?” (āti ākamam 11-24) eṉak kiṟittuva vētattiṉ pala viṭaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. iti ṉuṭiya poruḷ, oruttaṉ oru peṇṇi maṇantu tāyāṭci vaḻakkap paṭi avaḷ kuṭumpattic cērntavaṉāvāṉ eṉpatē.

pulveḷikaḷil vāḻnta epirēya makkaḷiṭiyē tāyāṭci muṉṉamē maṟintu pōyiṟṟu. āṭu māṭu vaḷarttu alintu tiriyum makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkikkum payiriṭṭu ōr iṭattil taṅkiyirukkum makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkikkum vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu.
tantiyiṉ atikāram
vēṭṭiyāṭum, āṭavarē toṭakkattil vilaṅkukaḷip piṭittup paḻakki vaḷarttārkaḷ. pulveḷikaḷil mēyntu maṉitariṉ kavaṉippukkuṭpaṭātu perukum āṭumāṭukaḷiṉ kūṭṭaṅkaḷukkum āṭavarē talivarākaviruntaṉar. mantikaḷ pāl, iṟicci mutaliya uṇavukaḷiyum, tōl, kampaḷi mutaliya uṭikaḷiyum koṭuttaṉa. itaṉāl avi mikavum matippukkuriya cottākapperukiṉa. āṭṭu manti, māṭṭu mantikaḷi atikamāka vittiruppatāl avikaḷiṉ contakkāraṉ putiya mēyccal nilattit tēṭa vēṇṭiyiruntatu. ākavē avaṉ taṉatu uṟiviṭatti aṭikkaṭi māṟṟa vēṇṭiyiruntatu. periya āṭu māṭukaḷiṉ kūṭṭaṅkaḷip pātukāppataṟku avaṉukku vēṭṭi yāṭutal, pōr ceytalkaḷiṟ payiṟciyuḷḷa utaviyāḷar tēvippaṭṭaṉar. ākavē avaṉ aṭimikaḷip peṟum avaciyam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. aṭimi kaḷip peṟṟatum avaṉ taṉatu putalvarkaḷukku manti mēykkum payiṟciyaḷittu mantiyi avarkaḷ kavaṉikkumpaṭi ceytāṉ. āppirikkāvilē āṭumāṭukaḷi vaḷarkkum kūṭṭattiṉariṭiyē avi viṭappaṭṭirukkum paṭṭikkuḷ peṇkaḷ cella aṉumatikkap paṭamāṭṭārkaḷ. avikaḷi mēyppatu, pālkaṟappatu ellām āṭavarāl ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

peṇkaḷukkum āṇkaḷukkumuḷḷa camūkat toṭarpi poruḷā tārat toṭarpāṉa māṟṟaṅkaḷum vēṟupaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉa. tāy vaḻiyiliruntu urimi uṇṭāvataṟkup patil tanti āṭci uṇṭāyiṟṟu. mantik kuriyavaṉē taṉṉi uṇavukku etirpārkkum kuṭumpattiṉ maṟṟiyavar kaḷukkellām talivaṉāṉāṉ. avaṉukkut tuṇi purivataṟkup pala āṭkaḷiṉ utavi vēṇṭiyiruntatu. ākavē avaṉukkup pala kuḻanti kaḷip peṟuvataṟkup pala peṇkaḷ tēvippaṭṭārkaḷ.

pāpilōṉilum ekiptilum peṇkaḷ campantamāṉa māṟṟam uṇṭākat toṭaṅkiyiruntaṉa. piṟkālattil ōriṭattil taṅkiyirunta epirēyariṭiyē mēlum tāyāṭci viḻukiyaṭivatāyiṟṟu. kiṟittuva maṟiyiṉ paḻiya ēṟpāṭṭil āṇ kuḻanti piṟantāl yēkōvāk kaṭavuḷukku naṉṟi celuttuvatu pōlap peṇ kuḻanti piṟantāl naṉṟi celuttum vākkiyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭavilli. peṇkaḷ peṟṟōr kaḷuṭiya cottāyiruntatu piṉpu kaṇavaṉiṉ cottu āṉārkaḷ. peṇ vili matippuḷḷa vēliyāḷāyiruntāḷ. avaḷ mikak kaṭumiyāṉa vīṭṭu vēlikaḷic ceytāḷ. avaḷ aṭimipōl vēli ceytatu māttiramallāmal avaḷiṭam tāṉ urimi pārāṭṭakkūṭiya cottu etuvum irukkavilli. pāpilōṉiyarāl ciṟi piṭikkappaṭum kālam variyil āṇkaḷ pala peṇkaḷi maṇantārkaḷ. āṭavaṉ peṇṇi vili koṭuttu vāṅkiṉāṉ, cottu āṇ vaḻiyic cērntatu. āṇ cantati illāviṭil cottup peṇṇic cērntatu; āṉāl avaḷ ak kuṭumpattil māttiram kaṇavaṉit terintu koḷḷutal vēṇṭum, peṇṇiṉ kaṭami piḷḷikaḷip peṟuvatum vīṭṭi oḻuṅkāka vittuk koḷḷuvatumē eṉṟu karutappaṭṭatu.

ilakkiya kāla makkaḷi nām nōkkumpōtu eratōtacu kūṟi yirukkum oru nikaḻcci itaṟku māṟākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ciṉṉa āciyā viṉ teṉmēṟku mūliyiṟ kuṭiyēṟiyiruntavarkaḷum, kirēttā (Crete) viṉiṉṟu vantavarkaḷ eṉappaṭṭavarkaḷumākiya iliciyariṭiyē piḷḷikaḷ tāy vaḻiyāl aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar. oruvaṉi nōkki avaṉ kuṭumpa vaḻiyi viṉāviṉāl avaṉ taṉatu tāy allatu tāyiṉtāy peyarik kūṟuvāṉ. peṇ, aṭimi oruvaṉic cērntu piḷḷiyip peṟṟāl piḷḷi muṟimiyāṉatākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. āṇ, aṭimip peṇṇic cērvatāl piṟanta piḷḷikaḷ aṭimikaḷākavum muṟikēṭākap piṟantaṉavākavum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. cottu tāyiṭa miruntu makaḷi aṭintatu; makaṉi aṭiyavilli.

kirēkka nākarikattiṉ muṉmuṟiyil nām tāyāṭciyik kāṇ kiṉṟōm. ivvaṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ paḻaṅkatikaḷilēyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. atēṉiya caṭṭam tanti vaḻiyiluḷḷa (tantiyiṉ cakōtaraṉiṉ peṇ ṇākiya) cakōtariyi maṇakka aṉumatikkiṉṟatu; āṉāl tāy vaḻiyil ivvāṟu maṇakkum paṭi aṉumatikkavilli. paḻiya kirēkkariṭattil kātal eṉpatu iṉṟiya kīḻ niliyiluḷḷa makkaḷiṭiyē kāṇappaṭum kātalip paṟṟiya karuttiviṭa vēṟāka irukkavilli. kaṉṉip peṇkaḷ peṇkaḷukku viṭappaṭṭuḷḷa aṟikaḷiliruntu vaḷarn tārkaḷ, peṇ maṇapparuvam aṭintatum peṟṟōr maṇamakaṉit tēṭi avaṉiṭattil avaḷik koṭuttārkaḷ. avaḷ kaṇavaṉiṉ vīṭṭukkuc ceṉṟu aṅkup peṇkaḷ irukkum aṟiyil vāḻntāḷ. avaḷ kalvi yaṟivillātiruntāḷ; avaḷukkup peṇkaḷukkuriya tiyal vēli māttiram teriyum. avaḷ veḷiyē cellavum āṭavarōṭu paḻakavum maṉa vaḷarccikkuriyavikaḷi aṟiyavum muṭiyāmal iruntāḷ.

piṟkālattil āṭci muṟikaḷil māṟṟam uṇṭāṉa pōtu kirēkkap peṇkaḷiṉ nili iṉṉum mōcamaṭintatu. maṉiviyiruk kum aṟiyik kāval kāppataṟku aṭimip peṇ orutti niyamikkap paṭṭāḷ. maṉiviyirukkum aṟiyi aṭittut tāḷ iṭṭu avaḷik kāval kāppatē nalla muṟi eṉṟu kaṇavaṉ karutiṉāṉ. paḻiya tattuva cāttirikaḷ, peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷukkuc camamāṉavarkaḷallar eṉak karuti ṉārkaḷ. ākavē caṭṭattil peṇkaḷukku vāyppāṉavi eviyum irukkavilli. kirīcilē spāṭṭiya, ṭōriyap peṇkaḷ viṭutali yaṭintiruntārkaḷ eṉac cilar kūṟuvatuṇṭu. ik karuttut tavaṟuṭiyatu. spāṭṭiya, ṭōriyap peṇkaḷ veḷiyē ceṉṟu viḷiyāṭṭukaḷilum malyuttaṅkaḷilum kalantu koḷḷumpaṭiyum viṭappaṭṭārkaḷ. itu avarkaḷ uṭal nalam peṟṟu nalla kuḻantikaḷip peṟum poruṭṭum, āṭavariṉ naṉmiyik karutiyum aṉumatikkappaṭṭa vaḻakkamēyākum.

ak kālattil kirīcil iṉṉoru vakiyākap peṇkaḷ muṉṉukku vantārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ parattiyarāvar (Hetiri). ivarkaḷ āṭavariṉ kavarccikkuṭiyavarkaḷāyiruntaṉar. vīṭṭu maṉiviyarkaḷilum pārkka ivarkaḷukku ulaka naṭiyum, kalviyum, āṭavari vaciyap paṭuttum taṉmiyum, nalla paḻakkaṅkaḷum teriyum. kirēkkak kaṇavarkaḷ ivarkaḷiyē cuṟṟit tirintaṉar. cākirāṭs, arisṭōṭṭil mutaliya tattuva ñāṉikaḷum ivarkaḷiṉ kavarccikkuṭpaṭṭiruntaṉar. vīṭṭukkāri eṉappaṭṭa peṇ ivvāṟu parattiyic cuṟṟittiriyum kaṇavaṉik kaliyāṇa nīkkam ceytu koḷḷalām. ap parattiyi vīṭṭukkuk koṇṭu varāta aḷavil avaḷ avvāṟu ceytal muṭiyātu. perikkiḷis (Pericles) ivvāṟu ceytamiyāl kaliyāṇa nīkkam ceyyappaṭṭār. avar aimpatu vayatuṭiyavarāyum, vaḷarnta makkaḷi uṭiyavarāyum iruntapōtum aspēciyā (Aspesia) eṉṉum parattiyi maṇantār. ṭemostīṉis eṉṉum kirēkkar kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu: “nāṅkaḷ eṅkaḷuṭiya tēvikaḷukkum ūḻiyattukkumāka vippāṭṭikaḷi (aṭimikaḷi) vittirukkiṟōm; eṅkaḷ kātalukkum iṉpattukku mākap parattiyar irukkiṟārkaḷ; vīṭṭik kavaṉikka maṉiviyar irukkiṟārkaḷ.”
1parattimi
muṟimiyāṉa toṭarpu illāta āṭavarōṭu cērkki vit tirukkum peṇ paratti eṉappaṭuvāḷ. āṭavaṉiṉ iyalpāṉa taṉmi oru peṇṇi maṇappataṉṟu. tirumaṇaṅkaḷ oḻukka campantattilum pārkkap poruḷātāra campantamuṭiyaṉa. muṟkālattil palavāṉ allatu celvavāṉ, tāṉ virumpiya attaṉi peṇkaḷik koḷḷiyiṉāl akappaṭutti allatu vili koṭuttu vāṅki vittirukkalām. kīḻ niliyiluḷḷa makkaḷiṭiyē iṉṟum immuṟi kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. kiṟittuva vētattiṉ paḻiya ēṟpāṭṭil kūṟappaṭum cālamaṉ aracaṉiṭat til maṉiviyarum vippāṭṭikaḷumāka āyiram peṇkaḷ variyilirun tārkaḷ. palamillātavarum, vaṟiyavarum oru maṉiviyi vittiruntaṉar, allatu maṉivi illātu iruntaṉar. poruḷ ātāra nilimi naṉṟā yillāviṭiṉum peṇtuṇi vēṇṭumeṉṉum viruppiṉāl āṭavaṉ oruttiyi vili koṭuttu vāṅkuvāṉ allatu tiruṭik koṇṭu varuvāṉ. ivvaṭippaṭiyiliruntē oḻukkam oḻukkamiṉmi eṉṉum niyāyaṅkaḷ tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. oḻukkam eṉpatu cātāraṇamāka makka ḷiṭiyē naṭappatu; oḻukkamiṉmi am makkaḷiṭiyē naṭavātatu. piṉpu caṭṭaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi oḻukkamiṉmikkut taṇṭaṉikaḷi vitikkiṉṟaṉa. peṇkaḷum āṇkaḷum vāḻkiṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil ic caṭṭaṅkaḷ nili peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

paḻiya caṭṭaṅkaḷi vakutta cōlaṉ1 (ki.mu. 504) eṉṉum kirēkkar viyapicāratti caṭṭatiṭṭappaṭutta niṉittu aracāṅka viyapicāra viṭuti oṉṟit tiṟantāṉ. av viṭutikku oṉṟarip ‘peṉs’ (oṉṟari aṇā) variyil kaṭṭaṇam koṭukkum āṭavaṉ aṉumatikkappaṭṭāṉ. ivvāṟu kiṭitta poruḷik koṇṭu cōlaṉ apirōṭiṟ eṉṉum kātalukkuriya peṇ teyvattiṟkuk kōvil kaṭṭiṉāṉ. itu piṟkālat til yūta kurumār erucalēm2 kōvilikkaṭṭuvataṟku oḻukkantavaṟiya peṇkaḷiṭamiruntu vari taṇṭiyatu pōṉṟatākum, parattiyarākiya ik kīḻttarap peṇkaḷ maṇavāḻkkikkup peruntīṅku viḷivittārkaḷ. kirēkkar āciya makkaḷiṉ ullāca vāḻkkiyi aṟintu pakaṭṭāṉa parattiyariṉ paḻakkattip peṟṟārkaḷ. muṉ kūṟappaṭṭa aspēciyā eṉṉum perikkiḷiciṉ maṉivi atēṉcil naṭattiya parattiyar paḷḷik kūṭattukku atēṉiya makkaḷiṉ peṇkaḷum, putalviyarum ceṉṟaṉar. piraṉi (Pyryne) eṉṉum paratti taṉatu uḻippil oru pakutiyi tīps nakariṉ aḻinta matilit tiruttuvataṟkuk koṭuttāḷ. cākiraṭīs, arisṭāṭil, piḷāṭō mutaliyōrukkup parattiyariṉ uṟavu iṉṟi yamiyātiruntatu. parattiyar tāymārum, maṉiviyarumāyumiruntaṉar. parattiyar peṟum piḷḷikaḷ muṟiyāṉa kuḻantikaḷākak koḷḷap paṭṭamiyāl iḷam peṇkaḷ parattimit toḻilik kikkoḷḷum paṭi tūṇṭappaṭṭārkaḷ.

ivvāṟu airōppāvil aḻakuk kalikkalviyum parattiyariṭa miruntu ārampittuḷḷatu. parattiyariṉ iyakkamē peṇkaḷiṉ mutal iyakkam eṉak kūṟalām. piṟkāla cīrtirutta vaḷarccik kālaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷ im muṟiyic ciṟitu māṟṟi āṭavar viṭuvittuk koḷḷa muṭiyātapaṭi avarkaḷukkup putiya vilaṅku iṭṭārkaḷ.
urōmap peṇkaḷ

urōmap peṇkaḷiṉ nili, pārvikkuk kirēkkap peṇkaḷiṉ niliyilum uyarntateṉat tōṉṟum. urōmarkaḷiṭiyum āṭavar kaṭṭuppaṭuttappaṭāta talivarkaḷāyiruntaṉar. maṇac caṭaṅkil peṇṇiṉ kiyil tiṟavukōl koṭukkappaṭṭatu. itu vīṭṭu atikāram avaḷiṭam aḷikkappaṭṭatik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. maṇamakaṉ taṉatu kiviralil irumpu mōtiratti aṇintāṉ. itu avaṉ kaṭṭuppaṭuttappaṭṭati aṟivikkum. peṇṇukkuc ciṟuvayatil maṇam niccayikkappaṭṭatu; paṉṉiraṇṭu allatu patiṉmūṉṟu vayatil maṇam nikaḻntatu. peṇ kaṇavaṉiṉ tanti yāl terintu eṭukkappaṭṭāḷ. avaḷ tirumaṇattukkut taṉatu cammatam koṭukka vēṇṭiyatilli; tantiyiṉ pātukāppiliruntu avaḷ kaṇavaṉ pātukāppukkuc ceṉṟāḷ. kirēkka maṉivikkum urōmaṉ maṉivikkumiṭiyil camūka nilimiyil vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. urōmaṉ peṇ, peṇkaḷukku eṉat taṉimiyākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa iṭattil vāḻa villi. avaḷ kaṇavaṉōṭu iruntu uṇṭāḷ; viruntukaḷil kalantu koṇṭāḷ. avaḷ vīṭṭukku varupavarkaḷi etirkoṇṭāḷ; virumpiya pōtu vīṭṭi viṭṭu veḷiyē ceṉṟāḷ; niyāya maṉṟaṅkaḷil vaḻak kāḷiyāka allatu cāṭciyākac ceṉṟāḷ. kaṇavaṉ vīṭṭikkut taliva ṉākaviruntāṉ. veḷiyiluḷḷa vēlikaḷ ellāvaṟṟukkum avaṉē poṟuppuṭiyavaṉ. kuṭumpattiluḷḷavarkaḷ aṭimikaḷ vilaṅkukaḷ ellām talivaṉukkuriyavi. mika muṟkālattil maṉivi matuvaruntum paḻakkamuḷḷavaḷāyiruntāl, avaḷik kaṇavaṉ koṉṟuviṭutal kuṟṟamākak karutappaṭavilli. ṭomiṭṭiyas eṉpavaṉ peṇ orutti vittiyaṉ coṉṉa aḷavukku atikam matu aruntiyataṟkāka avaḷ taṉ kaliyāṇattāl kiṭitta paṇatti iḻantupōkumpaṭi tīrppuc ceytāṉ. makkaḷiṉ āṭci uṇṭāṉapōtu urōm nāṭṭil tanti āṭci naṭipeṟṟatu. peṇkaḷ urimiyip paṟṟiyirunta paḻiya kālak karuttukkaḷ maṟintapōtu avarkaḷ taṅkaḷukku vāyppāka avikaḷi māṟṟikkoṇṭārkaḷ. vāḻkkiyiṉ ellāt tuṟikaḷilum avarkaḷukkum paṅku iruntatu. ippoḻutu avarkaḷ tamatu uṭimi kaḷiyum celvattiyum tamatu atikārattilēyē vittiruntārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ campantamāṉa karumaṅkaḷip paṟṟi ālōcippataṟku ivarkaḷ aracāṅkattilum vēṇṭappaṭṭārkaḷ.

“ellāc cātiyiṉarum taṅkaḷ peṇkaḷik kaṭṭiyāḷkiṟārkaḷ; nāṅkaḷ āṇkaḷi māttiram āḷkiṉṟōm; eṅkaḷukkum mēlākap peṇkaḷ āḷkiṟārkaḷ. nām namatu peṇṇi aṭakki āḷum tiṟamiyuḷḷavarkaḷāyiruntāl peṇkaḷ campantamāṉa tollikaḷ irā. nām vīṭṭuk karumaṅkaḷil atikārattiyiḻantuviṭṭapaṭiyāl potuk karumaṅkaḷilum peṇkaḷ eṅkaḷik kaṭṭiyāḷukiṉṟārkaḷ. nāṅkaḷ taṉippaṭṭu niṉṟu avarkaḷi etirkkamuṭiyātu. avarkaḷiṉ oṉṟu cērnta atikāratti nām añcukiṉṟōm. inta oḻuṅkaṟṟavarkaḷiṭattil eṅkaḷi nām oppuvittāl avarkaḷiṉ pērāciyāṉa ceyalkaḷukku muṭivu kāṇa iyalātu. uṇmiyākak kūṟumiṭattu peṇkaḷukku viṭutaliyē kūṭātu. avarkaḷukku ellāc ceyalkaḷilum kaṭṭuppāṭu vēṇṭum. peṇ eṅkaḷōṭu cama urimiyip peṟṟāl avaḷ eṅkaḷi virivil piṉṉāl taḷḷiviṭuvāḷ” eṉṟu kēṭō (Cato- ki.mu. 195) kūṟiyuḷḷār.

kirēkka nākarikam paraviyatālum, aracāṅkattil atikāram uṇ ṭāṉamiyiṉālum, celvam atikarittamiyiṉālum urōmap peṇkaḷ kēṭō kūṟiyatupōla āṇkaḷiṉ kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṉiṉṟum viṭupaṭṭu viyakkattakka muṉṉēṟṟamaṭintārkaḷ, kirēkka nāṭṭil kāṇappaṭṭa parattiyariṉ naṭimuṟikaḷ periya aḷaviṉatāyum kīḻāṉa taṉmi yuṭiyatāyum urōmil tōṉṟalāyiṉa. periya aracāṅka uttiyōkat tariṉ maṉiviyar, taṇṭaṉi peṟātu tamatu aḷavu kaṭanta icciyit taṇittuk koḷḷum poruṭṭut tammip pakiraṅka vili mātarākap pativu ceytu koṇṭārkaḷ. ak kālattil caṭṭam viyapicārak kuṟṟaṅ kaḷukkuk kaṭuntaṇṭaṉi vitittatu. tēva arampiyi otta kaṭṭaḻaki yākiya mecaliṉiyā (Messalinia) pōṉṟa irāṇikaḷē viyapicāra viṭuti kaḷil parattamit toḻili naṭattiṉārkaḷ. peṇkaḷiṉ viṭutali evvaḷavu tūram ceṉṟateṉpati nām aṟintu koḷḷalām.

urōmap peṇkaḷiṉ viṭutaliyiṉāl piṟappu vikitam kuṟintatu. itaṉic caṭṭaṅkaḷiṉāl uyar nilikkuk koṇṭu vara muṭiyavilli. itu urōmariṉ viḻukikku oru kāraṇamākaviruntatu.

peṇ pala kaṇavari maṇattal
urōmar piriṭṭaṉmītu paṭiyeṭutta kālattil pirittaṉil peṇkaḷ pala kaṇavari orē kālattil maṇantiruntārkaḷ. itaṉic cīcar ‘ṭi pellō kalikkō’ eṉṉum taṉatu nūliṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. oru peṇ cātāraṇamāka pattu mutal paṉṉiraṇṭu kaṇavari maṇantiruntāḷ. avarkaḷ perumpālum cakōtararākavum, tantiyum putalvarkaḷumāyu miruntaṉar. peṇ pala kaṇavari maṇakkum vaḻakkam, peṇ toki kuṟivatālum āṇ toki atikappaṭuvatālum uṇṭākiṉṟatu eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ, iv vaḻakkam iṉṟum vaṭamēṟku amerikka makkaḷ, teṉṉintiya makkaḷ cilar, tipettu, makkaḷiṭiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
evaṉ peṇṇi mutalil cērkiṉṟāṉō, piṟakkum piḷḷi avaṉuṭiyateṉap pirittāṉiya makkaḷ koṇṭārkaḷ eṉac cīcar kūṟiyuḷḷār. jermaṉi, ayarlāntu, skāttulāntu mutaliya nāṭukaḷil maṇattilaṉṟiya iravu peṇṇi naṇparukkuk koṭuppatākiya vaḻakku camīpa kālam variyil naṭipeṟṟatu. itu an nāṭukaḷil palatāra maṇam iruntateṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟākum. peṇ palari maṇakkum vaḻakkam tāyāṭciyi vaḷarac ceytatu.
jermaṉiyar peṇkaḷip paricuttamuṭiyavarkaḷākak karutiṉār kaḷ. avarkaḷ avarkaḷiṉ puttimatiyi allatu avarkaḷ ceyyum tīrppukkaḷit taṭṭamāṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟu tāciṟras eṉṉum paḻiya carittira āciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. mattiya āppirikkā, ciṉṉa āciyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum tāyāṭci vaḻakku uḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilum peṇkaḷ iṉṟum taliyārikaḷākavum kurumārkaḷākavum iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar. tāciṟras kālattil jermaṉiyil āṇkaḷ mukkiya nikaḻccikaḷip peṇkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu ālōcittārkaḷ. āppirikkāvilē cāmpaci nīkrōvar peṇkaḷ kūṭi ālōcikkum iṭaṅkaḷukkuc cellamāṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ maṉivi yariṉ cammatamiṉṟip piṟarukku oru utaviyum ceyya māṭṭārkaḷ. paṉs nīkrōvarkaḷukkiṭiyil āṇkaḷiṉ ālōcaṉik kūṭṭattil taliyāriyiṉ tāy kalantu koḷvāḷ.
tanti āṭci
tāyāṭcikku muṉ tanti āṭciyiruntatu. kaṉṟukkuṭṭi māṭṭuk kāraṉukkuc contamāvatupōla piḷḷi tantikkuc contamāṉavaṉ eṉṉum caṭṭam iruntatu. peṇ, tiruṭappaṭṭu allatu vili koṭuttu vāṅkappaṭṭuk koṇṭuvarappaṭṭāḷ. celvaṉ allatu palamuṭiyavaṉ pala peṇkaḷi vittirukkalām. ivvāṟu ceytal oruvakik kaṭami eṉṟu tāciṟras kūṟiyuḷḷār. ayarlāntiṉ uḷnāṭukaḷil peṇkaḷit tiruṭuvatākiya vaḻakkaṅkaḷ pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭuppakutivari yilum iruntatu. skāntinēviya makkaḷuḷ vikiṅ (Vikings) āṭavar tamatu atiṭṭattukku ēṟṟavāṟu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu allatu pala maṉiviyari vittiruntārkaḷ; celvarum taliyārikaḷum mikap pala maṉiviyari uṭiyarāyiruntaṉar. oru peṇṇi oruvaṉ vili koṭuttu vāṅki ṉāl avaṉ avaḷi viṟkavum muṭiyum, iv vaḻakkam jermaṉiyil ciṟu vakuppiṉariṭiyē patiṉmūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyum iruntatu. oruvaṉ virumpiyapōtu taṉatu maṉiviyiyum piḷḷikaḷiyum viṟṟāṉ.
iṅkilāntilē patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakuti variyil kaṇavaṉ taṉatu maṉiviyi avaḷatu cammatamiṉṟiyē tāṉ virumpiya paṭi āṭumāṭukaḷ viṟpatupōla viṟkum urimi peṟṟiruntāṉ. ciṟṟaracaṉ (Duke) oruvaṉ taṉatu vaṇṭi yōṭṭupavaṉiṉ maṉiviyi vilikoṭuttu vāṅkiya āvaṇamum, cerupput tippavaṉ kayiṟṟil kaṭṭic cantikkuk koṇṭu vantu viṟṟa peṇṇi aintu pavaṉukku vāṅkiya āvaṇamum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kiṟittuva matamum peṇkaḷum
muṟkālattil jermaṉiyil vitavi kollappaṭṭuk kaṇavaṉōṭu putikkappaṭṭāḷ. kālattil ivvaḻakkaṅkaḷ maṟintupōka peṇṇuk kuk kaṇavaṉit teriyum urimi uṇṭāyiṟṟu. āṭavaṉiṉ elliyil lāta atikāram taḷarntatu. ippoḻutu avaṉ peyaraḷavil peṇṇiṉ pātukāppāḷaṉākaviruntāṉ. patiṉintām nūṟṟāṇṭil peṇ, cot tukku urimiyuṭiyavaḷāṉāḷ. piṉpu kirēkkar urōmarkaḷiṭiyē nērntavi pōṉṟa māṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. pirāṅkiya (Frankish) makkaḷiṉ oḻukkattip paṟṟi akkālac caṭṭaṅkaḷē veḷiyiṭukiṉṟaṉa. ak kālattil kurumārōṭu kiṟittuva matattit taḻuviya peṇkaḷ toṭarpu vittirukka aṉumatikkum camayac caṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. kiṟittuva matam peṇkaḷiṉ camūka niliyi uyarttuvataṟku utavi purintateṉak kūṟa muṭiyātu. toṭakkattil atu araciyal campantamum vāṇika campantamum uṭiyatāyiruntatu. atu palatāra maṇaṅkaḷik kaṇṭikkavilli. kiṟittuva āṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyil pala peṇkaḷi maṇattal pāvamākak koḷḷappaṭavilli. kiṟittuva camayam peṇkaḷukkuc ciṟitum matippuk koṭukkavilli. piṟkālak kurumār, peṇkaḷ acuttamāṉavarkaḷum pāvam niṟintavarkaḷum eṉak kūṟiṉar.

piṟkālattil kiṟittuva nāṭukaḷil oruvaṉ oru peṇṇi maṇakkum vaḻakkam camūka, poruḷātāra ētukkaḷāl uṇṭāyiṟṟu. pala peṇkaḷi maṇakkum vaḻakkam māṟi oru peṇṇi maṇakkum vaḻakkam uṇṭāṉapōtum vippāṭṭiyiṉ eṇkaḷip paṟṟiya kaṭṭuppāṭu irukkavilli. vippāṭṭikaḷukkup piṟanta piḷḷikaḷukkuc cottil urimi irukkavilli; avarkaḷ muṟimiyāṉa piḷḷikaḷākavum koḷḷappaṭavilli. itaṉāl mariyātiyuṭiya peṟṟōr vippāṭṭi kaḷiṉ kumārarukkut taṅkaḷ kumārikaḷi maṇam muṭikka virumpavilli. itaṉāl oru peṇṇi maṇakkum vaḻakkam valimiyaṭintatu. poruḷātāram ētuvākat tōṉṟiya orutāra maṇattic camayamum kiriyikaḷ mūlam vaṟpuṟuttiṟṟu.
muṉpu milittiyā, icis, apiraṭiṭ mutaliya peyarkaḷāl makkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya kaṉṉit teyvattukkup patil mēri vaḻipāṭu uṇṭāyiṟṟu. itu peṇkaḷiṉ uyarvukku ētuvāyiruntatu.

15ām nūṟṟāṇṭil kiṟittuva nāṭukaḷil oḻukkam
patiṉintām nūṟṟāṇṭil kiṟittuva nāṭukaḷil oḻukkam mikavum tāḻvaṭintiruntatu. pirapukkaḷ kātaṟ pāṭalkaḷip pāṭikkoṇṭu maṇa māṉa peṇkaḷip peṟa muyaṉṟārkaḷ, maṇamāṉa peṇkaḷum urōmaṉ, kirēkka parattiyarip pōlat tamatu kātali avarkaḷukku viṟṟārkaḷ, ippeṇkaḷum kirēkka parattiyar pōlat tammi aḻakiya uṭikaḷāl alaṅkarittum, mēṉi miṉukkup poruḷkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiyum, naṟumaṇaṅkaḷip pūciyum tammi āṭavariṉ kavarccik kēṟṟavarkaḷākkik koṇṭārkaḷ. caṉṉiyācikaḷē (monks) avarkaḷukkup pāṭam kaṟpittārkaḷ.

ak kālattil uyarntavarkaḷiṭiyum tāḻntavarkaḷiṭiyum oḻukkam orēvakiyākaviruntatu. iraṇṭām payas eṉṉum pāppāṇṭa var patiṉintām nūṟṟāṇṭil vīyaṉṉā nakariṉ oḻukka nili evvāṟu iruntateṉa eḻutivittuḷḷār. “makkaḷ ellōrum kātal oḻukkaṅkaḷi lēyē poḻutu pōkkiṉārkaḷ. pakiraṅka viyapicārikaḷiṉ eṇṇikki atikarittatu. cila peṇkaḷē oru āṭavaṉōṭu tiruptiyaṭintirun tārkaḷ. pirapukkaḷ, kuṭiyāṉavaṉiṉ aḻakiya maṉivikkup piṉṉāl ōṭuvatu potu nikaḻcciyāyiruntatu. an niḻaccik kālaṅkaḷil peṇṇiṉ kaṇavaṉē viruntāḷiyākiya pirapuvukkuc cārāyam koṭuttu, avaṉi avaḷōṭu taṉimiyākak kūṭikkulāvumpaṭi viṭuvāṉ. peṇkaḷ palar tām virumpāta kaṇavari nañcu koṭuttuk koṉṟaṉar. aracāṅka uttiyōkattar tam maṉiviyar putalviyarōṭu oḻukuvati etirtta kuṭiyāṉavarkaḷil palar kollappaṭṭārkaḷ.
viyapicārattukkuk kāraṇam
viyapicāram oruvaṉ oruttiyi maṇappatākiya oru tāra maṇatti ṉāl taṭukkak kūṭiyataṉṟu. atu āṭavaṉiṉ peṇ cērkki viruppum, peṇṇiṉ āṇ cērkki viruppum takunta vaḻiyil kaṭṭuppaṭuttap paṭāviṭil uṇṭākiṉṟatu. camūkattil āṭavarilum atikamākap peṇka ḷiruntālum, peṇkaḷilum āṇ mikuntālum itu atikam nikaḻkiṉ ṟatu. iyaṟkiyāṉa tūṇṭutalkaḷiṉ nimittam āṭavaṉ viyapicāratti virumpum pōtu peṇkaḷ ataṉāl ilāpam aṭiya muntuvārkaḷ.

mattiya kālattil ivvakip pala kāraṇaṅkaḷāl viyapicāram atikappaṭṭatu. ataṉviḷivāka oru tāramaṇam camayattiṉāl kaṭṭāyap paṭuttappaṭṭatu. oruvaṉukkum oruttikkum vāḻnāḷ muḻumiyilum irukkak kūṭiyatallāta āṇ peṇ uṟavukaḷ pāvamāṉatākavum taṇṭaṉik kuriyaṉavum eṉṟu karutappaṭṭaṉa.

kiṟittuva matam mutalil kurumār tirumaṇam ceyvatit taṭukka villi; kōvilic cērnta caṉṉiyācikaḷ, caṉṉiyācip peṇkaḷ māttiram tirumaṇam ceyyat taṭukkappaṭṭiruntārkaḷ. ki.pi.869ām āṇṭu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa caṭṭattil pala maṉiviyariyuṭiya kurumār tamatu camaya camapantamāṉa uṭiyik kaḷintuviṭa vēṇṭumeṉac collappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ciṟitu kālattil caṉṉiyācikaḷum caṉṉiyācip peṇkaḷum taṅkum maṭaṅkaḷ kaḷiyāṭṭattukkuriya iṭaṅkaḷākavum, viyapicāra viṭutikaḷākavum māṟiṉa. oḻukkakkēṭukaḷ aḷavu kaṭantu perukiṉa. kurumāriṉ iṉpa nukarvu avarkaḷiṉ niliyāṉa cōmpal vāḻkkiyiṉāl ceḻippaṭintatu. kurumāriṉ ikaḻccikkuriya oḻukkak kēṭu kāraṇamāka mūṉṟām kirikari (Gregory III), kurumār ellōrum tuṟavikaḷāyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṉum caṭṭattik kaṭṭāyamākkiṉār. ippoḻutu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa kurumār tamatu iccikaḷit tamatu pātukāppilum tamatu atikārattilum iruntavar mūlam niṟivēṟṟik koṇṭārkaḷ. oḻukkaṅkeṭṭa icceyalkaḷip paṟṟip pāṭalkaḷum, katikaḷum, paḻamoḻikaḷum ak kālattu vaḻaṅkiṉa.

mattiya kālattil tirumaṇamiṉṟi alintu tiriyum oru vakic caṉṉiyācik kūṭṭattiṉarāl oḻukkak kēṭukaḷ paraviṉa. poṟāmi, piṟar pōṭṭiyiṭuvārkaḷ eṉṟa payam mutaliyavikaḷāl paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷil aṉṉiyarukku vēlikaḷ koṭukka maṟukkappaṭṭatu. ak kālaṅkaḷil vāḻkkiyiṉ poruṭṭum viyapicāram ārampittatu. pōrkaḷil pala āṇkaḷ maṭintaṉar. ciluvip pōrkaḷil īṭupaṭṭa āṇkaḷil ciṟantavar, pala āṇṭukaḷ ayal nāṭukaḷil taṅkiṉar. āṭavar palar kūṭṭaṅkaḷākac cērntu ayalnāṭukaḷiṟ ceṉṟu vāṇikam naṭattiṉar. ellāp paṭṭiṉaṅ kaḷilum vāḻum makkaḷil perumpālōr paruvamaṭinta peṇkaḷā yiruntaṉar. itu kāraṇamāka ‘peṇkaḷiṉ viṭutikaḷ’ eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ viyapicāra niliyaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. iv viṭutikaḷukku varikaḷ, eḻuttu mūlamuḷḷa kaṇakkukaḷik koṇṭu vitikkappaṭṭaṉa. iv viṭutikaḷil vāḻum peṇkaḷiṉ cārpākat taṉic caṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. iv viṭutiyic cērātu taṉippaṭṭa viyapicārattāl tammōṭu pōṭṭiyiṭum peṇkaḷ mītu ivarkaḷ vaḻakkut toṭukkak kūṭiyatākaviruntatu. ki.pi.1,414il kāṉsṭaṉs (Constance) eṉṉum iṭattil kūṭiya makānāṭṭiṉpōtu kiṭikkakkūṭiya ūtiyatti etirpārttu avviṭattil 1,500 ivvakip parattip peṇkaḷ tiraṇṭārkaḷ, cāḷs (Charles) eṉpavaṉiṉ paṭṭāḷattil 900 kurumārum, 1,600 parattip peṇkaḷum iruntārkaḷ. urōmaṉ irāṇikaḷ matuk kaṭikaḷukkuc ceṉṟu tammi muṭṭākkiṭṭu maṟittuk koṇṭu parattimiyāl tamatu iccikaḷit taṇittuk koṇṭārkaḷ.



parattimi ellāk kālaṅkaḷilum pāvamuṭiyatākak koḷḷap paṭṭatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil “kiṟittuva matattukku nērum palavakit tīmi kaḷip pōkkuvatu kāraṇamāka ik kuṟṟam paricutta tēvālayattāl maṉṉikkappaṭukiṉṟatu” ivvāṟu nēṇpek eṉṉumiṭattil patiṉin tām nūṟṟāṇṭil kūṭiya nakara makānāṭṭil kūṟappaṭṭatu. kiṟittuva matattil periya puraṭciyit tōṟṟuvitta ulūtar, āṇ cērkki campantamākat tammit tiruptip paṭuttik koḷvataṟkup peṇkaḷukkup pūraṇa urimiyuṇṭu eṉṟum, uṇpatu, uṟaṅkuvatu, viṭāykku nīr kuṭippatu pōla atu avaḷukku avaciyamāṉavikaḷuḷ oṉṟu eṉṟum, ivi kaḷukku māṟākat taliyiṭukiṉṟavaṉ iyaṟkikku iciya naṭakkātava ṉeṉṟum, neruppuc cuṭāteṉṟum, taṇṇīr naṉikkāteṉṟum, maṉitaṉ uṇṇak kuṭikka nittiri koḷḷa māṭṭāṉeṉṟum niṉippatu tavaṟu eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār.

paḻiya epirēyaruḷ oruvaṉ putalvaṉillāmal iṟantu viṭṭāl avaṉuṭiya vitaviyi avaṉuṭiya cakōtaraṉ, putalvaṉ piṟakkum variyil cērntirukkalām eṉṉum camayaviti uṇṭu. ivvaḻakkam niyōkam eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ pañcāppil naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. maṅkōli yar, cipīriyar mutaliya makkaḷiṭiyēyum ivvaḻakkam iruntu varukiṉ ṟatu. iv vaḻakkattit taḻuvip pōlum ulūtar, āṇmi (Impotent) aṟṟavaṉi maṇantavarkaḷil itu maṉṉikkattakkatākaviruntatu. orutti kaṇavaṉiṉ cakōtaraṉ allatu avaṉuṭiya kiṭṭiya uṟaviṉaṉic cēralām eṉac camayac caṭṭam koṇṭu vantār. āṇmiyillāta oruvaṉ taṉatu maṉiviyuṭaṉ ayal vīṭṭukkāraṉiṭañceṉṟu taṉatu maṉiviyiṉ kātal viruppatti niṟivēṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉak kēṭka vēṇṭum. avaṉ ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭāviṭṭāl avaṉ taṉatu maṉiviyi naṉṟāka uṭutti kiṭṭiya cantikkuc cella vēṇṭum. aṅku avaḷ taṉatu kaṇavaṉukkup piḷḷiyip peṟṟu aḷippataṟku utaviyāyuḷḷa oru kātalaṉik kaṇṭupiṭikkak kūṭum eṉap paḻiya jermaṉ āciriyar oruvar ak kālattil jermaṉiyil irunta vaḻakkattik kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār. ulūtar muṉṉirunta ivvitiyip piṉpaṟṟit taṉatu vitiyik kūṟiyirukkalām. peṇṇukkum āṇukkum cama urimiyiṉ poruṭṭu nērnta pōril ulūtariṉ karuttup peṇkaḷukkuc cārpākavuḷḷatu.

patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭuppakuti peṇkaḷ viḻippaṭinta kālamākum. appoḻutu alaṅkāram (fashion) eṉṉum eḻucci peṇka ḷiṭiyē eḻuntatu. mika alaṅkāriyākiya peṇ taṉṉi alaṅkārañ ceytu koḷvataṟku nāḷil ēḻumaṇi nēram celaviṭṭāḷ. peṇkaḷ iyaṟkiyākak kavarccikkuriya aḻaku vāyntavarkaḷāyirāviṭṭālum āṭavaṉik kavaruvataṟku allatu camūkattil matippaṭivataṟkuk kalvi kaṟkiṉṟārkaḷ. muṉ kūṟappaṭṭa kirēkka parattiyar kūṭṭattiṉarālum im muṟi kiyāḷappaṭṭatu. patiṉāṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil naṭuppakutiyiliruntu ittāliyil kalvi peṇkaḷiṉ cītaṉamākak karutappaṭṭatu.

peṇkaḷiṉ viṭutali ciṟitu ciṟitāka vaḷarntu koṇṭu vantatu. ataṉi evvakiyilum āṭavarāl taṭukka muṭiyavilli. iṭiyiṭiyē peṇkaḷiṉ viṭutali campantamāṉa kiḷarccikaḷ, varalāṟṟil iṭam peṟṟa palarāl naṭattappaṭṭu vantaṉa. poruḷātāra araciyal kāraṇaṅkaḷāl peṇkaḷiṉ viṭutali pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭil ēṟpaṭṭatu. ataṉāl peṇkaḷ vāṇikattuṟiyil āṇkaḷi otta urimi aṭintārkaḷ. toṭakkattil ellā makkaḷiṭiyum peṇkaḷ camūkattukkuriya virumpat takāta ellāk kaṭiṉa vēlikaḷiyum puriyumpaṭi kaṭṭāyappaṭuttappaṭ ṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ nūl nūṟṟal, necavu neytal, tittal, cavukkārañ ceytal pōṉṟa toḻilkaḷiyum ceytārkaḷ. poruḷātāra nerukkaṭi yiṉāl avarkaḷ ippoḻutu peṇkaḷukku uriyavaṉavallāta vēli kaḷiyum puriyalāṉārkaḷ. ivi nilimiyi māṟutal aṭiyacceytaṉa. ippoḻutu peṇkaḷ, āṇkaḷukku māttiram viṭappaṭṭirunta kit toḻil, vāṇikam mutaliyavikaḷiyum ceytu ūtiyam aṭintārkaḷ. iṅkilāntilē patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil vāḻkkip pōrāṭṭattiṉ nerukkaṭiyiṉāl peṇkaḷiṉ viṭutalip pōr aṉukūlamāka naṭipeṟṟatu. nūl nūṟkavum, necavu ceyyavum, āṭikku accaṭikkavum iyantirat toḻiṟcālikaḷil oru ilaṭcam peṇkaḷum eṇpatiṉāyiram ciṟuvarkaḷum vēli ceytārkaḷ, ippeṇkaḷum ciṟuvarkaḷum mikavum mōcamāṉa vāḻkki naṭatti vantārkaḷ.

1907il jermaṉiyil māttiramē 2,10,000 peṇkaḷ toḻiṟcāli kaḷil vēli ceytārkaḷ. ivarkaḷil nūṟṟukkup patiṉmūṉṟu vītattiṉar maṇamāṉavarkaḷ. āṇkaḷilum pārkkap peṇkaḷiṉ vēli tiṟami yaṟṟatākaviruntamiyāl peṇkaḷiṉ kūli kuṟivāyiruntatu. ōrōriṭaṅkaḷil itaṟku māṟākavum iruntu vantatu. amerikkāvil itu ivvāṟu iruntatu. amerikkāvilē meyiṉ (Maine) pōṉṟa toḻiṟ cālikaḷ mikunta paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ “peṇkaḷiṉ paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ” eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṅku āṇkaḷ camiyal, tuṇitōyttal, kuḻanti kaḷik kavaṉittal pōṉṟa vīṭṭu vēlikaḷip pārppārkaḷ. maṉivi mār toḻiṟcālikaḷil āṭavarkaḷip pārkkilum atika kūli peṟ ṟārkaḷ. ātalāl āṭavar peṇkaḷ ceyyum vīṭṭu vēlikaḷic ceytu vantārkaḷ. amerikkāvilē muṟpōkkil uyarntu ceṉṟa peṇkaḷ oru taṉippaṭṭa peṇ āṭciyi naṭattukiṟārkaḷ. itu peṇkaḷ kuṟivāyirunta muṟkālattil peṇkaḷ āṭavarit tamatu eṇṇattiṉ paṭi naṭattiya vaḻakkattiṉ uyirppēyākum. airōppiya makkaḷukku in nilimi iyaṟkikku māṟupaṭṭatupōl kāṇappaṭalām, iṉṟum amerikkāvil āṇkaḷiviṭap peṇkaḷ kuṟivāyiruppatāl in nilimi aṅku kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. aṅku nāṉku kōṭi peṇkaḷum nāṉku kōṭiyē nāṟpatu ilaṭcam āṇkaḷum uḷḷārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ poruḷātāra viṭutaliyiṉāl iṉṟu ellāt tuṟikaḷilum āṇkaḷi otta cama urimi aṭintuḷḷārkaḷ. ellā nākarika cātiyārum peṇ kaḷum āṇkaḷum orē urimi uḷḷavarkaḷ eṉpati ēṟṟuk koḷ kiṉṟaṉar. iṉṟu viṭutali aṭinta peṇkaḷiṉ oḻukka nili, iraṇ ṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ kēṭō eṉṉum urōmāṉiyar kūṟiyati oppa āṭavarukkup payam viḷippatāyirukkiṉṟatu. iṉṟu nām atē vaḻikkuttāṉ ceṉṟu koṇṭirukkiṟōm. nām kēṭō kūṟiyati otta niliyi aṭivataṟkuc ciṟitukālam cellalām eṉa oru cīrtirutta vāti kūṟiyuḷḷār.

tirumaṇam (mēṟkut tēcaṅkaḷil)
tirumaṇam kuṭumpa nikaḻcciyilum pārkkac camūka nikaḻcciyākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. tirumaṇattiṉāl maṟṟoru putiya kuṭumpam uṇṭā kiṉṟatu. āṭavaṉ oruvaṉatum, peṇ oruvaḷatum vāḻnāḷil tirumaṇam mikac ciṟappuṭiyatātaliṉ atu mukkiyattuvamuṭiyatākak koḷḷappaṭu kiṉṟatu. muṟkālattil intiya āṭavar tāmē tamatu maṉiviyarit terintukoṇṭaṉar. piṟkālattil icceyal peṟṟōriṉ kaṭamiyāka māṟiviṭṭatu. airōppiya nāṭukaḷil peṟṟōr tamatu putalvariyum putalviyiyum tāmākavē tamatu maṉiviyariyum kaṇavariyum terintu koḷḷa aṉumatikkiṉṟaṉar. āṇ peṇṇiyō, peṇ āṇiyō terivataṉ muṉ iruvarum cilakālam paḻakukiṟārkaḷ. itu āṭavaṉāl tāṉ ārampikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. vikṭōriyā irāṇi kālattil, peṇṇiṉ tanti taṉatu cammatattit terivittālaṉṟi āṭavaṉ avaḷōṭu paḻakutal muṭiyātu. peṇ “ām” eṉṟu taṉatu tirumaṇa viruppattit terivittatum terivu muṭivaṭikiṉṟatu. itu ‘vārttip pāṭu’ (Proposal) eṉṉum mariyātiyāṉa peyarip peṟukiṉṟatu. peṇṇiṉ tanti paḻaku vataṟku aṉumatittal, peṇ avaṉi ēṟṟukkoḷḷutal vēṇṭum eṉṉum kaṭṭuppāṭu ākamāṭṭātu. iṉṟu iḷiñaṉ oruvaṉ taṉakku viruppamāṉa iḷam peṇ oruttiyōṭu pēcip paḻakuvataṟku avaḷ tantiyiṭamiruntu aṉumati peṟavēṇṭiyatilli. cila kālattiṟku muṉ oruvaṉ peṇṇōṭu paḻaka vēṇṭumāṉāl takka oruvar avaṉi avaḷukku aṟimukappaṭutta vēṇṭum; allāta iḷiñaṉōṭu peṇ pēcamāṭṭāḷ. iṉṟu ivvāṟu evarum aṟimukappaṭutta vēṇṭiya tēvi illi. peṇṇum āṇum virumpiṉāl pēcik koḷḷalām. avarkaḷit taṭippaṭuttuvār yārum illi. iṉṟu peṇkaḷ vayatu mutirnta peṇkaḷiṉ tuṇi iṉṟi veḷiyē celkiṟārkaḷ. muṟkālat til ivvāṟu nikaḻavilli. ivarkaḷi ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum taṉimiyil cantikkalām. naṭaṉamāṭum maṇṭapattil allatu ciṟṟuṇṭic cāliyil irukkum peṇṇiṭam ceṉṟu avaḷuṭaṉ irukkum peṇ allatu āṇukkuk kuṉintu vaṇakkañ ceytapiṉ avaḷi naṭittuk kaḷiyāṭṭam āṭuvataṟku aḻippatu vaḻakkattukku māṟāṉa ceyalākak karutappaṭa māṭṭātu. iṉṟum āṇip peṇṇukku aṟimukappaṭutta vēṇṭum eṉṉum koḷkiyuṭiyavarkaḷ irukkiṟārkaḷ; āṉāl avarkaḷ mikac cilarē. āṭavaṉ aḻakum mariyātiyum uṭiyavaṉākak kāṇappaṭṭāl peṇ aṟimukappaṭuttuvatit tēvippaṭamāṭṭāḷ. paḻiya nāḷil paḻakutal eṉpatu āṇip poṟuttiruntatu. peṇ orupōtum taṉatu eṇṇatti mutalil aṟivippatilli.

vikṭōriyā kālattil peṇkaḷ peritum veḷiyē cella villi. ākavē avarkaḷ āṭavarōṭu atikam paḻakavilli. ippoḻutu peṇkaḷukkut tām aṟiya vēṇṭiya ellārip paṟṟiyum, ellāk karumaṅkaḷip paṟṟiyum teriyum. ākavē avarkaḷukku āṭavar vakuppiṉarip paṟṟiya mayakkam yātum illi. avarkaḷ vālipariṉ tēṉ pōṉṟu iṉippāṉa pēccukkaḷāl eṭupaṭamāṭṭārkaḷ; pakaṭṭākat tōṉṟukiṉṟavikaḷil avarkaḷ nampikki koḷvatum illi. ākavē avarkaḷi makiḻvippatu kaṭiṉam. iṉṟiya vālipaṉukkup peṇṇōṭu paḻakuvatu villaṅkamākat tōṉṟum. paḻiya pōkkil avaḷiṉ tirumaṇa viruppattik kēṭṭāl avaḷ uṭaṉē kekkaṭṭam iṭṭuc cirippāḷ. ikkālap peṇṇiṭattil, pēcum aṟivu atikam uṇṭu; avaḷ akaṉṟa aṟivum, kaṭamikaḷi niṟivēṟṟuvatil uṟuti yum uṭiyavaḷ. āviyāka māṟiviṭakkūṭiya kātaliṉālum pakaṭṭiṉālum makiḻcciyōṭu vāḻamuṭiyāteṉṟu avaḷukkut teriyum. kuṟikkappaṭṭa oruvaṉi maṇappatāl makiḻcciyum, vāḻkkiyil vāyppukkaḷum irukkumeṉak kaṇṭāl avaḷ tirumaṇattukku icikiṉṟāḷ. iṉṟu peṇ orutti kūṟuvatu eṉṉaveṉṟāl, “uṉṉik kātalikkum oruvaṉi aṉṟu, nī kātalikkum oruvaṉi eṉpatākum.

ikkālattil āṭavaṉ oruvaṉ taṉatu kātali aṟivikkum poruṭṭu marattiṉ kīḻ oruttiyiṉ kiyip piṭippatu allatu pulliṉ mītu muḻaṅkāl paṭivatu ippoḻutu illi; eṉṉi maṇañ ceykiṉṟāyā eṉṟu veḷippaṭiyākavum nāṇamiṉṟiyum kēṭkalām. “ceyvēṉ” eṉṟu nāṇamiṉṟiyum maṉa aciviṉṟiyum peṇ kūṟalām. tirumaṇat tukku oruttiyi icivupaṭuttuvataṟku avaḷic cuṟṟit tirivatākiya paḻiya pōkku virivil māṟupaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. kātal, pakaṭṭu eṉpaṉa tamatu kavarcciyi iḻantuviṭṭaṉa eṉṟāvatu kuṭumpa cēmattukkākavum kātal nukarccikkākavum tirumaṇaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭavilli eṉṟāvatu karututal ākātu. kātaliṉāl pala tirumaṇaṅkaḷ nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. mēṟkut tēcat tirumaṇaṅkaḷit tōlvi eṉṟu karutiviṭutal ākātu. makiḻcci yaḷiyāta tirumaṇattukku maruntu tirumaṇa nīkkam.
oru āṭavaṉum peṇṇum maṇam muṭikka icintatum peṇ taṉatu iṭatuki mōtira viralil kalpatitta mōtiramoṉṟi aṇintu koḷkiṉṟāḷ. immōtiram avaḷi maṇañ ceyyappōkum āṭava ṉāl aḷikkappaṭṭatu. peṟṟōriṉ viruppam kēṭkappaṭukiṉṟatu. matip pukkuriya kuṭumpaṅkaḷil orunāḷ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. aṉṟu uṟaviṉariṉ muṉṉāl tirumaṇam niccayamāṉa ceyti kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. aṉṟu viruntum naṭaṉamum uṇṭu.

tirumaṇattukku nāḷ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. mēl nāṭṭavar nāḷ pārppatilli. avarkaḷukku oru nāḷip pōlavē ellā nāṭkaḷum nallavi. tirumaṇam kāli eṭṭu allatu oṉpatu maṇikkum māli mūṉṟu maṇikkum iṭiyil nikaḻum. mūṉṟu maṇikkup piṉ tirumaṇam nikaḻvatilli. mēl nāṭukaḷil tirumaṇap pativu niliyaṅkaḷil tirumaṇam ceytu koḷḷalām. maṇamakaṉum maṇamakaḷum tirumaṇap pativu niliyattukku iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu cāṭcikaḷuṭaṉ celvārkaḷ. pativu ceypavar avarkaḷ kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyarāka vāḻa virumpukiṉṟaṉarā eṉakkēṭpār. ‘ām’ eṉṟu maṟumoḻi varumāyiṉ tirumaṇam pativu ceytu koḷḷappaṭum. maṇamakaṉ, maṇamakaḷ eṉpōr avarkaḷtāṉ eṉṟu attāṭcip paṭuttuvataṟkum tirumaṇattukkuc cāṭci aḷippataṟku māka uṭaṉ ceṉṟavarkaḷ utaviyaḷippar. pativukārar putiya kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyarukku tirumaṇa attāṭcic cīṭṭuk koṭuppār. muṉ oru muṟi kaliyāṇa nīkkam ceytavarkaḷum ēḻikaḷum perumpālum ivvāṟu tirumaṇatti muṭittukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar.

mēl nāṭṭil tirumaṇak koṇṭāṭṭam curukkamāṉatu. atu cila maṇi nēramē naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. mikakkiṭṭiya naṇparum cuṟṟattavarkaḷu mallāta piṟar maṇamakaḷ allatu maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭil taṅkiyirukkac celva tilli. mutalnāḷ māli allatu tirumaṇattaṉṟu kāliyil maṇa makaṉ tirumaṇam naṭakkum iṭattukku varuvāṉ; āṉāl avaṉ maṇamaka ḷiṉ vīṭṭil taṅkamāṭṭāṉ. maṇamakaṉ taṉatu cuṟṟattavar kaḷōṭum naṇparkaḷōṭum kōyilukkuc celvāṉ. avaṉ aṅku maṇamakaḷiṉ varavi etirpārttiruppāṉ. maṇamakaḷ taṉatu tanti cakōtarar mutaliya varkaḷōṭu avviṭattukku varuvāḷ. maṇamakaṉ avaḷik kōyil vāyilil etirkoṇṭu aḻittuk koṇṭu palipīṭattukkuc celvāṉ. maṇamakaḷiṉ pakkattār iṭappakkattilum maṇamakaḷiṉ pakkattār valappakkattilum iruppārkaḷ. maṇamakaḷ maṇamakaṉukku iṭappakkattil muḻaṅkāl paṭintu niṟpāḷ. iruvarum varumpōtu kōyiluḷḷa ellārum eḻuntu niṟpārkaḷ. tirumaṇak kiriyi nīṇṭataṉṟu, avarkaḷ kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyarāka virumpukiṟārkaḷō eṉṟu kuru kēṭkiṟār. ellōrum avarkaḷ kūṟum maṟumoḻiyi āvalōṭu etirpārkkiṟārkaḷ. iru
varum “nāṉ virumpukiṟēṉ” eṉṟu taṉittaṉiyē kūṟuvārkaḷ. uṭaṉē maṇamakaṉ tirumaṇa mōtirattip peṇṇiṉ iṭatu viralil aṇikiṉṟāṉ. intiya makkaḷ tāli tarippatu pōṉṟatu avarkaḷ mōtiram tarippatu. piṉpu kuruvāl tirumaṇap pativuc cīṭṭu ācīrvātattuṭaṉ avarkaḷ kiyil aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu. maṇamakaṉum maṇamakaḷum kōyilukku veḷiyē varutalum avarkaḷ makiḻcci āravārattuṭaṉ vara vēṟkappaṭuvārkaḷ. piṉpu kaliyāṇak kūṭṭam maṇamakaḷiṉ vīṭṭukkuc cellum. kaliyāṇa viruntu mika makiḻvukkuriyatu. mika nērttiyāṉavum urukiyuḷḷavumāṉa uṇavup poruḷkaḷ matu vakiyum parimāṟappaṭukiṉ ṟaṉa. “tirumaṇa appam” (Wedding Cake) avikaḷuḷ mukkiyamuṭiyatu. maṇamakaḷ appatti veṭṭuvāḷ; maṇamakaṉ avaḷiṭam kattiyi eṭuttuk koṭuppāṉ. appam viruntiṉarukkup parimāṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. viruntiṉar maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷ eṉṉum iruvariṉ uṭal nalaṉ naṉṟā yirukka vēṇṭumeṉa āci kūṟi ataṉi uṇkiṉṟaṉar. viruntukku varāta naṇparkaḷukkum cuṟṟattavarkaḷukkum appattuṇṭukaḷ aṉuppap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. viruntiṉar matu vakikaḷik kuṭikkum pōtum maṇamakaḷa tum maṇamakaṉatum uṭalnalaṉ naṉṟāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu col vārkaḷ. kuṭivakikaḷ aruntumpōtu ciṟiya coṟpoḻivukaḷum naṭattap paṭum. viruntu muṭintatum putiya kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum “haṉimūṉu”kkup puṟappaṭuvar. ellōrum avarkaḷip pārttu avarkaḷukku ulakil ellā makiḻcciyum nalaṉumuṇṭāka vēṇṭumeṉṟu āci kūṟuvar.

“haṉimūṉ” mōṭṭār puṟappaṭṭuc celkiṉṟatu. makiḻccic cirippum āravāramum ākāyattil ōciyum eḻukiṉṟaṉa. mōṭṭāriṉ piṉpuṟattil paḻiya cappāttum atiṭṭam tarum kutiri ilāṭamum toṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivi mikap paḻaṅkālac caṅkatikaḷ.

putiya kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyar iyalpuṭiyavarkaḷāyiṉ haṉimūṉi ayalnāṭukaḷil koṇṭāṭuvar. vaṟiyavar tamatu haṉimūṉit tamatu ūrilē allatu tamatu vīṭṭilē koṇṭāṭuvar. haṉimūṉ mika makiḻc cikkum iṉpattukkum uriyateṉak koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟatu. uṇmiyil oruvari oruvar kātalikkum maṇamakkaḷukku atu ivvāṟē uḷḷatu. cila camaṅkaḷil haṉimūṉ “kaṉiyāta mūṉāka” (Blue Moon) māṟi viṭuvatu muṇṭu. tirumaṇac celavu perumpālum maṇamakaṉip poṟuttatu. maṇamakaṉukku koṭukkum kaliyāṇa vekumati, maṇamakaḷiṉ uṭi campantamāṉa ciṟiya celavukaḷē peṇ vīṭṭārip poṟuttatu.

iṉṟu peṇkaḷ vēṇṭuvatu
1939il kūṭiya peṇkaḷ capi1 oṉṟil intiyap peṇkaḷiṉ camūkanili evvāṟu iruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatik kuṟittu niṟi vēṟṟappaṭṭa tīrmāṉaṅkaḷiṉ curukkatti iṅkut tarukiṉṟōm.
1. peṇkaḷiṉ nili ellāvakiyilum āṇkaḷukkuc camamā yiruttal vēṇṭum.

2. peṇkaḷ eṉṉum kāraṇamāka avarkaḷukku evvaki vēli kaḷum maṟukkappaṭutal kūṭātu.

3. peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷōṭu camamāṉa camūkap poruḷātāra urimi kaḷip peṟṟu vāḻvataṟkut tirumaṇam taṭiyāyiruttal ākātu.

4. aracāṅkam peṇṇiyum āṇiyum cama urimiyuḷḷa aṅkattavarkaḷākak koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.

peṇkaḷukku vākkurimi vēṇṭum. āṭavarippōla avar kaḷum uttiyōkaṅkaḷ peṟṟu, āṭavarippōl ūtiyam aṭital vēṇṭum. vayatāṉa peṇkaḷum āṇkaḷum virumpiṉāl maṇantu koḷvataṟkuc cāti matam eṉṉum kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷ iruttal kūṭātu. peṇkaḷ paruvamaṭivataṉ muṉ maṇañ ceytukoḷvatic caṭṭam taṭiceytal vēṇṭum. orē kālattil oruvaṉ oruttiyiyum, orutti oruvaṉiyum māttiram maṇam ceytukoḷḷa vēṇṭum. kaliyāṇa nīkka urimi iruttal vēṇṭum. tirumaṇaṅkaḷ pativu ceytukoḷḷum nili yaṅkaḷ kirāmaṅkaḷtōṟum iruttal vēṇṭum. kaliyāṇa nīkkam iruvaril evar virumpiṉālum ceytu koḷḷalām. āṉāl piḷḷikaḷiṉ parāmarippu, pātukāppu eṉpavikaḷippaṟṟiya ēṟpāṭukaḷ oḻuṅku ceyyap paṭutal vēṇṭum. vitaviyāyiruppatu kaliyāṇattukku taṭiyātal kūṭātu. āṭavarippōlap peṇkaḷukkuc cottu urimiyum avaṟṟi avarkaḷ vēṇṭiyapaṭi ceyyum atikāramum vēṇṭum. ciṟuvarum ciṟumiyarum kalantu kalvi payilum kalavaṉ pāṭacālikaḷ vēṇṭum.

ikkālat tāy
muṟkālattil tāyiṉ kaṭami piḷḷikaḷip peṟuvatu māttiram eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭṭatu. avaḷ peṟum piḷḷikaḷ peṇkaḷākavum, maṉitarākavum evvakiyilō vaḷarntu viṭuvārkaḷ eṉṉum eṇṇam avarkaḷiṭam iruntatu. taṅkaḷ kuḻantikaḷi evvāṟu uruppaṭutta vēṇṭumeṉpatu avarkaḷ mūḷiyiṟpaṭavilli. ikkālat tāy paḻaṅ kālat tāyilum pārkkac camūkap paḻakkamuṭiyavaḷ. ippoḻutu avaḷ tāṉ ulaka karumaṅkaḷil kalantukoḷḷātu taṉiyēyirukkalāmeṉa uṇaravilli. avaḷ taṉṉic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa ulaka naṭavaṭikkikaḷil kalantu koḷkiṟāḷ. piṟantatu mutal kuḻanti evvāṟu payiṟṟappaṭukiṉ ṟatō avvāṟākavē ataṉ piṟkāla vāḻkkiyum uruppaṭukiṉṟatu. tāy māriyum kuḻanti vaḷarppip paṟṟiyum eḻutappaṭṭa pala puttakaṅkaḷi ikkālat tāy paṭikkiṟāḷ. tāymārkaḷiyum piḷḷikaḷiyum paṟṟiya pala ceytikaḷ tiṉamum pēcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iv viṣayaṅkaḷ muṟkālat tāymārukku iṭakkarākat tōṉṟakkūṭiyaṉa. ivvakiyāṉa pēccukkaḷ tūymiyaṟṟaṉaveṉṟu iṉṟu karutappaṭuvatilli. iṉṟiya nilimi tāyiṉ kaṭami piḷḷikaḷip peṟuvatu eṉṟa aḷavil irātu avaḷ kaṭami maṉita camūkattiyē nalvaḻiyil paḻakki, varuṅkāla ulakattukku nampikki aḷippatu eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.



peṇkaḷ ulakam
muṉṉuri
ivvulakil nērpakutiyiṉar peṇkaḷ. ulaka varalāṟṟil ivarkaḷ pāti iṭam peṟutal vēṇṭum. āṉāl ulaka varalāṟu eṉpatu āṭavariṉ varalā ṟākavē amintuḷḷatu. iṭiyiṭiyē ovvoru kālattu viḷaṅkiya nāṭṭiyakkāriyō, araciyō, oru paṭiyit tāṅkic ceṉṟa oruttiyō pōṉṟa peṇkaḷ cilariṉ varalāṟukaḷē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. peṇkaḷē ivvulakil nākarikattit tōṟṟuvit tārkaḷ eṉpati ārāycciyāḷar orutaliyāka oppukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar1. “toṭṭilāṭṭuṅkiyē aracāḷukiṉṟatu”2, “nākarikameṉpatu nalla peṇ kaḷiṉ ūkkutalē”,3 eṉa aṟivāḷikaḷ pukaṉṟiruk kiṉṟaṉar. ivvuṇmikaḷi aṟiyātum, āṭavari oppap peṇkaḷum cutantiramuṭiyavarkaḷ eṉpati maṟantum, peṇ camūttiṉarukku āṇ camūkattiṉar iḻittu varum aṭakku muṟikaḷ nītiyaṟṟaṉa eṉpati viḷakki in nūl eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. varalāṟṟu muṟiyākap peṇkaḷiṉ uyarvi viḷakkum ceytikaḷ palavaṟṟi in nūliṟ kāṇalām.
na.ci.kantiyā






peṇkaḷ perumi
tōṟṟuvāy
maṉita vakuppukkuḷ māttiramaṉṟu, maram, ceṭi, paṟavi, vilaṅkukaḷ mutaliya aḵṟiṇip poruḷkaḷuḷḷum peṇ uyirkaḷē mikappaya ṉuṭiyaṉa. ōr āṇ paṉiyiyum, ōr peṇpaṉiyiyum eṭuttuk koḷvōm. ivaṟṟuḷ peṇ paṉiyē mikka payaṉuṭiyateṉpatu evarukkum teḷḷitiṟ pulappaṭum. vilaṅku, paṟavikaḷuḷ āṭu, kōḻi eṉpavikaḷiṉ āṇ peṇ iṉaṅkaḷiyum eṭuttu nōkki avi kaḷuḷ evi payaṉuṭiyaṉa eṉa aṟintu koḷḷuṅkaḷ. uyirkaḷil peṇṇiṉaṅkaḷē ellā vakiyilum ciṟantu viḷaṅkum iyaṟkip paṇpuṭiyaṉavāyiṉum, makkaḷ camūkattil ivviti māṟupaṭṭatu pōla namakkut tōṉṟukiṟatu. makkaḷuḷ peṇkaḷē āṇkaḷiviṭa uyarnta taṉmiyuṭiyavarkaḷāyiṉ avarkaḷ iṉṟu āṇkaḷukku aṭaṅki naṭakkavēṇṭiya nilimi evvāṟu uṇṭāyiṟṟu? avarkaḷ iyaṟki vitikku amiya āṇkaḷiviṭac ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷā? avarkaḷ āṇkaḷukku aṭaṅki naṭakkum kaṭṭāyam evvāṟu nērntatu? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa karuttukkaḷi ārāyntu aṟiyiṉ nām peṇ camūkattiṉariṉ varalāṟṟiyē aṟintavarkaḷ āvōm. peṇ camūkattiṉariṉ uṇmi varalāṟṟiṉi aṟiyātiruppataṉālēyē, nām peṇkaḷit tāḻnta varkaḷākak karutukiṉṟōm. uṇmiyil peṇkaḷē āṭavariviṭa ellā vakiyilum matikkattakkavarkaḷāvar. avarkaḷiṉ perumiyi uṇarntu nām avarkaḷukku mutaṉmi aḷiyāta aḷavil namatu camūkam muṉṉēṟa vaki iṉṟākum. iviyum ivipōṉṟa karuttukkaḷiyum varalāṟṟōṭum iyaṟkiyōṭum toṭarpuppaṭutti viḷakkuvatu innūliṉ nōkkamākum.
paṭippukkaḷ
ivvulakil uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇi eṉṉum uyirp paṭippukkaḷ ellām āṇ peṇ eṉṉum iruvakippaṭum. āṇ peṇ cērkki yiṉāl ovvoru āṇum peṇṇum tampōṉṟa uyirkaḷit tōṟṟuvittup perukkukiṉṟaṉa. tampōṉṟa uyirkaḷit tōṟṟuvikkum vaṉmiyi iḻakkum pōtu uyirkaḷ maṭintu pōkiṉṟaṉa. ellā uyirkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟikaḷiyum nām ūṉṟi ārāyumiṭattu, uyirp paṭippukkaḷiṉ nōkkam, tam pōṉṟa uyirkaḷit tōṟṟuvittu ulakil parappuvatē eṉṟu namakku naṉku tōṉṟum. itaṟkākavē avi vāḻkiṉṟaṉa.
vāḻkkip pōr
marañ ceṭi puḻukkaḷ mutal maṉitaṉ variyiluḷḷa ellā uyir kaḷum vāḻkkiyiṉ poruṭṭu ivvulakil iṭiviṭātu, pōrāṭik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. vāḻkkip pōrāṭṭameṉpatu tām nalluṇavu peṟutalōṭu tammip piṟa uyirkaḷ ūṟu viḷiyātapaṭiyum, koṉṟu tiṉṟuviṭātapaṭiyum pātukāttuk koḷḷutal. ivvāḻkkip pōrāṭ ṭattil mikac cuṟucuṟuppum vaṉmiyum uṭiyaṉavē nilipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟavi maṭintu pōkiṉṟaṉa.
takutiyuṭiyaṉa nilipeṟutal
ellā uyirkaḷiṭattum tampōṉṟa cantatikaḷi uṇṭu paṇṇum iyaṟki vaṉmi uṇṭu. vāḻkkip pōrāṭṭattil takutipeṟṟu nili peṟukiṉṟa oru peṇ taṉṉippōṉṟa takutiyuṭiya cantatiyip perukkum poruṭṭu oru āṇit terintukoḷkiṟāḷ. ivvāṟu 
oru peṇ āṇit terintu koḷvatu iyaṟkit terivu eṉappaṭum. ivvāṟu takutiyuṭiya oru āṇum oru peṇṇum cērntu tōṟṟu vikkum cantatikaḷ, ivvulakil vāḻkkip pōrāṭṭattil veṟṟi yaṭintu nīṇṭakālam vāḻntu pala cantatikaḷit tōṟṟuvikkum vaṉmi yuṭiyaṉavākiṉṟaṉa.
iyaṟkit terivu
oru peṇ oru āṇit terintu koḷvatē iyaṟki viti. āṇ, peṇṇit terintu koḷvatu iyaṟki vitiyaṉṟu. peṇ vilaṅku oṉṟu karukkoḷḷum kālattil pala āṇ vilaṅkukaḷ ataṉi aṭiya muntukiṉṟaṉa. appoḻutu avaṟṟuḷ pōṭṭi uṇṭākip pōr mūḷkiṉṟatu. avaṟṟuḷ iṟutiyāka veṟṟiyaṭiyum vīramuṭiya āṇ vilaṅkiyē peṇ vilaṅku aṭikiṉṟatu. peṇ vilaṅku pōriṟ kalantu koḷvatilli. atu, āṇkaḷukkiṭiyil naṭakkum pōri ayalē niṉṟu nōkkum. tārviṉ eṉṉum iyaṟki nūṟpulavar oru peṇ māṉukkākap pala nāṭkaḷ pōrāṭiya pala kalimāṉkaḷip paṟṟi yum, iṟutiyil veṟṟiyaṭinta kalimāṉip peṇmāṉ aṭintatip paṟṟiyum kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār. peṇ vilaṅkukaḷ karukkoḷḷum paruvakālattil, āṇ nāvippūṉi, puṉuku pūṉi eṉṉum vilaṅkukaḷ peṇ iṉaṅkaḷik kavarum poruṭṭu naṟumaṇattik kakkukiṉṟaṉa. pēṭumuṭṭiyiṭṭuk kuñcu porikkum kālattil cila āṇ paṟavikaḷiṉ niṟam mikavum miṉumiṉuppum aḻakum aṭikiṉṟaṉa. ivi peṇṇiṉaṅ kaḷukku āṇ iṉaṅkaḷ kavarcci aḷikkumpaṭi avikaḷukku iyaṟki aḷittuḷḷa cila paṇpukaḷākum.

cuyamvaram
muṟkālattil peṇkaḷum ivvāṟē ceytārkaḷ. peṇ oruvaḷ maṇapparuvam aṭinta pōtu, avaḷ māliyiṭṭu oruvaṉik kaṇava ṉākat teriyavirukkum ceyti, iḷam āṭavarukku aṟivikkappaṭṭatu. kuṟikkappaṭṭa nāḷil avarkaḷ peṇ vīṭṭil kūṭiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷuḷ, yātum oru vīracceyal kāṇpippavaṉi avaḷ māliyiṭṭut terintu koṇṭāḷ. eppoḻutum āṭavar, peṇkaḷ tammi vīrareṉṟu matikkak kūṭiya ceyalkaḷiyē ceyya virumpukiṉṟaṉar. muṟkālap pōrkaḷ perum pālum peṇkaḷ kāraṇamāka nērntaṉa eṉappaṭutal mikavum karuttiṟ koḷḷattakkatu. iṉṟu ulakil naṭakkum periya kuṟṟaṅkaḷ pala, āṭavar peṇkaḷi makiḻvippatu kāraṇamākac ceyyappaṭuvaṉavēyākum.

vilaṅkukaḷiṉ vāḻkki
nīṇṭakālam aṭikāttuk kuñcu porikkum paṟavikaḷallāta piṟa uyirkaḷil āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum cōṭikaḷāka vāḻvatilli. peṇkaḷ taṉiyē oru kūṭṭamākavum, āṇkaḷ taṉiyē maṟṟoru kūṭṭa mākavum vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. peṇ vilaṅkukaḷ ‘karukkoḷḷum’ kālattil māttiram peṇ vilaṅkukaḷiṭiyē āṇ vilaṅkukaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. karuk koṇṭa piṉ peṇ vilaṅkukaḷ orupōtum āṇ vilaṅkukaḷit tammi aṇuka aṉumatippatilli.

ātikāla makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki
makkaḷ vilaṅkiṉattic cērntavarkaḷē. ākavē, avarkaḷum ātikāla vāḻkkiyil vilaṅkukaḷip pōlavē iruntārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ oru kūṭṭamāka vāḻntārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ karukkoḷḷum kālaṅ kaḷil iyaṟkitterivu ceytu koṇṭārkaḷ. vilaṅkukaḷ, paṟavikaḷ mutaliyavikaḷukkup paruvakālaṅkaḷiṉ veppataṭpa nili, atika uṇavu kiṭittal pōṉṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷiṉāl āṇṭiṉ ciṟcila kālaṅ kaḷilēyē karukkoḷḷum vēṭki tōṉṟum. ivvitiyē makkaḷuk kum uṇṭāyiruntatu. peṇkaḷ kūṭṭamāka ōriṭattil taṅkuvōrākavum, āṇkaḷ vēṭṭiyāṭuvōrākavum iruntaṉar.



tāyāṭci
muṟkālattil kuḻantikaḷ tāy vaḻiyākavē aṟiyappaṭṭārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ ōriṭattil iruntamiyāl paḻamaraṅkaḷi vittu uṇṭākki yum, tāṉiyaṅkaḷip payiriṭṭum uṇavupporuḷkaḷi uṇṭākki ṉārkaḷ. *peṇkaḷ kūṭṭattukku vayatil mūtta tāyē taliviyākavirun tāḷ. maṟṟavarkaḷ avaḷukku aṭaṅki naṭantārkaḷ. ivvāṟu ulakil tāyāṭci uṇṭākiyiruntatu. cottu peṇkaḷukkē uriyatāyiruntatu. ak kālattil tantikkup piḷḷikaḷiṭattil urimi uṇṭākavilli.

contak kūṭṭattil maṇam illi
oru kūṭṭattil tōṉṟiya peṇkaḷum āṇkaḷum cakōtara cakōtarikaḷākak karutappaṭṭaṉar. oru kūṭṭattiluḷḷa peṇkaḷ iṉṉoru tāy vaṭṭattiluḷḷa āṇkaḷic cērntu piḷḷikaḷip peṟṟārkaḷ. ulakil ellāp pākattilum contak kūṭṭattil peṇ koḷḷutal kūṭātu eṉṉum caṭṭam iruntu vantatu. iṉṟum piṟpōk kuḷḷa makkaḷiṭiyē iccaṭṭam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. intiya nāṭṭilum pala kūṭṭattiṉar tamatu contak kōttirattil peṇ koḷvatilli. iviyellām tāyāṭci uṇṭāyirunta kālattil tōṉṟiyirunta vaḻakkaṅkaḷ.

tāyk kaṭavuḷ
ulakil mikap paḻamiyākat tōṉṟiyatu tāy vaḻipāṭu. tāy ellā atikāramum uṭiyavaḷāka iruntāḷ. piḷḷikaḷ avaḷic cūḻa iruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kaṭavuḷi ellā atikāramumuṭiya, tāy vaṭivil vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ. ivvulakam muḻuvatilum tāykkaṭavuḷiṉ vaḻipāṭu orē vakiyākak kāṇappaṭṭatu. tāy kaṉṉi eṉappaṭṭāḷ. niliyāṉa maṇak kaṭṭuppāṭu illāmiyāl, tāy piḷḷikaḷip peṟṟa pōtum, kaṉṉi yākavē karutappaṭṭāḷ. ulaka muḻumiyilum tāyk kaṭavuḷ kaṉṉik kaṭavuḷākavē koḷḷappaṭṭatu.

nākarikam peṇkaḷiṭiyē tōṉṟi vaḷarntatu
peṇkaḷ uṇavup poruḷkaḷi viḷivittārkaḷ eṉa muṉ kūṟiṉōm. avarkaḷ ōriṭattil taṅkiyiruntapōtu, peṭṭi, kaṭakam, kūṭi muṭital pōṉṟa palavakik kittoḻilkaḷic ceytārkaḷ; paruttiyi uṇṭākkip pañciliruntu nūl nūṟṟu āṭi neytārkaḷ. avarkaḷ tām aṟinta poruḷkaḷik kuṟikka ovvōr olikkuṟikaḷi iṭṭu avikaḷit tam kuḻantikaḷukkum kaṟpittārkaḷ. ivvāṟu peṇkaḷiṭiyē payirttoḻil, kittoḻil mutaliyaviyum, moḻiyum tōṉṟi vaḷarcciyaṭintaṉa. āṭavar vēṭṭiyāṭuvōrāka vum, cila camayaṅkaḷil uṇavukkup peṇkaḷiyē etirpārppavarkaḷāka vum iruntaṉar. makkaḷ muṟparuvattil vilaṅkukaḷi oppa oruvarōṭu oruvar pōrāṭik koṇṭiruntārkaḷ. taṟkāppiṉ poruṭṭē perum pālum makkaḷ oṉṟu cērntu vāḻntārkaḷ. oru kūṭṭattiṉar iṉṉoru kūṭṭattiṉarit tākkumpōtu avarkaḷi etirttup pōrāṭutal pōṉṟa ceyalukku māttiram āṇkaḷ payaṉpaṭṭārkaḷ.

kuḻantikaḷukkut tāyē ciṟantavaḷ
kuḻantikaḷ utaviyaṟṟavarkaḷākap piṟakkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ tāmākat tamatu vāḻkkiyi naṭattat takutiyaṟṟavarkaḷāyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. appoḻutu tāyē kuḻantikaḷukkup pālūṭṭi vaḷarkkiṉṟāḷ. atu, veḷi ulakil ceṉṟu taṉatu vāḻkkiyit tāṉē naṭattum takuti peṟumvariyum tāyiṉ ātaravil irukkiṉṟatu. tāy, kuḻantikaḷiṭat til vikkum paṟṟu mikap puṉitamāṉatu. kuḻantikaḷiṭattil tāy vippa tākiya paṟṟuk kīḻ iṉa uyirkaḷ mutal, mēl iṉa uyirkaḷ variyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. cila iṉappaṟavikaḷ tamatu kuñcukaḷik kāppataṟ kākat tamatu uyiriyum viṭukiṉṟaṉa. vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷum appaṭiyē. tāy pālūṭṭi vaḷarkkum vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷ kuṭṭikaḷ mītu vikkum paṟṟu mika mēlāṉatu. kuraṅkukaḷiṭattilum maṉitariṭattum ippaṟṟu aḷavu kaṭantu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. kuraṅkukkuṭṭi iṟantu viṭṭāl, ataṉ tāy iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷukku ataṉi uṭaṉ koṇṭu cellum; mikak kavali yōṭu amitiyāka irukkum; uṇavu koḷḷamāṭṭātu.

tāyaṉpu
tāy kuḻantiyiṭattil vikkum aṉpu mēlāṉatu. tāy patil palaṉi etirpārātu kuḻantiyiṭattē aṉpu vikkiṉṟāḷ. atu makiḻvatip pārttu avaḷum makiḻkiṉṟāḷ; atu varuntuvatik kaṇṭu tāṉum varuntukiṉṟāḷ. patil palaṉi etirpārātu celuttum aṉpu kaṭavuḷ taṉmiyuṭiyatu. taṉṉalam karutātu ivvulakiṟ cilavaṟṟic ceykiṉṟavarkaḷip puṉitamuṭiyavarkaḷāka vittu ulakam pōṟṟukiṉ ṟatu. “taṉakkeṉa vāḻāp piṟarkkuriyāḷaṉ” eṉṉum uyarnta paṇpu kāraṇamākap puttar ulaka makkaḷāl pōṟṟappaṭṭār. kiṟittunātar, mukammatu napi, koṉpiyūcas, makāvīrar mutaliyavarkaḷit teyvaṅkaḷāka makkaḷ vaṇaṅkip pōṟṟuvatu avarkaḷ piṟarukkāka vāḻntamiyiṉālē yākum. iṉṟu kānti, makāttumā eṉap pōṟṟappaṭuvatum ik karuttup paṟṟiyē. ik koḷkip paṟṟiyē, ovvoru tāyum taṉ kuḻanti kaḷukkut teyvam āvāḷ. “aṉṉiyum pitāvum muṉṉaṟi teyvam” eṉṟatum ik karuttup paṟṟiyatēyākum. kaṭavuḷ ovvoru vīṭṭilum iruppataṟkup patil, tāyi aṅku aṉuppiyuḷḷār eṉa oru aṟiñar kūṟiyuḷḷār.

tanti aṉpu
paṟavi, vilaṅkukaḷ, tanti aṉpip paṟṟi aṟiyā. avi kaḷukkut tāyaṉpu oṉṟē uḷḷatu. kuḻanti vaḷarntu veḷiyulakatti aṟiya varukiṉṟatu. appoḻutu kuḻantikku vēṇṭiya piṟa utavikaḷit tanti aḷikkiṟāṉ. ataṉāl kuḻanti tantiyiṭattil aṉpu koḷkiṉṟatu. āyiṉum, atu tantiyiṭattil koḷḷumaṉpu tāyiṭattuk koṇṭuḷḷa aṉpilum aḷaviṟ kuṟivuṭiyatē.

peṇkaḷiṭattil meṉmik kuṇam
ellā āṇ iṉaṅkaḷum vaṉmik kuṇam uṭiyaṉavākavum peṇ iṉaṅkaḷ meṉmik kuṇam uṭiyaṉavākavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ciṅkam, puli pōṉṟa mūrkka kuṇamuṭiya vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷil peṇ iṉaṅ kaḷi viṭa āṇ iṉaṅkaḷē pōr ceyyum atika mūrkkak kuṇamuṭi yaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paṭippukkaḷuḷ makkaṭpaṭippu iṟuti yāṉatu eṉṟu ōr iṭattiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷōm. ātiyil makkaḷukku vilaṅ kiṉaṅkaḷip pōṉṟu oruvarōṭu oruvar etirttup pōrceyyum mūrkkak kuṇam uṭiyavarkaḷākavē iruntaṉar. makkaḷ tōṉṟi eṇṇillāta kālam ivvulakil vāḻntu varukiṉṟamiyāl avarkaḷiṉ vilaṅkuk kuṇaṅkaḷ paṭippaṭiyē taṇivu paṭṭup paṇpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avvāṟāyiṉum peṇkaḷi viṭa āṇkaḷē muraṭṭuttaṉam uṭiyavarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. āṇkaḷiṉ mūrkkak kuṇam taṇivupaṭṭup paṇpaṭitaṟkuk kāraṇarāyiruntavarkaḷ peṇkaḷēyāvar. peṇkaḷ tāymārākum pōtu, avarkaḷ uḷḷattil aṉpu tōṉṟip perukukiṉṟatu. tāymār kuḻanti kaḷukku aṉpōṭu pāl ūṭṭi vaḷarttaliṉ aṉpuk kuṇaṅkaḷ avar māṭṭum cuvaṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu talimuṟi talimuṟiyākat tāymār tamatu aṉpuk kuṇaṅkaḷip piḷḷikaḷ mūlam parapputaliṉ āṇkaḷum paṭippaṭiyē paḻiya muraṭṭut taṉmiyiṉiṉṟum nīṅki, aṉpu ceṟinta uḷḷattavarkaḷāka vantuḷḷārkaḷ. makkaḷ uḷḷattiṉ iyalpukaḷi naṉku ārāynta aṟiñarkaḷ peṇkaḷē āṭavaric cīrtiruttiyavar kaḷāvar eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. aṉpukku iruppiṭam peṇṇē. peṇ ṇiṭamiruntē aṉpu veḷḷam ūṟṟeṭuttu āṇ pālārkaḷiṭiyē yum paravuvatāyiṟṟu.

peṇkaḷ uyarvaṭintirunta pōtu nākarikam ōṅkiyiruntatu
iṉṟikku aiyāyiram allatu āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ, intiyā, mēṟku āciyā, ekiptu, kirēttā (Crete) mutaliya nāṭukaḷ nāka rikattil mika ōṅkiyiruntaṉa. in nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ uṟaḻcci piṟaḻccikaḷē iṉṟiya nākarikaṅkaḷākum. ulaka nākarikaṅkaḷuḷ mikappaḻamiyuṭiyatu cintu veḷi nākarikam eṉap pērāciriyar piṟāṅpōṭ eṉṉum aṟiñar kūṟukiṉṟār. moñcatarō nākarika kālattil tāyāṭciyē iruntatu eṉa, “intiyāvil tāyāṭci” eṉṉum nūl eḻutiya paraṉ ōmar ralv ernels eṉpār kūṟiyuḷḷār. ekiptiya peṇkaḷ ellā nāṭṭup peṇkaḷiyum viṭa mēlāṉa nili eytiyiruntārkaḷ. ekiptiyap peṇkaḷip pōla vēṟenta nāṭṭup peṇkaḷum atikāram peṟṟu viḷaṅkavilli eṉa hāl eṉpār kūṟiyuḷḷār. nil āṟṟi aṭutta nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa makkaḷippōla vēṟenta nāṭṭiṉarum peṇkaḷukku avvaḷavu uyarvu koṭukkavilli eṉa māksmullar kūṟiyuḷḷār. maṇamāṉa allatu maṇamākāta entap peṇṇum, taṉatu viruppiṉpaṭi evvakik koṭukkal vāṅkalum ceyyakkūṭiyavaḷākaviruntāḷ. avarkaḷik kaṇavaṉō, tāy tantiyarō, tamiyaṉ tampimārō kaṭṭuppaṭutta urimiyillātavarkaḷā yiruntaṉar. peṇṇiṉ peyarāl kaṇavaṉ pattiraṅkaḷ eḻutavō koṭukkal vāṅkal ceyyavō atikāram peṟṟirukkavilli. āṉāl peṇ kaṇavaṉiṉ cārpil avvāṟu ceyya atikāram peṟṟiruntāḷ. mūṉṟām irāmcē eṉṉum aracaṉ kālattup paṭṭiyamoṉṟil “peṇ, tāṉ virumpiya evarōṭāvatu cellalām. avaḷi oruvarum kaṭṭuppaṭutta muṭiyātu” eṉa eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. ekiptilē peṇkaḷ kaṭit teruvukkuc ceṉṟu vāṇikam ceytārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ kaṇavar vīṭṭiliruntu āṭi neytārkaḷ eṉṟu heratōttacu kūṟiyuḷḷār. eḻuttu vēliyil ārampitta oru peṇ, paṭippaṭiyē pataviyil uyarntu, oru nāṭṭukku āṭci atikāriyākip (Governor) piṉpu irāṇuva atikāriyāka vanta varalāṟu oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.

arēpiyāvilē peṇkaḷē paṇattukkuc contakkārarāyirun taṉar; avarkaḷiṭattil periya mantik kūṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. avarkaḷiṉ kaṇavar mantikaḷi mēyppōrāyiruntaṉar. kaliyāṇam nīkkuvatiṉ aṭiyāḷam kaṇavaṉ “iṉi nāṉ mantikaḷi mēyccal nilattukku ōṭṭic cellamāṭṭēṉ” eṉak kūṟuvatākum.

ekiptilē piḷḷikaḷ tāykkē contamuṭiyavarkaḷ. kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum pirintu vāḻa nērntāl piḷḷikaḷ tāyōṭu taṅkiṉar. ekiptiya kuṭumpaṅkaḷ tāyāṭci muṟiyāṉa amippuṭiyaṉa. oru kuṭum pattil peṇṇi maṇappavaṉ kuṭumpattil mutaṉmi peṟavilli. ekiptiyak kīḻ vakuppu, naṭuttara vakuppup peṇkaḷ tirumaṇattukkuppiṉ cutantiramuṭiyavarkaḷākavum, mariyātik kuriyavarkaḷākavum iruntārkaḷ. maṉivi eṉṉum muṟiyil avaḷē vīṭṭukku araci (illāḷ). vīṭṭilē mutaṉmiyuṭiyavar tantiyallar; tāyē. kuṭumpattil kaṇavaṉit talimiyākak kūṟum caṭṭam etuvum ekiptil irukka villi. pillē pērar eṉṉum aracaṉ kālattiṟkup piṉpē, kaṇavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷi uṇarttum col ākkappaṭṭatu. āṭavaṉ, vīṭṭil taṅkupavaṉākavum cottuc cutantaraṅkaḷ ellām peṇṇukkē uriyaṉa vākavum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. oruvaṉ putiya vīṭu oṉṟik kaṭṭiṉāl atu, maṉiviyiṉ kikku māṟiṟṟu. maṇamāṉa peṇkaḷum, maṇamākāta peṇ kaḷum taṅkaḷ cottit tāmē āṇṭu aṉupavittārkaḷ. maṉiviyiṉ karumaṅkaḷip paṟṟik kaṇavaṉ oṉṟum aṟiyātavaṉāyiruntāṉ. kaṇavaṉ, maṉiviyiṉ cottil taliyiṭuvatu caṭṭa cammatamillātatākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. vayatāṉa peṟṟōrik kāppaṟṟum poṟuppup peṇ makkaḷiṉ kaṭamiyākaviruntatu. tirumaṇaṅkaḷ camaya campantamāṉa kiriyikaḷōṭu naṭattappaṭavilli.

kirēttāvil (Crete) cottu peṇkaḷ vacamē iruntatu. kaṇavaṉukkuc cottil taliyiṭum atikāram ciṟitum irukkavilli. piḷḷikaḷ tāy tanti eṉṉum iruvariṉ cottukkaḷiyum peṟṟārkaḷ. virumpiṉāl kaliyāṇatti nīkkik koḷḷum atikāram peṇṇukku iruntatu.

ivvāṟu muṟkālattil nākarikattāṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya nāṭukaḷi lellām, peṇkaḷē mutal iṭam peṟṟiruntārkaḷ eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm. muriya cantirakuptaṉiṉ araṇmaṉiyil tūtarākavirunta mekastaṉīs eṉṉum kirēkkar pāṇṭiya nāṭu peṇkaḷālāḷappaṭukiṟateṉak kūṟi yirukkiṉṟār. itaṉāl tamiḻ nāṭṭil paḻaṅkālattil cotturimi peṟ ṟiruntavarkaḷ peṇkaḷē eṉat teriya varukiṉṟatu. oru āyiram āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉ, mēṟku nāṭukaḷ mikap piṟpōkkāṉa niliyil iruntaṉa. mēṟku nāṭukaḷil peṇkaḷ mutaṉmi aṭiyat toṭaṅkiyatum, aṅku nākarikam paṭippaṭiyē vaḷarcciyaṭintu iṉṟiya niliyi aṭintu viṭṭatu. peṇkaḷukku mutaṉmi koṭāta kiḻakku nāṭukaḷil nākarikam iruḷaṭintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu. makkaḷ mikavum kīḻniliyil irukkiṉṟaṉar.

tanti āṭci
ulakil āṭci eṉpatu valimiyiyum celvaniliyiyum aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatu. makkaḷ vāḻkkiyiṉ muṟ paruvattup peṇkaḷē nākarikattukku vēṇṭiya aṭippaṭiyākavuḷḷa palavakit toḻilkaḷiyum ārampittu vittārkaḷ. muṟkāla makkaḷiṉ celvam eṉpatu uṇavē. evaṉiṭattu atika uṇavup poruḷuṇṭō avaṉē celvaṉākak karutappaṭṭāṉ. toṭakkattil peṇkaḷiṭamē atika uṇavup poruḷkaḷ iruntaṉa. ākavē, avarkaḷ celvarkaḷāka viruntaṉar. āṭavar avarkaḷ ēviyavaṟṟic ceytum, pōrkkālattil pakivarōṭu pōr ceytu peṇkaḷip pātukāttum vantaṉar. ēr pūṭṭi uḻutu atika tāṉiyam viḷivikkum muṟi aṟiyappaṭṭapōtu, vēṭar kaḷāka alintu tirinta āṭavar palar, tāṉiyaṅkaḷi viḷivikkat toṭaṅkiṉar. ituvariyum peṇkaḷ ceytuvanta payirt toḻili avarkaḷ kippaṟṟiṉar. ataṉāl celvanili peṇkaḷ kiyiliruntu āṭavar kikku māṟat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. āṭavaṉ ciṟitu ciṟitākac cottukkut talivaṉāṉāṉ. avvāṟu vantapōtum peṇ āṭcik kālattilirunta pala vaḻakkaṅkaḷum iruntu vantaṉa. peṇ āṭcik kālattil peṇṇuk kup pūraṇa cutantaram iruntatu. avaḷ virumpiya oruvaṉik kaṇavaṉākat terintu koḷḷalām. vēṇṭātapōtu avaṉi viṭṭu vēṟoru vaṉik kaṇavaṉākat terintu koḷḷalām. tanti āṭci uṇṭāṉa piṉpu, āṭavar ciṟitu ciṟitākap peṇkaḷiṉ cutantirattik kaṭṭuppaṭuttat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. oru peṇ oruvaṉi maṇantāl, avaḷ taṉatu vāḻkkik kālam muḻumiyilum avaṉuṭiya maṉiviyāka viruttal vēṇṭumeṉṉum caṭṭaṅkaḷum camūka vaḻakkak kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷum uṇṭāyiṉa. tantiyāṭci uṇṭāṉa piṉpē, niliyāṉa tirumaṇaṅkaḷ ulakil tōṉṟi valivaṭintaṉa.

peṇkaḷiṉ uyar niliyum tāḻ niliyum
tāy āṭci muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟi nākarika kālattil peṇkaḷ uyar niliyil iruntārkaḷ. makkaḷ mūrkkamāṉa vilaṅkukaḷippōla oru kūṭṭattiṉarōṭu iṉṉoru kūṭṭattiṉar pōr ceytal pōṉṟa muraṭṭuk kuṇaṅkaḷuṭiyavarkaḷāyiruntaṉar. iṉṟum makkaḷ pōrk kuṇamuṭiyavarkaḷākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ak kālattil oru kūṭṭattiṉar iṉṉoru kūṭṭattiṉarit tākki avarkaḷi veṉṟāl, avarkaḷ āṭavar ellōriyum koṉṟuviṭṭup peṇkaḷi aṭimi kaḷākak koṇṭu celvatu vaḻakkam. ivvāṟu aṭimikaḷākac ceṉṟa peṇkaḷ pakivarkaḷiṭam māṭukaḷ kutirikaḷpōla uḻittārkaḷ. avarkaḷ pakivaric cērntu piḷḷikaḷip peṟṟup pakivarkaḷiṭi yum tamatu irattak kalappi uṇṭākkiṉārkaḷ. ulakam muḻumiyilum contak kūṭṭattiṉaruḷ peṇkoḷḷutal ākātu eṉṉum vaḻakku iruntamiyiṉ, oru kūṭṭattavaṉ iṉṉoru kūṭṭattiliruntu peṇṇit tiruṭikkoṇṭu celvatum vaḻakkāka iruntatu. itaṉāl paḻaṅkāla makkaḷiṭiyē peṇkaḷit tiruṭi maṇam purintukoḷva tākiya vaḻakkamum valivaṭintiruntatu.

tirumaṇattiṉ carittiram
mika muṟkālattil tirumaṇam irukkavilli; atu iṉṉateṉṟum aṟiyappaṭavilli. āṭavaṉ oruvaṉ peṇ oruttiyōṭu naṭpāyirun tāl, ataṉāl avaṉ caṭṭapūrvamākavō camaya pūrvamākavō kaṭṭuppaṭa villi. āṭavarum makaḷirum tattam viruppappaṭi vāḻum urimiyip peṟṟiruntaṉar. āṭavar peṇkaḷi valimiyāṟ piṭittu maṇañceytu koḷḷum kālam variyil nilimi ivvāṟē iruntatu. peṇṇi valimiyāṟ piṭippatu, eṉpatu uṇmiyāka allatu pōliyākavirukka lām. oru peṇṇi valimiyāṟ piṭippatāl, avaṉukku oru aṭimi kiṭikkiṉṟatu. itaṉāl contak kūṭṭattil peṇ koḷvataṟkup patil ayaṟ kūṭṭattil peṇ koḷḷum vaḻakku uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ivvaki maṇaṅkaḷāl iraṇṭu kūṭṭattiṉarukkum iṇippu uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl avarkaḷ valimipeṟṟut tammip pakivariṭamiruntu kāppāṟṟik koḷḷat takkatākaviruntatu. piṟkālaṅkaḷil iru kūṭṭaṅ kaḷilum ciṟuvarukku maṇamceytu iṇippukkaḷ uṇṭākkappaṭṭaṉa.

peṇkaḷik kavarum vaḻakkattōṭu, kavarvataṟkup patil peṇ kaḷi vilikoṭuttu vāṅkum vaḻakkam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. tiruntāta makka ḷiṭiyē iv vaḻakku nīṇṭa kālam toṭarntu varuvatāyiṟṟu. ak kālaṅ kaḷil peṇ, tantiyōṭu iruntāḷ. tāṉ virumpiya evaṉukkāvatu avaḷi atika vilikku viṟṟuviṭum urimiyiruntatu. viṟpavaṉ, vāṅku pavaṉiṉ naṟkuṇattukkallāmal, poruḷiṉ tokikkākavē avaṉi virumpiṉāṉ. iv vaḻakkam irukkum kālattil peṇṇukku avaḷ virumpiya oruvaṉit terinteṭukkum urimi irukkavilli. tanti allatu avaḷiṉ pātukāppukkuriyavaṉ terinteṭukkum oruvaṉukku avaḷ viṟkappaṭalāṉāḷ. avaḷ avaṉuṭiya aṭimiyāṉāḷ.

ik koḷki niyāyamāṉataṉṟu eṉṟum, paṇattukkākap peṇ kaḷi aṭimikaḷ ākkutal kūṭātu eṉṟum, makkaḷ kālappōkkil uṇarat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. appoḻutu peṇṇi vilikoṭuttu vāṅkupavaṉ, allatu kaṇavaṉ, peṇṇiṉ tantiyiṭam cītaṉam kēṭṭāṉ. immuṟiyiṉāl peṇṇi vili koṭuttu vāṅkum vaḻakkam oḻintatu. vili koṭuttu vāṅkiya vaḻakkattiṉ niḻalē iṉṟu pariyam eṉṟum peyar peṟṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. pariyam koṭuppatu eṉpatu pōliyāka vāṅkuvatik kuṟikkiṉṟatu.



itaṉāl peṇṇukkuk koṭukkappaṭum paṇam peṇṇiṉ vili aḷavil irāmal, peyar maṭṭil ciṟu tokiyākaviruntatu; viṟpavaṉ vāṅkupavaṉukku allatu peṇṇukku vilipporuḷil oru pakutiyik koṭuttāṉ. ataṉpiṉ, tanti peṇ vaḻiyākak kaṇavaṉukkup poruḷ kiṭikkumpaṭi cītaṉam aḷittāṉ. inniliyilum peṇṇukkuk kaṇavaṉit teriyum urimi irukkavilli; tantiyē avaḷukkuk kaṇavaṉit terintāṉ. itaṉpiṉ peṇṇi vili koṭuttu vāṅkum vaḻakkam maṟintu pōyiṟṟu. taṉatu peṇṇukkut takunta kaṇavaṉit tēṭa vēṇṭum eṉṉum eṇṇam tantikku iruntamiyāl, avaṉ peṇṇukkēṟṟa kaṇavaṉit tēṭavēṇṭiyiruntatu. itaṉpiṉ peṇ, tāṉ virumpiya kaṇavaṉit terintu koḷḷum nili uṇṭāyiṟṟu. itu vaḷarcciyaṭintapōtu purōkitar maṇaṅkaḷik kiriyikaḷōṭu naṭattivittaṉar. peṇkaḷiṉ nili oru kālattil uyarvu peṟṟiruntu, piṉ tāḻvu nili aṭintu, maṟupaṭiyum nākarika kālattil uyarvaṭinta teṉa aṟikiṉṟōm.

iṭikkālattil peṇkaḷ nili vīḻcciyuṟṟataṟkuk kāraṇam
ivvulakam, vāḻkkip pōrāṭṭam, takutiyuṭiyatu nili peṟutal, eṉṉum iraṇṭu iyaṟki vitikaḷukku uṭpaṭṭuc celkiṉṟatu. valiyatu meliyatik koṉṟu tiṉṉutali allatu valiyatu meliyati aṭakki āḷutalik kāṇkiṉṟōm. vāḻkkip pōrāṭṭattil “valimiyē niyāyam”. iṉṟu valiya cātiyār meliya cātiyāri aṭakki āḷvatum ivviti paṟṟiyē. makkaḷ tiruntāta kālattil avarkaḷiṉ uṇarcci vilaṅkukaḷiṉ uṇarcci pōlavē iruntatu. āṇ peṇ eṉṉum iru pālāril peṇkaḷiviṭa āṇkaḷē uṭal valiyilum muraṭṭuk kuṇattilum mikuntiruntārkaḷ. ākavē, avarkaḷ tammiṉum melinta peṇkaḷi aṭakkit tamatu vāḻkkip pōrāṭṭattukkup payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭārkaḷ. makkaḷiṉ uḷḷam paṇpaṭṭu aṟivu ōṅkiya kālattil, avarkaḷiṭiyē niyāya uṇarcci tōṉṟiṟṟu. ak kālaṅ kaḷil avarkaḷ āṭavariviṭap peṇkaḷē ulakukkup payaṉuḷḷavarkaḷ eṉa uṇarntārkaḷ. appoḻutu peṇkaḷ paṭippaṭiyē ellā atikāraṅkaḷiyum peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ.

kirēkkar urōmarkaḷiṉ tirumaṇam
kirēkka nāṭṭil tirumaṇam taṉippaṭṭa urimiyuṭiyatu māttiramallāmal, aracāṅkattōṭu campantappaṭṭatākavum iruntatu. ispāṭṭāvil, itaṉ toṭarpāṉa caṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. ilikarkas (Lycurgus) eṉpavariṉ caṭṭappaṭi uriyakālattil maṇañceyyātavar mītum, mikavum kālañ ceṉṟu maṇañ ceypavarkaḷ mītum, icivillāta maṇañ ceytavarkaḷ mītum vaḻakkut toṭarakkūṭiyatākac caṭṭam iruntatu. ovvoruvarum nalla kuḻantikaḷip peṟṟu nāṭṭukku aḷikka vēṇṭumeṉpatē akkālak koḷki. maṇañceyvatu kuḻantikaḷip peṟuvataṟku māttiram eṉṟu karutappaṭṭatu. oru peṇ taṉ kaṇavaṉ mūlam kuḻantikaḷip peṟa iyalātavaḷāyiruntāl, avaḷ iṉṉoru vaṉic cēralām eṉṟu māttiramalla, cēravēṇṭumeṉṟu caṭṭam kaṭṭāyappaṭuttiṟṟu. piḷāṟṟō eṉpavariṉ caṭṭappaṭi, muppattintu vayatukku muṉ maṇam ceytu koḷḷātavar kaṭumiyākat taṇṭikkappaṭa lām. peṇṇi maṉiviyākat terivatu nāṭṭiṉ nalaṉikkarutiyē yaṉṟic conta nalaṉ karutiyaṉṟu eṉa avar kūṟiyuḷḷār.



tirumaṇam puṉitamuṭiyatu
tirumaṇam cantatikaḷit tōṟṟuvikkum nōkkamākat tōṉṟiyatu. ellā makkaḷum tirumaṇattip puṉitamuṭiyatākavē koṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. vilaṅkukaḷ, paṟavikaḷ mutaliya kīḻ iṉa uyirkaḷ cantatiyit tōṟṟuvikkum kālattilaṉṟi āṇ peṇ cērkki ceyva tilli. kīḻ niliyil uḷḷa makkaḷiṭiyil ivvaḻakkē nilipeṟu kiṉṟatu. makkaḷ nākarikam eṉṉum pōrviyil ceyaṟkiyāka vāḻat toṭaṅkiyapōtu, āṇ peṇ cērkkiyi iṉpattukkuriyatu eṉak koṇṭu atil aḻuntuvārāyiṉar. itaṟkākak kokkōkam pōṉṟa iṉpa nūlkaḷ eḻutappaṭalāyiṉa. annūlkaḷ eppaṭiyirukkiṉṟa teṉṟāl, ivvāṟu kaḻuttil curukkiṭṭuk koṇṭu (tūkkiliṭṭu) maṉitaṉ cākalām eṉpatupōla veṉka. maṉitaṉ āṇ peṇ cērkkiyil celaviṭum āṟṟalit tiraṭṭi, vēṟu vaḻiyil celaviṭuvatāl periya kāriyaṅkaḷic cātikkalām eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. ciṉa, putta, kiṟittuvat tuṟavikaḷ kalvit toṭarpāka atikavēli ceytataṟkuk kāraṇam ituvēyākum.

nāṭṭiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattukkut tāyē kāraṇam
piḷḷikaḷiṉ vaḷarccikkum naṟ paḻakkaṅkaḷukkum tāyē poṟuppāka irukkiṉṟāḷ. avaḷ kuḻantikaḷiṉ uṭal vaḷarcciyik kavaṉippatu pōla, aṟivu vaḷarcciyiyum kavaṉikkakkūṭiya niliyil iruppāḷēyāyiṉ avaḷ, kuḻantikaḷiṉ maṉattiyum māṟṟiviṭalām. iraṇṭu talimuṟikaḷil ivvulakil pōrkaḷ talikāṭṭāmal oḻintuviṭum. vāḻkkiyiṉ mattiya iṭam vīṭu. vīṭukaḷil tōṉṟum nākarikamē oru cātiyāriṉ nākarikamāka vaḷarum. oru cātiyār nākarikam aṭintu muṉṉēṟuvataṟkut tāyē kāraṇam.

kiḻakkut tēcap peṇkaḷ
kīḻ nāṭṭup peṇkaḷum, mēl nāṭṭup peṇkaḷum talimuṟi yāka varum paramparip paḻakkaṅkaḷāṟ kaṭṭuppaṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ nīṇṭakālamākat tamatu poruḷātārattukkum pātukāppukkum tam kaṇavariyē nampi varukiṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ ivvāṟu otukkappaṭ ṭiruppatu mēl nāṭṭavarkaḷukku aṭimi vāḻvākat tōṉṟum. peṇ kaḷi aṟiyāmiyil vittiruppatāl pala tīmikaḷ uṇṭākum. ekiptiyap peṇ taṉatu kuḻantikkuk kaṇṇūṟu paṭātirukkumpaṭi ataṟkuk kantikaḷi uṭuttirukkiṟāḷ. cīṉattāy, taṉatu āṇ kuḻantiyi peṇ kuḻantiyeṉṟu teyvaṅkaḷ niṉikkumpaṭi ataṉ kātik kuttivaḷiyam iṭukiṟāḷ. avaḷ, camayac caṭṭaṅkaḷāl, kaṇavaṉ, tanti, cakōtaraṉ pōṉṟa āṭavarukku aṭaṅki naṭakkavum, avarkaḷik kaṭavuḷāka niṉittu naṭakkavum paḻakkappaṭṭuḷḷāḷ. ataṉāl avaḷ tāṉāka oṉṟi niṉikkum āṟṟali maṟantuviṭṭāḷ. avaḷik kaṭṭuppaṭutti vikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu camayac caṭṭam collukiṉṟatu. avaḷukkut taṉatu kuḻantikaḷiyum, kirāmap peṇkaḷ cilariyum, avarkaḷ vīṭukaḷiyumē teriyum. avaḷ oṉṟip paṟṟi yōcaṉi ceyyātirukkumpaṭi paḻakkappaṭṭuḷḷāḷ. ataṉāl avaḷ taṉṉāl niṉikka muṭiyāteṉa nampukiṉṟāḷ. paḷḷikkūṭattil paṭittu veḷi yulakatti aṟinta peṇkaḷ tamatu kaṇavarāl vaṟpuṟuttappaṭum ivvaki vāḻkkiyāl tuṉpuṟukiṉṟaṉar. ciṟik kūṭattil iruppatākavē avarkaḷ uṇarukiṉṟaṉar. oru mirukattik kaṭṭi aviḻppavaṉip pōla, avaḷukku avaḷ kaṇavaṉ tōṉṟukiṉṟāṉ.




peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷukkuk kīḻppaṭital
toṭakkattil kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyarukkuḷḷa kaṭamikaḷ pirikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. peṇ, vīṭṭiluḷḷa kaṭamikaḷic ceytāḷ. āṭavaṉ veḷiyiluḷḷa cila kaṭamikaḷip pārttu vantāṉ. itaṉāl peṇ tāḻntavaḷ, kaṇavaṉ uyarntavaṉ eṉṟāvatu, peṇ uyarntavaḷ, kaṇavaṉ tāḻntavaṉ eṉṟāvatu karuttu uṇṭākavilli. iruvarum camamāṉa varkaḷ eṉṉum karuttē uṇṭāyiruntatu.

muṟkālaṅkaḷil perumpālōr aṭimikaḷākavē iruntaṉar. peṇkaḷum appaṭiyē. ulakanīti, valimiyil tāṉ kaṭṭappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟatu. ulakattil aṭimikaḷ aṭimikaḷallātār eṉa iraṇṭu pirivukaḷ irunta oru kālamuṇṭu. mika nākarikamaṭinta makkaḷ uḷḷattilum ituvē niyāyamāṉa oḻuṅkupōlat tōṉṟiṟṟu. āṉāl atu niyāya māṉataṉṟu. itupōlavē peṇkaḷi aṭimippaṭuttuvatu ulaka vaḻakka mākaviruntatu. peṇkaḷ aṭimippaṭuttappaṭāmal iruppārkaḷēyāṉāl, atu iyaṟkikku māṟupaṭṭatu eṉat tōṉṟalām. peṇkaḷ valimiyi ṉāl aṭakkappaṭṭirukkavilli. peṇkaḷē tām aṭaṅkiyirukka virumpukiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ atippaṟṟi veṟuppuk koḷḷavilli. avarkaḷ ataṟku iṇaṅki oḻukupavarkaḷāyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē peṇkaḷ, āṇkaḷukku aṭaṅki naṭattal tāṉ iyaṟki vitiyeṉap palar kūṟalām. uṇmi avvāṟaṉṟu. peṇkaḷiṉ oḻukkam āṇkaḷukku muṟṟum māṟupaṭṭatu eṉṟu koḷḷum muṟiyil, ellāp peṇkaḷum iḷami mutal payiṟṟappaṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷukkuc cuyaputti, cuya atikāram, cuya pātukāppu illiyeṉṟum, avarkaḷ piṟarukku aṭaṅki naṭakka vēṇṭumeṉavum uḷḷattiṟ koḷḷumpaṭi paḻakkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ellā nīti nūlkaḷum, ellā uṇarccikaḷum, avarkaḷ maṟṟavarkaḷiṭat til taṅki vāḻavēṇṭum eṉṉum karuttiyē vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa.

peṇkaḷi aṟiyāmiyil amiḻntumpaṭi ceytu, avarkaḷi aṭimikaḷāka irukkumpaṭi āṭavar ceytārkaḷēyaṉṟip peṇkaḷ āṇ kaḷukku aṭaṅkivāḻa iṇaṅkukiṟārkaḷ eṉak kūṟutal uṇmiyākat tōṉṟavilli. peṇkaḷukku viṭutali vēṇṭum; peṇkaḷukku āṭciyiṟ paṅku vēṇṭum; aracāṅka capikaḷukku uṟuppiṉarit teriva taṟkup peṇkaḷ tamatu viruppit terivikkum (ōṭṭu) urimi vēṇṭum eṉṉum kiḷarccikaḷ uṇṭāyirunta pōtellām, ataṉi ātarittup perun tokiyāṉa peṇkaḷ tiraṇṭārkaḷ. itaṉāl peṇkaḷiṭattil āṇkaḷukku aṭaṅki naṭakka vēṇṭumeṉṉum iyalpāṉa maṉappāṉmi illiyeṉṟum āṭavar, avarkaḷ aṭaṅki naṭakkumpaṭi, camayam eṉṟum, nīti eṉṟum peyar koṭuttup pala kayiṟukaḷāl avarkaḷik kaṭṭi vittuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟum namakku naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟataṉṟō?

intiya nākarikattiṟ peṇkaḷ nili
irukku vētattiṉ carvāṉukkira maṇikiyil irupatu peṇ pulavarkaḷ variyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum pala peṇ pulavarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cāḷukkiyar āṭciyil (ki.pi.980-1160) pala peṇ āṭcit talivarkaḷum (Governors) atikārikaḷum iruntārkaḷ. ak kālattil peṇkaḷ irāṇuvap payiṟci peṟṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ aracariṉ meykāppāḷarāka iruntu vantaṉar. avarkaḷ villip payaṉpaṭutta naṉku payiṟci peṟṟiruntaṉar. araca kuṭumpap peṇkaḷ āpattuk kālaṅkaḷil āyutantāṅki vīṭukaḷik kāttārkaḷ eṉṟu mattiya kālat teṉṉintiyap paṭṭiyaṅkaḷ oṉṟiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. kaṉṉaṭap peṇkaḷ im muṟiyil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉar. micūril cittaṅkāli eṉṉum iṭattil ōr vīrappeṇ kirāmattil nikaḻnta pōril ki.pi.1041-il māṇṭāḷ. 1264-il vīrap peṇ orutti, koḷḷikkāraṉi maṭakki aṭittataṟkāka mūkku aṇi oṉṟu vīrap patakkamākap peṟṟāḷ. nilkoṇṭa eṉṉum iṭattil kāṇappaṭṭa paṭṭiyamoṉṟil oru ciṟṟaracukkuriya irāṇi, paṭi oṉṟi naṭattic ceṉṟa nikaḻcci kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. avaḷ 144il taṉatu tantiyik koṉṟa pakivarip paḻi vāṅkum poruṭṭup purinta pōril māṇṭāḷ. irācaputtira kuṭumpap peṇkaḷ vāḷum, īṭṭiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. avarkaḷ paṭiyi naṭattic cellakkūṭiyavarkaḷākavum, vēṇṭiya pōtu āṭciyi naṭattakkūṭiyavarkaḷākavum iruntaṉar. kūrmatēvi eṉṉum araci, taṉṉuṭiya kaṇavaṉ iṟantapiṉ irācciyatti naṭatti, kutām uttiṉ eṉpavaṉiṉ paṭiyic citaṟa aṭittāḷ. yavākihir tēvi eṉpavaḷ taṉatu kaṇavaṉiṉ maraṇattukkuppiṉ pōr muṉiyiṉ muṉṉaṇiyil niṉṟu pōrceytu maṭintāḷ. irācaputtirar varalāṟṟil ivvaki nikaḻccikaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

peṇkaḷukku irāṇuvap payiṟci aḷikkum vaḻakkam marāṭṭiya araca kuṭumpaṅkaḷilum iruntu vantatu. paṭiyi naṭattuvataṟkuk kaṇavaṉ allatu putalvaṉ illātapōtu, araciyē paṭiyi naṭattic ceṉṟāḷ. kōlāppūr camastāṉattit tāpitta tātāpāy eṉpavaḷ, āṭci naṭattiyatōṭu tāṉē paṭiyiyum naṭattic ceṉṟāḷ. ivaḷukku ilakkumipāy eṉpavaḷ paṭit talimi vakittut taṉatu vīrattik kāṇpittāḷ.

puttar kāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷil, peṇkaḷ nūl nūṟṟal, necavu ceytal, kampaḷi neytal, tuṇikkuc cāyamūṭṭutal mutaliya toḻilkaḷāl varuvāyi uṇṭupaṇṇik kaṇavariṉ vaṟumiyip pōkkiṉārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. nūl nūṟṟal peṇkaḷiṉ toḻilāka nīṇṭakālam iruntu vantatu.
peṇkaḷum maṟumaṇamum

uyirp paṭippukkaḷiṉ nōkkam cantatikaḷip perukkuvatu eṉa, muṉ ōr iṭattiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷōm. uyirkaḷil āṇ peṇ eṉṉum vēṟupāṭukaḷum cantatiyip perukkuvataṟkākavē uṇṭākiyiruk kiṉṟaṉa. cila uyirkaḷil āṇ, peṇ cērkkiyiṉ piṉ maṭintu viṭu kiṉṟatu. itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭut tēṉī. uyirkaḷ cantatiyip perukku vatākiya iyaṟki vitikku māṟāka eḻuntatē vitavikaḷiṉ vivāka maṟuppākum. icceyaṟkik kaṭṭuppāṭu iyaṟki vitikku māṟupaṭṭatu. intiya nāṭṭil vitavikaḷ koṭumi aṭintu varuvatupōla, vēṟenta nāṭṭilum aṭintatilli.

vitavikaḷ uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟavēṇṭum, allatu vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum piramaccāraṇi virataṅkākka vēṇṭumeṉa, intiyariṉ oru cārāruṭiya camaya nūlkaḷil eḻuti vikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. piramacāriṇiyip pōla, avaḷ veṟṟili pōṭṭuk koḷḷakkūṭātu; veṇkala vaṭṭilil uṇavu koḷḷakkūṭātu. avaḷ oru nāḷil oru muṟi māttiram uṇṇa vēṇṭum; iraṇṭumuṟi uṇṇutalākātu; kaṭṭilil paṭuttal ākātu; vācaṉip poruḷkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttal ākātu. avaḷ tiṉamum iṟanta kaṇavaṉukku amutupaṭittal vēṇṭum. ivvakiyāṉa koṭiya caṭṭaṅkaḷ vēta kālattil irukkavilli. kaṇavaṉi iḻanta peṇ, kaṇavaṉiṉ cakōtaraṉi maṇantāḷ. itu ak kālattil potu vaḻakkāka viruntatu. tivārā eṉpataṟku iraṇṭāvatu kaṇavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ. ic col peṇṇiṉ koḻunaṉik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. piṇattik koḷuttuñ camayattil iṟantavaṉiṉ cakōtaraṉ koḻuntiyiṉ kiyip piṭittu avaḷit taṉatu maṉiviyākumpaṭi kēṭṭāṉ. iṟantavaṉiṉ maṉivi, avaṉ cakōtaraṉi cērtalippaṟṟi vētaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. tittiriya caṅkiti oru maṉivi, orē kālattil iraṇṭu kaṇavari vittiruttal takutiyaṉṟu eṉak kūṟukiṉṟatu. ikkaruttu aitarēyap pirāmaṇattilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. māpāratattil peṇkaḷiṉ maṟumaṇattit taṭukkum cila caṭṭaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu; āṉāl vēta kālattil peṇ kaḷukku maṟumaṇameṉpatu carva cātāraṇamāṉatu. māpāratattiṉ cānti paruvattil peṇkaḷukku maṟumaṇañ ceytal iyalpu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. yākñavalki, yārukkum contamallātirukkum peṇṇi (vitaviyi) maṇam muṭikkumpaṭi kūṟukiṉṟār. itikāca kālattil, maṇam ceytu koḷḷappaṭum peṇ, kaṉṉiyāka irukka vēṇṭumeṉṉum kaṭṭāyam irukkavilli. kiruṭṭiṇaṉ, kollappaṭṭa narakaṉiṉ peṇ kaḷic cērntāṉ. tiricaṅku, tāṉ koṉṟa vitarppa aracaṉiṉ maṉiviyi maṇantu iraṇṭu putalvarkaḷip peṟṟāṉ. tamayanti iṉṉoruvaṉiṉ maṉiviyāka viruntāḷ eṉpati aṟintum, irutupaṉṉaṉ avaḷi maṇakka virumpiṉāṉ. itikāca kālattil maṟumaṇaṅkaḷ putumiyāṉavi yalla. paḻiya nūlkaḷ, paḻam pōkkuṭiya purōkitarāl tiruttappaṭṭa piṉpum, vitavikaḷiṉ maṇattik kuṟippiṭum nikaḻccikaḷ kāṇappaṭutal viyappukkuriyatē.

vētakāla muṭivil peṇkaḷiṉ maṟumaṇam iḻivākak karutappaṭa lāyiṟṟu. orumuṟi maṇañceytavaḷi maṇakkum oruvaṉ pāviyāvāṉ eṉa āpattampar kūṟiyuḷḷār. āpattampar kālattum vitavikaḷiṉ maṇattit taṭuppataṟku ettaṉaṅkaḷ naṭantuvantaṉa veṉṟum terikiṉ ṟatu. vitavi vayiṟṟil pirāmaṇaṉ mūlam piṟappavaṉ pirāmaṇaṉ āvāṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ vāṇikam ceytu vāḻum pirāmaṇaṉi oppat tāḻntava ṉākak karutappaṭuvāṉeṉṟum maṉu kūṟiyuḷḷār. maṟumaṇam mūlam vitavi vayiṟṟil piṟantavaṉukkut tantiyiṉ cottil nāliloṉṟukku urimiyuṇṭu eṉak kutamar kūṟiyuḷḷār. vaciṭṭarum, viṭṭuṇuvum vitavi vayiṟṟil maṟumaṇattāṟ piṟakkum piḷḷikaḷ vaḷarppup piḷḷi kaḷukkuc camamāṉavarkaḷ eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. oru peṇ valitil kavarappaṭṭuk kiriyikaḷ mūlam maṇañceytu koṭukkappaṭāviṭṭāl, avaḷ iṉṉoruvaṉukkuk koṭukkappaṭalāmeṉṟu vaciṭṭar kūṟi yuḷḷār. kaṇavaṉirukkumpōtu, peṇ kiriyimūlam māttiram maṇañ ceyyappaṭṭiruntu uṇmiyāṉa maṇam (āṇ, peṇ cērkki) naṭavā viṭṭāl, avaḷ maṟupaṭiyum maṇakkappaṭalām eṉa āpattampar kūṟi yuḷḷār. ayalūrukkuc ceṉṟa kaṇavaṉ, aintu āṇṭukaḷ variyum tirumpi varāviṭiṉ, avaḷ vēṟu kaṇavaṉi maṇakkalām; kaṇavaṉ iṟantapiṉ pirāmaṇa vakuppiṉaḷ aintu āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittum viciyap peṇ nāṉku āṇṭukaḷukkup piṉṉum, cūttirap peṇ mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ kaḻintum, maṇakkalām eṉa avarē kūṟiyuḷḷār. itaṉāl kaṇavaṉ iṟanta peṇkaḷ māttiramallar; kaṇavaṉi iḻakkāta peṇkaḷum maṟumaṇam ceyyalām eṉṉum caṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉaveṉṟu terikiṟatu. kaliyāṇa nīkkam “mōkṣam” eṉappaṭṭatu. nāratar collum caṭṭam varumāṟu:

“kaṇavaṉ veḷināṭṭukkuc ceṉṟu tirumpāviṭil, pirāmaṇap peṇ eṭṭu āṇṭukaḷ kāttirukkavēṇṭum. avaḷukkup piḷḷi illāviṭṭāl nāṉku āṇṭukaḷ poṟuttirukkalām. piḷḷiyuḷḷa cattiriyap peṇ nāṉku āṇṭukaḷ kāttirukkalām. ataṉpiṉ avaḷ iṉṉoruvaṉi maṇakkalām. piḷḷiyuḷḷa viciyap peṇ nāṉku āṇṭukaḷum, piḷḷiyillātavaḷ iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷum poṟut tirukkalām. cūttirap peṇ kāttirukka vēṇṭiyatilli. itu piracāpati yiṉ caṭṭam.” ik kāraṇaṅkaḷāl maṟumaṇam kuṟṟamākātu.


nāratar kūṟuvatu: “peṇ, cantatiyip perukkuvataṟkākap paṭikkap paṭṭavaḷ. peṇvayal; āṇ vitiyi vitippavaṉ. vitiyillāta vaṉukku vayalik koṭukkakkūṭātu. vitiyillātavaṉ vayali āḷuva taṟkut takutiyaṟṟavaṉ. kaṇavaṉiṟantapiṉ taṉ koḻunaṉi alaṭciyañ ceytu ayalāṉic cērpavaḷ keṭṭavaḷāvāḷ.”

cātakak katikaḷil maṟumaṇaṅkaḷip paṟṟiya varalāṟukaḷ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. kōcala tēcattaracaṉ kāci tēcattaracaṉ maṉiviyi maṇantā ṉeṉṟu acatarūpa eṉṉum cātakam kūṟukiṉṟatu. maraṇa taṇṭaṉi vitikkap paṭṭa cakōtaraṉ, kaṇavaṉ, makaṉ eṉṉum mūvaril, “putiya kaṇavaṉit tēṭalām; putiya putalvaṉip peṟalām; cakōtaraṉip peṟutal muṭi yātu” eṉak kūṟi avaṉuṭiya viṭutalikkāka maṉṟāṭiya peṇṇip paṟṟi ucciṅka cātakam kūṟukiṉṟatu. kupta aracar kālattil vitaviyi avaḷ koḻunaṉ maṇakkum vaḻakku iruntatu. cantira kuptaṉiṉ makaṉākiya vikkiramātittaṉ taṉatu tamiyaṉ irāma kuptaṉik koṉṟu avaṉ maṉivi turuva tēviyit taṉakkup pariyākkiṉāṉ. tulpakaṉ eṉṉum aracaṉ, taṉ maṉivimītu āci koṇṭiruppati aṟinta oru celva vaṇikaṉ, avaḷi avvaracaṉukku maṇañ ceytu koṭuttāṉeṉṟu irāca taraṅkiṇi eṉṉum nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu. maṉukālattukku muṉ, vitavikaḷ kaṭuntavam ceyyavēṇṭumeṉṉum caṭṭam irukkavilli. ivvakic caṭṭam vēta kālattil irukkavum illi. paḻiya nīti nūlkaḷi eḻutiya kutamar atippaṟṟik kuṟippiṭavilli. pōtāyaṉarum, vaciṭṭarum, avarkaḷ cilakālam kaṭṭuppāṭāka vāḻavēṇṭumeṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ ōr āṇṭukku vitavikaḷ, tēṉ, iṟicci, veṟitarum kuṭivakikaḷ, uppu mutaliyavikaḷi nīkki nilattil nittiri koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉak kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. mutkaliyar eṉpavar, avarkaḷ avvāṟu āṟu tiṅkaḷ ceytāl pōtumāṉatu eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. ak kālattiṉ piṉ avaḷ koḻunaṉic cērntu piḷḷikaḷip peṟalām.

peṇkaḷ kalviyē ulaka muṉṉēṟṟam
peṇkaḷukkuk kalvi kūṭātu; avarkaḷiṉ kalvi eṉpatu āṭavarukkuk kīḻppaṭintu naṭappatē eṉṉum koḷki virivāka maṟintu varukiṉṟatu. maṇamākumuṉ tantikkum, maṇamāṉapiṉ kaṇava ṉukkum, kaṇavaṉiṟanta piṉ makaṉukkum aṭaṅki naṭappatē peṇkaḷ kaṭittēṟuvataṟku vaḻi eṉa nīṇṭa kālam nampappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. peṇ kaḷukkuk kalvi kūṭātu eṉṉum karuttu amerikkā, iṅkilāntu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum iruntu vantatu. jappāṉilum peṇkaḷ cilarē ārampak kalvi kaṟka aṉumatikkappaṭṭārkaḷ. innili ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟ pakuti variyil jappāṉil nilaviṟṟu. ataṉ piṉ peṇkaḷ āṇ kaḷukkut tāḻntavarkaḷ eṉṉum koḷki kiviṭappaṭṭatu. iṉṟu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa jappāṉiyap peṇkaḷ kallūrikaḷilum kalācāli kaḷilum kalvi kaṟṟu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. aṅku aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ uṇṭāṉa viḻippu iṉṟu intiyāvil uṇṭākavilli. eṇṇam, putti, oḻukkaṅkaḷi vaḷarppataṟku āṇukkāyiṉum peṇṇukkāyiṉum kalvi tēvi. vitipōṉṟa kuḻanti vaḷarntu marampōla āvataṟku, allatu puḻuppōṉṟuḷḷa kuḻanti vaḷarntu maṉitaṉ pōla viḷaṅkuvataṟkuk kalviyē tuṇipurikiṉṟatu. ivvakiyāṉa utavi āṇukkāvatu peṇṇukkāvatu maṟukkappaṭutal kūṭātu. kalvi nammi āḷvataṟkum, nam pōṉṟa piṟari āḷvataṟkum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu. iṉṟu kuṭiyāṭcik kālam. iṉṟu āḷum urimi ellā makkaḷiṉ urimiyāka māṟik koṇṭu varukiṉṟatu. ivvāṟirukkum pōtu peṇṇāvatu āṇāvatu taṉatu urimiyip payaṉpaṭuttāviṭil yātu payaṉ? potumakkaḷ evvaḷavu kalviyaṟivuṭiyavarkaḷākiṉṟārkaḷō, avvaḷavukku āṭci nili uyarkiṉṟatu. eṅkuc cuyāṭci vēṇṭappaṭukiṉṟatō, aṅkel lām ellā makkaḷukkum kalvi avaciyamākiṉṟatu.

intiya nāṭṭil makkaḷil pātit tokiyiṉar peṇpāliṉar. avarkaḷ kallātavarkaḷāyum, aṟiyāmiyuṭiyavarkaḷākavum irukka muṭiyātu. makkaḷ kalviyaṟivuṭiyavarkaḷāka iruntāl māttiram kuṭi āṭci naṭipeṟum eṉa araciyal aṟiñar kūṟikiṉṟaṉar.

eppoḻutum āṭavaṉ kalviyaṟivuḷḷa peṇṇiyē maṇakka virumpukiṟāṉ. nāḷ muḻutum uḻittu ōynta kaṇavaṉukkup putticāli yāṉa maṉiviyōṭu uriyāṭuvatu kaḷiyip pōkkuvatā yirukkum. maṉivi paṭiyātavaḷāyum, kaṇavaṉukkup putti kūṟamāṭṭāta aṉupavamaṟṟa vaḷāyum, mūṭattaṉamuṭiyavaḷāyum iruppāḷāyiṉ, avaḷ kaṇavaṉiṉ naṭpiṉaḷāyirukkat takutiyaṟṟavaḷāvāḷ. paṭippatāl peṇ kuḻanti kaḷi nērāṉa vaḻiyil paḻakkuvāḷ. peṇkaḷukkuk kalvi payiṟṟuvatāl nām paṭitta kuṭumpaṅkaḷi uṇṭākkukiṉṟōm. piyaṉ oruvaṉip paṭikka vippatāl nām paṭitta oruvaṉi māttiram peṟukiṉṟōm. poruḷātāra muṟiyilum peṇkaḷukkuk kalvi iṉṟiyamiyātatu. peṇkaḷ kalvi kaṟpatāl poruḷuṟpattikaḷum, poruḷkaḷi vāṅkum takutiyum atikarikkum. ākavē oru nāṭṭukkuriya poruḷuṟpatti mutaliyaṉa iraṭṭikkum. āṇ peṇ eṉṟa vēṟupāṭu iṉṟi ellōrum tamatu vāḻkkikkup pōtumāṉa poruḷīṭṭakkūṭiya kalviyi aḷippatu araciṉariṉ kaṭami.

ovvoru kaṇavaṉum, taṉ maṉivikkum piḷḷikaḷukkum cariyāṉa muṟiyil kalvi aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟatā eṉṟu kavaṉikka vēṇṭum. kaṇavaṉiṉ maraṇattukkuppiṉ peṇ ātaravaṟṟavaḷākak kūṭātu; avaḷ piṟar kiyi etirpārttu vāḻtal kūṭātu. ātaravaṟṟa kālaṅkaḷil peṇ kaḷukkuk kalviyē ātaravaḷikkiṉṟatu. peṇ, virumpiṉāl maṇañ ceytukoḷḷātu vāḻalām. appōtu avaḷ taṉatu vāḻkkic celavit tāṉē tēṭupavaḷāka viruttal vēṇṭum. peṇṇukkut takka kalviyaḷikkā viṭṭāl tanti taṉatu kaṭamiyic ceytavaṉāka māṭṭāṉ. oru nāṭṭukkuriya makkaḷiṉ oḻukkatti uyartti avarkaḷi mēl niliyil vittalāl kalvi ovvoruvarukkum avaciyamāṉatu. kalvi, aṟivi vaḷarppatu māttiramallāmal nērmi, potut toṇṭu kaḷil ūkkam mutaliyavikaḷiyum koṭukkum.
