āṭavar, peṇkaḷukkuk kalviyaḷikka maṟuttu vantārkaḷ. ataṟkuk kāraṇam, avarkaḷ aṟiviṉ mēṉmiyāl tamakku eṉṟum kīḻppaṭintu irukkamāṭṭārkaḷ. eṉṟa cuyanalamēyākum. ikkaruttu intiyāvil māttiramaṉṟu, ulakam muḻumiyilum iruntatu. urōmāpuri yil araca kuṭumpap peṇkaḷ cilar māttiram kalvi kaṟṟārkaḷ. maṟṟap peṇkaḷukkuk kalvi payila iṭam kiṭikkavilli; āṇkaḷip pōlavē peṇkaḷukkum kalvi vēṇṭumeṉa vātāṭiyavar mārṭṭiṉ lūtar. (ki.pi.1483-1546) vayāṉ reks eṉpār ikkaruttukkāka skāt lāntil vātāṭiṉār. patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉ iṅkilāntil peṇkaḷukkeṉat taṉippaḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷ irukkavilli. peṇkaḷ cilar kiṟittuva maṭaṅkaḷil ārampak kalvi kaṟka aṉumatikkappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ ārampap pāṭacālikaḷilum uyartarap pāṭacālikaḷilum aṉumatikkappaṭṭaṉar. pirisṭal eṉṉum iṭattil 1627il, mutal 
peṇ pāṭacāli ārampamāyiṟṟu. ataṉik kaṇṭa potumakkaḷ peritum viyappaṭintārkaḷ. lūtar ki.pi. 1500 variyil ellōrukkum paṭippu vēṇṭumeṉac cātittu vantār. āṉāl 1870 āṇṭilēyē ellōrukkum kalvi avaciyam eṉṉum kalvic caṭṭam niṟivēṟiṟṟu. ataṉ piṉpē iṅkilāntil āṇkaḷukkum peṇkaḷukkum kalvi kaṭṭāyamākkappaṭṭatu. airōppiya nāṭukaḷil peṇkaḷ nili iṉṉum mōcamāyiruntu vantatu. peṇkaḷ vīṭṭukkut talimiyāyiruntu kaṇavaṉukkuk kīḻppaṭintu naṭakka vēṇṭumeṉṉuṅkaruttu carmāṉiyar uḷḷattiṟ patintiruntamiyāl, avarkaḷ peṇkaḷukku uyarnta kalvi aḷittārkaḷ. 1848kkup piṉpē peṇkaḷ uyarnta kalvipeṟa aṉumatik kappaṭṭārkaḷ. intiyāvilum aṅkumiṅkumākac cila peṇkaḷ kalvi payiṉṟiruntārkaḷ. āṉāl avarkaḷiṉ eṇ mikac cila ākum. irupa tām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉ, intiyāvil peṇkaḷ kalvinili tiruptiyuṟa villi. maṟṟa nāṭukaḷil naṭappavi intiya makkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷit tiṟakkac ceytaṉa. iṉṟu peṇkaḷukkum āṇkaḷukkum kaṭṭāya ārampakkalvi vēṇṭumeṉṉum caṭṭaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

intiyāvil peṇkaḷukkuc ciṟuvayatilēyē maṇam ceyki ṟārkaḷ. itu avarkaḷ paṭippiṟku iṭiyūrāka irukkiṉṟatu. mukamatiyap peṇkaḷ vīṭṭōṭu irukkum vaḻakkam iruntu varukiṉṟatu. cila intuk kaḷiṭiyum ivvaḻakkam uṇṭu. itaṉāl avarkaḷ potup pāṭacāli kaḷukkup pōka muṭiyāmal irukkiṉṟaṉar. kuḻanti maṇamum, vālipat til vitaviyātalum paṭippit taṭukkum iraṇṭu taṭikaḷākum.

intiyāvil ovvoru āyiram pērukkum eṇpatu pēr māttiram eḻuta vācikkakkūṭiya paṭippuḷḷavarkaḷāyirukkiṉṟaṉar. intiyāvik kāṭṭilum parmā paṭippil mēl nili aṭintuḷḷatu. itaṟkuk kāraṇam ovvoru kirāmattilumuḷḷa putta maṭaṅkaḷil āṇkaḷum peṇ kaḷum kalvi kaṟpikkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. ataṟku aṭuttapaṭiyil kocci, tiruvitāṅkūr, parōṭā mutaliya irācciyaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivvirācciyaṅ kaḷiyum cīrtūkkip pārttāl peṇ kalvi apivirutti mika aṟpamē. āṇkaḷil nūṟṟukkup patiṉmar variyil paḷḷikkūṭañ ceṉṟu kalvi payilkiṟārkaḷ. itil peṇkaḷ mūṉṟu catavītattiṉarē paḷḷikkūṭañ celkiṉṟaṉar. kocciyilum tiruvaṉantapurattilum iraṇṭu paṭitta āṭavarukku oru paṭitta peṇ irukkiṉṟāḷ. maliyāḷattilum kūrkki lum mūṉṟu paṭitta āṭavarukku oru paṭitta peṇṇum, parōṭāvilum micūrilum aintu āṭavarukku oru peṇṇumuḷar.

amerikkāvil ovvoru piḷḷiyum 14 vayatu variyum paḷḷik kūṭañ cellavēṇṭumeṉac caṭṭamuṇṭu. iṅkilāntil aintu vayatukkum 14 vayatukkumiṭiyiluḷḷa piḷḷikaḷ kaṭṭāyamākap paḷḷikkūṭañ cellavēṇṭum. uruciya nāṭṭil ellā makkaḷukkum aṟivi vaḷarkkum tākamuṇṭu. ellāk kalvi niliyaṅkaḷilum āṇkaḷiviṭap peṇ kaḷē atikam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. cōviyat uruciyāvil ārampak kalvi māttiramaṉṟu, uyartarak kalviyum ilavacamāka aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. carmaṉiyil peṇkaḷukkuk kaṭṭāyak kalvi 1931-il vitikkappaṭṭatu. ittāliyil peṇkaḷukkuk kalācālip paṭippu maṟukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ākavē avarkaḷukkuc cukavaḻi, vīṭṭu vēlikkuriya kalvi mutaliyaṉa aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ṭeṉmārkkil iḷam peṇkaḷukku vīṭṭuc cikkaṉam, tiyal, cukavaḻi mutaliyavikaḷip payiṟṟuvataṟkup paḷḷik kūṭaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. immuṟiyil ciṟuvar, ciṟumikaḷip payiṟṟuvataṟku nāṭṭup paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷum uṇṭu. ṭeṉmārkkil oru peṇṇukku maṇam niccayamāṉavuṭaṉ, avaḷ vīṭṭic cemmiyāka vittirukkum paḻakkattip payilvataṟku nāṭṭu viṭutikku ōrāṇṭu kālam aṉuppap paṭuvāḷ. cīṉāvil kalviyiṉ poruṭṭu ettaṉaṅkaḷ naṭantu varukiṉṟaṉa. aṅkup potup paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷ amittup potukkalvi kaṟpikkum muyaṟci naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. pirāṉcil āṟu vayatukkum 13 vayatukkum iṭiyiluḷḷa āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupālārukkum, muḻucērap kaṭṭāyakkalvi iruntu varukiṉṟatu. aṅku ārampak kalvikku mēluḷḷa kalviyum ilavacamāka aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. ellā nāṭukaḷilum peṇ kaḷukkum āṇkaḷukkum ārampakkalvi ilavacamum kaṭṭāyamumāka iruntu varuvati aṟikiṉṟōm.

intiyāvil kalvi mikap piṟpōkkāṉa niliyil iruntu varukiṉṟatu. itu peṇkaḷiṭiyē peritum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. peṇ kaḷukku uriya iṭam vīṭutāṉ eṉṉum karuttu maṟintu, atuvē vāḻk kikkum uyarvukkum kāraṇam eṉṉum karuttu uṇṭāka vēṇṭum.

peṇkaḷip paṟṟiya camūkac caṭṭaṅkaḷ māṟa vēṇṭum
akpar intuc caṭṭattil (intu lā) cila māṟṟaṅkaḷik koṇṭuvantār. avar, vitavikaḷi avarkaḷ viruppattukku māṟāka uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟṟutal kūṭāteṉṟum, peṇkaḷ patiṉāṉku vayatukku muṉpum, āṇkaḷ patiṉāṟu vayatukku muṉṉum maṇañceytal kūṭā teṉṟum, maṟumaṇañ ceytalil taṭi irukkakkūṭāteṉṟum caṭṭam ceytār. iccaṭṭattip paḻam pōkkuḷḷa intukkaḷ etirttārkaḷ. kālattukkēṟpa nām namatu vāḻkki muṟikaḷi māṟṟik koḷḷa vēṇṭum. oru kālattil camūkak kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷ camayac caṭṭaṅkaḷāl kaṭṭup paṭuttappaṭṭiruntaṉa. appoḻutu intiyāvil kirāmaṅkaḷ māttiram iruntaṉa. kirāmap pañcāyattiṉ tīrppē kaṭici muṭivāka iruntatu. oru kirāmavāci camūkattukkuriya caṭṭattik kaṭantāl avaṉ kirāmap pañcāyattil cēravum muṭiyātu. avaṉuṭiya iṉpak kālattilō, allatu tuṉpak kālattilō avaṉukku oruvarum utavi aḷikkak kūṭātu. oruvaṉukku vēṇṭiyavi ellām kirāmattilēyē aṭaṅki yiruntaṉa. ākavē ovvoruvaṉum camūkakkaṭṭupāṭṭukku aṭaṅki yiruntāṉ. taṉippaṭṭavaṉiṉ ceyalukkuc cutantiram irukkavilli. evaṉāvatu tāṉ virumpiya toḻilic ceyya muṭiyātu. cātiyē avaṉavaṉ toḻili muṭivu ceytatu. iṉṟu av vakiyāṉa kālamaṉṟu. iṉṟu putiya, putiya poruḷkaḷiyum, iyantiraṅkaḷiyum, nāṭukaḷiyum kaṇṭaṟiyum kālam. oru irācciyamāvatu taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyil taṉit tirukka muṭiyātu. pala talimuṟikaḷukku muṉ irunta kirāmaṅkaḷ iṉṟu vēṟu vakiyil māṟiyuḷḷaṉa. iṉṟu camūkak kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷukku valimi yilli. niyāyam kēṭṭaṟiyāmalē, periyavarkaḷukkuk kīḻppaṭiyum kālam maṟintuviṭṭatu. intup pattirikiyiṉ āciriyarāyirunta cuppiramaṇiya aiyar orumuṟi kūṟiyuḷḷatu varumāṟu: “iṉṟu ettaṉip pirāmaṇak kuṭumpattiṉar vētaṅkaḷilum smirutikaḷilum collappaṭṭa kiriyi kaḷic ceykiṉṟaṉar. intiyā iṉi orupōtum paḻiya kālattukkut tirumpip pōkamāṭṭātu. itu āṅkilēyar turūyittiyar (Druid) kālat tukkup pōkamāṭṭātatu pōṉṟatu.” iṉṟu kittoḻilkaḷ uḷḷa paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ perukiyuḷḷaṉa. kirāmattiliruntu oruvaṉ veḷiyēṟṟap paṭṭāl avaṉ paṭṭiṉattiluḷḷa toḻiṟcālikaḷil vēṇṭappaṭu vāṉ. paṭṭiṉattil kuḻāyttaṇṇīr irukkiṟatu. ākavē, avaṉ kirāmak kiṇaṟṟukkut taṇṇīr eṭukkappōka vēṇṭiyatilli. ivvāṟu vāḻkkimuṟi virivil māṟi varukiṉṟatu. airōppiya nākarikattiṉ etiril cātik kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷ maṟintu varukiṉṟaṉa. kūṭṭuk kuṭumpa vāḻkkiyum maṟintu celkiṉṟatu. ivikaḷ ellām kālattukku ēṟṟa māṟṟaṅkaḷ. ellā iyaṟkiyum kālattukkuk kālam ciṟitu ciṟitāka māṟṟamaṭikiṉṟaṉa. uyiraṟṟaviyē māṟṟamaṭivatilli. putiya āṟṟu nīr ōṭik koṇṭirukkiṟatu; ati maṟittu vittāl atu tēṅki aḻuk kaṭikiṉṟatu: itu pōlēvē oru cātiyum ataṉ vāḻkkiyum. nām virumpiṉālum virumpāviṭṭālum mutumiyi aṭikiṉṟōm. kuḻantikaḷ ciṟu peṇkaḷākiṉṟaṉar; ciṟu peṇkaḷ periya peṇkaḷāki mutumiyaṭikiṟārkaḷ. immāṟṟaṅkaḷukkēṟṟavāṟu nām naṭantu koḷḷavēṇṭum. eṅkaḷil palar ulakam māṟavilli eṉa niṉik kiṟārkaḷ? avarkaḷ tamatu maṉati iṟuka mūṭi vittirukkiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ maṉatil putiya karuttukkaḷ nuḻiya muṭiyātu. cīrtiruttatti niṉippatip pōlap payam, avarkaḷukku vēṟoṉṟum illi.

maṉusmiruti ki.mu.200il eḻutappaṭṭatu. yākñavalkiyiṉ nīti ki.pi.4-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭatu. nārata smiruti ki.pi.6ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭatu. ivi irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭukku ēṟṟaṉavā eṉṟu nām niṉittal vēṇṭum. paḻiyaṉa vellām putiyaṉavaṟṟukku iṭam koṭukkavēṇṭum eṉṉum vitiyi nām maṟantu viṭutal ākātu. oru kālattil intiyā ellā nāṭukaḷilum pārkkac ciṟantiruntateṉpatu uṇmiyē. iṉṟu intiyāviviṭa airōppā ellā vakiyilum ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.

intiyāviṉ pātic celvanili pātikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. peṇ kaḷukku uriya iṭam vīṭumāttiram eṉa makkaḷ niṉippatē itaṟkuk kāraṇam. kuḻanti maṇam peṇkaḷiṉ kalviyik keṭukkiṉṟatu. iḷamiyiṟ kuḻantikaḷip peṟutal, avarkaḷiṉ cuka vāḻviyum valimiyiyum keṭukkiṉṟatu. intiya makkaḷiṉ carācari varuvāy maṟṟiya nāṭukaḷiviṭa mikak kuṟivu. itaṉip pōkkuvataṟkup perun tokip peṇkaḷ avaravarkaḷukkēṟṟa vēlikaḷip purital vēṇ ṭum. immuṟiyil jappāṉ, uruciyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷ ciṟantuḷḷaṉa. paṭitta tāy, kuḻantikaḷi naṟkāriyaṅkaḷil paḻakkuvāḷ. “tāyippōṟ piḷḷi; nūlippōṟ cīli” eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.

iṉṟu paṭitta vakuppiṉariṭiyē peṇkaḷip paṟṟiyē putiya uṇarcci valuvaṭintu varukiṉṟatu.

intiyāvil makaḷiriṉum pārkka āṭavar matippaṭinta kāraṇam
intiyāvilē tanti āṭcik kālattil tantiyē uyarnta taliva ṉākaviruntāṉ. avaṉē iṟantavar vaṇakkattukkuriya kiriyikaḷic ceytāṉ. iṟakkumpōtu avaṉ taṉatu kaṭamiyit taṉ mūtta makaṉiṭam oppaṭittāṉ. iṟantavar vaṇakkattip paṟṟiya iyaṟki viti, itu veṉa makkaḷ nampat talippaṭṭārkaḷ. itaṉāl ovvoru kuṭumpat tilum uḷḷa tanti, taṉatu kaṭamiyip pārppataṟkup putalvaṉē vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpiṉāṉ. itaṉāl peṇkaḷ putalvarkaḷippōlac ciṟantavarkaḷākak karutappaṭṭilar. peṇkaḷit tāḻvākak karutum koḷki, nāḷtōṟum palavāṟu vaḷarcciyaṭintatu; kuḻanti maṇam, peṇkaḷ maṟumaṇa maṟuppu, peṇ kalvi maṟuppuppōṉṟa tīmikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. iṉṟu airōppiya nākarikattiṉ toṭarpāl peṇkaḷi vīṭṭukkuḷ aṭittu vittalum, avarkaḷi aṟiyāmiyil āḻttu vatum nāṭṭiṉ palavīṉattukkuk kāraṇam eṉat teriyavarukiṉṟaṉa. cila kālattiṉ muṉ peṇkaḷukkuk kalvi ākātu eṉa irunta koḷki iṉṟu vali peṟavilli. peṇkaḷukkuk kalvi, maṟumaṇam pōṉṟaṉa vēṇṭumeṉṟum, avarkaḷi aṭittu vittal kūṭāteṉṉum koḷkikaḷ iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa. peṇkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattukku māṟāka irunta pala kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷi āṅkilak kalvi takarttu varukiṉṟatu. āṇ kaḷukku maṟumaṇam avaciyameṉṟu kūṟi, peṇkaḷukku atu kūṭāteṉṟu taṭuttal niyāyamaṟṟa ceyaleṉap palar karutukiṉṟaṉar.



oḻukkam peṇkaḷukkum vēṇṭum; āṇkaḷukkum vēṇṭum
oru kālattil āṇ peṇ irupālārum oruvariṉum pārkka maṟṟavar uyarntavar eṉak karutātu, vāḻkkiyiṉ kaṭamikaḷi avaravar kaḷukku ēṟṟavāṟu pirittukkoṇṭu vāḻkki naṭattiṉārkaḷ eṉa ōr iṭattil kūṟiṉōm. piṟkālattil peṇ, kaṇavaṉukku aṭimiyip pōl aṭaṅki naṭakkavēṇṭumeṉṟum, avaḷ orē kaṇavaṉ viratamuṭiya vaḷāyiruntu taṉatu oḻukkattik kākkavēṇṭumeṉṟum, āṭavaṉukku oḻukkam avaciyamilliyeṉṟum, avaṉ tāṉ virumpiya pala peṇ kaḷi maṇantu koḷḷalāmeṉṟum camūka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ tōṉṟalāyiṉa. peṇkaḷ āṭavariṉ ittavaṟāṉa karuttukkaḷukku māṟākak kiḷampātapaṭi peṇṇiṉ kaṭami kaṇavaṉiṉ viruppattukku iciya oḻukutalēyeṉac camayac caṭṭaṅkaḷum nītikaḷum uṇṭāyiṉa. avarkaḷ kalvi aḷikkap paṭātu iruḷil aṭikkappaṭṭum iruntaṉar. iṉṟu peṇkaḷ ulakam viḻippaṭintuḷḷatu. ulaka makkaḷil peṇkaḷiṉ toki pātiyākum. peṇkaḷukkum āṭavarip pōṉṟa cama urimikaḷ irukkavēṇṭu meṉa ellā nāṭukaḷilum kiḷarcci naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. atil avarkaḷ veṟṟiyaṭintuḷḷārkaḷ.

oru kuṭumpattik koṇṭu iḻuppatil kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum, oru vaṇṭiyi iḻukkum iru erutukaḷip pōṉṟu irukkiṟārkaḷ. ivikaḷil oṉṟu cemmiyāka iḻukkāviṭṭāl vaṇṭi cellātu. peṇ orē maṇam uṭiyavaḷāy irukka vēṇṭiyatu caṭṭamāṉāl, atē viti āṇkaḷukkum ēṉ irukkakkūṭātu eṉṉum kēḷvi eḻukiṉṟatu. peṇkaḷ oru kaṇavaṉiyaṉṟi vēṟu kaṇavaṉi virumpak kūṭātu eṉṉum caṭṭamirukkumāṉāl, āṭavar pala peṇkaḷi virumpa lām; maṇakkalām eṉṉum caṭṭaṅkaḷ iruppatum oḻukkak kēṭākavē karutappaṭum.

makāttumā kānti ōriṭattiṟ kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu: “peṇ āṇiṉ tuṇivi. avaḷukku avaṉippōṉṟa maṉa āṟṟal uṇṭu. avaḷukku avaṉ muyalum ovvoru muyaṟcikaḷilum paṅku peṟum urimiyuṇṭu. avaḷukku avaṉi otta cutantaramuṇṭu. tīya camūkac caṭṭa palattiṉāl mika aṟiyāmiyum takutiyiṉmiyumuṭiya āṇkaḷ tāṉum peṇkaḷi aṭakki āḷukiṉṟaṉar. avvāṟu avarkaḷ oru pōtum ceytal kūṭātu. eṅkaḷ iyakkaṅkaḷ pāti vaḻiyil niṉṟu pōvataṟkuk kāraṇam peṇkaḷiṉ nilimiyiṉāl ākum. eṅkaḷ muyaṟcikaḷ atika palaṉ aḷippatilli. cōṭiyāka vāḻum iruvaril, oruvarukku maṟṟavar utaviyāyirukkiṉṟār. oruvariṉ utaviyillāmal maṟṟavar niṟivaṭital muṭiyātu. oruvariṉ naṉmiyip pātikkak kūṭiya ceyal maṟṟavariṉ aḻivukkum kāraṇamākum.”

kamalātēvi caṭōpāttiyāya kūṟuvatu varumāṟu: “kaṇavaṉ maṉivikkuk kaṇṭippāṉa caṭṭatiṭṭaṅkaḷi uṇṭupaṇṇi viṭṭut tāṉ niṉittapaṭi naṭakkalām eṉṉum ik kālak karuttu mika āpat tāṉatu. iṉimēl irupālārukkum orē vakiyāṉa nītiyē irukka muṭiyum. ataṉik kaṭakka muṭiyātu. oruvarukkup piḻiyākat tōṉṟuvatu maṟṟavarukkuc cariyāka virukkamuṭiyātu. piṟappip pōla iṟappum potu vāṉa nikaḻcci. ākavē kaṇavaṉi iḻanta peṇṇik kīḻmippaṭutti avaḷukkuk koṭiya caṭṭaṅkaḷi vitittal kūṭātu. āṇ, peṇkaḷ palari maṇattali aṉumatittup peṇ oru kaṇavaṉittāṉ maṇakka lām eṉa vitittalum kūṭātu. nāḷi eḻum caṭṭattil iru pālārum orē maṇamtāṉ ceyyalām eṉa varutal vēṇṭum. irupālārukkum cila kāraṇaṅkaḷi muṉṉiṭṭu virumpiṉāl maṇaṅkaḷi nīkkik koḷḷavum aṉumati vēṇṭum.”

peṇkaḷ pōrāṭṭam
(Women’s Struggle)
varalāṟṟil peṇkaḷ pakuti
varalāṟṟukku eṭṭakkūṭiya kālam mutal peṇkaḷum varalāṟṟiṉ pakutiyāka iruntu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. peṇṇē varalāṟṟukku ātāramāka vuḷḷavaḷ; āyiṉum avaḷ varalāṟu iruḷaṭintuḷḷatu. araciyar, kaṭṭaḻakiyar, vilimātar, kaṭavuḷpaktarkaḷ pōṉṟa peṇkaḷ cilarē varalāṟukaḷil aṅkum iṅkum tōṉṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. maṟṟiya peṇ camūkattiṉariṉ vāḻkkic ceytikaḷ kālam iṉṉum iruḷil maṟintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.

namakku muṟpaṭṭa makkaḷ evvāṟu vāḻntārkaḷ eṉṟu namakkut terikiṉṟatu. kālantōṟum evvāṟu poruḷātāra māṟutalkaḷ uṇṭā kiṉṟaṉa; eṇṇaṅkaḷ, kalvimuṟi, pōr oḻuṅku, āṭcimuṟi pōlvaṉa vevvēṟu kālaṅkaḷil evvāṟiruntaṉa eṉpavikaḷip paṟṟiyum aṟikiṉṟōm. ivvaki māṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟa kālaṅkaḷil vīṭṭil vāḻum peṇṇiṉ nili evvāṟiruntateṉpati nām ūkiyiṉāl māttiram aṟiya muṭikiṉṟatu. āṭavar makaḷiriṉ toḻilmuṟi, paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭu avarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya poruḷkaḷ pōlvaṉa ikkālac carittirakkārarāl teḷivuṟuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl peṇkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟa vaḷarcci paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ uyttaṟital aḷavil iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa.

āṭavar tāmē nākarikatti muṉṉukkuk koṇṭu vantavarkaḷ eṉṟum, peṇkaḷ eppoḻutum tamakkup piṉṉāl tamakkāka aṭuppi yum, camiyaliyum, vīṭṭiyum kavaṉittuk kuḻantikaḷi vaḷarttu vantārkaḷ eṉṟum eṇṇukiṟārkaḷ. ik karuttu varalāṟṟil muṉṉum iruntatu, iṉṉum irukkiṉṟatu. varalāṟṟāciriyar uḷḷattil ik karuttup patintiruntamiyiṉ avarkaḷ peṇkaḷippaṟṟi atikam ārāycci ceyvatil kavali koḷḷavilli. āṭavar makaḷir oḻuk kaṅkaḷ, tirumaṇa vaḻakkaṅkaḷip paṟṟiya ārāyccikaḷ tamatu nūlkaḷil taṟceyalāka eḻumpōtu māttiram avarkaḷ peṇkaḷ toṭarpāṉa cila aṭikkuṟippukaḷi eḻutuvatu vaḻakkam. ivvāṟu avarkaḷ ceyvataṉ kāraṇam avar eḻutum nūlkaḷ maṉita varalāṟākaviruppatiṉālum, peṇṇiṉ varalāṟāyillāmiyiṉālumēyākum. peṇkaḷum āṭavari otta makkaḷ vakuppiṉarallavā? pañcam, neruppu, veḷḷam, nōy mutaliyavakikaḷāl āṭavarukkup pōlavē makaḷirukkum tuṉpam nērukiṉṟatu; irupālārukkum oruvāṟē kuḷir aṭikkiṉṟatu; veyilum avvāṟē kāykiṉṟatu; uyir vāḻkki, makiḻcci, iṉpam, maraṇam mutaliyaṉavum orē vakiyākavuḷḷaṉa. ivikaḷē maṉita camūkattiṉ varalāṟāyiṉ atil peṇkaḷi nīkkik kūṟavēṇṭiyatilli.

nam muṉṉōr kukikaḷilum marakkiḷikaḷilum vāḻntapōtum, maṇṇiṉāl kuṭicikaḷ amitta pōtum, vilaṅkukaḷukku añci naṭuṅkiyapōtum āṭavar, makaḷir varalāṟukaḷ orē vakiyākaviruntaṉa. kaḻintakāla makkaḷ vāḻkkiyil ivikaḷallāta aṭippaṭiyāṉa pala uṇmikaḷum iruntaṉa. maṉita vāḻkki, pulappaṭāta pala āṟṟalkaḷāl kaṭṭuppaṭuttappaṭṭu maṉita eṇṇattiṉ valimiyāl māṟṟamaṭintu vaḷacciyaṭintatu. eḻuttu muṟiyāṉa varalāṟṟukku muṟpaṭṭa paḻiya paṇpaṭāta vāḻkkiyōṭu putiya eṇṇaṅkaḷum cērkkappaṭṭaṉa. oḻuṅkupaṭṭa pōrmuṟi, payirttoḻil, mantiravitti mutaliyaṉa tōṉṟiyapōtu peṇkaḷatum, āṇkaḷatum vāḻkki muṟikaḷ vev vēṟu vakikaḷil pirintu ceṉṟaṉa. ivvaki māṟupaṭṭa vāḻkkikaḷ, makkaḷ camūkamāka vāḻattoṭaṅkiyatu mutal camayattiṉ atikārattuṭ paṭṭatu variyil ārampikkiṉṟaṉa. akkālam variyil āṭavarum makaḷirum cēmamāka vāḻntaṉar. irupālāriṉ varalāṟukaḷum orēvaki yāka iruntaṉaveṉpatu uṇmiyākātu. peṇkaḷiṉ camūkanili eppoḻutum āṭavariviṭa vēṟuvakiyākaviruntatu. irupālārum vevvēṟu vakiyāka vāḻntārkaḷ.

irupālāruṭiya varalāṟukaḷiyum ārāyntu evvaḷavukku avarkaḷ orē vakiyāka iruntārkaḷ eṉa aṟintukoṇṭāl pala ceytikaḷ namakkup pulappaṭum. peṇkaḷ āṭavariṭam taṅkiyirup pavarkaḷ, avarkaḷ āṭavariṉum kīḻppaṭṭavarkaḷ eṉak koḷḷappaṭṭa pōtum avarkaḷ maṉita camūkattil pāti aḷaviṉarāvar. ākavē avar kaḷiṉ varalāṟu maṉita varalāṟṟil pāti aḷaviṉatākum.
nāṉku taṉmikaḷ āṭavariliruntu peṇkaḷip pirittuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi: pāl vēṟupāṭu, pōrttoḻil, poruḷātāranili, karuttukkaḷ eṉpaṉa. iv vēṟupāṭukaḷ oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu oppiṭṭu aṟiya muṭiyāta vevvēṟu taṉmikaḷuṭiyaṉa; āṉāl ivi peṇkaḷiṉ niliyit tīrmāṉikkum kēḷvikaḷāka amintuḷḷaṉa.

irupālāriṉ uṭalamippuc campantam mutaṉmiyāṉatu. iḵtē ellāvaki niyāyaṅkaḷukkum aṭippaṭi. tāyāyirukkum uṭal campantamāṉa cumi illātavaḷāyiruntāl peṇ orupōtum āṭavaṉukku valimiyāl kuṟintavaḷākamāṭṭāḷ. āṇ peṇ eṉṟa pāl vēṟupāṭṭāl vēlikaḷ pirikkappaṭavum māṭṭā. ivi kāraṇa mākat tōṉṟiya vaḻakkaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭamāṭṭā. appoḻutu varalāṟu evvakiyāka irukkumeṉṟum niṉittu aṟiyamuṭiyātu. oruvēḷi nām tēṉīkkaḷippōla naṉṟāyaminta camūka vāḻvum, vāḻkkit takutiyum uṭiyavarkaḷākavum, taṉittaṉi vāḻvatil viruppillātavar kaḷāyum irukkalām.

peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷilum pārkka uṭal valiyāl kuṟintōr. uṭal valiyiṉmiyē avarkaḷi iṉṟiya nilikkuk koṇṭu vantatu. camūkamē taṉatu pātiyiṉari mūṭamāka vittirukkum koḷkiyi āmōtikkiṉṟatu. itu aṟivuṭimi eṉak kūṟa muṭiyātu.

makkaḷ oruvarōṭu oruvar neṭukilum pōr viḷittuk koṇ ṭiruntamiyāl peṇkaḷum āṇkaḷum vevvēṟu vāḻkki muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟum nili ēṟpaṭṭatu. pōr eppoḻutum peṇkaḷ niliyik keṭuttuvantatu.

pōril peṇkaḷ veṟṟiyāḷariṉ koḷḷip poruḷkaḷā ṉārkaḷ. āṭavar evvaḷavukku mūrkkamum pōrkkuṇamum uṭiyavar kaḷāyiruntārkaḷō avvaḷavukkup peṇkaḷiṉ nilimiyum kīḻmi yaṭintatu. pōriṉāl naṉmikkētuvāṉa cila māṟutalkaḷuṇṭāvatu muṇṭu. ceṉṟa iraṇṭu pōrkaḷāl peṇkaḷiṉ nili māṟutalaṭintatu. muṟkālappōrkaḷ pōlallāmal ceṉṟa iraṇṭu pōrkaḷum poruḷā tāratti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭavi. poruḷātāram pōr ceyvati otta mutaṉmiyuṭiyatu. ceṉṟa pōrkaḷil peṇkaḷiṉ utaviyaṟṟa nili maṟintu pōyiṟṟu. avarkaḷum avarkaḷiṉ vēlikaḷum pōrukku vēṇṭappaṭṭaṉa. muṉpōlap pōriṉ muṭivi etirpārt tirukka vēṇṭiya nilimi avarkaḷukku uṇṭākavilli. veṭi maruntu tayārikkum iṭaṅkaḷilum toḻiṟcālikaḷilum avarkaḷukkut tēviyiruntatu. pōr aṇikaḷilum avarkaḷ āṭavaruṭaṉ tōḷōṭu tōḷāka niṉṟu pōr ceytārkaḷ. muṟkālap pōrkaḷ ivvāṟirāmal peṇkaḷiṉ aḻivukkuk kāraṇamāyiruntaṉa. itaṉālēyē peṇkaḷ nīṇṭakālam kīḻnili aṭintiruntārkaḷ.

poruḷātāra nili peṇkaḷi viṭutali aṭiyac ceykiṉṟatu. itilum vitivilaṅkukaḷ pala uṇṭu. peṇkaḷ viṭutali aṭital āṭavarukkup payam viḷivikkiṉṟatu. ulakam muḻuvatilum peṇkaḷ kuṟinta kūlikku vēli vāṅkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum avarkaḷ tāḻnta vāḻkki naṭattukiṉṟaṉar; āṉāl toḻil purivataṟku avarkaḷ iṉṟiyamiyātavarkaḷāyirukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ naṉku pātukākkappaṭāmalum, naṉṟākak kūli koṭukkappaṭāmalum, oḻuṅkāka amikkappaṭāmalum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. iṉṟu vēli puriyum peṇ orutti kaṇavaṉuṭiya cumiyiyum taṉṉuṭiya cumiyiyum cumakkavēṇṭiya vaḷāyirukkiṉṟāḷ. peṇṇiṉ nilimi ivvāṟu varuntattakkatā kiṉṟatu. tamakku ivvaḷavu vāyppāṉa nilimi tōṉṟuvataṟku āṭavar eppaṭi aṉumatittaṉar? iyaṟkikku māṟupaṭṭa ikkoṭumi evvāṟu uṇṭāyiṟṟu? paḻampōkkuṭiya varalāṟu itaṟku maṟumoḻi aḷikkac ciṟitum muṉvaravilli. aracāṭciyum itaṉi nīkkuvataṟku muṉvara villi.

peṇkaḷik kaṭṭuppaṭuttukiṉṟa periya vallami camayam, oḻukkam, camūkac caṭṭaṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa. ikkaṭṭuppāṭukaḷi uṇṭākkiṉa varkaḷ peṇkaḷum, peṇ tattuva cāttirikaḷum, peṇ camayapōtakar kaḷumallar.

muṟkāla vāḻkkiyil peṇṇē mika mukkiyamuṭiyavaḷā yiruntāḷ. piṟkāla vāḻkkiyil peṇkaḷ aṭimikaḷ pōlāṉārkaḷ; avarkaḷ tamatu camūkattiṉ vēlikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum ceytārkaḷ; āṭavar uṇṭu eñciyati uṇṭārkaḷ. kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭilum piṟacamayak koḷkikaḷilum peṇṇip paṟṟiya eṇṇamē atikamākaviruntatu. āṇ teyvaṅkaḷettaṉiyō attaṉi peṇ teyvaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa. iyaṟki peṇṇākavē uruvakañ ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

peṇkaḷip paṟṟiya pala karuttukkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. avikaḷil oṉṟu mikavum kaṭumiyākak karutappaṭṭatu. peṇṇiṉ kaṭamikaḷ, avaḷ naṟkuṇaṅkaḷ, avaḷ āṇkaḷōṭu naṭantukoḷḷa vēṇṭiya muṟikaḷ campantamāṉavi eṉpaṉavām. ik karuttukkaḷ caṭṭaṅkaḷum, nampikki kaḷumāka māṟiṉa. ivi kaṇakkillāta vakiyil ovvoru kālattilum pōtikkappaṭṭaṉa. peṇkaḷ eppaṭi naṟkuṇamuḷḷavarkaḷāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉa virumpappaṭṭaṉar eṉpati itu niṉivūṭṭukiṉṟatu. peṇkaḷ campantappaṭṭa aḷavil avarkaḷ kaṟpāyirukkavēṇṭumeṉa vaṟpuṟuttappaṭṭatu. āṇ peṇ cērkki campantamāṉa iyaṟki vitiyic camayamum, valimiyum, pōtaṉikaḷum kaṭṭuppaṭuttutal muṟiyākumā?

muṟkālattil āṇ peṇ cērkkit toṭarpāṉa caṭṭaṅkaḷ uṇṭāvataṟkup peṇṇē kāraṇamāyiruntāḷ. oru kālattil camūkat til āṇkaḷ ellōrukkum ellāp peṇkaḷiṭattilum urimi yiruntatu; peṇkaḷ ellōrukkum āṇkaḷiṭattil urimiyiruntatu. āccariyamāṉa immuṟi evvāṟu naṭipeṟṟatu? piṉpu ivvoḻukkam evvāṟu tavirkkappaṭṭatu? pala kaṇavari maṇanta peṇṇukkum, pala maṉiviyari maṇanta kaṇavaṉukkum avvaḻakkaṅkaḷ evvāṟu tōṉṟiṉa. peṇ eti virumpiṉāḷ? cila kūṭṭattiṉar oṉṟi ēṉ virumpukiṟārkaḷ? vēṟu kūṭṭattiṉar māṟṟatti ēṉ virumpukiṟārkaḷ? ulakil tāyāṭci evvaki māṟṟattic ceytatu? ivi pōṉṟavi ārāyattakkaṉa. tantiyi aṟutiyiṭa muṭiyāmal iruntapōtu piḷḷikaḷ tāy vaḻiyāl aṟiyappaṭṭārkaḷ. cottu tāy mūlam piḷḷikaḷi aṭintatu. tāyāṭciyil iruvaki vaḻakkukaḷ iruntaṉa. oṉṟu avaḷic cottuṭiyavaḷāyum atikāramuṭiyavaḷāyum ākkuvatu. peṇkaḷiṉ varalāṟu teḷḷitil aṟintu koḷḷa iyalātatāyirukkiṉ ṟatu. atu ōr iṭattil oru vakiyākavum, iṉṉōriṭattil iṉṉoru vakiyākavum iruntateṉa aṟikiṉṟōm.

kiṭikkiṉṟa cāṉṟukaḷāl peṇkaḷ peritum tāḻvāka naṭattap paṭṭārkaḷ eṉat terikiṟatu. ikkālattilēyē peṇkaḷiṉ kaṭṭukkaḷ taḷarvaṭintaṉa. kāṭṭumirāṇṭittaṉattiliruntu muṉṉēṟṟam oḻuṅkāka uṇṭāṉateṉṟu nām niṉikkap paḻaki viṭṭōm. evvakiyāṉa nākarikattilum ceṉṟa nālāyiram aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷil peṇkaḷ uyar nili aṭiyavilli.

pāpilōṉil peṇkaḷ nili
paḻiya kāṭṭumirāṇṭikaḷ nakaril vāḻpavarkaḷāka māṟiṉar. avarkaḷ vāḻnta ulakiṉ pala pakutikaḷil niliyāṉa caṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. avi pala āyiram āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ tōṉṟiyavi. avi peṇkaḷi viruppampōl vāḻa urimi aḷittaṉa eṉak kūṟamuṭiya villi. paḻiya nākarikaṅkaḷiṟ pala iṉṟu muṟṟilum maṟintu pōy viṭṭaṉa. maṟṟavikaḷip paṟṟip paḻaṅkatikaḷ mūlam uyttaṟiyalām. ekiptiyar cumēriyarkaḷippaṟṟiya eḻuttu mūlamāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avikaḷikkoṇṭu nīlanati, tikirās, aipirāttu āṟukaḷi aṭutta nāṭukaḷil vāḻnta makkaḷippaṟṟi nām aṟiyalām. avarkaḷiṉ varalāṟu ki.mu.4000 variyil celkiṉṟatu. mecapaṭōmiyāvil peṇkaḷ āṇ kaḷi otta urimi peṟṟu vāḻntārkaḷ eṉat terikiṟatu. maṇamāṉa peṇkaḷum maṇamākāta peṇkaḷum cottuṭiyavarkaḷāyiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ uṭaṉpaṭikkikaḷiṟ kiyeḻuttiṭavum, cāṭci niṟkavum, poṟup pāṉa karumaṅkaḷi naṭattavum urimi peṟṟiruntaṉar. irupālārum vāṇikam purintaṉar; niyāyattārarākavum (Judges) mākāṇa āṭcit talivar (Governors) kaḷākavum iruntaṉar. irupāliṉarum eḻutak kaṟṟuk koḷḷalām, eḻuttu vēlikaḷ ceyyalām. kōyilkaḷil kaṭavuḷaric cēvikkalām, kurumārākiya peṇkaḷ celvamum matippum uṭiyavar kaḷāyiruntaṉar. tāymār piḷḷikaḷip pātukāppōrāyiruntaṉar. putalvar, putalviyarukkup peṟṟōriṉ cottil cama urimi iruntatu. curuṅkak kūṟumiṭattu mecapeṭōmiyāviṉ nili iṉṟiya niliyi ottuḷḷatu. tirumaṇattoṭarpāka viyappāṉa caṭṭaṅkaḷ cila iruntaṉa; avi avvaḷavu viḷakkamaḷippaṉavāyirukkavilli. peṇkaḷum āṇkaḷum virumpiyavāṟu kaliyāṇa nīkkam ceytu koḷḷalām. peṇṇiṉ etirppu ataṟkut taṭiyāka irukkavilli. peṇ kōpak kāri allatu kaṇavaṉiṭam vēṇṭā veṟuppāy iruppavaḷ eṉṟu cāṉṟuppaṭuttiṉāl avaḷ nīril amiḻttappaṭṭāḷ. vipacārak kuṟṟaṅ kaḷukkum vēṟu kuṟṟaṅkaḷukkum kuṟṟavāḷikaḷi nīril amiḻttik kolvatu potuvāṉa taṇṭaṉiyākaviruntatu. ak kālattukkuppiṉ attaṇṭaṉikaḷ iṉṟuvari kāṇappaṭavilli. ki.mu. 2100 variyil hamurapi eṉpavaṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa caṭṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭu akkālap peṇkaḷ vāḻkki evvāṟiruntateṉṟum nām aṟintu koḷḷalām.




ekiptiyap peṇkaḷ
atē kālattil ekiptiya peṇkaḷ nilaṅkaḷuṭiyavarkaḷāyirun taṉar; cottu, peṇvaḻiyi aṭintatu, peṟṟōrik kāppāṟṟum poṟuppu avaḷiyē cērntatu. paḻiya cumēriyāvil iruntatu pōṉṟa uyarnta viṭutali avarkaḷukku irukkavilli. aintām āṟām araca paramparik kālaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷ vayal vēlikaḷ ceytārkaḷ. peṇ orutti cukkāṉ piṭittu marakkalatti ōṭṭum ōviyam oṉṟu kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. araca kuṭumpattavarkaḷallāta peṇkaḷ araciṟ karumaṅkaḷil paṅku eṭukkavilli. peṇkaḷ vāṇikam allatu potu vēlikaḷip purintārkaḷ eṉa viḷaṅkavilli. haṟceppaṟ eṉṉum irāṇi ulakil periya aṟivāḷi eṉak karutappaṭṭāḷ. irāṇikkuḷḷa cutantiraṅkaḷukkum, potuvāṉa peṇkaḷ peṟṟirunta cutantiraṅkaḷukkum yātum toṭarpu irukkavilli.

peṇkaḷ kiḷarcci ceytatāl cumēriyap peṇkaḷ viṭutali yaṭintiruntārkaḷ eṉak kūṟa muṭiyātu. atu uyarvāṉa tāyāṭci muṟiyiṉāl uṇṭāṉatu. avvaki makkaḷ nakaraṅkaḷil kuṭumpaṅkaḷāka vāḻat toṭaṅkiyapōtu peṇkaḷ camūkattil perumiyuṭiyavarkaḷā ṉārkaḷ. nakaraṅkaḷ periyaṉavākumpōtu taṭiyiṉṟi āṭavar makaḷiriṉ perumikaḷ camamuṭiyaṉavākum; ataṟkut taṭi uṇṭākamāṭṭātu.

tāyāṭci muṟiyiliruntu peṇkaḷiṉ atikāram tōṉṟi yirukku meṉṟu varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ cintittup pārkkavilli. paḻaṅkatikaḷil vīrappeṇkaḷip paṟṟik kāṇkiṉṟōm. iv vakip paḻaṅkatikaḷi liruntē ‘amēcaṉ’ eṉṉum vīrat taṉmi vāynta makaḷir kati tōṉ ṟiṟṟu. cila kūṭṭattiṉaril peṇkaḷum āṇkaḷiṭiyē pōrukkuc ceṉṟārkaḷ. itaṉāl attakiya vīrap peṇkaḷiṉ kati piṉṉar tōṉṟiṟṟu.

pōrvīrap peṇkaḷ
kālattukkuk kālam pōr ceyyum peṇkaḷ tōṉṟiṉārkaḷ. āpirikkāvil takōmi (Dahomey) makkaḷuḷ paṭiyil pērpātiyiṉar peṇkaḷāvar. pokēmiyil koṭuṅkōlāṭci ceyta ciṟṟaracaṉi vīḻttuvataṟku avarkaḷil oru kūṭṭattiṉar tiraṇṭaṉar. 1915 variyil uruciyappaṭiyil peṇkaḷ pativu ceyyappaṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷukkup peṇ atikāri niyamikkappaṭṭiruntāḷ. āṇ vīrarukkup pōlavē avarkaḷukku uṇavum ūtiyamum koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. 1cōṉ āp ārk allatu 2poṭīciyā (Boadicia) pōṉṟavarkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷ kaṟpaṉik katikaḷalla. ākavē āṟāyiram allatu ēḻāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ iliciyāvil vīrap peṇmaṇikaḷum iruntārkaḷ eṉak kūṟum varalāṟu kaṟpaṉiyākamāṭṭātu.
cumēriya vaḻakkaṅkaḷ evvāṟu tōṉṟiya pōtum avi maṟin taṉa, avi caṭutiyil maṟintaṉa allatu kālantāḻntu maṟintaṉa eṉṟu nammāl kūṟamuṭiyātu. ippoḻutu pāpilōṉ oru iṭipāṭṭu mēṭāka virukkiṉṟatu. ekiptiya aracarum ciṅkācaṉattāl vīḻntuviṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷukkup piṉvanta cemittiyarum intu airōppiyarum vēṟu vaki nākarikatti nuḻintārkaḷ.

kirēkka nāṭṭup peṇkaḷ
ki.mu.1000 variyil kirēkkar eccātiyiṉariliruntu tōṉṟiṉār kaḷō accātiyiṉarum peṇkaḷiṉ cutantirattiviṭṭu vikkavilli. āyiṉum avarkaḷ paḻaṅkatikaḷil vīrap peṇkaḷip paṟṟiya varalāṟukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṇṭikōṉ3. elakrā4 mutaliyōr varalāṟukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, tiṉacari vāḻkkiyil mukkiyamuṭiyavarkaḷākak kirēkkap peṇkaḷ koḷḷappaṭavilli; avarkaḷiṉ cēvi vēṇṭi yiruntamiyāl avarkaḷ uyirkaḷ kāppāṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. uṇṇutal, uṭuttal, parukutal, tuṭittal pōṉṟa toḻilkaḷukkāka avarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭārkaḷ, avarkaḷiṉ aṟivum avvaḷavukkē iruntatu. avarkaḷ uṭaṟpayiṟci moḻip payiṟci aṟṟavarkaḷākavēyiruntaṉar. vīṭṭukku vēṇṭappaṭum aḷavu pēcciyē avarkaḷ aṟintiruntaṉar. ivarkaḷukkup puṟampākavuḷḷa vilimātar vakuppiṉar āṭamparamāṉa vāḻvu naṭattiṉar. oḻukkamuṭiyavarkaḷukku maṟukkappaṭṭirunta kalvi yum cutantaramum avarkaḷukkuk koṭukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. vīṭṭil vāḻum peṇkaḷ kīḻppaṭivum aṟiyāmiyumuṭiyavarkaḷāy irukkumpaṭi kaṭṭuppaṭuttappaṭṭiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ piḷḷikaḷukkum aṭaṅki yiruttal vēṇṭum. tanti virumpiṉāl piṟanta peṇ kuḻantiyi maṇ cāṭiyiliṭṭu mali aṭivārattil allatu vīti ōrattil iṟakkumpaṭi viṭalām; tāy yātum pēcutal kūṭātu. avaḷ vāḻkkiyiṉiṉṟum otukki vikkappaṭṭa viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷāvaḷ. paḻiya ulakattil aḻivu uṇṭākkik koṇṭirunta koṭiya pōrkaḷāl peṇkaḷ oṭukkamaṭintirukkalām. oru cātiyār pōril veṟṟi peṟṟāl ellā āṭavariyum koṉṟu viṭṭup peṇkaḷ ellōriyum koḷḷip poruḷākak koṇṭu celvatu vaḻakkam. veṟṟipeṟṟa talivar niṉitta taṟku māṟākap peṇkaḷ periya puraṭci uṇṭākkiṉārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ veṟṟiyāḷariṉ celvamāvar. ivarkaḷ atika vēli puripavarkaḷākavum, atika piḷḷikaḷi īṉṟu avarkaḷukkuk koṭuppavarkaḷākavum karutap paṭṭārkaḷ. aṟiya muṭiyātapaṭiyum, cantēkikka muṭiyātapaṭiyum avarkaḷ veṟṟiyāḷarukkup palavīṉatti uṇṭupaṇṇiṉārkaḷ.

aṭimip peṇkaḷ
cila camayaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ tāḻnta nikirōva irattakkalappi ac cātiyiṭattē uṇṭākkiṉārkaḷ, metuvāka avarkaḷ veṟṟiyāḷariṉ valiyi uṟiñciṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kalakañ ceyyavilli eṉpatu uṇmiyē. ivi ellām virivil oṉṟu cērntaṉa. avarkaḷiṉ valimi ōṅkiyiruntatu. avarkaḷ tām eṅkuniṉṟu vantārkaḷō an nāṭṭu mēlāṉa nākarikattik koṇṭu varavilli; kīḻāṉa taṉmikaḷi yum, mūṭanampikkikaḷiyum, tam vāḻkkiyiṉ mattiyilirunta ciṟumi kaḷiyum koṇṭuvantārkaḷ. avarkaḷ aṭimippaṭuvataṉ muṉpum piṉpum aṭittu vikkappaṭṭamiyālum, kalviyiyum, kālattuk kēṟṟa muṉṉēṟṟa muṟikaḷiyum aṟiyāmiyālum, pōrkkālattil piṭikkappaṭṭa peṇkaḷ ik kīḻmikaḷi oru cātiyiṉiṉṟu iṉṉoru cātikkup parappiyiruttal vēṇṭum.

peṇkaḷē paḻiya nampikkikaḷit toṭarpākak kāppāṟṟi vantārkaḷ. avarkaḷ oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāka avikaḷik kōttut toṭarpupaṭutti vaḷarttārkaḷ. avi iṉṟiya mūṭanampikkikaḷum aṟiyamuṭiyāta karuttukaḷumākavuḷḷaṉa. ivarkaḷē kuḻantikaḷukkup putumiyāṉa paḻaṅkatikaḷic coṉṉārkaḷ; tamakku aṉṉiyamum puṉita mallātaṉavumākiyavaṟṟi avarkaḷ maṉattiṉiṉṟum nīṅkum paṭi ceytārkaḷ. paḻiya teyva vaḻipāṭukaḷum irattap palikaḷum kiṟittuva matam tōṉṟiya piṉṉum naṭipeṟṟaṉa. maṟimukamāka vēlipuriyum aṭimikaḷē tammuṭaṉ iruntu vēli puriyum makkaḷiyum kīḻē iḻuttiruttal vēṇṭum. avarkaḷiṉ iṭam vīṭātaliṉ avarkaḷ itaṉit tavaṟātu ceytiruttal kūṭum. 

kirēkka nāṭṭup peṇkaḷ poṟkāla nākarikatti aṭintirun tārkaḷāṉāl atu nīṇṭa kālam nilittirukkum. atil māṟutal uṇṭākiyirukkamāṭṭātu. peṇkaḷ vīḻtti uḻakkap paṭṭārkaḷ; ulakam metuvākac ceṉṟatu. iṉṟiya niliyil avarkaḷ nākarikatti nām ivvāṟu māttiram kūṟalām.
airōppiyap peṇkaḷ
pāpilōṉiṉ viḻukikkum kiṟittuva matam tōṉṟuvataṟku miṭiyil peṇkaḷiṉ nili palapaṭikaḷākaviruntaṉa. vaṭa airōppiyar peṇkaḷukku mika uyarnta nili aḷittaṉar; ataṟkuk kāraṇam nāṭṭiṉ veppa nili kāraṇamō, allatu avarkaḷ iṉṉum pōrk kālattit tāṇṭāmiyō teriyavilli. oru āṇ oru peṇṇi maṇakkum vaḻakkam mika nīṇṭa kālattiṉ muṉpē uṇṭāyiṟṟu. carmaṉiya makkaḷuḷ peṇkaḷiṉ ālōcaṉiyē mutaṉmiyākak karutappaṭṭatu. keltiya cātiyiṉar, pōr allatu cāmātāṉattip paṟṟi ālōcippataṟkup peṇkaḷiṉ puttimatiyik kēṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟum, avarkaḷukkum, avar kaḷic cērnta cātiyiṉarukkumiṭiyil māṟupāṭu uṇṭāṉaviṭattil peṇkaḷē naṭuvarkaḷāka niṉṟu camātāṉañ ceytārkaḷeṉṟum puḷuṟṟā eṉṉum varalāṟṟāciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. avarkaḷ haṉipal eṉṉum kātēciya aracaṉōṭu ceytu koṇṭa oppantattil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu piṉvarumāṟu: “keltiyar kātēciyarōṭu piṇaṅka nērntāl speyiṉ nāṭṭiluḷḷa kātēciya āṭcit talivarkaḷum atikārikaḷum ap piṇakkit tīrttu vikka vēṇṭum, kātēciyar keltiyarik kuṟṟañ cāṭṭiṉāl keltiya peṇkaḷ appiṇaktit tīrttu vikka vēṇṭum”. avarkaḷiṉ tīrppu, nallatu, pollātatu, pōr ceyyumpaṭi vaṟpuṟuttiṟṟu allatu camātāṉattik kiyāḷum paṭi tūṇṭiṟṟu eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyātu. tāciṟṟaṅ eṉpār keltiya peṇkaḷ mikap pōr kuṇamuṭiyavarkaḷ eṉak kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār. veṟṟikkālaṅkaḷilēyē avar peṇkaḷip pārttatākak kūṟiyuḷḷār. potu makkaḷiṉ poṟuppu avarkaḷiṭam iruntapōtum, vāḻkkiyil peṇkaḷ vēliyāṭkaḷ pōlavē uḻittārkaḷ; payiriṭṭārkaḷ; āṭavar uṇṭu uṟaṅkiṉārkaḷ.

urōmil kikalantu pōr ceyvatu māttiram āṇkaḷiṉ vēliyāka irukkavilli. aṅku uṟutiyāṉa nākarikam vaḷarntatu. peṇkaḷ caṭṭapūrvamāka matikkattakka niliyip peṟṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷukku cottil niyāyamāṉa urimiyiruntatu. cōmiyā (Soaemia) eṉṉum oru peṇ aracāṅka capiyil iṭam peṟṟiruntāḷ, avaḷ caṭṭattiruttaṅkaḷukkut taṉatu uṭaṉpāṭṭiyō, uṭaṉpāṭiṉmi yiyō terivittāḷ. iṉṟiya araciyal maṉṟaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷ palar ivvāṟu ceykiṉṟaṉar. cōmiyā eṉpavaḷ cakkaravarttiyiṉ tāy. avaḷ maṟṟiya aṅkattavarkaḷiṉ ayalē iruppatu tiṭukkiṭattakkatā yiruntatu. peṇ evaḷāvatu aracāṅka capikkut terinteṭukkappaṭutal ākāteṉṉum caṭṭam virivil niṟivēṟiṟṟu.

peṇkaḷ atika mutaṉmiyuṭiyavarkaḷāṉa pōtum avarkaḷ āṭavariṉ toṭarpip peṟṟu irukka vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ eṉak karutap paṭṭatu. avarkaḷiṉ vīrattip paṟṟiya paḻaṅkatikaḷ ellām, tantikku, kaṇavaṉukku allatu makaṉukkup paṇivāyirukkum katikaḷākamāṟiṉa. avarkaḷ naṉmiyiṉ poruṭṭāvatu tīmiyiṉ poruṭṭāvatu āṭava rāl kuṟikkappaṭātiruppatē avarkaḷiṉ pukaḻkaḷuḷ mēlāṉatākak karutappaṭṭatu.

nākarika kālattil peṇkaḷiṉ tāymi eṉṉum nili kavaṉikkap paṭavilli. peṇ karuvi vaḷarppavaḷākavum karuvuṟpatti āṇukkuriya teṉṟum karutappaṭṭaṉa. muṟkāla yūtar kaṭavuḷ taṅkaḷip peṇkaḷāka vum aṭimikaḷākavum paṭikkāmikkākak kaṭavuḷukkut tuti celuttiṉar.

camayat toṭarpāṉa pōtaṉikaḷ makkaḷ uḷḷattil atikam patiyak kūṭiyaṉavāka viruntaṉa. evvakic camayak koḷkikaḷ tōṉṟiya pōtum avi peṇkaḷik kīḻmippaṭṭavarkaḷākak kūṟiṉa. kiṟittuva matattiṉ ārampa kālak kiṟittava ālayaṅkaḷ peṇkaḷilēyē taṅki yiruntaṉa. avarkaḷē cuṟucuṟuppāṉa matapōtakarkaḷāka viruntaṉar; avarkaḷ atikāramuṭiyavarkaḷāyiruntaṉar. muṟkālak kiṟittuvak kōyilkaḷil peṇkaḷ mutaṉmi peṟṟiruntamiyiṉālēyē ammatattukku aṉukūlam uṇṭāyiṟṟu, piṟkālattil avarkaḷ tamatu mutaṉmiyi iḻantārkaḷ, avarkaḷiṉ nili iruḷaṭintiruntapōtum peṇkaḷ uyiruḷḷavarkaḷākavē karutappaṭṭārkaḷ. kiḻakkut tēcamakkaḷ peṇkaḷ tamatu cuyamuyaṟciyiṉāl kaṭavuḷi aṭital muṭiyāteṉak kūṟiṉārkaḷ.

airōppāvil peṇkaḷ palar perumi peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ, cappō (Sappho) eṉpavaḷ perum pulami peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉāḷ. elicapet iṅkilāntiṉ araciyāka viḷaṅkiṉāḷ. ciṉāyil tōṉṟiya katariṉāḷ, mēriyā ticiyā, pulōṟaṉs niṭṭiṅkēl, cōṉ āp ārk pōṉṟa palar kālattukkuk kālam tōṉṟiṉārkaḷ. avvāṟu tōṉṟiya cilariṉ peyarkaḷē varalāṟṟuḷ nuḻintuḷḷaṉa. ip peṇkaḷiṉ vāḻkki varalāṟukaḷ, peṇkaḷ oṉṟit tāmākac cintittu aṟiya māṭṭātavarkaḷ, uṟutiyaṟṟavarkaḷ, tēvi uṇṭāṉa pōtu vīraṅ koḷḷa māṭṭātavarkaḷ eṉṉum karuttukkaḷip pōkkiṉa. mēṟkut tēca nūlkaḷ peṇkaḷ mika āṟṟal paṭittavarkaḷ eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. kirēkka nāṭakaṅ kaḷilum, cēkspiyar nāṭakaṅkaḷilum peṇkaḷiṉ iyalpāṉa kaṇaṅkaḷ cittirikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kālantōṟum peṇkaḷiṉ muṉṉēṟṟatti nacukka eḻunta pala taṭikaḷāl avarkaḷiṉ vaḷarcci taṭippaṭavilli. peṇkaḷ kīḻniliyil irunta pōtum avarkaḷ tamatu aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷip piṟkālattavariṭattē viṭṭuc cellum valimiyuṭiyavarkaḷāyiruntaṉar.

intiyap peṇkaḷ
kiḻakkut tēcaṅkaḷil ippaṭi irukkavilli. intiyāvil paḻaṅ kālap peṇkaḷ uyarniliyiliruntārkaḷ. ak kālattil peṇkaḷ aracāṅkattilum atikārattilum paṅku āṟṟiṉārkaḷ. āriyaruṭiya maṉu nūlil peṇkaḷiṉ nili mika iḻivupaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷatu. maṉu kūṟum caṭṭaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkum nāṭṭil peṇkaḷ oru pōtum tali eṭukka muṭiyātu. “peṇkaḷ poyyip pōlap pāvamuṭiyavarkaḷ. āṇkaḷi acuttañ ceyvatē peṇkaḷiṉ iyalpu. peṇ eppoḻutumē viṭutaliyaṭin tirukkat takutiyaṟṟavaḷ” eṉpavi pōṉṟa caṭṭaṅkaḷ maṉunūliṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ciṟupiḷḷi maṇam, peṇkaḷi uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟṟutal, peṇkaḷi vīṭṭil aṭittuvittal pōṉṟa vaḻakkamuṭiya nāṭṭil āṇkuḻantikaḷip peṟuvatil māttiram peṇkaḷiṉ perumi taṅkiyiruntatu. putta camayamum mukamatiya camayamum peṇkaḷukku āṟutal aḷikkavilli. acōka maṉṉaṉ (ki.mu.264) putta camayattip parappum nōkkamākap peṇkaḷukkuk kalvi kaṟpikkum oḻuṅku ceytāṉ. pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka vanta māṟupaṭṭa koḷkiyi acōkac cakravarttiyāl taṉatu kālattil māṟṟa muṭiyāmal iruntatu.

cīṉap peṇkaḷ
mukamatiya matam kiḻakkē paraviyapōtu peṇkaḷiṉ nili mikat tāḻvuṟṟatu, mukamatiyar pala nāṭukaḷi veṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ veṟṟi koṇṭa iṭaṅkaḷilellām peṇkaḷiṉ nili mōcamaṭintatu. cīṉāvilē peṇkaḷiṉ nili itē vakiyākaviruntatu. peṇkaḷ utavi yaṟṟavarkaḷākavirukkumpaṭi avarkaḷiṉ kālkaḷ noṇṭi yākkappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl cīṉariṉ peṇkaḷip paṟṟiya karuttu veḷiyākiṉṟatu. uyarnta kuṭumpaṅkaḷiṭiyum celvarkaḷiṭiyum māttiram ivvāṟiruntateṉṟu kūṟutaṟkilli. cīṉa nāṭu muḻumiyilum inniliyiruntatu. nākarikat tiṉaḷavukkē peṇkaḷiṉ nilimiyum iruntateṉṟa karuttum nilavu kiṉṟatu. itaṟkuk koḷkikaḷil māṟṟam aṭiyāta kiḻakkut tēcaṅkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭākak kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mēṟkut tēcattil peṇkaḷ viṭutali aṭintataṟkuk kāraṇam annāṭṭiṉ piṟkāla nākarikap pōkku eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉiṟ cila uṇmikaḷ irukkalām.

piṟpōkkuṭiya peṇkaḷum airōppāvil vāḻntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ veḷiyē ulāviṉārkaḷ. taṅkaḷ naṇparkaḷōṭu kalantārkaḷ; tāmpiṟanta ulakattip paṟṟic ciṟitaḷavu aṟintiruntārkaḷ. kiḻakkut tēcap peṇkaḷ mikak kīḻāṉa orē vaki vāḻkkiyi naṭattiṉārkaḷ; vīṭukaḷiṉ mūlikaḷil aṭittu vikkappaṭṭārkaḷ; iv vakic ciṟumikaḷukkiṭiyē vaḷarnta peṇkaḷiṭam evvāṟu aṟivālum vīrat tālum ciṟanta makkaḷ tōṉṟutal kūṭum? ivvāṟu vaḷarkkappaṭum kuḻantikaḷ tāṉum ulaka naṭiyi evvāṟu aṟital kūṭum?

kiṟittuva matamum peṇkaḷum
peṇkaḷiṉ niliyik koṇṭu nākarikam aṟiyappaṭumāyiṉ nākarikam nīṇṭa kālam kaṇṭikkattakka niliyil iruntatākum. ataṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷum taṇṭaṉikaḷum kiḻakkil mika atikamākaviruntaṉa. aṅkiruntē perumpālum tīya karuttukkaḷ paraviṉa. kiṟittuva matam tōṉṟic cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ piṉ tipettiṉiṉṟum mēṟkē cila tīya karuttukkaḷ paraviṉa. vāḻkki mōcamāka uḷḷa iṭattilēyē tuṟavu vāḻkki tōṉṟum. kiṟittuva vētam tōṉṟuvataṉ muṉ atu aṅku tōṉṟip pārāṭṭappaṭṭatu. peṇkaḷiṉ kaṟpu mikavum ciṟappuṭiyatākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. ik karuttē mēṟkilum mutaṉmi peṟṟatu. camayap paṟṟuḷḷa makkaḷiṭiyē celvākkup peṟṟa eḻuttāḷar, peṇkaḷ iruppatē tūymikkup paṅkam eṉak karutiṉārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ tām peṇ kaḷāyiruppatip paṟṟi nāṇavēṇṭumeṉṟum, peṇṇāyp piṟanta pāvattukkāka avarkaḷ vāḻnāḷ muḻutum tavam ceyya vēṇṭumeṉa vum kūṟiṉārkaḷ. maṟupaṭiyum maṉuviṉ pātikaḷ piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. peṇkaḷ naraka vācaliṉ katavukaḷ; pāvattiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟap paṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ uṭal picācu campantamāṉa paṭippu eṉak kūṟap paṭṭatu. ivvāṟu kūṟuvōr camayattil nampikki koṇṭavarkaḷ eṉak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉar. ivvakik karuttukkaḷ malinta kālattil peṇṇāka viruttal mikavum maṉac cōrvu aḷippatākaviruntatu. kōyilkaḷil peṇkaḷ mutaṉmi peṟṟirunta kālam maṟintatu.

ik karuttiṉpaṭi āṇ cērkkiyip paṟṟiya viruppu mikap pāvamāṉatu; atuvē pāvaṅkaḷellāvaṟṟuḷḷum payaṅkaramāṉatu; atuvē maṉitaṉiṉ vīḻccikkuk kāṇaramāṉatu. peṇkaḷ muḻuvatum āṇ cērkki iṉṟiyiruttalē vāḻkkiyiṉ paṇpu. peṇkaḷ iruppatu ācikkuk kāraṇam. peṇkaḷ veḷiyākak kuṟṟañcāṭṭat takuntavarkaḷ. cāttāṉ peṇ vaṭivu eṭuppatu vaḻakkam. kukikaḷiliruntu tavam ceyyum ñāṉikaḷ cāttāṉi ivvaṭivil paṉ muṟi pārttārkaḷ. peṇṇip paṟṟiya niṉivu āpattāṉatu. peṇ eṉṉum uyir tuṉpatti uṇṭākkuvatu. ivvāṟu peṇkaḷip paṟṟiya karuttu iruntatu.

iyaṟkikku māṟupaṭṭa ik karuttu maṉita iyalpukku ēṟṟatākātu. muṟkālaṅkaḷil naṭantatu pōlavē makkaḷ kaliyāṇam eṉṉum tavaṟil viḻuntārkaḷ. maṉitap piṟappiṉ muṭivik kāṇpataṟku peṇkaḷ ellōrum kaṉṉiyarāyirukkavēṇṭumeṉa koṇṭu varappaṭṭa putiya koḷki, maṉitaṉiṉ iyaṟki, uṇarcci, paḻakkam mutaliyavaṟṟāl etirttu niṟka muṭiyavilli. itaṉ piṉpu oru cātiyiṉar aḻiyāmal iruppatik karutik kaliyāṇañ ceytu koḷḷuvatu pāvamaṉṟu eṉac camayam kūṟiṟṟu. kaṉṉip peṇṇāyiruppatu cuvarkkatti aṭivataṟkuc cariyāṉa vaḻi eṉṟum atuvē uyarnta nili eṉṟum karutappaṭṭaṉa. peṇkaḷ pāvattiṉ uruvam eṉṉum karuttu maṟiyat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. āṉāl ataṉ ciṉṉaṅkaḷ cila iṉṟum kāṇappaṭāmal illi, avarkaḷ kurumārāvataṟkut taṭi, paricuttamāṉa poruḷkaḷukku eṭṭi niṟpatu pōṉ ṟaṉa iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. peṇkaḷiṉ nili mika mōcamāyirunta kālattum peṇ tuṟavikaḷ iruntārkaḷ; avarkaḷ mariyāti ceyyap paṭṭārkaḷ. cātāraṇa peṇkaḷ iraṅkattakka niliyil iruntārkaḷ.

āṇkaḷum kaliyāṇamiṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṉum karuttum paraviṟṟu. tirumaṇamiṉṟiyiruttalē irupālariṉ ciṟanta koḷkiyākak karutappaṭṭatu. ivvāṟu tirumaṇamiṉṟiyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṉum iyaṟkikku māṟāṉa karuttu, airōppāvil iyaṟkiyāṉa pēṟupēṟṟi aḷittatu. putiya poyyāṉa camaya oḻukkam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. itu paḻiya ulakil nikaḻavilli. mattiya kālattil peṇkaḷatum, āṇkaḷatum tīya oḻukkaṅkaḷ malintaṉa1. oḻukkattilum pārkka oḻukkakkēṭē peritum kāṇappaṭṭatu. itaṟkuk kāraṇam camūkattil tuṟavāyirukkum koḷki paraviyatālākum. mattiya kāla oḻukkak kēṭukaḷ camayak kaṭṭaḷikaḷāl atikappaṭṭaṉa. ciṟitu kālattil peṇkaḷ tāymārāy iruttalē ciṟantatu eṉṉum karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. pirapukkaḷ (Knights) peṇkaḷiṭattil koṇṭa kātalāl pōrukkuc ceṉṟārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ avarkaḷi etir pārttuk koṇṭiruntu tiyal vēli ceytārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ tammi iraṇṭu kaṇkaḷālum nōkkuvatu tamatu vēliyiṉ kūliyeṉa avarkaḷ karutiṉārkaḷ. pirapukkaḷ pukaḻ īṭṭuvataṟku veḷiyē ceṉṟaṉar. peṇkaḷ vīṭṭil taṅki nilaṅkaḷik kavaṉittārkaḷ; vēṭṭi āṭutal, pārvip paṟavikaḷāl paṟavikaḷip piṭittal pōṉṟavikaḷil poḻutip pōkkiṉārkaḷ. itaṉāl vīram vaḷarntatu. vīram nākarikatti uṇṭāk kum āṟṟal eṉak karutappaṭṭatu. mattiya kālattil peṇkaḷip pukaḻum pāṭalkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. itaṉāl peṇkaḷ nili ciṟitu māṟutalaṭintirunta teṉat terikiṟatu. vīra uṇarcciyiṉāl iṉṉoru māṟṟam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. camūkattiṉ poruḷātāra nerukkaṭiyiṉāl aṭakku muṟiyum kaṭumi yākkappaṭṭatu. atu mattiya kālam mutal 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyil nilittatu.
pirāṉcup puraṭci

pirāṉcup puraṭcikkup piṉ uṇṭāyirunta āṭciyil peṇkaḷ tiruptiyuṟavilli. peṇkaḷukku viṭutaliyum, āṇkaḷi otta urimiyum vēṇṭumeṉa koṇṭar ceṭ (Condorcet) vātāṭi vantār. puraṭcik kālattil peṇkaḷ irattakkaḷarikaḷil paṅku koṇṭaṉar. kūṭṭaṅkaḷāka eḻuntu taṅkaḷ kaḻutti vāḷ muṉikkuk koṭuttārkaḷ. avvāṟiruntum avarkaḷ nili māṟavilli. mīṇṭum peṇkaḷ iyakkattukkup pirāṉcup puraṭciyē kāraṇamāyiruntatu. peṇkaḷ iyak kaṅkaḷ payaṉaḷikkavilli. avarkaḷiṉ kiḷarccikaḷ niyāyamāṉavi yalla eṉṟu karutappaṭṭaṉa. āṉāl maṉitaṉukku viṭutali irukka vēṇṭumeṉṉum koḷki ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu. pulavarkaḷ pukaḻum muṟiyil peṇkaḷ vīṭṭil vittuk kākkappaṭṭārkaḷ. putuvaḻiyāṉa poruḷ uṟpatti muṟiyiṉālum, celvam ataṟkēṟpap pirintu paravuva tālum avarkaḷi viṭṭuc celvam ciṟitu ciṟitāka akaṉṟatu. patiṉeṭ ṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭuppakutiyil peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷiṉ irattatti uṟiñci vāḻpavarkaḷāyiṉar. oru māṟṟam uṇṭākāviṭil avarkaḷiṉ vāḻkki payaṉaṟṟatāka viruntatu. ākavē māṟṟa muṇṭākāmal taṭuttal muṭiyātiruntatu. toḻil vaḷarcci, poruḷātāra vaḷarccikaḷip paṟṟi ārāyccikaḷ naṭattappaṭṭaṉa. toḻiṟcaṅkaṅkaḷum, vāṇikac caṅkaṅkaḷum eḻuntu, viḻuntaṉa. toḻiṟcaṅka amippukkaḷ, vāṇikaccaṅka amip pukkaḷip paṟṟiya muyaṟcikaḷ naṭantaṉa. ivikaḷōṭu peṇkaḷ evvaḷavu tūram toṭarpu peṟṟavarkaḷ eṉa niṉittavar yār? peṇkaḷ periya paṭippāḷikaḷākavum, varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷākavum varum variyil in niṉivu oruvarukkum eḻavilli. vīṭṭuvēlikku mēṟpaṭṭa karumaṅ kaḷilum tamakkut taliyiṭa iṭamiruppatākap peṇkaḷ karutiṉārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ paṇivāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṉum koḷki avarkaḷ uḷḷattil tontaravi uṇṭupaṇṇiṟṟu.

kuṭicit toḻilkaḷ vīḻcciyaṭintaṉa. kuṭicit toḻilkaḷik koṇṭu vāḻkki naṭattiya makkaḷ tuṉpattukkuḷḷāṉārkaḷ. peṇ kaḷiṭiyē tōṉṟiya poruḷātāra nerukkaṭiyāl puraṭci uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ivarkaḷiṉ toḻilkaḷ toṭakkattiliruntu kavaṉikkappaṭṭāl ik kālac camūkak kuḻappaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyirā. toḻiṟcālikaḷ vaḷarumpōtu toḻilāḷarukku vīṭukaḷ koṭuppatippaṟṟi eṇṇiyiruntāl toḻiṟ cālikaḷil iṉṟiya niliyip pārkkap palar uḻippārkaḷ.

18ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyilum 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟ pakutiyilum mēlnāṭṭup peṇkaḷ eṉṟumillāta kīḻ niliyilirun tārkaḷ; ak kālattil peṇkaḷ iyakkam eḻuntatu. avviyakkattiṉāl avarkaḷ tamatu niliyi māṟṟik koḷḷavum naṉṉilippaṭuttavum muyaṉṟārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ viṭutali metuvāka nakarntu ceṉṟatu.

āṇkaḷiṉ maṉamāṟṟam
āṭavariṉ maṉam ippoḻutu māṟṟattukku icintu iruntatu. māṟāṉa paḻiya karuttukkaḷ maṟintaṉa. avvāṟiruntum peṇkaḷiṉ viṭutali paṭippaṭiyākavē ceṉṟatu. iraṇṭu talimuṟikaḷil atu aṭintuḷḷa māṟṟatti nōkkiṉ atu kaṟpaṉipōlat tōṉṟum. māṟṟam vicālamāṉa kalvi muṟiyāl tōṉṟiṟṟu.

iṉṟu peṇkaḷ, muṉ maṟintupōṉa cumēriyap peṇkaḷiṉ niliyi aṭintuḷḷārkaḷ. āṉāl poruḷātāra muṟiyil muṉṉēṟu vatu avarkaḷukkuk kaṭiṉamāyuḷḷatu. varuṅkālattil peṇkaḷāl ev vakip payaṉ uṇṭākumeṉṟu nammāl aḷantu kūṟa muṭiyātu. atu naṉmiyākavum, camātāṉak koḷkiyuṭiyatākavum, camūkattukku naṉmi aḷippatāyum irukkumeṉṟu kūṟalām. ceṉṟakāla nikaḻccikaḷi nōkkumpōtu peṇkaḷiṉ kīḻāṉa niliyē ulaka muṉṉēṟṟattukkut taṭiyāyiruntateṉat terikiṉṟatu. iṉṟiya peṇkaḷ āṭavarōṭu kalantu naṭappārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ tāyāka viruppataṟkēṟṟa uṭal vaḷarcci yuḷḷavarkaḷāy iruntāl māttiram pōtum eṉṟum, vēṟuvakiyil avarkaḷukku muṉṉēṟṟam vēṇṭiyatilli eṉṟum niṉippavarkaḷ, peṇkaḷukku muṉṉēṟṟam vēṇṭiyatilli eṉak kūṟuvar.

aṭimikaḷi vittirunta nāṭukaḷil oḻukka campantamāṉavum piṟavum tollikaḷ uṇṭāṉamipōlavē peṇkaḷi aṭakki vippatālum tontaravu uṇṭāṉatu. āṇum peṇṇum tām vāḻum ulakil paṅku peṟṟuḷḷārkaḷ. iruvarum toḻiṟpaṭṭu ulaki iṉṟiya nilikkuk koṇṭu vantārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ varalāṟṟi nām naṉku ārāyntālaṉṟi itaṉi nām naṉku aṟiyamuṭiyātu. iṉṟu peṇkaḷip paṟṟiya varalāṟu ārāyccikku ēṟṟateṉak karutappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu.

teṉ intiya nāṭṭil oru kālattil peṇ āṭci iruntateṉap paḻaṅkatikaḷil paṭikkiṉṟōm. ki.mu.mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻnta mekastaṉis eṉṉum kirēkkar pāṇṭiyanāṭu peṇkaḷālāḷappaṭṭatu eṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. āravalli, cūravalli, pavaḷakkoṭi, alli katikaḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkum paḻaṅkatikaḷ teṉṉāṭṭil oru kālattil peṇkaḷ āṭci iruntateṉpati valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟum kuṉṟu kaḷilum maliccarivukaḷilumāka aṅkumiṅkum vāḻum taṉittuviṭap paṭṭa tirāviṭa makkaḷ perumpālāriṭiyē tāyāṭci vaḻiyē kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu. avarkaḷiṉ cottum peṇvaḻiyiyē cērukiṉṟatu. tāyam eṉṉum paḻantamiḻc colliṉ poruḷum cottut tāyiṭamiruntu varuvatu eṉṉum poruḷiyē tarukiṉṟatu. vētakālattil tirāviṭap peṇkaḷ paṭṭāḷattil cērkkappaṭṭamikku ātāram uṇṭu eṉa varalāṟṟukkārar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. kālam cellaccellat tāyāṭcimuṟi ciṟitu ciṟitāka maṟintu pōkat tantiyāṭci taliyeṭuttatu. āyiṉum peṇkaḷ mariyātiyāka naṭattappaṭṭārkaḷ. maṉiviyik kuṟikkum illāḷ eṉṉum collukku vīṭṭi āḷpavaḷ eṉpatu poruḷ. tiruvaḷḷuvar kālam iṉṟikku iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷ muntiyatu. tirukkuṟaḷil maṉiviyip paṟṟiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉ ṟaṉa. avikaḷik koṇṭu akkālap peṇkaḷiṉ nili evvā ṟiruntateṉa nām aṟital kūṭum.
“teyvan toḻāaḷ koḻunaṟ ṟoḻuteḻuvāḷ
peyyeṉap peyyu maḻi.”
eṉṉum kuṟaḷ, kaṇavaṉukkup peṇ aṭaṅkik kīḻppaṭintu naṭakka vēṇṭumeṉpatik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. piṟkālattavarāṉa oḷaviyārum “kaṟpeṉappaṭuvatu coṟṟiṟam pāmi” eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār.
“aṭiciṟ kiṉiyāḷē yaṉpuṭiyāḷē
paṭicoṟ ṟavaṟāta pāvāy - aṭivaruṭip
piṉṟūṅki muṉṉeḻunta pētiyē pōtiyō
eṉṟūṅku meṉka ṇirā.”
eṉat tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār taṉ maṉivi yiṟantapōtu pāṭiyatāka vaḻaṅkum pāṭalālum akkālattil peṇkaḷ nili evvāṟu iruntateṉa nām uyttaṟiyalām. piṟkālattil “kalleṉṟālum kaṇavaṉ; pulleṉṟālum puruṣaṉ” eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum vaḻaṅkalāyiṟṟu. peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷukku ellā vakiyilum aṭaṅki naṭakka vēṇṭumeṉṉum kaṭṭāyamē piṟ kālattil nilavuvatāyiṟṟu; iṉṟum akkaruttē nilavukiṉṟatu. muṟkālap peṇkaḷiṟ cilar tam kaṇavar iṟantapōtu tīyiṟpāyntu taṟkoli ceytu koṇṭārkaḷ. nāḷ āka āka peṇkaḷ kaṇavaṉōṭu uṭaṉ kaṭṭi ēṟṟappaṭa vēṇṭumeṉṉum kaṭṭāyam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. teṉṉāṭ ṭilum vaṭanāṭṭilum āyirakkaṇakkāṉa peṇkaḷ iṟanta kaṇavarōṭu kaṭṭiyil vittu uyirōṭu koḷuttappaṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kaṭṭiyi viṭṭu eḻuntu ōṭiviṭātapaṭi cila camayaṅkaḷil muḷikaḷ iṟukki muḷi kaḷil kayiṟṟāl kaṭṭappaṭṭārkaḷ. āṅkilar intiya nāṭṭukku vantu peṇkaḷi uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟṟum caṭṭatti oḻikkiṉṟa variyil “peṇṇāyp piṟanta ceṉmampōtum pōtum” eṉap peṇkaḷ alaṟip pulampumpaṭi peṇkaḷ tuṉpattukkāḷāṉārkaḷ. peṇkaḷi uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟṟum vaḻakkatti nēril kaṇṭa mēlnāṭṭavarkaḷ ataṉi urukkamāṉa eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. avarkaḷ eḻutiyatip paṭikkumpōtu namatu uḷḷam karintupōkiṉṟatu. uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟṟuvatil iṉṉoru viṉōtam kavaṉikka vēṇṭiyirukkiṉṟatu. peṇkaḷi uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟṟuvataṟkup pirāmaṇarāl kiriyi muṟikaḷum eḻutivikkappaṭṭaṉa. uṭaṉkaṭṭi purōkitak kiriyikaḷuṭaṉ naṭipeṟṟatu. purōkitarālēyē tī mūṭṭappaṭṭatu. purōkitar ataṟkāka “takkaṇi” eṉṉum peyarāl kūli peṟṟārkaḷ; uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟṟappaṭum peṇṇiṉ poṉṉuṭimi kaḷit tāṉamākap peṟṟārkaḷ. uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟum peṇ, taṉatu uṭampil ettaṉi mayirt tuvāraṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉavō avvaḷavu āṇṭukaḷ cuvarkkattil kaṇavaṉōṭu intirapōkam aṉupavippāḷ eṉṟum purāṇaṅkaḷ eḻutivittaṉar purōkitar. āṅkilar āṭciyāl uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟṟum caṭṭam oḻintapōtu camayam pāḻākiviṭṭatu eṉṟu kaṇṇīr vaṭittatu ippurōkitak kūṭṭam. peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷukkut tāḻntavarkaḷallar; peṇkaḷukku āṇkaḷōṭotta urimi vēṇṭum eṉṉum karuttukkaḷ camīpa kālattiltāṉ namatu nāṭṭil uṇṭāyiṟṟu. itu āṅkilēyak kalviyiṉāl uṇṭāṉatu. peṇkaḷ virumpiṉāl kaṇvaṉik kaliyāṇa nīkkam ceyyalām eṉṉum caṭṭatti iṉṟum palar etirkkiṉṟaṉar. kalvi payiṉṟa peṇkaḷ cilar āṭcic capi aṅkatta varkaḷākavum, piṟa araciṉar uttiyōkattarkaḷākavum, paḷḷikkūṭa āciriyarkaḷākavum iruntapōtum ēṉiya peṇkaḷiṉ nili kavalik kiṭamākavē irukkiṉṟatu. intiya caṉat tokiyil nūṟṟukkup patiṉ mūṉṟu pēr eḻuta vācikkak kaṟṟavarkaḷ eṉak kaṇakkiṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. ip patiṉmūvaril peṇkaḷiṉ eṇ mūṉṟu allatu nālukku atikappaṭātu. in nilimiyil peṇkaḷ nilimi uyarvu eṉa evarum kūṟa muṭiyātu. piṟanāṭukaḷil ārampak kalvi kaṭṭāyamākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. kalviyē makkaḷukkuk kaṇ. peṇkaḷ kalviyaṟivuṭiyavarkaḷākum pōtē peṇkaḷiṉ nili uyarum. mēlnāṭukaḷil peṇkaḷ nili ivvāṟē uyarntatu. paṭitta ovvoru peṇṇum ovvoru paṭitta kuṭumpatti uṇṭākkukiṉṟāḷ. pala kuṭumpaṅkaḷ cērntatē oru camūkam. peṇkaḷ nili uyara vēṇṭumāṉāl peṇkaḷukkuk kaṭṭāyak kalvi vēṇṭum. peṇkaḷ tamatu vāḻkkiccelavukku āṇkaḷiṉ kikiyē etir pārttuk koṇṭirāmal poruḷātāra viṭutali aṭital vēṇṭum. iṉṟu uruciya nāṭṭil naṭappateṉṉa? poruḷātāra muṟiyil peṇkaḷ viṭutali peṟṟāl avarkaḷ āṭavarukku aṭaṅki naṭakka vēṇṭumeṉ ṉum kaṭṭāyam orupōtum irukkamāṭṭātu. iṉṟu tāmē poruḷ campātikkak kūṭiya peṇkaḷukku avvāṟu poruḷīṭṭa muṭiyāta kaṇavar kīḻppaṭintu oḻukutali nām kaṇṇārap pārkkiṉṟōmallavā!

peṇkaḷip paṟṟiya caṭṭaṅkaḷ
muṟkālattil peṇkaḷ campantamāka uṇṭāyirunta caṭṭaṅkaḷ cila vaṟṟip paṟṟip paṭippōm. 1epirēyar āṇ piṟappatip pōlap peṇ piṟappati makiḻccikkuriyatākak koḷḷavilli. tanti avaḷit taṉatu kaṭaṉukkāka viṟṟuviṭalām. avaṉ virumpiṉāl avaḷi vili mātu ākkalām. ivviti piṟkālattil taṭukkappaṭṭatu. kuruviṉ makaḷ vitaviyāṉāl tantiyiṉ vīṭṭukkut tirumpi vantāḷ. kaliyāṇa niccayam ceyyappaṭṭa peṇ oruvariṉ maṉiviyākak karutappaṭṭāḷ. kaliyāṇa niccayam ceyyappaṭṭa peṇṇip palavantañ ceytavaṉ maraṇataṇṭaṉi aṭintāṉ. kaliyāṇa niccayam ceyyappaṭāta peṇṇi palavantam ceytavaṉukkup paṇattaṇṭam vitikkappaṭṭatu. tirumaṇattiṉ pōtu peṇṇiṉ tanti avaḷukkuc cila vekumati aḷittāṉ. avi aṭimikaḷum cottukkaḷumāyiruntaṉa. ivikaḷē avaḷuṭiya cītaṉam. peṇṇik koṭuppataṟku avaḷ tanti kaṇavaṉiṭamiruntu poruḷ kēṭṭāṉ. kaliyāṇattiṉpōtu peṇṇukku mukkāṭu iṭap paṭṭatu.

kaṉṉimi peritum matikkappaṭṭatu. oruvaṉ oru peṇṇiṉ kaṉṉimiyi aḻittāl ataṟku avaṉ naṣṭa īṭu koṭukka vēṇṭum. kaṉṉimi aḻikkappaṭṭa peṇ maṇamuṭittuk koḷvatu kaṭiṉamāka viruntatu. peṇ iṭum kūccal maṟṟavarkaḷukkuk kēṭkakkūṭiya paṭṭi ṉaṅkaḷil peṇ palavantañ ceyyappaṭṭāl peṇ akkuṟṟattukkup poṟuppāvāḷ. taṉippaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil avvāṟu ceyyappaṭṭāl ak kuṟṟam kuṟivākak karutappaṭṭatu. peṇṇip palavantam ceyvatu oḻukkat tavaṟākak karutappaṭavilli; tiruṭṭuk kuṟṟamākak karutappaṭ ṭatu. peṇṇiṉ kaṉṉimi campantamākak kaṇavaṉ poyyākak kuṟṟañ cāṭṭiṉāl avaṉukku nūṟu ‘cekkal’ taṇṭam vitikkappaṭṭatu; kuṟṟam uṟutippaṭṭāl avaḷukku maraṇa taṇṭaṉi vitikkappaṭṭatu. epirēyariṉ talimikkuru kaṉṉip peṇkaḷi māttiram maṇakkalām. kuruviṉ makaḷ oḻukkan tavaṟiṉāl maraṇa taṇṭaṉi aṭintāḷ. pōril piṭikkappaṭṭa peṇkaḷ maṉiviyarākkappaṭṭārkaḷ. peṇṇiṉ cītaṉap poruḷkaḷākavum ivarkaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭārkaḷ. maṉivi piḷḷi yillātiruntāl pōril piṭikkappaṭṭa peṇkaḷil oruttiyi avaḷ (maṉivi) kaṇavaṉukku maṉiviyākkalām. avaḷ mūlam piṟakkum kuḻantikkuc cottil urimi illi. tanti kaṭaṉukkāka viṟṟa peṇṇi oruvaṉ taṉatu maṉiviyākkik koḷḷalām. avaṉ avaḷit taṉatu makaṉukku maṉiviyākavum aḷikkalām. avaṉ avaḷit taṉatu makaḷpōl naṭatta vēṇṭum.

niyōkam epirēyariṉ paḻiya vaḻakku. uṭaṉ piṟantavaṉiṉ vitavi piḷḷi illātiruntāl avaṉuṭiya cakōtaraṉ avaḷi maṇantāṉ. peṇkaḷ pali celuttum kurumārāka varutal muṭiyātu. camayattōṭu campantam peṟṟa vipacārikaḷ iruntaṉar. kaṭaṉukkākap peṇṇip piṭikkalām. ivvāṟu piṭikkappaṭṭa peṇ pāpilōṉil mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷum. icirēl nāṭṭil āṟu āṇṭukaḷum, acīriyāvil kuṟikkappaṭāta kālamum talivaṉukku ūḻiyam purintārkaḷ.

tantikkup peṇṇiṭattu ellā atikāramum uṇṭu. avaḷ kaṭaṉkāraṉukku aṭivākak koṭukkappaṭalām. aṭivākap peṟṟavaṉ avaḷi avaḷ tanti cakōtaraṉ eṉpavarkaḷiṉ cammatam peṟṟu maṇantu koḷḷalām. tanti makaḷit tāṉ virumpiyavāṟu taṇṭikka lām. peṇṇiṉ talimītu eṇṇeyi ūṟṟi vekumati koṭuttuk kaliyāṇa muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭatu. tantiyē kaṇavaṉit terinteṭut tāṉ. peṇkaḷ mukkāṭiṭṭārkaḷ. mukkāṭiṭuvatu avaḷ oruvaṉukku uriyavaḷ eṉpatiyum avaḷi yārum tīṇṭutal kūṭāteṉpatiyum kuṟittatu. tirumaṇamākāta peṇ mukkāṭiṭṭāl taṇṭikkappaṭṭāḷ. kaṉṉimi vili matikkappaṭṭatu. oruvaṉ oru peṇṇiṉ kaṉṉimiyi aḻittāl avaṉ kaṉṉimiyiṉ viliyippōl mūṉṟu paṅku atikam naṭṭīṭu koṭukka vēṇṭum. maṇañceytu koṇṭavaṉ oru peṇṇip palavantam ceytāl avaṉ maṉivi peṇṇiṉ tanti yiṭam palavantam ceytu koḷḷumpaṭi koṭukkappaṭṭāḷ. avaḷ kaṇava ṉiṭam tirumpic cellāviṭiṉ avaṉtāṉ palavantam ceyta peṇṇi maṇakkalām.

peṇ kaṇavaṉ vīṭṭil iruntāḷ; kaṇavaṉ aṅku iṭiyiṭiyē vantu pōyk koṇṭiruntāṉ; tanti vīṭṭilirunta pōtum avaḷ kaṇavaṉiṉ kaṭaṉukkup poṟuppākaviruntāḷ. kaṭaṉukkāka avaṉ maṉiviyi viṟka muṭiyātu; āṉāl aṭivākak koṭukkalām. kaṇavaṉ tāṉ virumpiyapaṭi maṉiviyit taṇṭikkalām. kaṇavaṉ tūra tēcam ceṉṟu pirintirunta kālattil peṇ iṉṉoruvaṉi maṇantāl, muntiya kaṇavaṉ tirumpi vanta pōtu avaḷiyum piḷḷiyiyum peṟṟuk koḷḷalām. pōrukkuc ceṉṟavaṉ iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷuḷ mīḷāviṭil maṉivi maṟumaṇam ceytu koḷḷalām. ataṉpiṉ kaṇavaṉ tirumpi vantāl avaṉ avaḷip peṟṟuk koḷḷalām; maṟṟavaṉ mūlam piṟanta piḷḷikaḷip peṟṟuk koḷḷa muṭiyātu. kaṉṉip peṇṇip palavantam ceytāl paṇam taṇṭamāka vitikkappaṭṭatu. maṇamāṉavaḷik kaṇavaṉ tāṉ virumpiyavāṟu taṇṭittāṉ. kūṭā naṭattiyuḷḷa peṇṇik kaṇavaṉ kollalām; uṟuppukaḷi veṭṭiviṭalām; virumpiṉāl taṇṭikkātum viṭalām.

tanti taṉatu makaḷik kaṭaṉukkāka viṟkalām allatu kaṭaṉkāra ṉukku vēliyāḷākak koṭukkalām. oruvaṉ oru peṇṇiṉ karuc citiyumpaṭiyāṉa kāyam allatu nōy viḷittāl avaṉuṭiya makaḷiṉ uyir pōkkappaṭṭatu. 1peṇṇiṉ tantiyē avaḷukkuk kaṇavaṉit tēṭiṉāṉ. kaṇavaṉiṉ tantiyiṉ aṉumatiyiṉṟic ceytu koṇṭa maṇam cellupaṭiyākātu. vitavi, kaliyāṇam nīkkappaṭṭavaḷ, kaṇavaṉāl viṭṭuviṭappaṭṭavaḷ ākiyavarkaḷ maṟumaṇam ceytu koḷḷalām. tirumaṇam cellupaṭi āvataṟku eḻuttu mūlam oppantam ceytu koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. tanti peṇṇukkup paṇam, āṭumāṭum, aṭimikaḷ, vīṭṭut taḷapāṭaṅkaḷ, tuṇikaḷ pōṉṟavikaḷic cītaṉamāka aḷittāṉ. cītaṉap poruḷik kaṇavaṉiṉ kaṭaṉkārar tīṇṭa muṭiyātu. avaḷik kaṇavaṉ kaliyāṇa nīkkañceytāl, allatu kaṇavaṉ avaḷi viṭṭup pirintāl, allatu avaḷ vitaviyāṉāl, cītaṉap poruḷ avaḷiṉ vāḻkkip poruṭṭākum. peṇ kaṇavaṉ vīṭṭil vāḻntāḷ. kaṇavaṉ avaḷik kaṭaṉukkākak kaṭaṉāḷiyiṭam mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ cēvikkumpaṭi viṟṟuviṭalām. pāpilōṉil kaṭaṉuk kāka makkaḷ viṟkappaṭṭārkaḷ. pāpilōṉiyap peṇ, tāṉ virumpiyapaṭi kaṇavaṉi kaliyāṇa nīkkam ceyya muṭiyātu. kaliyāṇa nīkkam ceyyap paṭṭa peṇtāṉ virumpiyapaṭi maṟumaṇam ceytu koḷḷalām.

pōrukkuc ceṉṟa kaṇavaṉ mīṇṭu varāviṭṭāl avaḷ iṉṉoruvaṉi maṇakkalām. mīṇṭu vantāl avaṉ pintiya kaṇavaṉ mūlam piṟanta piḷḷikaḷi viṭṭuviṭṭu avaḷip peṟṟuk koḷḷalām. iṉṉoruvaṉiṉ maṉivikku allatu camaya campantappaṭṭa peṇṇukku nērē oruvaṉ virali nīṭṭiṉāl avaṉ neruppiṉāl cuṭappaṭṭāṉ. kaṇavaṉ iṟantapiṉ maṉivi avaṉuṭiya vīṭṭil āyuṭkālam muḻumi yum iruntu vāḻalām. avaḷ ataṉi viṟka muṭiyātu. veḷiccappāṭu (Oracle) kūṟuvōr, mantira vittikkārar, iḻavu vīṭukaḷil aḻuvōr peritum peṇkaḷākavē iruntaṉar. iṟṟikku nālāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ camūkaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷ nili evvāṟiruntateṉpati mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷil vaḻaṅkiya akkālac caṭṭa muṟikaḷ naṉku viḷakkukiṉ ṟaṉa. peṇkaḷ campantamāṉa iṉṟiya camūkac caṭṭaṅkaḷōṭu ivikaḷi oppunōkkuka.

cila kuṟippukaḷ
“peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷ tam āṟṟaliṉ niṟivu. peṇkaḷ kūrntu nōkkum iyalpum vacappaṭuttum aṉpum niṟintavarkaḷ. atikāram, tiriyam, uḻippu eṉpavikaḷiṉ ciṉṉam maṉitaṉ. peṇkaḷ meṉmi, aḻaku, nampikki eṉpavikaḷiṉ ciṉṉam. maṉitavarkkattiṉ vacaṉam āṇ; peṇ ataṉ pāṭal, āṇ āciyiṉāl muyaṟci ceykiṟāṉ; peṇ aṉpiṉāl muyalkiṉṟāḷ. peṇ taṉatu valimiyi irutayattilum, āṇ ataṉi taliyilum vittirukkiṉṟaṉar. kaṭavuḷ aṉpuvaṭivāka irukkiṟār; ākavē nām niṉippatilum pārkka aṉpu koḷḷumpōtu perumi aṭikiṉṟōm. aṉpu uṭimiyāl peṇ āṇilum pārkkak kaṭavuḷukku aṇmiyil irukkiṉṟāḷ. peṇ poṉ mayamāṉa aṉpu ēṇip paṭikaḷil ēṟic celkiṉṟāḷ; maṉitaṉ kīḻē niṟkiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ avaḷukkākavē veḷip poruḷkaḷip paṟṟi ārāycci ceytu aṟikiṉṟāṉ. avaḷ kaṭavuḷil uṟintu avaṉuk kākak kaṭavuḷi vēṇṭukiṟāḷ.” - Women a charm and power p.23.

“uyarnta paṇpukaḷiyum tūymik kuṇattiyum peṇkaḷ mūlam ulakiṟ parappak kaṭavuḷ virumpiṉār eṉṟu nāṉ karutukiṟēṉ. ivvulakiṟ kaṭavuḷ paṭittavikaḷuḷ mikamēlāṉatu uyarnta taṉmikaḷuṭiya peṇkaḷiṉ uḷḷam eṉa nāṉ paḻiya mārṭṭiṉ lūtari oppak karutukiṟēṉ”- heṉrivāṭ pīccar - (Hentry Ward Beecher.) 
“peṇkaḷiṉ kuṟipāṭukaḷ palavaṟṟukku āṇkaḷē kāraṇam; eṅkaḷ mēlāṉa kuṇaṅkaḷukku peṇkaḷē kāraṇam” - cāḷs lemicil (Charles Lemesle).

“vāṉattil naṭcattiraṅkaḷ pāṭalkaḷāka viḷaṅkutal pōla peṇkaḷ ulakiṉ pāṭalkaḷāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉar”-hārkirēv (Hargrave).

“peṇṇē maṉitaṉiṉ uyarnta ūkkaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟukkum viḷakku; avaṉuṭiya eṇṇaṅkaḷ, āṟṟalkaḷ, tiriyam eṉpaṉa avaḷuṭiya aṉpu eṉṉum āṟṟaliruntu tōṉṟiyavi” - jēms ellis (James Ellis).

“peṇṇum āṇum oru kattirikkōliṉ iraṇṭu alakukaḷ pōṉṟavarkaḷ. āṇum peṇṇum cērntu oruvari oruvar niṟivaṭi vikkiṉṟaṉar” - peṉcamiṉ pirāṅkiḷiṉ. (Benjamin Franklin).

nām maṟṟap poruḷkaḷi oppiṭṭup pārttuk kūṟuvatupōla āṇkaḷ mēlāṉavar allatu peṇkaḷ mēlāṉavar eṉṟu mūṭattaṉat tāl kūṟukiṉṟōm. iru vakiyāriṭattum maṟṟavariṭattil illāta ciṟanta taṉmikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa; oruvariṭattil iruppatu maṟṟavariṭattiṟ kāṇappaṭuvatilli. oruvariṭam illātavaṟṟi maṟṟavar kēṭṭup peṟṟuk koḷvatāl niṟivum makiḻcciyum uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa - jāṉ raskiṉ (John Ruskin).

peṇkaḷiṉ mūḷi maṉitaṉiṉ mūḷiyiviṭac ciṟiyateṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟatu. mūḷiyil iraṇṭu taṉmikaḷ uṇṭu eṉa nām aṟital vēṇṭum. avi aḷavum kuṇamum eṉpaṉa. timiṅkilam oṉṟiṉ mūḷi oru maṉitaṉiṉ mūḷiyilum irumaṭaṅku atika pāra muṭiyatu! āyiṉum atu maṉitaṉiṉum pārkka vivēkamuṭiyatu eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭavilli.





















tirumaṇam
tōṟṟuvāy
maṉitaṉuṭiya vāḻkki, pōrāṭṭam malintatāyirukkiṉṟatu. ippōrāṭṭatti āṇ peṇ eṉṉum iruvarum cērntu ātimutal naṭatti varukiṉṟārkaḷ. ivvāṟu irupālārum iṇintu cellum vakiyil kālantōṟum paṇpāṭukaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. āṇum peṇṇum oṉṟu cērntu orē nōkkattuṭaṉ orumittu vāḻum vāḻkkiyē tirumaṇam eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. tirumaṇam evvāṟu tōṉṟiṟṟu? kālattil atu aṭintu vanta māṟutalkaḷ yāvi? iṉṟu tirumaṇattip paṟṟi evvakik karuttukkaḷ nilavukiṉṟaṉa? itip pōṉṟa varalāṟukaḷi aṟital ellōrukkum iṉṟiyamiyātatākiṉṟatu.

tirumaṇattukku ētu
āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupālārukkum oruvari oruvar virum pum iyaṟkiyuṇarcci oṉṟu uṇṭu. itik kātal uṇarcci eṉavum kūṟalām. ivvuṇarcci tirumaṇattukku oru kāraṇamākum. piḷḷi kaḷip peṟutal vēṇṭum eṉṉum iyaṟki uṇarcciyum irupālā riṭiyum uṇṭu. itu tirumaṇattukku maṟṟoru kāraṇamākum. kaṇavaṉ maṉivi eṉṉum muṟiyil oruvarukku oruvar tuṇiyāyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṉum viruppu tirumaṇattukku maṟṟoru ētuvākum.

muṟkālat kātal
muṟkāla maṉitaṉ peṇṇiṭattil poṟāmi koḷḷavilli. irupālārukkum putumuṟiyāṉa kātalum tōṉṟavilli. āṭavaṉ peṇṇiṉ kaṟpik kuṟittō allatu avaḷ peṟum piḷḷiyiṉ tantiyik kuṟittō kavali koḷḷavilli. avaṉuṭiya kātal uṇarcci, vēṟupāṭṭi virumpuvatāyum peṇkaḷ palariṭam celvatāyu miruntatu. muṟkāla maṉitaṉukku virumpiyapaṭi peṇ cērkki vittuk koḷvatu iyalpāyiruntatu. avaṉ tirumaṇam ceytu eppaṭi niliyāka vāḻat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ eṉṉuṅ kēḷvi eḻukiṉṟatu.

*paḻaṅkāla makkaḷē ikkēḷvikku viṭi aḷikkiṟārkaḷ. cippivās (Chippeivas) talivaṉ oruvaṉ pirayāṇi oruvaṉukkuk kūṟi yatu varumāṟu: “peṇkaḷ vēli purivataṟkākap paṭikkappaṭṭiruk kiṟārkaḷ. oruttiyāl iraṇṭu āṇkaḷ iḻukka allatu cumakkakkūṭiya cumiyik koṇṭu cella muṭiyum. avarkaḷ eṅkaḷ kūṭāraṅkaḷi aṭikkiṟārkaḷ; avikaḷip paḻutu pārkkiṟārkaḷ. irākkālaṅkaḷil eṅkaḷi veppamāka vittirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. namatu nāṭṭil peṇkaḷ illāmal nām kālam kaḻikka muṭiyātu. avarkaḷ ellā vēlikaḷiyum ceykiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷāl atika paṇaccelavu illi. avarkaḷ eppoḻutum camiyal ceykiṟārkaḷ. cāppāṭu kiṭiyāta kālaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ tamatu viralkaḷi nakkik koṇṭu tirupti aṭikiṉṟārkaḷ.”

kīḻ niliyiluḷḷa maṉitaṉukku avaṉ kātal uṇarcciyit taṇippatilum pārkkap peṇ, poruḷātāra campantamuṭiyavaḷāyirun tāḷ; avaḷ aṭimiyākavum, virumpi vēli ceyyum āyutamākavum, kaṇavaṉiṉ viliyuḷḷa cottākavum payaṉ aḷittāḷ. muṟkālat tirumaṇattiṉ kāraṇam piḷḷip pēṟaṉṟu; poruḷātāra campantamē. irupālārukkumuḷḷa vēlikaḷ pirikkappaṭṭiruppatāl avarkaḷ oruvaril oruvar taṅkiyirupparāyiṉar. kālattil ivvāṟu oruvarukku oruvar tuṇiyāyiruttal avarkaḷiṉ paṇpāka māṟukiṉṟatu. peṇ vali kuṟintavaḷātaliṉ avaḷ āṭavaṉukkuk kīḻppaṭikiṉṟāḷ. yārum iraṇṭu ecamāṉarukkuk kīḻppaṭintu vēli ceyyamāṭṭārkaḷ. ākavē, ovvoruvaṉum ovvoru peṇṇit taṉatu nlikaḷukkāka vittuk koṇṭāṉ. paḻiya makkaḷ niliyāka ōriṭattil vāḻavilli. āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupālārukkum vēlikaḷ pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ataṉāl āṭavaṉukkut tirumaṇam avaciyamāyiruntatu. avaṉ taṉatu mantikaḷip peṇṇiṉ utaviyiṉṟi vittiruttal muṭiyāmal iruntatu.

peṇ mutalil aṭimiyākiṉṟāḷ. muṟkāla maṇameṉpatu peṇṇi aṭimiyākkutalākum. muṟkāla maṇattippaṟṟi aṟiva taṟkut tiṟavukōl ituvēyākum.

muṟkāla makkaḷ tirumaṇañ ceyyum iraṇṭāvatu nōkkam piḷḷikaḷip peṟutal. immuṟiyilum avaḷ kaṇavaṉiṉ cottu ākiṉṟāḷ tāy eṉṉum taṉmiyiṉāl avaḷ muṉṉilum pārkka matippu uṭiyavaḷākiṉṟāḷ. ivaḷ tāṉ vēliyāḷāyiruppatu mallāmal pala vēliyāḷariyum avaṉukkuk koṭuppavaḷāyumākiṉ ṟāḷ. ippiḷḷikaḷum kaṇavaṉiṉ cottākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. āṇpiḷḷikaḷ utaviyāṭkaḷākiṉṟaṉar. peṇkaḷ viṟkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ.

muṟkāla makkaḷuḷ tirumaṇanōkkam orē vakiyāka iruntatu. mattiya āppirikkāvil pala maṉiviyari vittiruppavaṉ periya celva ṉākak karutappaṭṭāṉ. maṉiviyar kūliyiṉṟi ellā vēlikaḷiyum ceytaṉar. avarkaḷ mutalākavum piḷḷikaḷ mutal aḷikkum vaṭṭi yākavum karutappaṭṭārkaḷ.

iraṇṭāvatu paṭi
kuṭicikaḷ vīṭāka māṟiṉa. kirāmaṅkaḷ nakaraṅkaḷāka māṟiṉa. ippoḻutu aḻakippaṟṟiya eṇṇam uṇṭākiṉṟatu. peṇ vīṭṭil irukkiṟāḷ. avaḷ puriyum varuttamāṉa vēlikaḷi āṇ aṭimikaḷ ceykiṟārkaḷ. avaḷiṉ kaṭamikaḷ vīṭṭu vēliyaḷavil curuṅkukiṉ ṟatu. makkaḷiṭiyē eḻum māṟṟaṅkaḷālum vāṇikattālum celvam uṇṭākiṉṟatu. tiraṇṭa celvamuṭiyavaṉ iṟantapōtu avaṉuṭiya celvatti aṉupavippavaṉ yār eṉṉum kēḷvi eḻukiṉṟatu. avaṉ iratta uṟavuṭiyavarkaḷ ataṉippeṟa muṉvarukiṉṟaṉar. nīṅkaḷ ēṉ maṇam ceykiṟīrkaḷ eṉṟu muṟkāla makkaḷik kēṭṭāl avarkaḷ immaṟumoḻiyiyē aḷippārkaḷ. ākavē, peṇṇiṉ uyarvāṉa paṇpu piḷḷikaḷip peṟuvatu eṉak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. malaṭiyāka virut tal māṉakkēṭu. ākavē, piḷḷiyillātavaḷ tirumaṇa nīkkañ ceyyap paṭṭāḷ. urōmil piḷḷiyillāta tirumaṇattik kulittuviṭumpaṭi caṭṭam iruntatu. iṟantavar vaṇakkam nilavum nāṭukaḷil ivvaḻakku mēlum valiyuṟuttappaṭṭatu.

kātaṟ poṟāmi
paḻiya makkaḷiṭiyē kātal toṭarpāṉa poṟāmi eḻavilli. itaṉi akkāla makkaḷiṭiyē kāṇappaṭṭa vaḻakkaṅkaḷik koṇṭu aṟital kūṭum. akkāla makkaḷ viruntāḷikaḷukkut tam maṉiviyari iraval koṭuttal potu nikaḻcci. āppirikā, amerikkā, airōppā, intiyā, ilaṅki mutaliya ellā nāṭukaḷilum ivvaḻakkam kāṇap paṭṭatu; iṉṟum cila iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. avvāṟu iraval koṭukkat tavaṟuvatu avamatippākak karutappaṭṭatu. cāṇṭvictīvil naṇpar mariyātiyiṉ aṭiyāḷamākat tamatu maṉiviyari māṟṟik koṇṭaṉar. tāpāṉil (Dhaban) viruntāḷi vīṭṭukkāraṉiṉ maṉi viyit taḻuvalām, muttamiṭalām. ic cutantirattik kaṭantu vēṟu yātum nikaḻntāl avaṉkattiyāl kuttappaṭuvāṉ. in nāṭṭiṉ iṉṉoru pakutiyiṉar tamatu maṉiviyaruṭaṉ viruntāḷikaḷ virumpiyavāṟu naṭantu koḷḷumpaṭi avarkaḷi avarkaḷiṭam oppaṭittaṉar.

paḻiya jermaṉ vaḻakkappaṭi vīṭṭukkāraṉiṉ maṉivi virun tāḷiyi avaṉukkāka āyattañ ceyyappaṭṭa paṭukkikku aḻittuc ceṉṟu avaṉukku vēṇṭiya ellā vacatikaḷiyum ceyya vēṇṭum. muṟkālattil viruntāḷiyāka vantavaṉ vīṭṭukkāraṉiṉ maṉiviyiṉ paṭukkiyil avaḷōṭu paṭuttāṉ. patiṉāṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkat tilum netarlāntil matippukkuriya viruntāḷiyiṭam vīṭṭukkāraṉ taṉ maṉiviyi oppuvittu viṭuvatu vaḻakkam; viruntāḷi uṭiyik kaḷiyavum, ataṉi aṇiyavum kaṉṉip peṇkaḷ utavi purintārkaḷ; avarkaḷ avaṉik kuḷippāṭṭi avaṉuṭiya tuṇiyāl uṭampiṉ īrattit tuṭittārkaḷ. vaṭajermaṉiyil maṉivi allatu makaḷiṉ paṭukki viruntāḷikkuk koṭukkappaṭṭatu. hōmar kālattil kirīcil viruntāḷikaḷik kuḷippāṭṭi uṭalukku vācam pūcuvatu kaṉṉip peṇ kaḷiṉ kaṭamiyāka viruntatu. araca kuṭumpaṅkaḷilum ivvāṟu naṭi peṟṟatu. itu muṟkālattiya maṉiviyari iraval koṭukkum vaḻakkattiṉ niḻal ākalām. ivikaḷāl muṟkāla maṉitaṉ ikkāla maṉitaṉiviṭak kātaṟ karumaṅkaḷil vēṟuvakiyāka viruntāṉ eṉat terikiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu vaḻakkaṅkaḷ iruntapōtum viyapicārak kuṟṟattukkup peṇkaḷ taṇṭikkappaṭṭārkaḷ. akkāla āṭavaṉ taṉatu uṭaṉpāṭiṉṟit taṉatu maṉiviyip piṟar tīṇṭutal ākātu eṉa niṉittāṉ. itaṉāl kātal campantamāṉa poṟāmi oru vakiyil iruntateṉat terikiṉṟatu.

peṇkaḷip paṅkāka vittukkoḷvatum piṟpōkkāṉa makka ḷiṭiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. āstirēliyā paḻaṅkuṭikaḷuḷ tamiyaṉ, taṉatu iḷiya cakōtararkaḷukkut taṉatu maṉiviyip paṅkupaṟṟumpaṭi aṉumatippāṉ. iḷiyavarkaḷ maṇam puriyumpōtu tamiyaṉ appeṇ kaḷi paṅku paṟṟuvāṉ. itu muṟkālak kūṭṭu maṇaṅkaḷiṉ ciṉṉamākalām. kūṭṭu maṇam eṉpatu pala āṭavar pala peṇkaḷi orē muṟiyil maṇap patu allatu oru peṇ pala āṭavari ēka kālattil maṇappatu, allatu pala āṭavar cērntu oru peṇṇi maṇappatu. tipēttu, ilaṅki mutaliya nāṭukaḷil cakōtarar palar cērntu oru peṇṇi maṇantārkaḷ; piriṭṭaṉ tīvil uṟaviṉar palar cērntu potuvāṉa oru maṉiviyi vittiruntaṉar. in nikaḻcciyi cūliyar cīcar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.

piṟpōkkuṭiya makkaḷ cilaruḷ āṭavar aṭikkaṭi tamatu maṉivi yari māṟṟik koṇṭārkaḷ; ciṟitu kālattil avarkaḷ maṟupaṭi tattam maṉiviyarip peṟṟārkaḷ. āstirēliyar maṉiviyarik kuṟikkap paṭṭa cila kālat tavaṇiyil māṟṟik koṇṭaṉar. ivvaki vaḻakku pīmā (Beama) eṉappaṭṭatu. eskimōvar naṭpiṉ aṟikuṟiyākat tamatu maṉiviyari oru nāḷ allatu iraṇṭu nāṭkaḷukku māṟṟikkoḷvar. āppirikkāvil puṣmaṉ cātiyāriṉ maṉivi kaṇavaṉiṭattil aṉumati peṟṟu evaruṭaṉāvatu vāḻalām.

maṉiviyarik kūlikku viṭuvatākiya iṉṉoru vaḻakkam irun tatu. āppirikkāvil kāpaṉ (Gabun) makkaḷiṭiyē kaṇavaṉ kūlikkuk kēṭpavarukku maṉiviyik koṭukka āyattamāka viruntāṉ. avvāṟu piṟakkum kuḻantikaḷi avarkaḷ tamatu kuḻantikaḷ pōlakkaruti vaḷarttārkaḷ. ivvaki vaḻakku maṉivi, kaṇavaṉiṉ uṭami eṉṉum karuttup paṟṟit tōṉṟiṟṟu.

oru urōmaṉ kaṇavaṉukkup pōtiya piḷḷikaḷ iruntāl, avaṉ, piḷḷikaḷip peṟa vēṇṭum oruvaṉ piḷḷikaḷip peṟṟuk koḷḷumpaṭi taṉatu maṉiviyi iraval koṭukkavum, tirumpap peṟavum urimi peṟṟiruntāṉ. spārṭṭāvil taṉatu vīṭṭil taṅkiyirukkum viruntāḷi kēṭpiṉ avaṉ aṉupavikkumpaṭi kaṇavaṉ taṉatu maṉiviyik koṭuttāṉ. annāṭṭil kaṇavar palar, tamatu kaṭamiyi ēṟkumpaṭi āṭavar palari vēṇṭiṉar; cila camayaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ āṭavari vīṭṭukku aḻittuc ceṉṟu maṉiviyarukku aṟimukappaṭutti, avarkaḷ mūlam nalla aḻakiya kuḻantikaḷip peṟalām eṉak kūṟiṉar; āteṉsil iruvarukkiṭiyil, paratti orutti, yārukkuc contam eṉṉum piṇakku nērntatu. appōtu avaḷ orunāḷ oruvaṉōṭum, aṭutta nāḷ maṟṟavaṉōṭumāka vāḻa vēṇṭumeṉṉum nalla tīrppu aḷikkappaṭṭatu. paḻiya jermaṉiyil piḷḷiyillāta oruvaṉ taṉṉuṭiya iṭatti ēṟkumpaṭi iṉṉoru vaṉi aṉumatittāṉ. ivvaḻakkam ulūtarālum aṉumatikkappaṭṭatu. ivvaḻakkam piḷḷiyip peṟuvataṟku māttiramaṉṟu.

iṉṉoru vaḻakkam pala nāṭukaḷil iruntu vantatu. atu peṇṇik kaṇavaṉallāta iṉṉoruvaṉ mutal mutal aṉupavittal. immūṉṟāvatu maṉitaṉ perumpālum aracaṉ allatu camayakkuruvāka viruntāṉ. cila camayaṅkaḷil maṇamakaṉiṉ naṇpaṉē ik karumatti niṟivēṟṟiṉāṉ. cila makkaṭ kūṭṭattiṉariṭiyē peṇ, kaṇavaṉiṉ cuṟṟattavarkaḷukku allatu kaliyāṇattukku vanta viruntiṉarkaḷukkel lām taṉatu tayavi aḷittāḷ. ivvaḻakku iṉṟum teṟkuc cilāviya makkaḷiṭiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. koṅkōviluḷḷa lōṉkōvil tirumaṇattiṉ muṉ peṇ potu uṭamiyākak karutappaṭṭāḷ. mēṟku aciriyāvil peṇkaḷ ellōrum milittiyāviṉ kōyilil iruntu tam vāḻnāḷiṉ oru muṟi tamatu kaṟpi viṟka vēṇṭum eṉṉum camayac caṭṭam iruntuvantatu. kamaskaṟkāvil vitavi orutti maṟumaṇam ceyya vēṇṭumāyiṉ avaḷ iṉṉoru āṭavaṉōṭu paṭuttu uṟaṅkavēṇ ṭum; illiyāyiṉ iraṇṭāvatu kaṇavaṉ iṟantu viṭuvāṉ eṉa nampap paṭṭatu.

muṟkālaṅkaḷil viruntukaḷil āṭavarum makaḷirum kaṭṭup pāṭiṉṟic cērntukoḷḷum vaḻakkam iruntuvantatu. iṉṟum makkaḷ cilar āṇṭiṉ cila kālaṅkaḷil ivvakik kāmak kaḷiyāṭṭaṅkaḷ naṭattu vatuṇṭu. akkālattil āṇ peṇ cērkki campantamāṉa kaṭṭuppāṭu nīkkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉ ciṉṉaṅkaḷ iṉṟiya kaḷiyāṭṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

mūṉṟāvatu paṭi
piḷḷikaḷip peṟum ilakkuk kuṟikiṉṟatu. iṟantavar vaṇakkam maṟikiṉṟatu. ulakam makkaḷāl nirampiyirukkiṉṟatu. piḷḷikaḷi atikam peṟuvatāluḷḷa cumi muṉṉilum atikamāyiruntatu. ippoḻutu piḷḷikaḷ tammi mutumiyil tāṅkuvārkaḷ eṉṟu peṟṟōrkaḷ niṉikkavilli. maṉitaṉ atika piḷḷikaḷip peṟāmal irukka muyalkiṉṟāṉ. itaṟku utaviyāṉa vaḻikaḷ atikam atikamāka eḻukiṉṟaṉa. malaṭu kāraṇamākap peṇṇik kaliyāṇa nīkkam ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṉum caṭṭam vaṟpuṟuttappaṭavilli.

kuṭumpa kālattil vaḷarcciyaṭiyātirunta putiya kātal ippoḻutu vaḷarcciyaṭikiṟatu. ippoḻutu kātalē kaliyāṇattukkut tūṇṭutalāka virukkiṉṟatu. ippoḻutu kātalikkappaṭukiṉṟavaḷ vēli ceyyum allatu piḷḷikaḷip peṟum vilaṅkupōl nōkkappaṭavilli. toḻiṟcālikaḷ tōṉṟip poruḷuṟpatti atikarittatu. ākavē, vīṭu kaḷil poruḷātāra nili tāḻvaṭintatu. peṇkaḷ toḻiṟcālikaḷil vēli ceytārkaḷ. tirumaṇattil cītaṉam eṉpatu poruḷātāra campantam. iṉṟum kātalum, maṇaṅkaḷum poruḷātārattiliruntu pirintu celkiṉṟaṉa. kātalē āṇiyum peṇṇiyum niliyākap piṇippataṟku ētuvākiṉṟatu.

muṟkālattil maṇamākātavaḷ kuḻantiyip peṟṟāl avaḷ avamāṉattukkuḷḷāṉāḷ. avaḷ kuṟṟavāḷiyākak karutappaṭṭāḷ. perumpālum avaḷ kuḻantiyik koṉṟāḷ. tantikku ataṉāl avamāṉam nēravilli. iṉṟu aracāṅkam maṇamāṉa tāymārukkup pōlavē maṇamākāta tāymārukkum utavi aḷikkiṉṟatu. muṟkālattil vitavi iraṇṭāvatu maṇam ceytu koḷḷutal avamāṉamākavum māṉaṅkeṭṭa ceyalākavum karutappaṭṭatu. iṉṟu avvāṟu irukkavilli. nirvāṇam, nāṇamiṉmi, karuvaḻippu, kaṭṭuppāṭillāta kaliyāṇam mutaliyaṉa ēṟṟaṉavākak koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. muṟkālattil āṇ peṇ campantamāṉa karumaṅkaḷ pēcuvatu mika irakaciyam eṉak karutappaṭṭatu. iṉṟa āṇ peṇ cērkki campantamākat tōṉṟum nōykaḷip paṟṟiya pēcappaṭum potuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷil uyarniliyiluḷḷa peṇkaḷum camūkamaḷikkiṉṟaṉar.
kaṟpu
āṭavar makaḷiriṭattiṟ koṇṭa kātal campantamāṉa poṟāmi yiṉālēyē kaṟpu uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ivvakip poṟāmi tōṉṟā viṭṭāl kaṟpuc ciṟanta kuṇamākak karutappaṭamāṭṭātu. muṟkālattil maṇamāṉa peṇkaḷiṉ kaṟpu matippukkuriyatāka irukkavilli. ākavē peṇkaḷ viruntiṉarukku iravalaḷikkappaṭṭārkaḷ. piṟpōkkāṉa makkaḷ maṇattukku muṟpaṭṭa kaṉṉimiyiyum matikkavilli. maṇamākāta peṇ, kaṭṭuppāṭaṟṟavaḷāyiruntapōtum maṇamāṉa peṇ kaṭṭuppāṭāka irukka vēṇṭumeṉṉum koḷki iruntu vantatu; maṇamāṉavaḷip pōla maṇamākātavaḷ kaṭṭuppaṭuttappaṭavilli.
pala kūṭṭattāriṭiyē peṇkaḷ maṇattukku muṉ tamatu tayavi āṭavarukku viṟṟu ataṉāl cītaṉam campātittārkaḷ. ilitiya makkaḷ cītaṉañ campātikkum poruṭṭut tamatu putalviyarip parattit toḻilukku aṉumatittārkaḷ eṉṟu eratōtacu kūṟiyuḷḷār. parattit toḻil avarkaḷ maṇamākum variyum naṭipeṟṟatu. mekcikka makkaḷ maṇapparuvamaṭinta peṇkaḷit tamatu kātal mūlam aḻakiya uṭi kaḷic campātikkumpaṭi veḷiyē pōkkiṉārkaḷ. pēlō (Pelu) tīvup peṇkaḷ tamatu kātali āṭavarukku viṟkiṟārkaḷ. nikarakuvā amerikkar parattit toḻilāl cītaṉam campātikkumpaṭi tamatu peṇkaḷi aṉumatittārkaḷ. jappāṉiya tanti paṇamuṭi nērntāl taṉatu kumārattikaḷi civārā eṉṉum kaḷiyāṭṭu viṭutikaḷukkup pōkkiṉāṉ. itu peṇkaḷiṉ matippi evvakiyilum kuṟikkavilli.

cila makkaṭ kūṭṭattiṉar tammiṭiyē vaḷarnta peṇkaḷ kaṉṉiya rāy iruppatil veṭkamaṭintārkaḷ. tirumaṇattukku muṉ avarkaḷ pala riṉ kātaliyarāyiruttal matippukkuriyatāyiruntatu. kuḻantiyip peṟṟut tāṉ malaṭi allaḷ eṉak kāṭṭiya oruttiyiyē annāṭṭu vālipar maṇañ ceytukoḷḷa virumpiṉar.

intiyāvil tēvaṭiyāḷ eṉṉum nāṭṭiyap peṇkaḷ mariyāti peṟṟaṉar; peṟukiṉṟaṉar. tamatu peṟṟōrukku allatu kaṇavaṉukku utavi aḷikkum poruṭṭut tammip parattiyar viṭuti oṉṟukkuc cila āṇṭukaḷ viṟkappaṭṭa jappāṉiyap peṇkaḷ uyarviṉarākak karutap paṭṭārkaḷ. parattiyarōṭu āṭavar cērtalil avarkaḷiṉ kaṟpup paṅkam aṭikiṉṟateṉṟu karutappaṭavilli; atu ciṟupoḻutiya tirumaṇa mākak karutappaṭṭatu. āteṉs paṭṭiṉattil parattiyar palar aracik kuriya matippip peṟṟiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ palarukku uruvac cilikaḷ nāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. cila camayak koḷkikaḷōṭu parattimiyum kalantirun tatu. muṟkālattiṟ camaya campantamāṉa parattimi potuvāka viruntatu. kirīcil apiraṭōyiṟ eṉṉum teyvattiṉ kōyilic cērnta parattiyar viṭutikaḷ iruntaṉa. kōmam eṉṉum kōyilil 6,000 parattiyar iruntaṉar eṉṟu tayatōras kūṟiyuḷḷār.

vāṇikamum toḻilum vaḷarntapōtu celvam vaḷarntatu. ak kālattil maṟṟap paṇṭaṅkaḷi vāṅkuvatippōla maṉiviyari vāṅku vatum vaḻakkamāyiruntatu, vili koṭuttu vāṅkuvatu. peṇṇip paṇṭaṅkaḷpōl ākkiṉālum camūkattil avaḷukkuc cila vāyppukaḷ iruntaṉa. tāṉ aritil muyaṉṟu tēṭi poruḷkaḷip peṇṇukkākak koṭuttamiyāl avaṉ avaḷit tāṉē vittirukka virumpiṉāṉ. ākavē, eṉatu uṉatu eṉṟa vakiyāṉa uṇarcci eḻuntatu. vili koṭuttu vāṅkappaṭṭamiyiṉ peṇ naṉku kavaṉikkappaṭṭāḷ. ava ṉuṭiya cottu avaṉ piḷḷikaḷic cērntatu. taṉ mūlam piṟakkum piḷḷikaḷallāta maṟṟavarkaḷukkut taṉatu cottuc cellutal kūṭāteṉa avaṉ niṉittāṉ.
peṇṇi vili koṭuttu vāṅkutalāl peṇṇiṉ kaṟpil māṟṟam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. maṇamākāta peṇkaḷiṉ niliyiyum māṟutal aṭiyac ceytatu. peṇṇukku oru kuṟitta vili uṇṭu. avvili koṭuttu vāṅkappaṭā viṭṭāl avaḷukkum avaḷ kuṭumpattukkum mariyātik kuṟiveṉak karutappaṭṭatu. vāṇika muṟiyil, oru poru ḷiṉ viliyākak koṭukkappaṭṭa poruḷ perumatippuḷḷatāka māṭ ṭātu. nākarikattiṉ kīḻniliyil ivvakik karuttē uṇṭākiyiruntatu. peṇṇukkākak koṭukkappaṭum poruḷōṭu avaḷ mariyāti iṇikkappaṭṭiruppatākak karutappaṭṭatu. vili koṭuttu vāṅkap paṭāta peṇkaḷ parattiyarākac cila kūṭṭattiṉariṭiyē karutappaṭṭaṉar.

toṭakkattil kaṟpu kīḻāṉa karuttōṭu ārampittateṉat terikiṉ ṟatu. pala kūṭṭattiṉariṭiyē kāṇappaṭum kuḻanti maṇap pēccukkaḷum kaṟpukkuk kāraṇamākum. iraṇṭu kuṭumpaṅkaḷiyum iṇikkum poruṭṭu iḷamiyiṟ kuḻanti maṇaṅkaḷ muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ākavē, maṇamāṉa ciṟumikaḷ maṇamāṉa vaḷarnta peṇkaḷippōlak kaṭṭuppāṭu uṭiyavarkaḷāṉārkaḷ.

āṇ peṇ poṟāmikkup poruḷātāramum oru kāraṇamāka viruntatu. āṭavariṉ toḻilkaḷ vevvēṟu vakikaḷāyiruntaṉa. peṇ vīṭṭu vēli oṉṟiyē kavaṉippavaḷāyiruntāḷ. avaḷ poruḷ īṭṭa villi. avaḷ taṉakku vēṇṭiya ellāvaṟṟiyum kaṇavaṉiṭamiruntu taṉatu kātaliṉ viliyākap peṟṟāḷ. itil iṭiyūṟu nērumpōtu poṟāmi tōṉṟuvatu iyalpu.

āṭavariṉ vēlikaḷ vevvēṟāka iruntamiyāl vēṟu viḷivukaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. āṭavar potuk karumattiṉ poruṭṭu oṉṟu cērvatāl camūkamum āṭciyum tōṉṟiṉa. itaṉāl nāṭṭu amippil māṟutal uṇṭāyiṟṟu. nāṭṭiṉ nalaṉukkāka oḻuṅkāṉa kuṭumpaṅkaḷum, tirumaṇac caṭṭaṅkaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. makkaḷ kuṭikaḷākavum, kulaṅkaḷākavum pirintu vāḻntapōtu maṇamākāta tāymār tāḻntavarākak karutappaṭṭa ṉar; āyiṉum, kuṭumpaṅkaḷ avarkaḷip pātukāttaṉa. kulaṅkaḷ eṉṉuṅ kaṭṭuk kulintatum nāṭu kuṭumpaṅkaḷil taṅkiyiruntatu. ākavē, āṭci orutāra maṇattiyum kaṟpiyum vaṟpuṟutti āṇ peṇ toṭarpāṉa poṟāmiyi vaḷarac ceytatu.

oruvaṉ taṉatu maṉivi iṉṉoruvaṉōṭu maṟivāṉa toṭarpu vittirukkiṟāḷ eṉak kaṇṭavuṭaṉ, avaṉ taṉatu payaṉuḷḷa maṉiviyi iḻantu viṭavum kūṭum eṉap payamaṭintāṉ. avaḷ putiya kātalaṉ poruṭṭuk kaṇavaṉi viṭṭup piriyalām. itu tirumaṇak kuḻappamāva tuṭaṉ avaṉ ēykkappaṭṭavaṉumākiṉṟāṉ. ikkāraṇam paṟṟiyum kaṟpu vaṟpuṟuttappaṭṭatu. uṭampu campantamāṉa veṟuppum āṇ peṇ poṟā mikkuk kāraṇamākum. oruvaṉ kātalikkiṉṟa orutti iṉṉoruva ṉōṭu kaḷḷac cērkki uṭiyavaḷākiṉṟāḷ. kaṇavaṉ taṉatu eṇṇam taṭippaṭuvati uṇarvati māttiramallāmal, tāṉ paṟṟu vittirukkum oru poruḷi iṉṉoruvaṉiṉ veṭkattōṭu iṇittuc cintaṉi ceyyavun tūṇṭappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. oruvaṉ kuṭitta pāttirattil iṉṉoru vaṉ kuṭikka aruvaruppaṭivatōṭum cilar itaṉi uvamippar.

nalla tōṟṟattiṉālum maṉitaṉ iṉpam aṭikiṉṟāṉ. paḻaṅ kāla maṉitaṉ kaṭṭāṉa tēkamum peṇṇukkuriya taṉmikaḷum uḷḷa oruttiyi virumpiṉāṉ. avaṉ peṇṇiṉ naṭi, uṭi, acivu, aḻaku, niṟaṅkaḷippaṟṟip poruṭpaṭuttavilli. ivvāṟu peṇṇip paṟṟi muṟkāla maṉitaṉiṉ uḷḷattil eḻunta uṇarcciyē iṉṟiya putumuṟiyāṉa kātalāka vaḷarcciyaṭintatu.

kaṟpu oru kaṭamiyāka vaḷarntatu
kaṟpu peṇṇik kaṭṭuppaṭuttiṟṟēyaṉṟi āṇik kaṭṭuppaṭutta villi. cila camayaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷiyum kaṭṭuppaṭuttavilli. maṇañ ceyta oruvaṉ āṇtaṉmi illātavaṉāyiruntāl (Impotent) ava ṉuṭiya maṉivi kaṇavaṉiṉ viruppōṭu avaṉuṭiya cakōtaraṉi allatu neruṅkiya naṇpaṉic cēralām eṉa ulūtar kūṟiyuḷḷār. āṇ, āṇ taṉmi uṭiyavaṉāyirunta pōtu peṇ kaṟpāyirukka vēṇṭu meṉṟu koḷḷappaṭṭatu. āṇ campantappaṭṭavaḷavil avaṉ ivvā ṟirukka vēṇṭiyatilli; piṟamātaric cērtal avaṉ vallamikkuṭ paṭṭiruntatu. uyarnta kuṭumpaṅkaḷil avvāṟiruttal avaciyam eṉavum karutappaṭṭatu. mattiya kālaṅkaḷil parattiyar viṭutikaḷ nakara vāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉaveṉṟum karutappaṭṭaṉa; tamatu maṉiviyarallāta peṇkaḷic cērtal oḻukkakkēṭu eṉṟu karutappaṭavilli. kōyiṟ kurumār māttiramallar vicuppāṇṭavarkaḷum parattiyar viṭutikaḷil vari taṇṭiṉārkaḷ. urōmil pōppu parattiyarkaḷiṭamiruntu vari taṇṭiṉār. pattām nūṟṟāṇṭil pōppiṉ kāriyālayattil 20,000 ṭakkaṭ (Dueat) ivvakiyil variyākak kiṭittataṟkup paṟṟuc cīṭṭukaḷ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. jermaṉiyiluḷḷa mēyiṉ eṉṉumiṭattil ‘āṭci vicuppu’ ivvaki vari taṇṭiṉār. potup peṇkaḷ, parattiyarōṭu pōṭṭiyiṭu vatāl tamatu varuvāy kuṟintatippaṟṟi avar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. srās pēkkiṉ vicuppu parattiyar viṭuti oṉṟit toṭakkiṉār.

cila vakuppiṉariṭiyē vilimātar mika matippup peṟṟaṉar. avarkaḷ maṉiviyarilum mēlāṉavarkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉar. apicīṉiyāvilē parattiyar aracaṉ araṇmaṉiyil mika matippuk kuriyavarkaḷāyiruntaṉar. oru nakar allatu mākāṇattiṉar āṭci avar poṟuppil viṭappaṭṭatu. araciyarip pōl uṭuttu avarkaḷ tamatu mattiyil iruppatip pārttu muṟimiyāṉa maṉiviyar perumi koṇṭaṉar.

tantiyippaṟṟi mukkiyamiṉmi
maṉiviyi viruntāḷikaḷukku iraval koṭuttal, tirumaṇamāṉa peṇṇik kaṇavaṉallāta piṟar mutalmutal cērutal pōṉṟavikaḷuk kum maṉivi mūlam piṟakkum piḷḷikaḷukkum toṭarpu irukkavilli. kaṇavar, piṟa āṭavar mūlam tamatu maṉiviyar, piḷḷikaḷip peṟa aṉumatikkum nikaḻccikaḷ pala uṇṭu. avvāṟu piṟakkum piḷḷikaḷik kaṇavaṉ taṉatu piḷḷikaḷākavē pāvikkiṉṟāṉ. eskimōvar tam maṉiviyar tam matakuru mūlam piḷḷikaḷip peṟuvati virumpiṉārkaḷ. mēlāṉa cantatiyip peṟa virumpum arāpiyaṉ oruvaṉ maṉivi pūppu aṭintatum iṉṉoruvaṉit taṉatu maṉiviyōṭu cērntirukkum paṭi viṭuvāṉ. avaḷ viruntāḷi mūlam karuvaṭintāḷ eṉṟu aṟiyu muṉ kaṇavaṉ avaḷit tīṇṭamāṭṭāṉ. peṇ, piḷḷi illātavaḷāṉa pōtu nākarika kālattilum ivvakic ceyalkaḷ nikaḻntaṉa.

paḻiya yūtariṉ tirumaṇac caṭṭapaṭi iṟantupōṉa tamiyaṉiṉ maṉiviyic cērumpaṭi oruvaṉ aṉumatikkappaṭṭāṉ. av vakiyiṟ piṟakkum piḷḷi iṟantavaṉiṉ piḷḷiyākak karutappaṭṭatu. kapīr makka ḷuḷ tantiyiṉ maṉivi avaṉ maraṇattukkuppiṉ avaḷ makaṉukkuc contamāṉāḷ. makaṉ tantiyiṉ maṉiviyit tīṇṭa māṭṭāṉ. avaṉ avaḷi iṉṉoruvaṉōṭu irukkumpaṭi cilakālam aṉumatittāṉ. avaḷ vayiṟṟil piṟakkum piḷḷi tanti mūlam vanta cottākak karutap paṭṭatu. intiyāvil niyōka maṇattiṉāl, piḷḷiyillātavaṉiṉ maṉivi kaṇavaṉ irukkumpōtē avaṉuṭiya cakōtaraṉ mūlam allatu vēṟu uṟaviṉar mūlam piḷḷikaḷip peṟṟāḷ; piḷḷikaḷ kaṇavaṉiṉ piḷḷikaḷākak karutappaṭṭaṉa. ōceṭis (Ossetes) makkaḷiṭiyē oruvaṉ taṉatu āṟu vayatu makaṉukku vaḷarnta peṇṇik kaliyāṇam kaṭṭi vittāṉ. kaṇavaṉiṉ tanti mūlam avaḷ vayiṟṟil piṟakkum piḷḷikaḷ kaṇavaṉiṉ piḷḷikaḷākak karutappaṭṭaṉa. teṉṉintiya piṟpōkkāṉa makkaḷ cilariṭiyum ivvaḻakkam kāṇappaṭu vatit teṉṉintiya kulaṅkaḷum kuṭikaḷum eṉṉum nūlil tēsraṉ kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.

kaṟpiṉ ciṉṉamākiya nāṇam
nāṇam iyaṟkiyāṉa kuṇamaṉṟu. atu piṟkālattil tōṉṟi vaḷarntatu. cila nāṭukaḷil āṇ peṇ cērkki veḷippaṭiyāka nikaḻkiṉṟatu. teṉ kaṭal tīvukaḷ, tākiti pōṉṟa paṟpala iṭaṅkaḷil itu potu nikaḻcci. cila piṟpōkkāṉa makkaḷuḷ āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum uṭiyiṉṟic celkiṉṟaṉar. pakkāri (Bakire) makkaḷik kuṟittu sṭiṉeṉ (Steinen) kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu: “namatu ūravarkaḷukku uṭampil maṟivāṉa iṭameṉpatu kiṭiyātu. maṟivāṉa uṟuppu eṉak kūṟuvatē mūṭattaṉam eṉa avarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. oru āṇiyō peṇṇiyō avarkaḷiṉ piḷḷiyik kāṭṭi avar yār eṉak kēṭṭāl avarkaḷ tamatu irakaciya uṟuppukaḷit toṭṭukkāṭṭi avarkaḷ avikaḷiliruntu tōṉṟiyavarkaḷ eṉak kūṟuvar.” avarkaḷ maṟṟavarkaḷukku muṉ niṉṟu uṇṇa nāṇukiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟavarkaḷ kāṇāmal uṇpatu naṟpaḻakkameṉa avarkaḷāl koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟatu. mukam, kāl, mayir, pātam, vayiṟu eṉpavikaḷil ovvoṉṟik kāṭṭa nāṇum makkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. mukamatiyap peṇkaḷ mukattik kāṭṭamāṭṭārkaḷ. pala makkaḷiṭiyē makaṉ paṉṉiraṇṭu vayatāṉa piṉ tāy avaṉip pārkka aṉumatikkappaṭamāṭṭāḷ. ekiptilē velakīṉ peṇkaḷ āṭavariṉ mattiyil tamatu uṭiyi urintu uṭuppil nāṇa māṭṭārkaḷ. āṉāl, tamatu mukatti maṟṟavarkaḷ pārkkumpaṭi viṭamāṭ ṭārkaḷ. hoṟṟaṉ rōvar eṉṉum āppirikkap peṇkaḷ. yūtap peṇkaḷip pōlat talikku mēl oru tuṇiyip pōrttiruppārkaḷ avarkaḷ tamatu mayiri maṟṟavarkaḷ pārkkumpaṭi orupōtum viṭamāṭ ṭārkaḷ. peluttit tīvil cāmā eṉṉum iṭattup peṇkaḷ tamatu irakiya uṟuppukkaḷi veḷiyē kāṭṭuvatil nāṇamaṭiya māṭṭārkaḷ; koppūḻi veḷiyē kāṭṭuvatil nāṇamaṭivar. cīṉap peṇkaḷ tamatu pātatti veḷiyē kāṭṭa nāṇuvar. pātattik kuṟittup pēcuvatē virumpattakāta ceyalākum. ūkaṇṭāvil oruvaṉ taṉatu kāliṉ ciṟiya pakuti tāṉum veḷiyē teriyumpaṭi aracaṉukku etiril niṉṟāl avaṉ kolit taṇṭaṉi aṭivāṉ; āṉāl, avaṉic cēvikkum makaḷir muṟṟum uṭiyiṉṟi niṉṟaṉar. mattiya āppirikkāvil pala kūṭṭattiṉa riṭiyē peṇkaḷ marakkiḷi oṉṟi aṇivar. atu muṟpakkatti maṟippataṟkup patil piṟpakkatti maṟittatu. immarakkiḷi taṟceya lākat tavaṟi viṭṭāl avarkaḷ peritum nāṇamaṭivar.

amerikka makkaṭ kūṭṭattiṉar palar aṉṉiyarukku muṉ uṭalil niṟaṅkaḷ pūcāmal cellamāṭṭārkaḷ. āṭiyillāmal tiriyum makkaḷukku āṭi uṭuppatu tiṭukkāṭṭam viḷivippatāyirukkiṉṟatu. mukammatiyap peṇkaḷ, mukatti maṟikkāta peṇkaḷit tāḻntavarākak karutuvar. uṭali maṟiyāmal viṭuvatāl tōṉṟum nāṇam uṭi aṇivatālum alaṅkāram ceyvatālum uṇṭāṉateṉat terikiṉṟatu. nāṇattiṉāl uṭi tōṉṟavilli. uṭiyiṉāl nāṇam tōṉṟiṟṟu. uṭiyiṉṟi iruntu veyiṟ kāyum (Sun bath) peṇkaḷ mutalil nimirntu pārkka nāṇuvārkaḷ. paḻakkam uṇṭāṉa piṉpu avarkaḷ maṟṟavarkaḷōṭu naṉṟākak kalantu pēcuvārkaḷ.

kaṟpu matippukkuriyatāṉa pōtu atu uṭal campantamāṉa nāṇat tōṭu iṇittup palappaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ciṟuvar iḷami mutal tamatu cila uṟuppukaḷik kuṟittu nāṇam aṭiyumpaṭi paḻakkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ic caṭṭaṅkaḷi mīṟukiṉṟavarkaḷit taṇṭippatāka araciṉarum payamuṟuttu kiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl iṉṉum nāṇam valiyuṟuttappaṭukiṉṟatu.

mattiya kālattil jermaṉiyil kuṭicikaḷil vāḻpavarkaḷ nirvāṇamākap paṭukkikkuc ceṉṟārkaḷ. ciṟiya vīṭukaḷiluḷḷavarkaḷ orē paṭukkiyil paṭuttārkaḷ. talikkuk kaṭṭik koḷḷum nāṭāmāt tiram irākkāla uṭiyāka viruntatu. ṭeṉmārkkil iravil muṟṟum uṭiyiṉṟi paṭukkikkuc cellum vaḻakkam 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari yil iruntatu. 1658il ṭeṉmārkkukkuc ceṉṟa pōlīs irāṇuva atikāri ṭeṉmārkkiluḷḷavarkaḷ eppaṭip paṭukkikkuc ceṉṟārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ ōviyaṅkaḷ eppaṭi āṭavarum makaḷirum kuḷittārkaḷ eṉpatik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. uṭiyillāta āṇum peṇṇum orē kālattil orē iṭattil niṉṟu kuḷittārkaḷ. ōviyam oṉṟil āṭavaṉ ariyil tuṇikaṭṭi niṟkiṉṟāṉ. peṇ uṭiyiṉṟik kaḻuttil māli aṇintu koṇṭu taliyi maṟittuk koṇṭu niṟkiṉṟāḷ. mattiyakāla uroṭṭic curuḷkaḷ āṇ peṇkaḷiṉ irakaciya uṟuppukkaḷiṉ vaṭivākac ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. avvuṟuppukaḷiṉ peyar kaḷālēyē attiṉpaṇṭaṅkaḷ aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. mariyātiyuḷḷavarkaḷ, paricuttam niṟintavarkaḷ, kurumār, kaṉṉippeṇkaḷ, tantimār, tāy mārkaḷiṉ vāykaḷāl appeyarkaḷ uccarikkappaṭṭaṉa. avvāṟu ceyvatil taṭiyātum eḻavilli.

tantiyiṉ matippu
